### [1] [Classification of quasi-lisse simple affine vertex algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12723)
*Drazen Adamovic,Xuanzhong Dai,Kaiwen Sun*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文对所有有限维复单李代数（finite-dimensional complex simple Lie algebra）$\mathfrak{g}$，完整分类了使简单仿射顶点代数（simple affine vertex algebra）$L_k(\mathfrak{g})$ 为准光滑（quasi-lisse）的非临界水平（noncritical level）$k$，并刻画了相应准光滑水平下的关联簇（associated variety）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简单仿射顶点代数的准光滑性（quasi-lisse 性质）与关联簇的维数、四维超共形代数有深刻联系，但此前对非临界水平缺乏对任意 $\mathfrak{g}$ 的统一分类，因此本文旨在给出完整的非临界水平准光滑性分类。

Method: 将 Fiebig 关于范畴 $\mathcal{O}$（category $\mathcal{O}$）非临界块之间的等价（equivalence）与抛物 Wakimoto 模（parabolic Wakimoto modules）、普适仿射顶点代数同态（homomorphisms from universal affine vertex algebras）的相容性相结合，并利用关联簇中半单点（semisimple points）的刻画，导出约化条件；再借助 Drinfeld–Sokolov 约化（Drinfeld–Sokolov reduction）、奇异向量（singular vectors）及极大理想约化中的关系完成分类。

Result: 证明了：对互素正整数 $p,q$，$L_{-h^\vee+p/q}(\mathfrak{g})$ 准光滑当且仅当 $L_{-h^\vee+p/\gcd(q,r)}(\mathfrak{g})$ 准光滑，其中 $r$ 为 $\mathfrak{g}$ 的系带数（lacing number）；并据此得到所有非临界水平下准光滑性的完整分类，同时确定了秩不超过六的所有 $\mathfrak{g}$ 以及 $E_7$、$E_8$ 在准光滑水平下的关联簇。

Conclusion: 该文给出了任意有限维复单李代数非临界水平准光滑性的完整分类，并明确了部分情形的关联簇，为非临界水平下仿射顶点代数的表示与几何性质提供了统一刻画。

Abstract: We classify all noncritical levels $k$ for which the simple affine vertex algebra $L_k(\mathfrak{g})$ is quasi-lisse, for every finite-dimensional complex simple Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$. We prove that Fiebig's equivalence between the corresponding noncritical blocks of category $\mathcal{O}$ identifies the relevant parabolic Wakimoto modules and the homomorphisms from the corresponding universal affine vertex algebras. Combining this compatibility with a characterization of semisimple points in associated varieties, we establish that for coprime positive integers $p,q$, $L_{-h^\vee+p/q}(\mathfrak{g})$ is quasi-lisse if and only if $L_{-h^\vee+p/\gcd(q,r)}(\mathfrak{g})$ is quasi-lisse, where $r$ is the lacing number of $\mathfrak{g}$. The classification at the resulting levels is obtained using Drinfeld--Sokolov reduction, singular vectors and relations in the reductions of the maximal ideals of $V^k(\mathfrak{g})$. We also determine the associated varieties at the quasi-lisse levels for all $\mathfrak{g}$ of rank at most six and for $E_7$ and $E_8$.

</details>

### [2] [A Poisson-bracket operator for Hamiltonian separability: intrinsic geometry and exact defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12015)
*Vladimir Martinusi*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文引入一个五输入泊松括号算子（five-input Poisson-bracket operator），用以刻画哈密顿-雅可比方程（Hamilton-Jacobi equation）的可加分离性，并据此发展出线性化分离算子与缺陷泛函（defect functional），用于判断分离性、预测分界线分裂、选择坐标以及改进作用量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可加分离（additive separation）依赖于哈密顿量与正则变量的选取，缺乏统一且便于计算的判据；同时坐标选择与作用量估计的精度在星系动力学与卫星理论等场景中具有实际需求，这构成引入新算子的动机。

Method: 构造五输入泊松括号算子，其在正则对上的取值复现 Levi-Civita 可分离性表达式；在辛乘积结构（symplectic product structure）上，其非对角值等于两个扇区哈密顿量场的辛配对，并度量拉格朗日分布的 Frobenius 障碍，其消失给出局部分离定理。算子关于哈密顿量为三次，故微扰展开精确，并定义线性化分离算子与缺陷泛函；对含循环角的哈密顿量，线性化算子除以转子频率平方为 Melnikov 被积函数的角度导数。

Result: 对卫星理论的极-节点正规形式（polar-nodal normal forms）与 Kerr 测地线（经算子在能级集上的协变性）得到闭合形式的缺陷；缺陷在 Euler 双中心问题中唯一非退化地极小化于可分离焦距，并表明 Sanders 与 Vasiliev 的方案是同一 Stäckel 残差的两种加权。数值上，残差选择器结合 Binney 壳层轨道拟合在扁平对数势中使 50 条轨道中的 48 条与 50 条作用量优于中位数方案；算子选择器将 Miyamoto-Nagai 势中 50 条轨道的 90 分位作用量离散度从 2.90% 降至 0.91%。

Conclusion: 该泊松括号算子为可加分离性提供了统一的代数与几何刻画，其缺陷泛函兼具解析可算性与数值有效性，可作为分界线分裂判据、坐标选择准则与作用量估计的改进工具。

Abstract: Additive separation of the Hamilton-Jacobi equation depends on the Hamiltonian and on the canonical variables, and this paper introduces a five-input Poisson-bracket operator whose values on canonical pairs reproduce the Levi-Civita separability expressions. On a symplectic product structure its off-diagonal values are intrinsic, since they equal a symplectic pairing of the sector Hamiltonian fields and measure the Frobenius obstruction of a Lagrangian distribution, and their vanishing yields a local separation theorem. Because the operator is cubic in the Hamiltonian, its perturbation expansion is exact and defines a linearised separability operator and a defect functional. For Hamiltonians with a cyclic angle, the linearised operator divided by the squared rotor frequency is an angle derivative of the Melnikov integrand, so that the defect integrated along a homoclinic orbit decides the first-order splitting of the separatrix. Closed-form defects are obtained for the polar-nodal normal forms of satellite theory and, through a covariance of the operator on level sets, for Kerr geodesics. Used for coordinate selection, the defect singles out the separating focal distance of the Euler two-centre problem as its unique nondegenerate minimum, and for axisymmetric potentials it shows that the prescriptions of Sanders and Vasiliev are two weightings of the same Stäckel residual. The residual selector and Binney's shell-orbit fit improve both actions over the median prescription for 48 and 50 of 50 orbits in a flattened logarithmic potential, whereas the operator selector lowers the 90th-percentile action spread of 50 orbits in a Miyamoto-Nagai potential from 2.90% to 0.91%.

</details>

### [3] [Data-Driven Conservation Laws for Newtonian Systems: Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Eisenhart-Duval Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12401)
*Andronikos Paliathanasis*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Eisenhart-Duval提升（Eisenhart-Duval lift）几何化的物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural network）方法，从数据中重建牛顿动力学系统的对称性与守恒律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在实现数据驱动的牛顿动力学系统对称性与守恒律重建，并借助动力学几何化将对称性条件转化为更易处理的线性方程。

Method: 在Eisenhart-Duval提升下，对称性条件约化为扩展度规的线性Killing方程或共形Killing方程（conformal Killing equations）；结合由数据重建势函数的哈密顿神经网络（Hamiltonian neural network）与求解扩展几何共线（collineations）的随机特征神经网络（random-feature neural network）。由学习得到的向量场构造李代数（Lie algebra），并经由诺特定理（Noether's theorem）导出守恒量；同时计算扩展几何的曲率不变量（curvature invariants）以进行独立一致性检验。

Result: 在一维Ermakov-Pinney方程与一维谐振子（含与不含四次微扰）上测试，并在相空间数据中施加不同噪声水平。Ermakov-Pinney方程中恢复了平直扩展平面的三个Killing向量及相应非局域守恒律；谐振子中确定了构成so(3,2)李代数的十个共形Killing向量，噪声水平可达10%。加入四次项后，被破缺的对称性被识别为近似对称性（approximate symmetries）。

Conclusion: 该方法可在含噪数据下有效恢复牛顿动力学系统的对称性代数与守恒量，并通过曲率不变量提供独立验证，对微扰导致的破缺对称性亦能给出近似刻画。

Abstract: We introduce a Physics-Informed Neural Network approach for the data-driven reconstruction of the symmetries and conservation laws of Newtonian dynamical systems, based on the geometrization of the dynamics through the Eisenhart-Duval lift. Within the Eisenhart-Duval lift, the symmetry conditions reduce to the linear Killing or Conformal Killing equations of the extended metric. We combine a Hamiltonian neural network, which reconstructs the potential function from the data, with a random-feature neural network, which solves the symmetry conditions for the collineations of the extended geometry. From the learned vector fields we construct the Lie algebra and, through Noether's theorem, the corresponding conserved quantities, while the derivation of the curvature invariants for the extended geometry provide allow us to perform an independent consistency test. We apply the method to the one-dimensional Ermakov-Pinney equation and to the one-dimensional harmonic oscillator, with and without a quartic perturbation, by using different level of noise in the phase-space data. For the Ermakov-Pinney equation, the three Killing vectors of the flat extended plane, and the corresponding nonlocal conservation laws, are recovered for, while for the harmonic oscillator, the ten conformal Killing vectors which form the $so(3,2)$ Lie algebra are determined for noise levels up to $10\%$. When the quartic term is added, the broken symmetries are identified as approximate symmetries.

</details>

### [4] [A new butterfly in the meadow: magnetic lattice graphs with a circulant vertex coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10767)
*Pavel Exner,Jan Pekař*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文分析在均匀磁场（rational flux，有理通量）下量子正方格图（quantum square lattice graph）的谱结构，覆盖顶点耦合（vertex coupling）为循环型（circulant）且标度不变（scale-invariant）的全部非平凡情形，特别关注破坏时间反演不变性（time reversal invariance）的动力学情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于：在保持循环型与标度不变性的顶点耦合框架下，系统讨论磁场中正方格图的量子动力学，尤其是识别那些违反时间反演不变性的耦合情形，以厘清该类图模型谱行为的分类。

Method: 方法为对正方格图（square lattice graph）施加以每个格面（plaquette）内有理通量的均匀磁场（homogeneous magnetic field），考察顶点耦合为循环型（circulant）且标度不变（scale-invariant）的所有非平凡情形，并对其谱（spectrum）进行分类。

Result: 除两个边缘部分解耦（partially decoupled edges）的情形外，其余十二个情形分成两个谱类（spectral class）；其中第二类呈现出此前未知的蝶形图案（butterfly pattern）。

Conclusion: 结论表明：该类耦合的谱性质仅由少数谱类刻画，且第二谱类对应一种新型蝶形谱图案，为磁场下格图量子谱研究提供了新的情形分类与结构。

Abstract: We analyze the spectrum of quantum square lattice graph exposed to a homogeneous magnetic field with a rational flux through plaquette, assuming all nontrivial cases of the vertex coupling which is circulant and scale-invariant, with a particular attention to those of them that give rise to a dynamics violating the time reversal invariance. Except for two cases with partially decoupled edges, the remaining twelve split into two spectral classes; the second one offers a hitherto unknown butterfly pattern.

</details>

### [5] [Thouless pumping and Haldane-model topology in a driven extended SSH chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10507)
*Zhen Dong,Xin-Lei Zhao,Yi Zheng*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作在一维扩展Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）模型（含可调次近邻跃迁）中研究Thouless泵浦，借助Bloch空间中的闭合回路图像确定合成能带的Chern数（Chern number），并证明Haldane模型可通过以泵浦时间替代准动量映射到受驱扩展SSH链，从而用一维量子化电荷泵浦读出二维拓扑不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thouless泵浦提供通过一维晶格中的量子化输运探测Chern拓扑的有效途径，本文旨在扩展这一框架，探索其在具有次近邻跃迁的一维模型中的实现，并建立与二维拓扑模型之间的对应关系。

Method: 采用扩展SSH模型并引入可调的次近邻跃迁（next-nearest-neighbor hoppings）；利用Bloch空间中的图形表示构造泵浦方案，其中Bloch矢量随时间演化描出闭合回路；分别对满带情形和由Bloch振荡（Bloch oscillations）驱动的玻色子波包演示量子泵浦；并通过以泵浦时间替换准动量，将Haldane模型映射为受驱扩展SSH链。

Result: 合成能带的Chern数由Bloch矢量闭合回路的运动确定；在满带与Bloch振荡驱动的玻色子波包两种情形下均演示了量子泵浦现象；映射关系表明二维模型的Chern数可由一维泵浦协议读出，体现为量子化电荷泵浦。

Conclusion: 一维泵浦协议可作为探测高维拓扑（higher-dimensional topology）的可及途径，适用于光子（photonic）与冷原子（cold-atom）平台等实验体系。

Abstract: Thouless pumping provides a powerful route for probing Chern topology through quantized motion in one-dimensional lattices. We investigate such a topological pump in an extended Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model with tunable next-nearest-neighbor hoppings. A graphical representation in Bloch space is used to construct pumping schemes. In this framework, the Bloch vector traces a closed loop that evolves in time. The Chern number of the synthetic band is then determined by the motion of this loop. Quantum pumping phenomena are demonstrated for a filled band and for a bosonic wave packet driven by Bloch oscillations. We further show that the Haldane model can be mapped to the driven extended SSH chain by replacing a quasimomentum by the pumping time. The quantized charge pump indicates that the Chern number of a two-dimensional model can be read out from a one-dimensional pumping protocol, offering an accessible route for detecting higher-dimensional topology, such as in photonic and cold-atom platforms.

</details>


### [6] [Three-dimensional multipole states of the Bose-Einstein condensate of polar molecules in a shallow two-dimensional trapping potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10896)
*S. Sanjay,S. Saravana Veni,Boris A. Malomed*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究极性分子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中三维拉长多极量子液滴（quantum droplets, QDs）的形成、稳定性与动力学，发现其稳定性受囚禁势强度、分子数与涡旋输入阶数共同调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究长程偶极相互作用与囚禁几何如何影响三维拉长多极量子液滴的形成与结构稳定性，特别是高阶多极态的可行条件。

Method: 以含偶极-偶极相互作用与超越平均场Lee-Huang-Yang（LHY）修正的扩展Gross-Pitaevskii（GP）方程建模，凝聚体受二维谐波势与中心高斯势垒组合囚禁；采用虚时传播（imaginary-time-propagation, ITP）方法，从涡旋度$S\leq 2$及$S\leq 6$的初始态生成$n=2S$瓣的多极液滴。

Result: 弱、强囚禁下均可由$S\leq 2$输入获得稳定的多极态且不携带角动量；高阶态（$S\geq 3$至$S=6$，即12瓣）仅在强囚禁且分子数$N$超过阈值时稳定。化学势、单粒子能量与峰值密度随$N$和$S$变化；当$N$低于另一阈值时，最小能量（基）态由$S=1$而非$S=0$的输入生成。微扰演化显示，满足能量稳定性判据的液滴中仅部分真正稳定，高阶态易失稳或弛豫为低阶态。

Conclusion: 囚禁势、偶极相互作用各向异性与LHY诱导的排斥共同决定多极量子液滴的形成与结构稳定性，能量稳定性判据不足以保证动力学稳定性。

Abstract: We investigate the formation, stability, and dynamics of three-dimensional (3D) elongated multipole quantum droplets (QDs) in a Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) of polar molecules with long-range interactions between molecular electric dipoles. The condensate is confined by a 2D trapping potential, which is a combination of the harmonic-oscillator potential and a central Gaussian potential hill. The system is modeled by an extended Gross-Pitaevskii (GP) equation, incorporating the dipole-dipole interactions and beyond-mean-field Lee-Huang-Yang (LHY) correction, which play a crucial role in stabilizing self-bound QD structures. In regimes of weak and strong confinement, stable multipole states with the number of lobes $n=2S$ are created by means of the imaginary-time-propagation (ITP) method from inputs with vorticity $S\leq 2$. The so created multipole QDs carry no angular momentum. Stable higher-order multipole states can be produced by the ITP method from inputs with $S\geq 3$ up to $S=6$ (i.e., $2S=12$ lobes) only under strong confinement and when the molecule number $N$ exceeds a threshold value. We analyze the dependence of the chemical potential, energy per particle, and peak density on $N$ and $S$ in the weak- and strong-confinement regimes. A noteworthy fact is that, below another threshold value of $N$, the minimum-energy (ground) state is created by the input with $S=1$, rather than $S=0$. Simulations of perturbed evolution reveal that, despite satisfying energetic stability criteria, only a subset of the multipole QDs are truly stable, with higher-order ones tending to develop instabilities or relax toward lower-order states. These results highlight the essential roles of the trapping potential, anisotropy of the dipole-dipole interactions, and LHY-induced repulsion in the formation and structural stability of multipole QDs.

</details>


### [7] [Coherent Conversion of an Interacting Dark-State-Polariton Condensate into a Photonic Bose-Einstein Condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11180)
*G. D. de Moraes Neto,M. A. de Ponte,N. G. de Almeida,Miled H. Y. Moussa*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作提出多模腔方案，在原子-腔耦合撤除时将相互作用的暗态极化激元凝聚体相干转换为受限光子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体，从而在光子上保留相互作用制备阶段的可观测印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用凝聚体在相互作用关闭后仍可保留其制备过程的可观测印记，但对光而言，需将光子凝聚的形成与重新分配能量的材料介质分离，才能进入该区域。

Method: 研究多模腔方案：随原子-腔耦合被移除，相互作用的暗态极化激元凝聚体被相干转换为受限光子凝聚体；结合理想模式保持转换与有限时间少体计算进行分析。

Abstract: Extended emission (EE) lasting hundreds of seconds has been observed in a fraction of short gamma-ray bursts (sGRB) containing both nonthermal and thermal components, and representing a significant puzzle for models involving binary neutron stars (BNSs). Recent GRMHD simulations of BNS mergers found that the late-time fallback is approximately quasi-spherical and motivated an r-process powered thermal model for EE. However, the available radioactive-heating power may be insufficient to sustain the luminosity inferred by such a model. We therefore propose a fallback-fed secondary-jet scenario in which a fraction of the fallback material circularizes on the black-hole equatorial plane and powers the jet, while polar fallback impedes its propagation. Using a semi-analytic toy model, we follow the propagation of the jet-head through the fallback envelope and identify jet-dominated, cocoon-dominated, and choked scenarios. The boundaries of these regimes depend primarily on the circularization fraction $f_{\rm circ}$, on the jet half-opening angle $θ_j$, and on the polar refilling factor $ξ_{\rm pol}$, with the approximate scaling $\widetilde{L}\propto f_{\rm circ}/(ξ_{\rm pol}θ_j^2)$. By post-processing data from BNS-merger simulations we find that the fraction of bound fallback material that avoids prompt capture is $f_{\rm npc}\simeq0.52$, thus providing an upper bound on the circularizable fallback supply. Under suitable prescriptions for nonthermal jet emission and thermal emission from the jet head, the estimated isotropic-equivalent $0.3$--$10$ keV energies reproduce well both the observed EE distribution for the jet-dominated branch and its low-energy end for the cocoon-dominated branch. Overall, these results suggest that fallback-fed secondary jets represent a dynamically feasible and energetically consistent explanation of the EE in sGRBs.

Abstract: Motivated by the observed horizon-scale polarization variability in multi-epoch Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) data, we develop a time-dependent general relativistic polarized ray-tracing framework to study how localized magnetic reconnection near a black hole imprints itself on synchrotron polarization. </details>


### [15] [Long before plunge: Uncovering intermediate-mass black holes through their inspiralling compact object companions with LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12496)
*Jean Maurice Meus,Christian Chapman-Bird,Alberto Vecchio*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文系统刻画了LISA对“小质量致密天体旋入中等质量黑洞（IMBH）”这类极端质量比旋进（EMRI）系统的可探测参数空间，给出探测深度、随时间变化的敏感体积及潜在探测源数量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中等质量黑洞（~10^2–10^5 M_⊙）位于矮星系中心与致密星环境中，观测证据稀缺；LISA被预期提供发现与研究此类天体的全新能力，因而有必要明确其可探测的参数边界。

Method: 针对质量比约10^-5–10^-2、赤道轨道、任意偏心率（eccentricity）的致密双星系统，系统映射LISA可探测的参数空间，并计算探测深度（红移）与敏感时间-体积（sensitivity time-volume）随主黑洞质量的变化。

Result: 对m_1~10^4 M_⊙（10^3 M_⊙）的主黑洞，探测深度达红移~5（~0.1）；敏感时间-体积在m_1=10^5 M_⊙（10^4 M_⊙）时约1–10^3 Gpc^3 yr（10^-2–10^3 Gpc^3 yr），降至m_1=10^3 M_⊙时约10^-4–1 Gpc^3 yr。偏心率对最大探测距离影响弱，但强烈影响可探测的旋进（inspiral）阶段占比。

Conclusion: 总敏感时间-体积的数十个百分点至绝大多数来自未在任务寿命内发生 plunge、处于旋进早期阶段的源；尽管并合率（merger rates）不确定，LISA有望识别数个至~10^3个IMBH，为研究这类难探测天体及其环境开辟新观测途径。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will provide novel capability to discover and study intermediate-mass (~$10^2-10^5 M_\odot$) black holes at the centres of dwarf galaxies and in dense stellar environments. We systematically map out the LISA-detectable parameter space of binaries consisting of a low-mass compact object spiralling into an intermediate-mass black hole (mass ratio ~$10^{-5} - 10^{-2}$) on equatorial orbits with generic eccentricity. The depth of the LISA survey reaches a redshift ~$5$ ($0.1$) for binaries with a primary black hole mass $m_1$ ~$10^4 M_\odot$ ($10^3 M_\odot$). If the orbital companion is a white dwarf, which could be disrupted prior to plunge, LISA's survey depth is well matched to (and, in many cases, exceeds) the one of electro-magnetic surveys. Although the maximum distance at which a source can be detected depends weakly on the orbital eccentricity, the latter strongly affects the portion of the binary inspiral over which a source is detectable. The instrument's sensitivity time-volume varies by many orders of magnitude depending on the binary's component masses: it reaches ~$1 - 10^3 Gpc^3 yr$ (~$10^{-2} - 10^3 Gpc^3 yr$) for the more massive systems with $m_1 = 10^5 M_\odot$ ($10^4 M_\odot$) and decreases to ~$10^{-4} - 1 Gpc^3 yr$ for lighter primary black holes with $m_1 = 10^3 M_\odot$. Importantly, from tens of percent to the overwhelming majority of the total sensitivity time-volume (and therefore number of potential detections) comes from sources that do not plunge during the yr-long mission lifetime and are observed at an earlier stage of their inspiral. Despite the poorly known merger rates of this class of sources, our results show that LISA could identify from a handful to ~$10^3$ intermediate-mass black holes providing a new observational avenue for studying these elusive objects and the environments in which they reside.

</details>

### [16] [Two-dimensional liquid-like order in a confined population of swimming Eudorina colonies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09946)
*Nicholas Tufillaro*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 微泳微生物（swimming microorganisms）在边界累积并形成二维层，其空间统计此前鲜有实测。该研究报告了直径约71 μm的群体绿藻在封闭1–2 mm腔体上壁形成非接触二维排布，其配对相关函数与键取向序分析表明该排布是二维液体（two-dimensional liquid）而非晶体或六角相（hexatic）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游泳微生物在边界处富集是已知现象，但由此形成的边界层的空间统计特征（spatial statistics）很少被定量测量，本文旨在补足这一测量空白并判定该二维排布的相态。

Method: 观测约71 μm的群体绿藻（colonial green algae）种群，在其于封闭1–2 mm腔体刚性上壁累积、保持约105 min的二维非接触排列期间，测量配对相关函数（pair correlation function）、最近邻间距、键取向序参量（bond-orientational order parameter）⟨|ψ6|⟩与取向相关函数g6(r)，并与同面积分数下的硬盘（hard-disc）零模型对照；同时分析单个群体的持续随机游走（persistent random walk）与雷诺数（Reynolds number）。

Result: 第一壳层位于r/D̄=1.73处、g=4.8，远高于硬盘零模型的g=2.1，说明有序化并非仅由排除体积（excluded volume）所致；最近邻间距为1.80±0.13个直径。⟨|ψ6|⟩=0.456（零模型0.385），仅36%的内部群体为六配位；g6(r)在1.5个直径尺度上衰减，η6=1.31，远高于六角相界（hexatic bound）1/4。群体以重取向时间1.4–1.7 s的持续随机游走运动，雷诺数约9×10⁻⁴；127 min时开始解体。基于瑞利数（Rayleigh number）排除了热对流解释。

Conclusion: 该边界层排布被判定为二维液体而非晶体或六角相，且其有序性超出纯排除体积效应。作者指出局限：长度标定仅为名义值（nominal）而非计量学标定，且为单一种群的一次性观测；全部数据与代码已存档。

Abstract: Swimming microorganisms accumulate at boundaries, but the spatial statistics of the resulting layers are rarely measured. We report a population of colonial green algae, of order 71 $μ$m across, that accumulates against the rigid upper wall of a closed 1-2 mm chamber and holds a two-dimensional non-contacting arrangement for about 105 min. The pair correlation function has a first shell at $r/\bar{D} = 1.73$, $g = 4.8$; a hard-disc null at the same area fraction reaches only $g = 2.1$, so the ordering is not excluded volume alone. Nearest-neighbour separation is $1.80 \pm 0.13$ diameters. The bond-orientational order parameter is $\langle|ψ_6|\rangle = 0.456$ against 0.385 for the null, only 36% of interior colonies are six-coordinated, and the orientational correlation $g_6(r)$ decays over 1.5 diameters with $η_6 = 1.31$ against the hexatic bound of 1/4: the arrangement is a two-dimensional liquid, not a crystal or a hexatic. Colonies execute a persistent random walk with reorientation time 1.4-1.7 s at Reynolds number $\sim 9\times 10^{-4}$. Breakup begins at 127 min. Thermal convection is excluded on Rayleigh-number grounds. Caveats: the length calibration is nominal rather than metrological, and this is a single population observed once. All data and code are deposited.

</details>


### [17] [Interception Focusing of Colloid Transport in Granular Porous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09947)
*William P. Johnson,Noah G. Willis,Luis A. Ullauri,Bashar M. Al-Zghoul,Diogo Bolster*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究提出“拦截聚焦（interception focusing）”机制：胶体在颗粒表面二次势阱中沿流线集合运输，被汇聚到不完全混合的流线网络（lamellae）中，从而提高其被下游颗粒拦截的概率与收集效率（collector efficiency, η）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体（colloid）在多孔介质中的迁移与滞留关乎地下水保护与修复，而现有基于溶质（solute）完全混合假设的框架可能不适用于胶体，需要解释非单调滞留剖面与长尾穿透曲线等观测现象。

Method: 基于对胶体在颗粒表面沿二次极小值（secondary minimum）平移的动力学分析，其中较长程范德华吸引（van der Waals, VDW）与较短程双电层排斥（electric double layer, EDL）相互竞争；胶体在后侧滞流区（flow stagnation zones）被排出至主体流体，进而被聚焦进流线网络（incomplete mixing lamellae）。

Result: 拦截聚焦提高了胶体与下游颗粒的拦截概率，即增大收集效率（η）；该机制可解释环境条件下观测到的非单调、非指数型滞留剖面，以及初始淋出后低浓度延展拖尾的非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）特征。

Conclusion: 拦截聚焦是区分胶体与溶质的基本过程，使常被用于溶质的“孔隙尺度完全混合（complete pore scale mixing）”假设不适用于胶体颗粒在多孔介质中的传输。

Abstract: Colloid transport and retention in environmental granular porous media underlie groundwater resource protection from, and remediation using, biological and non-biological nano- and micro-particles (colloids). During transport, colloids periodically intercept grain surfaces and translate along them at separation distances across several tens of nm corresponding to the secondary minimum between the opposing forces of longer-range of van der Waals (VDW) attraction and shorter-range electric double layer (EDL) repulsion. Relatively weak diffusive translation of colloids supports their continued translation in secondary minimum association until they are expelled to bulk fluid at rear flow stagnation zones on grain leeward sides. We herein show that this dynamic focuses colloids into a network of fluid streamlines (incomplete mixing lamellae) that enhances their subsequent likelihood of interception with downgradient grains, i.e., increases the so-called collector efficiency (typically denoted by Greek letter eta). Interception focusing of colloids in granular porous media explains well-observed outcomes of colloid transport under environmental conditions, including nonmonotonic nonexponential profiles of retained colloids and the non-Markovian nature of extended tailing of low colloid concentrations following initial elution. Interception focusing is a fundamental process that distinguishes colloids from solutes and renders inapplicable to colloids the assumption that is often invoked for solutes of complete pore scale mixing in granular porous media.

</details>


### [18] [Rapid Antigen Detection Tests Empowered by Cholesteric Spherical Reflectors for Expanded Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09948)
*Chih-Chieh Chien,Yan-Song Zhang,Jean-François Conrotte,Carole L. Linster,Sivashankar Krishnamoorthy,Jan P. F. Lagerwall*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出以胆甾型球形反射器（Cholesteric Spherical Reflectors, CSRs）作为侧流抗原快速检测（RADTs）的增强型光学标记探针，利用圆偏振反射与背景扣除提升对比度、降低检测限，并借助反射颜色与手性可调实现单次检测中多种疾病的甄别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RADTs在呼吸道疾病即时检测（point-of-care）中价值明确，但读出对比度差，限制了其在病毒载量较低的感染早期阶段的应用；且多数RADTs仅检测单一疾病，阴性结果会使患者对潜在的其他疾病一无所知。

Method: 利用CSRs的圆偏振反射（circularly polarized reflection）实现背景扣除以最大化对比度；通过调节CSR反射颜色及选择右旋或左旋圆偏振反射实现多疾病的光学区分；开发将CSRs与相应抗体功能化的方法，并设计专门适配CSRs作为检测标记的新型RADT。

Result: CSR尺寸虽达数十微米，但并不妨碍病毒分析物将其系连（tether）至抗体包被的检测表面；CSR光学信号强度可定量反映抗原浓度；颜色与手性的可调性支持多疾病的光学甄别。

Conclusion: CSRs有潜力作为RADTs的增强型光学标记，在降低检测所需分析物浓度、提升早期检测能力的同时，为单次检测筛查多种疾病提供可行路径。

Abstract: While Rapid Antigen Detection Tests (RADTs) have proved their value in point-of-care detection of respiratory diseases, their poor-contrast readout limits their use at early stages of infection where viral concentrations are low, and most RADTs test only for one disease, leaving a sick patient ignorant in case of a negative result. We explore the potential of Cholesteric Spherical Reflectors (CSRs) as enhanced optical labeling probes for RADTs, leveraging their circularly polarized reflections to maximize contrast by background subtraction and thereby lower the analyte concentration required for detection. Moreover, we demonstrate how the ease in tuning the CSR reflection color and the freedom to choose between right- and left-handed circularly polarized reflection allow us to design CSRs to distinguish optically between multiple diseases, eventually enabling RADTs capable of screening for a set of multiple diseases in a single test. We develop a method for functionalizing CSRs with appropriate antibodies and we propose a new type of RADT specifically designed for use with CSRs as detection label, demonstrating that the comparatively large size of CSRs (tens of microns in diameter) does not prevent viral analytes to tether them to an antibody-coated test surface. The strength of the optical CSR signal quantitatively reflects the antigen concentration.

</details>


### [19] [Manufacturing Complex Structures Through Interfacial Self-Organization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09950)
*Jacob Garrett,Abhinit Sati,Edward Chien,Emily Whiting,Valeri Frumkin*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一种将目标几何编码为稀疏线框支架（sparse wireframe scaffolds）、借助界面力（interfacial forces）引导液体自组织（self-organization）成形的制造方法，并结合常平均曲率（constant-mean-curvature）曲面近似的计算框架，实现复杂几何的规模化制造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统制造依赖局部材料的沉积、去除或成形（deposition, removal, or shaping），制造成本随几何复杂度显著上升，亟需降低复杂三维结构制造难度的新路径。

Method: 采用计算框架以常平均曲率曲面域近似任意目标几何并提取其边界构建线框支架；在零浮力（neutral buoyancy）条件下向支架内注入可固化液体（curable liquids），使其在界面力作用下自组织为预设几何，随后固化为结构。

Result: 所制结构具有力学各向同性（mechanically isotropic）与光学光滑表面（optically smooth surfaces）；成功制造了复杂解剖模型及含精细分支几何、内部空腔与集成材料的拓扑优化（topology-optimized）结构。

Conclusion: 该方法为复杂几何设计的规模化制造（scalable fabrication）提供了新可能，将几何复杂度从制造负担转化为可由液体自组织承担的编码信息。

Abstract: Conventional manufacturing constructs three-dimensional geometries through localized material deposition, removal, or shaping, with fabrication effort greatly increasing with geometric complexity. Here, we introduce a manufacturing approach in which target geometry is encoded into sparse wireframe scaffolds that guide liquid self-organization through interfacial forces. We apply a computational framework that approximates arbitrary target geometries using constant-mean-curvature surface domains and extracts their boundaries to construct the scaffolds. Under neutral buoyancy conditions, curable liquids injected into these scaffolds self-organize into the prescribed geometries, which are subsequently solidified into mechanically isotropic structures with optically smooth surfaces. We demonstrate the fabrication of complex anatomical models and topology-optimized structures featuring intricate branching geometries, internal cavities, and integrated materials. This approach opens new possibilities for scalable fabrication of complex geometric designs.

</details>


### [20] [Hierarchical Design of Ambidirectional LCE Sheets for Three-State Gaussian Shape Morphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09952)
*Atalaya Milan Wilborn,Gurminder K. Paink,Raphael Kay,Patryk Wasik,Friedrich Stricker,Jacopo Movilli,Shucong Li,Joanna Aizenberg*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作利用多相液晶弹性体（multiphase liquid crystal elastomers, LCEs）作为双向形变构建单元，仅通过单调升温即可使单一平面片材依次呈现负高斯曲率（negative Gaussian curvature）与正高斯曲率（positive Gaussian curvature）的连续形态转换，并在此基础上实现曲率与结构色（structural color）耦合的动态响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯曲率（Gaussian curvature）对不可延展表面是固定的，而可延展材料可借应变调节高斯曲率，从而获得运动、动态显色与可调刚度等能力；但此前要遍历完整的高斯曲率谱系，通常依赖多材料构造（multi-material constructs）或空间变化的刺激场（spatially varied stimulus fields），工艺与调控复杂。

Method: 以多相 LCE 为双向形变单元，利用其在相变（phase transition）过程中先收缩后伸长的顺序响应，设计并研究径向取向（radially aligned）平面 LCE 片材在单调升温下的形变行为；进而考察细观尺度不对称性（mesoscale asymmetry）与宏观几何（macroscale geometry）对曲率幅值和弯曲对称性的影响；最后仿照头足类（cephalopods）将机械形变与动态光学性质耦合的策略，制备结构色 LCE 片材。

Result: 径向取向的平面 LCE 片材随温度单调升高，依次转变为负曲率与正曲率的曲面；细观不对称性与宏观几何可调控曲率幅值与弯曲对称性，表明单一外形因子（single form factor）即可编程出丰富的形变行为库；所制备的结构色 LCE 片材实现了动态高斯曲率与动态变色响应的耦合。

Conclusion: 该策略为无需多材料构造或空间变化刺激场即可获得宽范围动态高斯形貌提供了途径，可用于软体机器人（soft robotics）、可展开系统（deployable systems）与响应性表面（responsive surfaces）等具备多状态形状重构能力的自适应结构。

Abstract: Modulation of surface curvature is a commonly exploited strategy to achieve adaptability within natural and synthetic systems where structure governs function. While Gaussian curvature of inextensible surfaces is fixed, surfaces made of extensible materials can adapts their Gaussian curvature under strain, unlocking capabilities such as locomotion, dynamic coloration, and tunable stiffness. This feature is commonly exploited to develop soft actuators capable of complex functionalities; however, traversing the entire spectrum of Gaussian curvature was previously difficult to achieve without the use of multi-material constructs or spatially varied stimulus fields. In this work, we present a strategy for generating a diverse range of dynamic Gaussian shape morphs using multiphase liquid crystal elastomers (LCEs) as ambidirectionally deforming building blocks. Taking advantage of the materials sequential contraction and elongation during phase transitions, we design and investigate the deformation response of radially aligned flat LCE sheets which consecutively morph into negatively and positively curved flat surfaces in response to monotonically increasing temperature. We then investigate the influence of mesoscale asymmetry and macroscale geometry on curvature magnitude and bending symmetry, thereby demonstrating that a single form factor can be programmed to undergo a rich library of deformation behaviors. Finally, inspired by the strategy of cephalopods to couple mechanical deformability to dynamic optical properties, we fabricate a structurally colored LCE sheet which couples its dynamic Gaussian curvature to a dynamic color-changing response. This approach offers a route toward adaptive structures capable of multistate shape reconfiguration for applications in soft robotics, deployable systems, and responsive surfaces.

</details>


### [21] [A liquid implant that grows into a stable scaffold in situ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09961)
*Wenqiang Li,Qichen Peng,Fuhong Wang,Zhentan Quan,Shengxiang Cai,Zhuoming Zhou,Jin Zhang,Ting Liu,Xuanping Xie,Lin Zhu,Bo Wang,Shuang Wang,Yuan An,Yingtong Zhou,Zirong Liu,Haohui Li,Songshen Li,Ben Wang,Xinjian Yang,Quanshui Zheng,Hongzhang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种无线激活的液态前驱体（liquid precursor），仅在病灶定位后于体内原位构建支架结构，用于血管内栓塞（endovascular embolization）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血管内栓塞材料需同时满足可通过狭窄微导管（microcatheter）与在形态不规则血管病变内形成稳定贴合的封堵这两项相互冲突的要求，现有材料难以兼顾。

Method: 采用含铁微粒与气体微腔（gas microcavities）的镓基液态金属（gallium-based liquid metal），激活前保持可变形、可磁导航（magnetically navigable）与可回收；通过无线感应加热（wireless induction heating）激活内部反应微环境，将气体驱动的微腔生长与新生成镓界面的氧化矿化（oxidative mineralization）耦合，形成多孔自支撑支架（self-supporting scaffold）。

Result: 在动脉瘤（aneurysm）与动静脉畸形（arteriovenous malformation）模型中实现持久封堵与瘤颈愈合，并在猪动脉瘤模型中验证了六个月的稳定性。

Conclusion: “递送后构建”（post-delivery construction）可作为由微创液态前驱体形成稳定治疗性植入物（therapeutic implants）的策略。

Abstract: Endovascular embolization requires materials that can pass through narrow microcatheters yet form stable, conformal occlusions within irregular vascular lesions, but reconciling these competing requirements remains challenging. Here we report a wirelessly activated liquid precursor whose final architecture is constructed only after target localization. A gallium-based liquid metal containing iron microparticles and gas microcavities remains deformable, magnetically navigable, and retrievable before activation. Wireless induction heating activates the internal reactive microenvironment, coupling gas-driven microcavity growth with oxidative mineralization of newly generated gallium interfaces to form a porous, self-supporting scaffold. In aneurysm and arteriovenous malformation models, activation enabled durable occlusion, aneurysm-neck healing, and six-month stability in a porcine aneurysm model. These findings establish post-delivery construction as a strategy for creating stable therapeutic implants from minimally invasive liquid precursors.

</details>


### [22] [Topology and Information in Regularized Transport on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09968)
*Shintaro Yoshizawa*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文研究有限图（finite graph）上两类"质量"几何的相互作用：用于正则化传输的广义熵（generalized entropy）$F_q$ 的信息几何（information geometry），以及由 Baker–Norine Riemann–Roch 定理与雅可比簇（Jacobian）支配的除子（divisor）算术。作者引入"传输缺陷（transport defect）"，给出其上下界与等式判据，并分析正则化拓扑间隙（topological gap）$\Gamma_{q,\gamma}$ 的极限、窗口与正性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于将两类原本分立的几何结构统一起来：一方面是用 $F_q$ 正则化的最优传输（optimal transport），另一方面是图上除子的组合算术（chip-firing 与 Jacobian）。核心问题是：雅可比簇的整性（integrality）约束如何以及在多大程度上真正影响传输，而非仅在实数域上被"抹平"。

Method: 利用 $F_q$ 修正项的仿射性，说明强度为 $\gamma$ 的 $F_q$-正则化等价于温度为 $\gamma/q$ 的 Tsallis 正则化。将 chip-firing 视作在实域上连通等次除子，从而把 Jacobian 的作用归结为整性约束；随后引入传输缺陷 $\delta_{\TV}(D)$，即通过 chip-firing 将 $d$ 个 chips 输运至目标的最坏代价，并证明其上界与下界，同时给出顶点重数（vertex multiplicity）形式的下界和等式判据。进一步定义正则化拓扑间隙 $\Gamma_{q,\gamma}(D,E)$，分析其 $\gamma\to0$ 极限、显式取值窗口、高温斜率及正性条件；并用对偶势（dual potentials）刻画线性系统的凸松弛（convex relaxation），将余项解释为整性间隙（integrality gap）。最后在 $1784$ 个除子类上做精确计算，并在蜂巢格（honeycomb）与 Erdős–Rényi 网络上进行实验。

Result: 证明了当 $g\ge1$ 时 $1/d\le\delta_{\TV}(D)\le(d-r(D))/d=(g-1-r(K-D))/d\le\min(1,g/d)$。正则化拓扑间隙 $\Gamma_{q,\gamma}$ 在 $\gamma\to0$ 时趋于 chip-move 缺陷，落在显式窗口内，其高温斜率由 Tsallis 耦合熵（coupling entropies）给出，且存在正性区域当且仅当 $g\ge1$。最优方案中的零点仅在 $q>1$ 时出现，且机制不同于 $\Gamma$ 的符号变化。精确计算中，$1784$ 个除子类里有 $1769$ 个达到秩界 $(d-r(D))/d$；网络实验中，$10^3\le n\le10^5$ 范围内的传输代价分别与 $n^{1/2}$（蜂窝）和 $\log n$（Erdős–Rényi）形式的增长相符。

Conclusion: 结论是：在实数域上，雅可比簇仅通过整性约束进入传输问题，对偶势精确刻画线性系统的凸松弛，其余项为整性间隙；正则化在 $\gamma\to0$ 时恢复组合缺陷，且其正性区域与图的亏格（genus）$g\ge1$ 严格对应。秩界在绝大多数精确算例中被达到，实验补充验证了正则化方案在两类网络上的传输代价增长行为。

Abstract: We study how two geometries of mass on a finite graph interact: the information geometry of the generalized entropy $F_q$ used for regularized transport, and the arithmetic of divisors governed by the Riemann--Roch theorem of Baker and Norine and by the Jacobian. Because the correction term of $F_q$ is affine, $F_q$-regularization at strength $γ$ is Tsallis regularization at temperature $γ/q$. Over the reals, chip-firing connects all configurations of equal degree, so the Jacobian enters transport only through integrality. We introduce the transport defect, the worst-case cost of steering $d$ chips towards a target by chip-firing, and prove $1/d\leδ_{\TV}(D)\le(d-r(D))/d=(g-1-r(K-D))/d\le\min(1,g/d)$ whenever $g\ge1$; vertex multiplicities give a lower bound and an equality criterion. A regularized topological gap $Γ_{q,γ}(D,E)$ tends to the chip-move defect as $γ\to0$, lies in an explicit window, has a high-temperature slope given by Tsallis coupling entropies, and admits a positive regime if and only if $g\ge1$. Dual potentials certify exactly the convex relaxation of a linear system; the remainder is an integrality gap. Zeros in optimal plans occur only for $q>1$, by a mechanism different from the sign change of $Γ$. Exact computations on $1784$ divisor classes find the rank bound $(d-r(D))/d$ attained in $1769$ of them, an observed frequency. Experiments on honeycomb and Erdős--Rényi networks with $10^2$ to $10^5$ nodes test the statements on regularized plans, not $Γ$; over $10^3\le n\le10^5$ their transport costs are consistent with growth like $n^{1/2}$ and $\log n$, respectively.

</details>

Abstract: The phenomenon of discontinuous shear thickening (DST) is observed in suspensions of solid particles with a very high-volume fraction. An obstacle for the applications of MRS remains the sedimentation and aggregation linked to the high density of the iron microparticles. We have recently found that a small fraction of ferromagnetic particles within a concentrated suspension of calcium carbonate particles is not only sufficient to trigger the transition by application of a magnetic field but also optimum for the amplitude of the jump in viscosity observed in DST. We shall explain this result from stress transmission between the magnetic particles and nonmagnetic ones, both inserted in the percolated network of particles. We will also highlight the role of the polyelectrolyte adsorbed on the surface of the particles in the DST transition through the balance between the repulsive entropic force between two layers of polymer adsorbed on different particles and the magnetic attraction force.

Main category: cond-mat.soft

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米孔阵列电导（electrical conductance）随孔数N的标度关系长期存在分歧，该问题对膜分离（membrane separation）与跨生物膜离子输运等应用至关重要，需厘清分歧根源。

Conclusion: 纳米孔阵列电导的标度分歧可由储液几何统一解释，所提框架可预测电导随孔排布与储液几何的变化，对纳米流体学（nanofluidics）研究与功能器件定向设计具有意义。

Abstract: The scaling of electrical conductance with the number of nanopores, $N$, in an array is of fundamental importance for applications in membrane separation and ion transport across biological membranes, and has been the subject of longstanding disagreement in the field. Combining finite element calculations with theory, we resolve this disagreement for pores with negligible surface charge by showing that two previously conflicting predictions are each correct in their own limiting reservoir geometry: cuboidal reservoirs commensurate with the interpore spacing, for which the conductance scales linearly with $N$, and infinite reservoirs, for which the scaling is sublinear in $N$ for closely spaced pores. We show the transition between these limits with increasing reservoir size and, for infinite reservoirs, we show that a simple analytical model from electrical contact theory is mathematically equivalent to an existing point-charge capacitance model for nanopore arrays and validate its applicability against finite element and method-of-moments calculations. Using this model, we derive a characteristic coupling length, $\ell_\mathrm{c,95}$, the interpore spacing at which the array conductance reaches $95\%$ of that of the same number of independent pores. $\ell_\mathrm{c,95}$ scales linearly with pore diameter $d$, and grows with $N$ as $\sim\log N$ for linear arrays and $\sim\sqrt{N}$ for square arrays, so that pore coupling persists to surprisingly large separations in large arrays. Our work provides a general framework for predicting nanopore array conductance as a function of pore arrangement and reservoir geometry, of importance to nanofluidics research and the targeted design of functional devices.

</details>


### [25] [A transversely isotropic constitutive model for semisoft elasticity in main-chain liquid crystal elastomers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10060)
*Donghao Li,Xin Yi,Fan Feng*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个有限应变、等温的横观各向同性（transversely isotropic）网络弹性模型，通过引入随变形变化的二次“半软”（semisoft）惩罚项，解释单畴主链液晶弹性体（liquid crystal elastomers, LCE）在起始应变后的宽软平台与刚度各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典新古典模型（neo-classical model）在固定链各向异性下预测相等的主杨氏模量，而采用常数半软模量的横观各向同性扩展又对重取向起始与后续应力增长施加了过强约束，难以描述实验观测的宽软平台与有限起始行为。

Method: 构建有限应变、等温弹性模型，将横观各向同性网络弹性与随变形相关的二次半软惩罚相结合；在弱涨落近似（weak-fluctuation approximation）下，采用基于固定平均各向异性下有效局域各向异性涨落线性收缩的约化统计闭合（statistical closure），并在无穷小极限下恢复 de Gennes 应变–指向矢耦合。

Result: 推导出垂直拉伸的解析关系：转动阶段应力同时依赖当前半软模量及其对第一变形不变量的导数，从而产生有限起始 strain 及比常数模量情形明显更平坦的转动响应；斜向拉伸则耦合指向矢转动、剪切与横向收缩。与取向相关测量比较，采用同一参数集可再现主刚度对比与宽垂直平台，但在斜向应力与中间角度初始模量上仍有偏差。

Conclusion: 变形相关的半软模量机制可统一解释有限起始应变与宽软平台，模型在垂直加载方向定量合理，而对斜向应力及各向异性中间角度的模量仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Monodomain main-chain liquid crystal elastomers exhibit pronounced stiffness anisotropy and broad semisoft plateaus following finite onset strains. At fixed chain anisotropy, the neo-classical model predicts equal principal Young's moduli, while its transversely isotropic extension with a constant semisoft modulus constrains the relation between reorientation onset and subsequent stress growth. We develop a finite-strain, isothermal elastic model combining transversely isotropic network elasticity with a deformation-dependent quadratic semisoft penalty. Within a weak-fluctuation approximation, a reduced statistical closure based on assumed linear contraction of effective local anisotropy fluctuations at fixed mean anisotropy yields an exponential transition from an initial to a residual semisoft modulus. The network energy preserves ideal soft modes, and the formulation recovers the de Gennes strain-director coupling in the infinitesimal limit. Analytical relations for perpendicular tension show that the rotating-stage stress depends on both the current semisoft modulus and its derivative with respect to the first deformation invariant. This permits a finite onset followed by a substantially flatter rotating response than in the constant-modulus limit. Oblique tension couples director rotation, shear and lateral contraction. Comparisons with orientation-dependent measurements using a common parameter set capture the principal stiffness contrast and broad perpendicular plateau, with discrepancies remaining in oblique stresses and intermediate-angle initial moduli.

</details>


### [26] [Pinching an elastic potato chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10546)
*Kosei Kataoka,Tomohiko G. Sano*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄壳通过表面传递应力而变形，但一般形状壳体如何承载局部载荷尚不清楚，缺乏统一的理解框架。

Method: 结合实验、数值模拟与理论分析，研究受捏压的鞍形弹性薄壳（saddle-shaped elastic shells），并将等距（isometry）映射下的壳体中面视为满足空间波方程（wave equation）的介质。

Result: 渐近线主导变形与刚度；被捏压“照亮”的材料点上抬，处于“阴影”中的材料点下沉；有限厚度（finite thickness）使明暗边界模糊，同一规则还可预测夹持（clamped）壳体的刚性区域分布。

Conclusion: “光与影”（light and shadow）提供了理解应力沿壳结构传播的统一框架，可用于预测壳体变形与刚度分布。

Abstract: Thin shells deform by transmitting stress across their surface, yet how a shell of general shape carries a localized load remains unclear. Combining experiments, simulations, and theory on pinched saddle-shaped elastic shells, we show that the asymptotic lines, curves of zero normal curvature, dictate both deformation and stiffness. Isometry turns the shell mid-surface into a medium obeying a wave equation in space. Material points lit by the pinch are lifted, while those in its shadow sink. Finite thickness blurs the lit--shadow boundary, and the same rule maps where a clamped shell is rigid. Our findings indicate that light and shadow offer a unified framework for understanding mechanical stress propagation along shell structures.

</details>


### [27] [Hyperuniformity in active matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10605)
*Hartmut Löwen,Erdal C. Oğuz*

TL;DR: 该文评述超均匀性（hyperuniformity）与活性物质（active matter）两个研究领域的交汇，重点介绍Lei等（2019）关于相互作用圆形游动体自组织形成无序超均匀结构的工作及其后续发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序超均匀系统具有长波密度涨落的异常强烈抑制，表现为近乎不可压缩；活性物质由自驱动个体组成。二者长期基本互不相干，需要梳理其联系的形成与意义。

Method: 采用综述与展望方式，以Lei等（2019）将超均匀性与活性物质相联系的发现为起点，描述该进展的影响与外延，并给出进一步联系的简要展望。

Conclusion: 超均匀性与活性物质的联系已形成活跃研究方向，作者对其影响与外延进行总结，并对二者未来更多交叉联系提出展望。

Abstract: Disordered hyperuniform systems are characterized by an anomalously strong suppression of long-wavelength density fluctuations. As a result, these unusual materials behave as though virtually incompressible, even though the particle arrangement itself can remain fully disordered at short range. This peculiar state of matter has been the topic of intense research in physics and mathematics in recent years. Another flourishing research arena is active matter, composed of self-propelled agents that convert energy into motion. While the two fields remained largely disjunct for a long time, the paper by Lei and coworkers (2019) created a link between hyperuniformity and active matter by demonstrating that interacting circle swimmers self-organize into disordered hyperuniform structures. Inspired by this work, many other examples of hyperuniformity in active matter have been identified. We shall describe the impact and outgrowth of this development, and give a brief perspective on further links between hyperuniformity and active matter.

</details>


### [28] [This Is Water: Relative Humidity as a Hidden Parameter in the Self-Assembly of CdSe Nanoplatelets into Threads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10625)
*Benoit Wagnon,Seungho Lee,Lilian Guillemeney,Frédéric Chaput,Rodolphe Valleix,Laurent Coolen,Benjamin Abécassis*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究指出，纳米片（nanoplatelet, NPL）蒸发自组装形成长条堆叠结构的关键被忽视参数是相对湿度（relative humidity, RH）：只有在 RH > 50% 且分散液中存在游离油酸时才能形成微米级堆叠。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体纳米片有序堆叠成线可产生高效、超快的福斯特共振能量转移（Förster Resonance Energy Transfer, FRET），距离可达数百纳米，因此可靠、可重复地制备此类组装体十分重要；然而蒸发自组装过程中 NPL 形成堆叠线的机理尚不明确。

Method: 采用湿度可控腔室（humidity-controlled chamber）进行 NPL 分散液的蒸发自组装实验，比较不同相对湿度条件及游离油酸（oleic acid）存在与否对堆叠形成的影响，并讨论外来水可能通过改变溶剂极性、耗尽力（depletion）、毛细或溶剂化相互作用而修饰 NPL 间胶体作用力。

Result: 微米长的 NPL 堆叠仅在蒸发发生于 RH > 50% 且分散液中含游离油酸时形成，干燥条件下则不形成；由于该湿度范围处于常规实验室条件之内，该参数容易被忽略。

Conclusion: 相对湿度是纳米颗粒自组装的关键参数，研究结果亦将自组装问题指向纳米尺度的胶体相互作用（colloidal interactions）。

Abstract: When nanocrystals order into large-scale assemblies, interparticle coupling can give rise to new properties. In the case of semi-conducting nanoplatelets (NPL), stacking them orderly into threads yields highly efficient, ultrafast Förster Resonance Energy Transfer (FRET) over distances of hundreds of nanometers. Producing such assemblies reliably and reproducibly therefore matters. Yet, the mechanism by which dispersed NPL form stacked threads under evaporative self-assembly remains unclear. Here, we show that the relative humidity (RH) under which the NPL dispersion evaporation occurs is a critical, and so far overlooked, experimental parameter. Using a humidity-controlled chamber, we find that micrometer-long NPL stacks form only when evaporation occurs at RH > 50% rather than under dry conditions and when free oleic acid is present in the dispersion. Because such values lie within typical laboratory conditions, humidity is an easily overlooked parameter. We discuss how adventitious water may modify colloidal forces between NPL, through changes in solvent polarity, depletion, capillary or solvation interactions. Our experiments demonstrate that relative humidity is a key parameter for nanoparticle self-assembly and, more broadly, point to colloidal interactions at the nanoscale.

</details>


### [29] [A new approach to measuring both the steady flow curve and the planar extensional viscosity of complex fluids using exponential shear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10780)
*Nicholas King,Eugene Pashkovski,Reid Patterson,Gareth H. McKinley*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文重新审视1986年提出的指数剪切（Exponential Shear, ES）瞬态强流测试协议，通过分析剪切运动学与应力状态随时间的特征值（eigenvalues）和特征向量（eigenvectors），指出该测试可同时定量获得完整的流动曲线（flow curve）与稳态平面拉伸黏度曲线（planar extensional viscosity）。结论由非线性黏弹本构模型数值模拟、渐近分析（asymptotic analysis）及多种聚合物液体实验共同支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指数剪切可在商用应变控制流变仪（strain-controlled rheometer）上便捷实施，但长期以来针对该指数时变变形应如何定义合适的材料函数（material function）存在多种相互竞争的定义，该问题始终未获解决，本文旨在重新厘清这一争议。

Method: 研究重新考察指数材料运动学（exponential material kinematics）及相应材料函数的正确定义，借助剪切运动学与演化应力状态二者的时变特征值与特征向量分析，并结合若干非线性黏弹本构模型（nonlinear viscoelastic constitutive models）的数值模拟、渐近分析与多种聚合物液体的实验进行验证。

Result: 分析表明，借助特征值与特征向量框架，指数剪切测试能够定量映射出完整的流动曲线（剪切黏度对剪切速率）以及作为变形速率函数的稳态平面拉伸黏度曲线。

Conclusion: 指数剪切测试并非仅能提供单一材料函数，而可作为同时表征剪切与平面拉伸稳态响应的有效手段；该结论得到数值模拟、渐近分析与实验三方面证据的一致支持。

Abstract: We revisit the protocol known as Exponential Shear (ES), first proposed by Doshi and Dealy in 1986. This is a transient strong flow that can be readily imposed in the laboratory using commercial strain-controlled (separate motor and transducer) rheometers which accurately measure both the (exponentially) time-varying torque and normal force exerted by the sample. Numerous studies over the years have proposed competing definitions of an appropriate material function for reporting data obtained from the exponentially time-varying imposed deformation. We revisit this unresolved challenge and carefully reconsider the exponential material kinematics involved and the proper definition of the corresponding material functions. These findings are supported by numerical simulations of a number of nonlinear viscoelastic constitutive models, asymptotic analysis, as well as experiments with several polymeric liquids.

</details>

Abstract: Plain weave is one of the simplest woven structures, formed by two sets of yarns that cross at right angles in an alternating over-under pattern. While such woven fabrics are usually confined to a 2D surface, here we propose a practical approach to fabricate volumetric plain weaves, entanglements featuring three mutually orthogonal families of yarns threaded through a cubic lattice. We develop an analytic framework that converts the crossing pattern topology into continuous yarn centreline geometries, which we then leverage to fabricate volumetric weave specimens. Each crossing is encoded by a matrix that defines the offsets of the three yarns at a vertex. These are formulated to preserve the plain weave topology in every plane, where each pair of yarn families interlaces as in a planar plain weave. Eight configurations in four opposite pairs exist, yet all are related by rotations and translations, so that the fully alternating volumetric plain weave has a single topology. We adopt sinusoidal yarn trajectories and show that the crossing matrix defines their phases. We select two solutions: in-phase planar zigzags and out-of-phase circular helices. Both are members of a family of elliptical helices that also admits a free choice of handedness for each yarn. We then derive the conditions under which yarns intersect between vertices. In-phase yarns never intersect, whereas helical yarns of equal handedness intersect at a critical offset of $\sqrt{2}λ/16$, with $λ$ the wavelength of the cubic lattice. </details>


### [31] [Non-reciprocal Rouse chain model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10904)

Result: 非互易性相对经典Rouse模型增强有效扩散（effective diffusion），并分离平移的左、右零模（zero modes），使算术质心一般不再是动力学解耦的平移坐标；归一化左零模给出有效扩散率随非互易参数与珠子数N的闭式表达，且仍按N^{-1}标度。欠阻尼线性模型与有限自然键长非线性模型给出相同的长时扩散系数。

Abstract: We study the center-of-mass mean-squared displacement (MSD) of a free non-reciprocal Rouse chain. We show that non-reciprocity enhances the effective diffusion with respect to the classic Rouse model, and that it separates the translational right and left zero modes, so the arithmetic center of mass is not, in general, the dynamically decoupled translational coordinate. It is concluded that inertia and finite bond length, modify the transient dynamics of the system, while non-reciprocity determines the long-time diffusion coefficient.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典二维 Eshelby 问题（Eshelby problem）的四极响应（quadrupolar response）被广泛用于描述非晶固体的塑性变形（plastic deformation）以及弹塑性模型（elasto-plastic models）对外部应变的响应，因此在存在反常弹性（anomalous elasticity）时须仔细检验该问题。

Method: 在同时引入偶极屏蔽（偶宇称与奇宇称）的非晶固体中解析求解 Eshelby 问题，对位移场与应变场（strain field）给出完整的解析结果，并对位移场进行分解以分析其模式成分。

Result: 应变场计算清晰显示其结果偏离完全弹性固体的经典对应解；位移场分解表明除 Eshelby 几何所施加的模式外还存在高阶傅里叶模式（Fourier modes）的激发。

Conclusion: 解析结果对理解实验与数值模拟中直接测得的位移场反常响应至关重要；超出所施加 Eshelby 几何的高阶激发提示可能存在非线性模式耦合（nonlinear mode coupling），从而影响并修正反常响应。

Abstract: The so-called "2-dimensional Eshelby problem" was defined by JD Eshelby, where a circle is cut out of an elastic sheet, deformed into an ellipse, and forced back into the material. This creates a quadrupolar response which turned out to be very relevant for describing plastic deformations in amorphous solids. Moreover, the resulting strain field has been used to describe how elasto-plastic models respond to external strains. It is thus important to examine carefully the Eshelby problem in amorphous solids, especially when anomalous elasticity is at play. Here we solve the Eshelby problem in amorphous solids with both even and odd dipole screening and a complete analytical result is obtained for the displacement and strain fields. The analytical results are crucial for understanding the anomalous responses in displacement fields measured directly from experiments and numerical simulations. The computation of strain fields clearly demonstrates the deviations from their classical counterparts for perfectly elastic solids. The decomposition of the displacement field shows the excitation of higher-order Fourier modes. The presence of higher excitations other than the imposed Eshelby geometry therefore suggests the possible nonlinear mode coupling that can influence and modify the anomalous responses.

</details>

*Vanessa K. Ward,Karen E. Johnston*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出从分子动力学轨迹中识别单个"扩散步（diffusive step）"并量化往返运动（back-and-forth motion）等自相关，从而提取具有物理意义的阿伦尼乌斯活化能（activation energy），并处理超阿伦尼乌斯（super-Arrhenius）曲率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿伦尼乌斯定律（Arrhenius law）被广泛用于拟合实验与计算数据，但所得到的"活化能"物理意义不明确；尤其对潜在固态电解质（solid electrolytes）的自扩散系数（self-diffusion coefficient），存在非阿伦尼乌斯行为（non-Arrhenius behaviour）。

Method: 针对潜在固态电解质，通过识别体系内各个"扩散步"来提取物理解释明确的活化能；对超阿伦尼乌斯曲率情形，通过量化往返运动等自相关（self-correlation）恢复潜在的阿伦尼乌斯定律。

Result: 研究展示了可从扩散步层面获得具有物理意义的活化能，并证明即使存在有效活化能随温度降低而升高的超阿伦尼乌斯曲率，仍可提取其背后的阿伦尼乌斯定律。

Conclusion: 该方法论与具体材料无关（material-agnostic），可用于分析有序与无序材料的分子动力学（molecular dynamics）轨迹。

Abstract: The Arrhenius law is ubiquitous in the physical sciences. It describes how an observable property scales with temperature, $T$, as $\exp(\pm E_A/k_BT)$ for a constant $E_A$, the 'activation energy'. Fitting to the Arrhenius law is a common procedure for both experimental and computational data. However, the physical significance of any 'activation energy' calculated is unclear. Here we focus on self-diffusion coefficients for potential solid electrolytes, and by identifying the individual 'diffusive steps' within the system, show how a physically significant activation energy can be extracted. There are also many examples of non-Arrhenius behaviour including `super-Arrhenius' curvature, where the effective activation energy appears to increase with decreasing temperature. We demonstrate how it is still possible to extract the underlying Arrhenius law, by quantifying self-correlations such as back-and-forth motion. The methodology described here is material-agnostic and can be utilised to analyse the molecular dynamics trajectories of both ordered and disordered materials.

</details>

*Hu Cang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该摘要检验了修正模式耦合理论（mode-coupling theory, MCT）的两条路径，并指出MCT失效的根源在于未保持碰撞粒子对（colliding pair）的不可穿透性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密液体如何进入玻璃态（glass transition）仍是物理学的核心难题，而主流的微观理论MCT无法重现平均场笼效应（mean-field caging），故需厘清何种修正方案有效。

Method: 在精确基准液体（exact benchmark liquid）上对比检验两种修复路线：追踪更多粒子关联（more-particle correlations）与在每一碰撞对内部施加排斥（pair impenetrability）。

Conclusion: 修复MCT的关键不在于引入更高阶关联，而在于恢复碰撞对的不可穿透约束；后者是重现平均场笼效应的必要机制。

Abstract: How a dense liquid arrests into a glass remains a central problem in physics. Mode-coupling theory (MCT), the leading microscopic theory of the glass transition, fails to reproduce mean-field caging. Two routes are proposed to repair it: tracking more-particle correlations, or enforcing repulsion within each colliding pair. Testing both on an exact benchmark liquid, we show that MCT lets touching spheres pass through each other at all orders: adding particles dilutes memory and worsens the transition, whereas restoring pair impenetrability recovers mean-field arrest and the cage size.

</details>

*Avinash Kumar Jha*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在既有热非仿射度量（thermal nonaffine metrics）与热应变场（thermal strain fields）工作基础上，探究局部位阻几何（local steric geometry）如何控制非晶玻璃形成固体中热驱动的重排。

Method: 采用拓扑位阻“theta-mismatch”序参量作为结构描述符，量化键角相对位阻参考几何的局部偏差，据此划分低失配与高失配粒子群体；在多个态点、较宽温度范围内比较两者的热非仿射响应、热偏应变（thermal deviatoric strain）与热体积应变。

Result: 高Δθ位点在深度堵塞（deeply jammed）区优先表现出更大的热非仿射运动与更强的热偏应变；热体积响应呈失配依赖的交叉：低Δθ位点主要贡献小应变响应，高Δθ位点对 nagy应变尾部贡献更强。低/高失配群体的对比在深度堵塞区最显著，经中间粘弹性（viscoelastic）区仍可辨识，升温趋向液态时逐渐减弱。

Abstract: Building on our previous work on thermal nonaffine metrics and thermal strain fields, we examine how local steric geometry controls thermally driven rearrangements in an amorphous glass-forming solid. As a structural descriptor, we employ the topological steric "theta-mismatch" order parameter, which quantifies local deviations of bond angles from the steric reference geometry and is used to distinguish low- and high-mismatch particle populations. We then compare their thermal nonaffine response, thermal deviatoric strain, and thermal volumetric strain over a broad temperature range at several state points. High-Delta-theta sites preferentially exhibit larger thermal nonaffine motion and stronger thermal deviatoric strain, particularly in the deeply jammed regime, indicating that geometrically frustrated regions are preferentially associated with localized mechanical distortions. The thermal volumetric response exhibits a distinct mismatch-dependent crossover: low-Delta-theta sites predominantly contribute to the small-strain response, whereas high-Delta-theta sites contribute more strongly to the large-strain tail. The contrast between low- and high-mismatch populations is most pronounced in the deeply jammed regime, remains identifiable through the intermediate viscoelastic regime, and progressively weakens upon heating toward the liquid state. These results establish a direct connection between local steric frustration and thermally activated mechanical response, showing that thermal nonaffine metrics and thermal strain fields provide a physically transparent bridge between local structure and thermally driven rearrangements in amorphous glasses.

</details>


### [37] [Frank Elastic Anisotropy Induced by Anisotropic Attractive Interactions in Nematic Liquid Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11954)
*Akihiko Matsuyama*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种平均场理论（mean-field theory），用于计算具有各向异性 Maier–Saupe（MS）吸引相互作用的向列相液晶（nematic liquid crystal）的 Frank 弹性常数（Frank elastic constants），其中相互作用体积被限制为高度 $L_z$、直径 $L_c$ 的圆柱体，并用长径比 $\alpha_z=L_z/L_c$ 描述其空间各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察吸引相互作用体积的空间各向异性如何影响向列相液晶三类 Frank 弹性常数（splay、twist、bend）及其各向异性关系，并据此判断能否出现一常数关系（one-constant relation）及弹性常数次序的反转。

Method: 推导展曲（splay, $K_{11}$）、扭曲（twist, $K_{22}$）与弯曲（bend, $K_{33}$）弹性常数的解析表达式，并在平均场框架下将其作为浓度与温度的函数进行数值计算，同时以长径比 $\alpha_z$ 作为控制参数。

Result: 在特殊长径比 $\alpha_z=\sqrt{3}/2$ 时，横向与轴向二阶矩相等，在所考察的浓度与温度范围内均满足一常数关系 $K_{11}=K_{22}=K_{33}$；偏离该点后，扁长（oblate）与长椭球（prolate）相互作用体积产生不同的弹性各向异性，且浓度或温度的变化可反转三个弹性常数的次序。

Conclusion: 相互作用体积的空间各向异性是调控 Frank 弹性常数各向异性与次序的关键因素，$\alpha_z=\sqrt{3}/2$ 对应弹性各向同性的一常数极限，而浓度与温度可作为调节弹性常数层级的外部参量。

Abstract: We present a mean-field theory to calculate the Frank elastic constants of a nematic liquid crystal with an anisotropic Maier--Saupe (MS) attractive interaction restricted to a cylindrical interaction volume of height $L_z$ and diameter $L_c$. The shape of the interaction volume is characterized by the aspect ratio $α_z=L_z/L_c$, which controls the spatial anisotropy of the attractive interaction. We derive expressions for the splay $(K_{11})$, twist $(K_{22})$, and bend $(K_{33})$ elastic constants and calculate them numerically as functions of concentration and temperature. At the special aspect ratio $α_z=\sqrt{3}/2$, the transverse and axial second moments are equal, yielding the one-constant relation $K_{11}=K_{22}=K_{33}$ over the full ranges of concentration and temperature considered. Away from this point, oblate and prolate interaction volumes produce distinct elastic anisotropies. Changes in concentration or temperature can also reverse the hierarchy of the three elastic constants.

</details>


### [38] [Where reconfiguration makes a structure stronger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12081)
*Iman Mohagheghian*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该摘要讨论可重构机械超材料（mechanical metamaterials）能否通过重配置提升强度：与同质量下的最优固定设计相比，重配置的优势往往恰好为零；只有当执行器施加的自应力（self-stress）能同时降低所有达到极限的构件利用率时才有效，否则增益消失或受限，而在两者均不构成阻碍时增益可显著（十二辐轮毂示例中最危险构件利用率降低29.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对“重配置能否让超材料更强”这一核心问题展开，指出答案取决于比较基准：与同质量的最优固定设计（best fixed design）相比，重配置的优势常常并不存在，因此需要明确其生效条件。

Method: 以自应力（self-stress）与受载下决定承载能力（capacity）的构件之间的匹配关系为分析框架，区分两类阻断情形：一是达到极限的构件受几何固定、任何自应力都无法改变；二是这些构件相互耦合，降低其一会使另一升高（同时取决于几何与载荷）。据此给出可预先检验的判据。

Abstract: Mechanical metamaterials can reconfigure on command and learn, so adaptation is a central promise of the field. Can reconfiguration make a metamaterial stronger? The answer depends on the comparison. Against the best fixed design of the same mass, the advantage of reconfiguring is often exactly zero. It vanishes where a member at its limit is one no self-stress can change, which the geometry fixes, or where those members are coupled so that lowering one raises another, which depends on the geometry and the loads together. Where neither block applies the gain can be large, as in a twelve-spoke hub with its worst member utilisation reduced by 29.1%. A nontrivial four-dimensional spatial-cutoff construction and a positive spectral bound along it are not established; no exact continuum mass is asserted.

Abstract: The central difficulty in quantum many-body physics is that the Hilbert space dimension grows exponentially with the total degrees of freedom. It applies to initial states with controlled moments of local particle number and energy and is thus thermodynamically stable. Our result breaks the main technical barrier that has existed for using the vast locality toolbox on continuum systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In non-archimedean numerical analysis, a verified $p$-adic approximation can determine an absolute value, and hence downstream probabilistic rankings and classification decisions, with mathematical exactness long before the underlying number is fully resolved. We turn this observation into rigorous finite-precision certificates for norm-based readouts of amplitude families. For a common error radius, a nonempty resolved set already fixes every exact maximizer, ties included, at the sharp integer absolute precision $v_*+1$, and conditioning on that set yields an exact total-variation identity separating unresolved $p$-adic mass from real rounding error. Conclusion: 半直线 ASEP 的转移概率存在两种不同的多重围道积分表示，新公式经 Bethe 拟设与围道变形严格验证，并可导出粒子数守恒概率的积分表达式。

Abstract: We study the asymmetric simple exclusion process on the half-line with particle insertion and no particle removal at the boundary. We point out that there exist two distinct multiple contour integral formulas for the transition probability between two finite particle configurations containing the same number of particles. We recall one of these formulas from the literature and prove a second, new formula by showing that it satisfies the evolution equation using a coordinate Bethe ansatz calculation and verify that the initial condition is implemented by the contour integrals. As an application we sum this formula over all final configurations and obtain an integral formula for the probability that the number of particles is conserved up to a given time. The proofs use symmetric rational functions and their properties together with contour deformations.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Picard-Lefschetz 理论通过 thimble 的交数回答"哪些复经典路径有贡献"，但该交数在无穷维下极难计算，因此需要更可操作的判据。

Method: 用费曼图（Feynman diagrams）与累积量（cumulants）的输运递推（transport recursion）将实与复经典路径的渐近级数算到高阶，再用 Borel-Padé 方法从级数发散尾部读出经典路径的邻接关系（adjacency），最后以 South-East 规则将邻接转化为相关性。

Conclusion: 重求和方法为实时路径积分的 thimble 关联判据提供了可行途径，并进一步给出向受哈密顿约束（Hamiltonian constraint）支配的相对论性路径积分推广的框架。

Abstract: Which complex classical paths contribute to a real-time Feynman path integral? Picard-Lefschetz theory answers this question with the intersection numbers of the thimbles, which are notoriously hard to compute in infinite dimensions. We show that resurgence provides a practical alternative. We compute the asymptotic series of the real and complex classical paths to high order, with Feynman diagrams and with a transport recursion for the cumulants, and read off the adjacency of the classical paths from the divergent tails of the series with Borel-Padé methods. The South-East rule then turns adjacency into relevance. For a particle meeting a Rosen-Morse barrier, we identify the relevant complex classical paths in front of and behind the barrier, including the complex path that carries quantum tunnelling, follow ordinary and higher-order Stokes phenomena as the final point moves, and verify the predictions with the exact propagator. The resummed series reproduce the propagator with exponential accuracy. Finally, we outline how the expansion extends to relativistic path integrals governed by a Hamiltonian constraint.

</details>

In the Clifford--Fock model, we construct representatives as sums of pure basis spinors, and the coefficients are chosen so that the representative is a critical point of the squared norm of the momentum map. Completeness follows from Vinberg's theory of graded Lie algebras and stabilizer actions, independently of the classical spinor classifications. </details>

Result: 对任意固定维度、粒子数、子体积（subvolume）与归一化能量本征态，粒子数方差被证明不超过 $NL^{d-1}$ 阶；相对涨落 $\eta$ 的本征态热化估计为 $L^{d-1}/(N\eta^2)$ 阶，在正粒子密度下随线性尺度倒数衰减。这些界在各维度上均为最优：最大非零能隙重数（nonzero-gap multiplicity）为 $L^{d-1}$ 阶，且存在多体本征态达到匹配的方差下界。

Conclusion: 所得上界在每个维度上都是最优的，且对容差参数（tolerance parameter）一致估计而言，集中界（concentration bound）不可进一步改进，从而完整刻画了该类体系中粒子数本征态涨落的尺度行为。

Abstract: We study eigenstate fluctuations of the particle number in the nearest-neighbour free-fermion model on the periodic cubic lattice $(\mathbb{Z}/L\mathbb{Z})^d$. For every fixed dimension, every particle number, every subvolume, and every normalized energy eigenstate, we prove a bound of order $NL^{d-1}$ for the particle-number variance. These bounds are optimal in every dimension: the maximal nonzero-gap multiplicity has order $L^{d-1}$, and suitable many-body eigenstates realize matching variance lower bounds and show that the concentration bound cannot be improved in estimates uniform in the tolerance parameter.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有孤子气体（soliton gas）的数学研究通常假设归一化常数为确定性或无穷小量，缺乏对归一化常数本身随机且可任意大情形的处理；本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 建立概率型 Riemann–Hilbert 框架（probabilistic Riemann–Hilbert framework）证明自平均性；再对连续极限问题施行 Deift–Zhou 非线性最速下降分析（nonlinear steepest descent）。

Result: 随机 Toda 格点变量以多项式速率 O(N^{-δ})（0<δ<1/2）确定性收敛到有效连续模型；全时空平面解分解为五个不同的有限间隙区（finite-gap regimes），由调制椭圆波与亏格-2 波（genus-2 waves）刻画，误差 O(1/ln N)，且区域间界面为真正的非线性曲线。

Conclusion: 在所设矩条件下建立了 Toda 孤子凝聚的非线性色散关系（Nonlinear Dispersion Relations），并证明宏观系综平均由孤子气体态密度（density of states）决定。

Abstract: We study a dense $N$-soliton condensate for the infinite Toda lattice in the limit $N \to \infty$. In contrast to previous mathematical studies of soliton gases where the norming constants are assumed to be deterministic or infinitesimal, we consider initial scattering data where both the discrete eigenvalues and the norming constants are independent random variables. The norming constants $γ_j$ are allowed to be arbitrarily large under a mild moment condition $\mathbb{E}[γ_j^ν] < \infty$ ($ν> 1$). Our main result is divided into two parts. First, we develop a probabilistic Riemann--Hilbert framework to show that the system self-averages: despite large, independent fluctuations in the initial data, the random Toda lattice variables converge deterministically to an effective continuum model at a polynomial rate $\mathcal{O}(N^{-δ})$ for $0 < δ< 1/2$. Second, by performing the Deift--Zhou nonlinear steepest descent analysis of the continuum problem, we obtain the complete leading-order macroscopic description of the lattice across the entire space-time plane. The solution decomposes into five distinct finite-gap regimes, characterized explicitly by modulated elliptic and genus-2 waves with an error of order $\mathcal{O}(1/\ln N)$. In contrast to the straight characteristic rays typical of standard soliton gas limits, the interfaces separating these five macroscopic regimes in the space-time plane are genuinely non-linear curves; in particular, the boundary of the modulated genus-2 region is completely non-linear. Finally, under our assumptions, we establish the Nonlinear Dispersion Relations for the Toda soliton condensate and prove that the macroscopic ensemble averages are determined by the soliton gas density of states.

</details>


### [61] [Eigenvector Occupation Statistics and Extremes in the Rosenzweig-Porter Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12858)
*Yan V. Fyodorov,Mariya Shcherbina,Tatyana Shcherbina*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文精确刻画了 GUE Rosenzweig-Porter 模型（Rosenzweig-Porter model）本征向量占据概率（occupation probabilities）的有限 N 投影密度，并证明其渐近由有效共振尺度 $K_N(E)\asymp N^{2-\gamma}$ 的自洽 Lorentzian 剖面与局域指数涨落支配，在 $q=1/2$ 处存在广义逆参与比转变，且占据场两端分别呈 Gumbel 与指数型标记 Poisson 极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rosenzweig-Porter 模型处于局域化与遍历之间的中间相，其本征向量占据概率的统计规律是理解该相结构的关键，但缺乏精确的有限 N 描述及严格的渐近刻画。

Method: 以正则能量处的单本征对 Kac-Rice 定律（Kac-Rice law）为条件，导出精确有限 N 投影密度，并将渐近分析化为 $2\times2$ Hermitian 矩阵上的 Hubbard-Stratonovich 积分，用鞍点法（saddle-point method）求值。

Result: 占据场由有效共振尺度 $K_N(E)\asymp N^{2-\gamma}$ 的自洽 Lorentzian 剖面与局域指数涨落支配；证明全部广义逆参与和（generalized inverse participation sums）的渐近并在 $q=1/2$ 处识别转变，得到双支加厚的振幅计数谱；最大分量呈 Gumbel 涨落、最小分量在 $N^{-1-\gamma}$ 尺度上呈指数极限。

Conclusion: 对任意固定 $q>0$，最大分量对逆参与和的贡献渐近消失，说明固定 $q$ 统计量不发生冻结（freezing）；结论为淬火（quenched）意义下在高概率正则事件上一致成立，因而也具有退火（annealed）意义。

Abstract: We study the occupation probabilities $p_i=|u_i|^2$ of a normalized bulk eigenvector in the GUE Rosenzweig-Porter model $A_N=B_N+τ_NG_N$, with $τ_N^2=N^{1-γ}$ and $1<γ<2$. Conditioning on an eigenvalue at a regular bulk energy $E$ through the normalized one-eigenpair Kac-Rice law, we derive an exact finite-$N$ projective density and reduce its asymptotic analysis to a Hubbard-Stratonovich integral over $2\times2$ Hermitian matrices, which we evaluate by the saddle-point method. The resulting occupation field is governed by a self-consistent Lorentzian profile with effective resonant size $K_N(E)\asymp N^{2-γ}$ and local exponential fluctuations. We prove the asymptotics of all generalized inverse participation sums and identify a transition at $q=1/2$. We also obtain a two-branch thickened amplitude-counting spectrum. At the two ends of the occupation field we prove marked Poisson point-process limits: the largest components have Gumbel fluctuations, whereas the smallest component has an exponential limit on the scale $N^{-1-γ}$. Finally, for every fixed $q>0$ the largest component contributes an asymptotically vanishing fraction of the inverse participation sum, showing that these fixed-$q$ statistics do not freeze. The statements are quenched, uniformly on a high-probability regularity event for the diagonal disorder, and hence also hold in the annealed setting.

</details>

### [62] [Markov-Shielded Typicality for Thermal Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10593)
*Atsushi Iwaki,Chisa Hotta*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出确定性变分框架构建“马尔可夫屏蔽典型态（MST）”，用局部马尔可夫熵替代非局域冯诺依曼熵进行自由能最小化，得到从基态到无穷高温极限唯一的热纯态代表，其局域约化密度矩阵近似吉布斯态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统典型态（typical states）虽能重现小系统的吉布斯态期望值，但本质随机、源于随机高温态、存在统计涨落，且与基态无连续联系，故需确定性替代方案。

Method: 以确定性变分框架重构自由能最小化：将非局域冯诺依曼熵（von Neumann entropy）替换为局部、可计算的马尔可夫熵（Markov entropy），构建马尔可夫屏蔽典型态（MST）。

Result: 建立了MST自由能的严格上下界，证明其紧邻热自由能，且MST的局域约化密度矩阵紧密近似吉布斯态，覆盖基态至无穷高温极限。

Conclusion: 将典型性（typicality）概念推广至热平衡的确定性纯态表示，提供可扩展的波函数方法，具备在经典与量子计算中应用的潜力。

Abstract: Pure states of quantum many-body systems at thermal equilibrium are known to be typical, in a sense that a randomly chosen pure state from an appropriate energy shell reproduces the Gibbs-state expectation values of most observables acting on a small subsystem. However, such typical states are intrinsically stochastic, conventionally generated from the random high-temperature state, suffer from statistical fluctuations, and do not provide a continuous connection to the ground state. Here, we introduce a deterministic variational framework for constructing a Markov-Shielded Typical (MST) state, a unique thermal pure-state representative whose local reduced density matrices closely approximate those of the Gibbs state, from the ground state up to infinite-temperature limit. Our approach reformulates free-energy minimization by replacing the nonlocal von Neumann entropy with a local, computationally tractable Markov entropy, where we establish rigorous upper and lower bounds of MST free energy to lie in the very vicinity of the thermal free energy. This framework extends the concept of typicality to deterministic pure-state representations of thermal equilibrium, offering a scalable wave-function approach with potential applications to both classical and quantum computing.

</details>


### [63] [Dynamic Scaling and Aging in Hydrodynamic Coarsening of Vapor Bubbles in a Lennard-Jones Fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10621)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究液-气相分离中气泡这一不连续（disconnected）形态的粗化与老化行为，并与液滴形态比较，以厘清不同形态下的畴生长与老化指数差异。

Method: 对均匀高温态进行温度淬火至两相共存区，采用大规模分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）观察液态背景中气泡的成核与生长，并计算特征气泡尺寸ℓ(t)及两时间自相关函数C_ag(t, t_w)。

Result: 特征气泡尺寸呈幂律增长ℓ(t)∼t^α，生长指数α≈2/3，符合惯性流体力学粗化（inertial hydrodynamic coarsening）特征；两时间自相关函数表现出依赖等待时间的弛豫，即老化（aging）；以ℓ(t)/ℓ(t_w)为变量时不同等待时间的数据坍缩至同一标度函数，该函数呈指数λ≈3的代数衰减。

Conclusion: 气泡与液滴虽同属不连续形态，却呈现明显不同的畴生长与老化指数，表明相分离形态对粗化与老化标度行为具有显著影响。

Abstract: We investigate the coarsening and aging dynamics of vapor bubbles during liquid-vapor phase separation in a three-dimensional single-component Lennard-Jones fluid using large-scale molecular dynamics simulations. Following a temperature quench from a homogeneous high-temperature state into the two-phase coexistence region, vapor bubbles nucleate and grow in the liquid background. The characteristic bubble size follows a power-law growth, $\ell(t)\sim t^α$, with a growth exponent $α\simeq 2/3$, characteristic of inertial hydrodynamic coarsening. We also investigate the two-time autocorrelation function $C_{ag}(t,t_w)$ which follows waiting-time dependent relaxation, revealing aging. Furthermore, data at different waiting times collapse onto a scaling function when plotted as a function of the length ratio $\ell(t)/\ell(t_w)$, demonstrating dynamical scaling during aging. The corresponding scaling function exhibits an algebraic decay with an exponent $λ\simeq 3$. Interestingly, bubble and droplet growth exhibit distinctly different domain-growth and aging exponents despite both being disconnected morphologies.
