### [64] [Ising model on Calandre lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10834)
*F. A. Kassan-Ogly,A. V. Zarubin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种尚未被研究的平面格子——Calandre 格子，并在伊辛模型（Ising model）框架下用精确解析方法求得其自由能、Kramers–Wannier 转移矩阵主本征值及自发磁化的精确表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望引入一个此前未被开发利用的平面格子（Calandre lattice），以拓展伊辛模型可精确求解的格子类型，并借此系统研究其自身及一系列衍生格子的热力学与磁学性质。

Method: 采用精确解析的 Kramers–Wannier 转移矩阵（transfer-matrix）方法，并结合组合 Vdovichenko–Feynman 方法的推广，对 Calandre 格子上的伊辛模型进行求解。

Result: 获得了亥姆霍兹自由能（Helmholtz free energy）的精确解及 Kramers–Wannier 转移矩阵主本征值的表达式，并推导出自发磁化（spontaneous magnetization）的精确表达式；据此对 Calandre 格子及其多种衍生格子进行了热力学与磁学性质的系统研究。

Conclusion: 分析揭示了 Calandre 格子与已有乃至若干新格子的多种相互关系，并在这些格子中发现了磁阻挫（magnetic frustrations）、多重相变（multiple phase transitions）和磁再入（magnetic reentrancy）等效应。

Abstract: In this paper, we proposed to consideration a noval (not yet exploited) planar lattice --- Calandre lattice with the Ising model applied to it. In the framework of an exact analytical Kramers--Wannier transfer-matrix approach and using an extension of the combinatorial Vdovichenko--Feynman method, we have obtained exact solution for Helmholtz free energy and consequent expression for the principal eigenvalue of the Kramers--Wannier transfer-matrix. In addition, we derived the exact expression for spontaneous magnetization. The usage of both expressions made it possible to perform the comprehensive studies of thermodynamic and magnetic properties of the Calandre lattice itself and a wide variety of its affiliated lattices. Among them, diversified interrelations between Calandre lattice and already known and even some novel lattices. These lattices have also revealed such effects as magnetic frustrations, multiple phase transitions and magnetic reentrancy.

</details>


### [65] [Ergotropy as distance from equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11343)
*Michele Campisi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文证明热孤立系统（thermally isolated system）的遍历能（ergotropy，又称可用能 available energy）等于两个能量概率密度函数之间的1-Wasserstein距离，即系统状态能量分布与其对应被动态（passive state）能量分布之间的距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遍历能通常被视为对系统偏离平衡程度的量化指标，但这一直觉缺乏严格的几何刻画。本文旨在为这一直觉提供形式化表述，即把遍历能解释为一种距离度量。

Method: 将遍历能与最优输运理论（optimal transport theory）中的1-Wasserstein距离相联系，针对经典与量子系统分别给出该等价关系的证明，并通过若干示例进行说明。

Result: 在经典与量子两类系统中均证明了遍历能等于系统状态能量概率密度函数与对应被动态能量概率密度函数之间的1-Wasserstein距离。

Conclusion: 该结果将“遍历能是偏离平衡程度的量化指标”这一直觉形式化，为遍历能提供了基于概率分布间距离的严格几何解释。

Abstract: We show that the ergotropy (a.k.a available energy) of a thermally isolated system is the $1$-Wasserstein distance between two energy probability density functions. Specifically, between the energy profile of the system state, and the energy profile of its associated passive state. We prove this for both classical and quantum systems, and illustrate it with examples. Our result formalises the intuition that ergotropy is a quantifier of distance from equilibrium

</details>


### [66] [Computing Classical Partition Functions via Cycle-Space Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11850)
*Yoav Feigenbaum,Lev Barash,Itay Hen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将配分函数改写为相互作用图上偶子图（even subgraphs）之和，利用二元关联矩阵零空间（null space of the binary incidence matrix over F₂）的权重分布计算经典自旋系统的热力学性质，并提出在零空间中直接随机游走的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法（如 Pfaffian 方法）在存在多自旋相互作用（multi-spin interactions）时难以适用，需要一种不依赖可积性、可推广至多自旋与高维体系的配分函数计算方法。

Method: 从高温展开（high-temperature expansion）出发，证明展开系数由二元关联矩阵在 F₂ 上零空间的权重分布决定，该结论在超图（hypergraph）情形下对多自旋相互作用同样成立；随后基于 1/t Wang-Landau 算法构造在零空间中直接随机游走的蒙特卡洛方案。

Result: 由于权重分布与温度无关，单次估计即可给出零场配分函数及其温度导数所导出的任意温度热力学量。方法在二维三角晶格 Ising 模型上与精确有限尺寸结果一致，并应用于三维、四维超立方 Ising 模型及二维、三维 Baxter-Wu 模型；两体模型与传统能量空间 Wang-Landau 采样对比，Baxter-Wu 模型则与各维度最小尺寸的精确枚举（exact enumeration）对比。

Conclusion: 将配分函数表述为零空间权重分布的求和方法，可作为一种通用框架处理含多自旋相互作用、缺乏精确解的经典自旋系统，并借助单次 Wang-Landau 随机游走获得全温度范围的热力学性质。

Abstract: We present a method for computing thermodynamic properties of classical spin systems by reformulating the partition function as a sum over even subgraphs of the interaction graph. Starting from the high-temperature expansion, we show that the expansion coefficients are determined by the weight distribution of the null space of the binary incidence matrix over $\mathbb{F}_2$, and that this statement holds verbatim for multi-spin interactions once the graph is replaced by a hypergraph. We introduce a Monte Carlo scheme based on the $1/t$ Wang-Landau algorithm that performs a random walk directly in this null space. Because the weight distribution is temperature independent, a single estimate yields the zero-field partition function, and the thermodynamic quantities obtained from its temperature derivatives, at arbitrary temperature. We validate the approach against exact finite-size results on the two-dimensional triangular-lattice Ising model, apply it to the three- and four-dimensional hypercubic Ising models where no exact solution is available, and to the two- and three-dimensional Baxter-Wu models, whose three-spin interactions place them outside the reach of Pfaffian methods. For the two-body models we compare against conventional Wang-Landau sampling in energy space; for the Baxter-Wu models we validate against exact enumeration at the smallest size in each dimension.

</details>


### [67] [The role of preparation protocols in the Mpemba effect under time-delayed Newton cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12328)
*Andrés Santos*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文以时间延迟牛顿冷却律（time-delayed Newton cooling law）为模型，系统比较不同制备方案对姆潘巴效应（Mpemba effect）出现条件与幅度的影响，发现同步双储热器协议在考察范围内最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姆潘巴效应不仅取决于弛豫动力学（relaxation dynamics），还依赖于初始态的制备协议（protocol），但二者如何共同决定效应尚缺乏系统刻画，因此有必要在可控模型下比较不同制备方案。

Method: 采用时间延迟牛顿冷却律，比较两个样品先经历分段恒定浴温（piecewise-constant bath temperatures）再置于共同末态浴中的各类协议；对至多两次淬火（quench）且等待时间相同的协议推导效应出现的精确条件，并通过允许异步淬火或对单样品追加淬火进行数值优化检验最优性的稳健性。

Result: 精确条件在该模型中排除了 Pontus 协议，并表明同步双储热器协议（synchronous two-reservoir protocol）在所考虑协议类中使效应幅度最大化；在异步淬火与追加淬火等扩展情形的数值优化中，该协议仍作为最大化极限出现。

Conclusion: 同步双储热器协议在已研究情形下为最优制备方案，作者据此提出其在更一般浴历史（bath histories）下仍最优的猜想，但该更广泛命题未被证明。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect depends on both the relaxation dynamics and the protocol used to prepare the initial states. Here, this dependence is investigated using the time-delayed Newton cooling law, comparing protocols in which two samples are exposed to piecewise-constant bath temperatures before being placed in a common final bath. For protocols with at most two quenches and a common waiting time, exact conditions for the occurrence of the effect are derived. They rule out the Pontus protocol within this model and show that the synchronous two-reservoir protocol maximizes the effect magnitude within the class considered. The robustness of this optimum is then examined by allowing asynchronous quenches or an additional quench for one sample. Numerical optimization of these extensions identifies the synchronous two-reservoir protocol as the maximizing limit in the cases studied. This evidence motivates a conjecture that the same protocol remains optimal for more general bath histories, although that broader statement is not proved.

</details>


### [68] [Discrete time crystals stabilized by bounded measurement noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12400)
*Pallabi Chatterjee,Tanay Nag,Ranjan Modak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 连续弱测量（continuous weak measurement）可使长程相互作用自旋链（long-range interacting spin chain）中原本被周期性驱动加热破坏的离散时间晶体序（discrete time-crystalline order）得以稳定；其中测量诱导的面积律纠缠相（area-law entanglement phase）是必要条件但非充分条件，而限制测量涨落的有界噪声协议（bounded-noise protocol）是获得稳健周期倍增（period doubling）的关键途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性驱动的干净相互作用系统（clean interacting systems）通常会持续加热，从而破坏离散时间晶体序。本文追问连续弱测量能否稳定该序，并试图厘清低纠缠与时间晶体序之间的因果与充分性关系。

Method: 研究一个局域占据数被连续监测的长程相互作用自旋链。除考察典型量子轨迹（typical quantum trajectories）与最可几轨迹（most-probable trajectory，即抑制测量涨落所得）两个极限外，还引入有界噪声协议，将测量涨落限制在有限窗口内，并扫描窄、中、宽三类窗口。

Result: 测量普遍诱导从体积律到面积律纠缠（volume-law to area-law entanglement）的转变，但低纠缠本身不足：典型轨迹仍趋于无次级响应（subharmonic response）的无特征态。最可几轨迹则表现出稳健的周期倍增。窄窗口保持周期倍增；中等窗口出现时间晶体与非时间晶体轨迹的共存，轨迹随时间逐个退出时间晶体组，故序寿命长但有限；宽窗口则破坏该序。

Conclusion: 有界测量噪声（即舍弃大偏差轨迹，large-deviation trajectories）是在干净系统中获得时间晶体序的一条可行途径；同时表明测量诱导的面积律相对该序是必要但非充分条件。

Abstract: Periodically driven clean interacting systems generically heat up, which destroys discrete time-crystalline order. Here we ask whether continuous weak measurement can stabilize such order. We study a long-range interacting spin chain whose local occupation is continuously monitored. Measurements generally induce a transition from volume-law to area-law entanglement. Yet low entanglement alone is not enough: along typical quantum trajectories, the system still reaches a featureless state with no subharmonic response. In contrast, the most-probable trajectory, obtained by suppressing measurement fluctuations, shows robust period doubling. To connect these limits, we introduce a bounded-noise protocol that restricts measurement fluctuations to a finite window. Narrow windows preserve period doubling. Intermediate windows produce coexisting time-crystalline and non-time-crystalline trajectories. Over time, trajectories leave the time-crystalline group one by one, so the order is long lived but finite. Wide windows destroy the order. Our results establish bounded measurement noise, discarding the large-deviation trajectories, as a route to time-crystalline order in clean systems. They also show that the measurement-induced area-law phase is necessary, but not sufficient, for this order.

</details>


### [69] [Deriving Tsallis Entropy from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12441)
*J. A. S. Lima,M. H. Benetti*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文通过"自下而上（bottom-up）"程序，从动力学方法出发导出与 q-分布对应的熵公式，从而由第一性原理确立 Tsallis 熵公式 S_q(W) = k_B ln_q(W) 的合法性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsallis 熵公式此前多作为假设或唯象出发点使用，其从第一性原理的有效性尚未被充分建立，作者试图为 q-分布与 q-熵之间的关系提供自洽的动力学推导。

Method: 采用"自下而上（bottom-up）"程序，将 q-分布的动力学方法扩展至相应熵公式的导出，并与"自上而下（top-down）"方法进行对照；离散与连续情形下的熵、能量及相关量均以标准（线性）平均计算。

Result: 所得到的 q-熵具有可加性（additivity），且因常规识别 beta = 1/(k_B T) 依然成立，热力学第零定律（zeroth law of thermodynamics）自动满足；"自下而上"与"自上而下"两种途径给出相同的速度 q-分布，并在 q -> 1 极限下自然回到标准结果。

Conclusion: 研究由第一性原理确立了 Tsallis 熵公式的有效性，表明 q-熵框架在保持可加性与热力学一致性的同时，可自然退化为标准统计力学结果。

Abstract: The kinetic approach for q-distribution is extended through a "bottom-up" procedure to the corresponding entropic formula. In this way, the validity of Tsallis' formula, S_q(W) = k_B ln_q(W), is established from first principles. The resulting q-entropy is additive and the zeroth law of thermodynamics is automatically satisfied because the conventional identification beta = 1/(k_B T) remains valid. Entropy, energy, and all relevant quantities for both discrete and continuous cases are evaluated using standard (linear) averages. The "bottom-up" and "top-down" approaches yield the same q-distribution for velocities. The standard results are naturally recovered in the limit q -> 1.

</details>


### [70] [Giant Diffusion at Flocking Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12465)
*Sohini Chatterjee,Wasim Akram,Subir K. Das*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文通过模拟二维取向活性物质（aligning active matter）模型，发现速度场有序-无序相变临界点处单粒子扩散系数（single-particle diffusivity）显著增强，且该动力学行为与平衡系统临界性本质不同，并以此解释二维连续对称性系统可发生相变的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非平衡群集相变（flocking transitions）中的输运行为是否与平衡系统临界现象一致，并检验Mermin-Wagner定理对二维连续对称系统有序-无序相变的限制在活性物质中是否依然成立。

Method: 对呈现速度场有序-无序相变的二维取向活性物质模型进行动力学模拟，采用有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）方法量化临界点处扩散系数的增强，并在群集理论（flocking theory）框架下解释输运异常。

Result: 单粒子扩散系数在临界点显著增强，且在整个有序相中表现出明显的有限尺寸效应，这两点在平衡系统临界行为中均不存在；增强的扩散被认为产生有效长程相互作用（effective long-range interactions）。

Conclusion: 增强扩散所导致的等效长程相互作用放松了Mermin-Wagner定理的约束，使二维连续对称系统仍可发生有序-无序相变；结果对实验研究及非平衡输运（nonequilibrium transport）的定量理论构建具有重要意义。

Abstract: We simulate dynamics in two-dimensional aligning active-matter models exhibiting order-disorder transitions in the velocity field. Analysis reveals that the single-particle diffusivity, $D_s$, exhibits strong enhancements at criticality which we quantify via a finite-size scaling method. This dynamical behavior at nonequilibrium flocking transitions is fundamentally different from that at the criticality of equilibrium systems, where diffusivities either vanish or remain non-critical. Furthermore, $D_s$ exhibits pronounced finite-size effects throughout the ordered phase, which also are absent in equilibrium settings. We interpret these counterintuitive transport anomalies within the framework of a flocking theory. Our results suggest that enhanced diffusion generates effective long-range interactions. This relaxes the constraints of the Mermin-Wagner theorem that otherwise prevents order-disorder transitions in two dimensions for systems possessing continuous symmetry. These results are of much experimental relevance and have important implications in formulating quantitative theories for nonequilibrium transports.

</details>


### [71] [Holonomy, Spectral Topology, and Detailed Balance in Classical and Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12514)
*Alvan Hunt*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该综述以"和乐（holonomy）"为统一线索，系统比较能带 Berry 相位、位错 Burgers 矢量与马尔可夫网络循环亲和度三类和乐，论证谱拓扑可以阻碍细致平衡（detailed balance）却无法决定耗散大小，并给出可桌面实现的检验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能带拓扑与非厄米谱理论已被应用于力学超材料、手性活性流体、随机网络与开放量子系统，但各领域的谱判据与热力学（细致平衡、熵产生）之间的关系缺乏统一梳理，易被误用为平衡性判据。

Method: 采用教程式（tutorial）综合，按和乐组织内容：重导可压缩奇粘性流体的 Chern 数，将 Paulose–Chen–Vitelli 的位错零模计数重述为积分挠率与拓扑极化的配对，并以 Markov 生成元的点隙绕数、例外点（exceptional point, EP）与谱实性讨论细致平衡；细致平衡等价于在稳态所定内积下的自伴性。

Result: 非实本征值、EP 或非零点隙绕数可排除细致平衡并强制熵产生 σ>0，且 σ≥k_B j²/D；但实且可对角化的谱一般不能证明细致平衡。Hatano–Nelson 环虽为正规矩阵却具复谱，固定绕数下 σ 可遍历 (0,∞)。EP 需另行判断动力学：含时间反演宇称的广义细致平衡下，EP 仅通过时间反演奇动力学与平衡相容（如朗之万振子临界阻尼），而仅含偶变量的系统（如奇弹性介质）的 EP 则标志非平衡稳态；两种情形下 σ 在 EP 处均解析。

Conclusion: 谱拓扑对细致平衡构成必要而非充分式的阻碍，且不约束耗散幅度；对 EP 与谱实性须结合变量宇称与动力学单独评估。文中为各结论提出相应的桌面实验（benchtop）检验，作为跨领域可操作的判据框架。

Abstract: Band topology and non-Hermitian spectral theory are now applied to mechanical metamaterials, chiral active fluids, stochastic networks, and open quantum systems. We give a tutorial synthesis organized by holonomy and by one question: what can the spectral data of a linear generator reveal about detailed balance? Three holonomies recur: the Berry phase of a band, the Burgers vector of a dislocation (the integrated torsion of the defect geometry), and the cycle affinity of a Markov network (the holonomy of the connection $\ln(W_{ij}/W_{ji})$). We rederive the Chern numbers $C_m=m(\mathrm{sgn}\,Ω+\mathrm{sgn}\,ν_o)$ of a compressible odd-viscous fluid and restate the dislocation zero-mode count of Paulose, Chen, and Vitelli as a pairing of integrated torsion with topological polarization. Our central message is that spectral topology can obstruct detailed balance but does not determine the magnitude of dissipation. Detailed balance is self-adjointness in the inner product fixed by the stationary state, so a non-real eigenvalue, an exceptional point (EP), or a nonzero point-gap winding number of a Markov generator rules it out and forces entropy production $σ>0$, with $σ\ge k_B j^2/D$ for any current, whereas a real, diagonalizable spectrum does not in general certify it. In the Hatano-Nelson ring, which is normal yet has complex spectrum, $σ$ sweeps $(0,\infty)$ at fixed winding number. EPs need a separate assessment of the dynamics: with generalized detailed balance including time-reversal parity, an EP is compatible with equilibrium only through time-reversal-odd dynamics, as at critical damping of a Langevin oscillator, whereas an EP of a system with only even variables, such as an odd elastic medium, certifies a nonequilibrium steady state; in both cases $σ$ is analytic across the EP. We propose benchtop tests for each statement.

</details>


### [72] [Memory in biological Lévy walks from the dynamic-entropy formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12547)
*Adriano Barra,Andrea Ladiana*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文以动态熵（dynamic-entropy）形式检验Lévy行走（Lévy walk）的更新假设（renewal hypothesis），将其推广为有限深度记忆链，并在果蝇幼虫、白蚁、狒狒、信鸽及斑马鱼T细胞数据中区分个体记忆、延迟交互与共同驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lévy行走通常仅凭飞行长度直方图被识别，而教科书模型所依赖的“每次飞行独立重新抽取”的更新假设很少被直接检验；同时群体数据中个体记忆、延迟相互作用与共同驱动易被群体平均所混淆。

Method: 在固定滞后相关性下最大化对数飞行长度序列的因果熵（causal entropy），得到紧区间上的连续自旋链，其深度由数据选择；单滞后情形可精确求解，并借助交换长短飞行的对偶性（duality）进行分析。

Result: 对偶性揭示飞行在上截断处的一级凝聚（first-order condensation），飞行对数范围充当系统尺寸；正记忆使直方图比生成规则更重尾。群体层面可将个体记忆与延迟交互、共同驱动分离，并给出集体速度的临界点。

Conclusion: 在公开数据上，更新假设在果蝇幼虫10种条件中的7种及全部白蚁中被拒绝，白蚁记忆回溯数次飞行并使指数降低逾半个单位；狒狒与前方最近邻10–30 s前的朝向一致，信鸽在小群体内耦合；斑马鱼T细胞作为阴性对照仅保留自身速度记忆。

Abstract: Lévy walks are recognized by the histogram of their flight lengths, while the renewal hypothesis on which the textbook model rests, that every flight is drawn afresh, is seldom tested. We test it with the dynamic-entropy formalism. Maximizing the causal entropy of a sequence of logarithmic flight lengths at fixed lagged correlations yields a chain of continuous spins on a compact interval, whose depth, selected by the data, tells a renewal walker from a Markovian one and from one with a longer memory. With one lag the chain is exactly solvable. A duality that exchanges long and short flights locates a first-order condensation of the flights at the upper cutoff, with the logarithmic range of the flights in the role of system size, and a positive memory makes the histogram heavier-tailed than the rule that generates each flight. For groups, the same construction separates the memory of each individual from delayed interactions and from drives common to all units, which an analysis of the group average confounds, and it gives the collective speed a critical point. On open data, renewal is rejected in seven of ten conditions of Drosophila larvae and in every termite, whose memory reaches a few flights back and lowers the exponent of the histogram by more than half a unit below that of the one-step rule. Wild baboons align with where their nearest neighbors, those ahead above all, were heading 10 to 30 s earlier, and homing pigeons are coupled across their small flocks. Zebrafish T cells, a negative control, remember their own speed, but no delayed coupling between the speeds of different cells survives the controls.

</details>


### [73] [Resolving the backbone of multiscale interactions through mutual information rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12554)
*Alberto Raiola,Giorgio Nicoletti,Daniel Maria Busiello*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种框架，通过结合重标度的互信息率（rescaled mutual information rate）、其条件版本（conditional counterpart）以及时间尺度排序，重建多尺度交互树（multiscale interaction tree）。对平稳高斯过程（stationary Gaussian processes）给出了精确理论，表明这些度量可识别直接相连的过程，并能区分快→慢与慢→快耦合方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然系统常由多个时间尺度（timescales）上的过程组成，并通过复杂交互网络耦合。当时间尺度相差悬殊时，解析最快过程与充分采样最慢过程难以兼顾，导致网络推断（network inference）尤为困难。作者希望将多时间尺度的存在从障碍转化为结构可识别性（structural identifiability）的来源。

Method: 方法上组合重标度互信息率、条件互信息率与时间尺度排序，构建多尺度交互树；并以平稳高斯过程为对象推导精确理论，且证明可仅从谱矩阵（spectral matrix）完成重建。

Result: 理论上，互信息率及其条件版本可直接识别直接相连的过程，渐近互信息率（asymptotic mutual information rate）可区分快→慢与慢→快耦合。数值/分析表明该重建对有限轨迹长度（finite trajectory lengths）、中等时间尺度分离（moderate timescale separation）及弱非线性（weak nonlinearities）具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用真实系统中的多时间尺度特征进行网络重建提供了途径，将时间尺度分离视为结构可识别性的资源而非推断障碍。

Abstract: Natural systems are often composed of processes evolving on multiple timescales and coupled through complex interaction networks. Inferring such networks is especially challenging when timescales are widely separated, since resolving the fastest processes while accurately sampling the slowest ones is generally prohibitive. In this work, we propose a framework to reconstruct a multiscale interaction tree by combining a rescaled version of the mutual information rate, its conditional counterpart, and the ordering of timescales. We derive an exact theory for stationary Gaussian processes, finding that these measures identify directly connected processes, with the asymptotic mutual information rate distinguishing fast-to-slow from slow-to-fast couplings. We show that this reconstruction can be achieved from the spectral matrix and illustrate its robustness to finite trajectory lengths, moderate timescale separation, and weak nonlinearities. Our work paves the way for exploiting the presence of multiple timescales in real-world systems as a source of structural identifiability rather than an obstacle to network inference.

</details>


### [74] [A Hierarchy of Fast and Strong Signatures for Vertex Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12653)
*Joe Germany,Mohamad Al Sayed,Rana Dbouk,Amer E. Mouawad,Sara Najem*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文围绕顶点轨道划分问题（vertex-orbit partition problem），构建了一系列逐级增强表达能力的自同构不变特征（automorphism-invariant signatures），基于图拉普拉斯算子（graph Laplacian）与狄拉克算子（Dirac operator）的谱性质，并引入探测技术（probing techniques）以揭示原始未扰动图中的隐藏结构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图中的两个顶点可能在结构上看似难以区分，却并不在任何图自同构（graph automorphism）下可互换，因此区分“表观相似”与“真正对称”是顶点轨道划分问题的核心。论文旨在超越低维 Weisfeiler–Leman 精化（Weisfeiler–Leman refinement）等既有方法的表达局限。

Method: 方法构建了表达力递增的自同构不变特征层级，利用图拉普拉斯算子与狄拉克算子的谱性质刻画结构差异；并依据一系列反例（counterexamples）逐步强化这些特征，最终纳入探测技术——即临时性的结构扰动，用以暴露原始未扰动图中隐藏的区分信息。

Result: 在多种基准图族（benchmark families）上评估，方法在困难实例中恢复了精确的轨道划分（exact orbit partitions），包括那些抵抗低维 Weisfeiler–Leman 精化的图；同时分析了所构造方法的计算与数值局限性，区分了经验成功与理论完备性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基于谱方法与探测技术的方法在揭示定义顶点轨道的结构差异方面具有潜力，但经验上的有效性并不等同于理论完备性，相关局限仍需进一步考察。

Abstract: The defining features of a physical system are its symmetries and its invariants. For a network, the symmetries are the automorphisms, and the invariants are the vertex orbits they define. Two vertices of a graph may appear structurally indistinguishable without being interchangeable under any graph automorphism. Distinguishing apparent similarity from genuine symmetry lies at the heart of the vertex-orbit partition problem. In this work, we develop a hierarchy of increasingly expressive, automorphism-invariant signatures that draw on the spectral properties of the graph Laplacian and the Dirac operator to reveal subtle structural differences. Guided by a sequence of counterexamples, we progressively strengthen these signatures, ultimately incorporating probing techniques, which are temporary structural perturbations, that expose the hidden distinctions in the original unperturbed graph. We evaluate our approach across diverse benchmark families, recovering exact orbit partitions in challenging instances, including graphs that resist low-dimensional Weisfeiler--Leman refinement. We also examine the computational and numerical limitations of our constructions, distinguishing empirical success from theoretical completeness. Our findings highlight the potential of spectral and probing-based methods for uncovering the structural distinctions that define vertex orbit.

</details>


### [75] [Renewal Bias in Parallel Simulations of Absorbing Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12692)
*Sandro M. Reia,José F. Fontanari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文揭示异步并行模拟吸收态随机过程时，因轨迹时长与吸收结局相关而产生的系统性采样偏差，并提出"首轨迹过滤"策略与均方误差交叉点分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行worker在吸收后立即重启，会在固定计算预算内优先记录快速终止的轨迹，扭曲系综统计。作者旨在量化这一偏差机制并寻找更可靠的采样策略。

Method: 构建含两个吸收态、时长依赖状态的极小更新模型（renewal model），用更新理论（renewal theory）推导完成轨迹数量与构成的精确表达式；对比连续采样与首轨迹过滤（First-Trajectory Filtering）；并进行均方误差（mean-squared error）的解析计算。

Result: 偏差随执行时间代数衰减，但在固定预算下随并行worker数增加而持续存在；首轨迹过滤的残余删失偏差（censoring bias）随预算指数衰减；解析MSE给出预算依赖的交叉worker分配点，超过该点首轨迹过滤虽收集轨迹更少却更准确。

Conclusion: 提高计算吞吐量并不必然提升统计精度；在吸收态过程的并行模拟中须考虑轨迹时长异质性。Axelrod模型在非平衡转变（nonequilibrium transition）附近的模拟亦验证了该更新机制的 relev ance。

Abstract: Asynchronous parallel simulations of stochastic processes with absorbing states can exhibit systematic sampling bias when trajectory durations depend on their eventual absorbing outcomes. Workers that continuously restart upon absorption preferentially record rapidly terminating trajectories within a fixed computational budget, distorting the observed ensemble statistics. We investigate this effect using a minimal renewal model with two absorbing states and state-dependent exponential durations. Renewal theory yields exact expressions for the expected number and composition of completed trajectories, showing that the resulting bias decreases algebraically with execution time but persists as the number of parallel workers increases at fixed budget. We compare continuous sampling with First-Trajectory Filtering, which retains only the initial trajectory attempted by each worker. Although finite execution deadlines introduce residual censoring bias, this bias decreases exponentially with the computational budget. An analytical mean-squared error calculation reveals budget-dependent crossover worker allocations beyond which First-Trajectory Filtering becomes more accurate despite collecting fewer trajectories. We also examine the limitations of statistical inference under severe censoring when the absorbing-state probabilities and characteristic durations are unknown. Finally, simulations of the Axelrod model near its nonequilibrium transition demonstrate the preferential sampling of rapidly absorbing fragmented configurations, illustrating the relevance of the renewal mechanism in a spatially interacting system. Our results show that increasing computational throughput does not necessarily improve statistical accuracy and highlight the importance of accounting for trajectory-duration heterogeneity in parallel simulations of absorbing processes.

</details>

### [76] [Superconductivity in the Metallic Wigner Crystals of Rhombohedral Graphene: Possible Realization of an Electronic Supersolid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10117)
*Félix Desrochers,Mark R. Hirsbrunner,Yong Baek Kim,Ashvin Vishwanath*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作以电子掺杂菱面堆垛多层石墨烯的真实模型为对象，论证金属维格纳晶体（metallic Wigner crystal, MWC）中由集体模式（collective mode）介导的超导电性可行，并指出主导配对不稳定性出现在有限动量上，需多带分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验在菱面堆垛多层石墨烯中观测到金属维格纳晶体（MWC），其邻近时间反演对称破缺（time-reversal-symmetry-breaking）超导，提示电子晶体的集体激发可能介导剩余巡游载流子的配对，形成“超导维格纳晶体”这一超固体（supersolid）的电子类比。

Method: 采用包含自旋与谷极化的电子掺杂菱面堆垛多层石墨烯真实模型（低对称性抑制常规库珀不稳定性（Cooper instability）），以自洽与含时哈特里–福克理论（Hartree–Fock theory）追踪自掺杂分数演化并刻画集体模式谱，随后用多带线性化埃利亚什伯格分析（Eliashberg analysis）研究配对。

Result: 随密度增加，MWC 因一条弱色散的空带（unoccupied band）接近化学势而失稳；在稳定 MWC 深处配对较弱，但在接近失稳时出现由带间电子–空穴激发（interband electron-hole excitations）组成的软化集体模式，该模式可在实验相关温度下驱动配对。主导配对不稳定性位于有限动量，并混合费米面态与初生带（incipient band）态。

Conclusion: 结果表明，集体模式介导的超导电性在 MWC 中是菱面堆垛石墨烯的可行情景，并支持在其超导相中寻找共存的电子电荷序；同时说明费米面或单带配对分析在此类体系中不充分。

Abstract: Recent experiments on rhombohedral multilayer graphene have reported metallic Wigner crystals (MWCs), in which spontaneously broken translational symmetry coexists with mobile charge carriers. Their proximity to time-reversal-symmetry-breaking superconductivity raises the intriguing possibility that collective excitations of the electron crystal mediate pairing among the residual itinerant carriers, giving rise to a superconducting Wigner crystal---an electronic analog of a supersolid. Here we investigate this possibility using a realistic model of electron-doped rhombohedral multilayer graphene with spin and valley polarization, whose low symmetry removes the usual Cooper instability. We use self-consistent and time-dependent Hartree-Fock theory to map the evolution of the self-doping fraction and characterize the collective-mode spectrum of the MWCs, followed by a multiband linearized Eliashberg analysis of pairing. Upon increasing density, we find an instability of the MWC, triggered by a weakly dispersing unoccupied band approaching the chemical potential. Pairing remains weak deep in the stable MWC. However, on approaching the instability, we identify a collective mode composed of interband electron-hole excitations that softens. We show that this mode drives pairing at experimentally relevant temperatures. The leading pairing instability is at finite momentum and combines Fermi surface states with states in the incipient band, making a Fermi surface or single-band pairing analysis inadequate. Our results establish collective-mode-mediated superconductivity in MWCs as a viable scenario for rhombohedral graphene and motivate experimental searches for coexisting electronic charge order in its superconducting phases.

</details>


### [77] [Energy-resolved current response and the crossover between two approximately linear-in-$T$ resistivity regimes in the infinite-$U$ Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10124)
*Shuo Liu,Aman Kumar,Yuhao Ma,Philip W. Phillips,Hitesh J. Changlani,B. Andrei Bernevig*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在大U极限二维Hubbard模型（单个掺杂空穴，N=16、18格点方团簇）中，通过完全精确对角化（full exact diagonalization）证明：定义的直流f_x函数在简并情形下取常数值，是电阻率严格随温度线性（ρ_x(T)=αT）的充要条件；数值结果显示f_x在谱内部近似平坦、仅在谱边缘尺度ε_{f_x}内变化，并对应高低温两个近似线性区之间的渐变过渡（crossover），伴随宽比热峰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为二维无限U Hubbard模型中单个空穴的电荷输运（charge transport），核心问题是厘清“电阻率线性于温度”（linear-in-T resistivity）这一经验规律成立的微观条件及其在高低温区间的表现。

Method: 在N=16与18格点方团簇（square clusters）上采用完全精确对角化（full exact diagonalization），在显式处理简并（degeneracies）的前提下给出关于直流f_x函数的严格证明，并对数值外推的f_x与电阻率、比热进行温度依赖分析。

Result: 证明了常数dc f_x函数是ρ_x(T)=αT（对所有T>0、α为常数）的充要条件；数值外推的f_x在谱（spectrum）内部一大片区域近似平坦，在谱边缘约ε_{f_x}范围内剧烈变化，该尺度对应特征温度T_cr。ED电阻率在T/t≥0.05区间显示高、低温两个近似线性区之间的渐变过渡，交叉温度T_cr/t≈0.414（N=16）与0.467（N=18）；相应比热宽峰出现在T/t≈0.70与0.68。

Conclusion: 电阻率的线性行为由谱内部f_x的平坦性控制，而偏离高温线性的程度由谱边缘尺度ε_{f_x}（即T_cr）刻画；比热宽峰与电阻率的渐变过渡相关联，表明二者可能共享同一多体能量尺度。

Abstract: We study charge transport in the two-dimensional infinite-$U$ Hubbard model with hopping amplitude $t$ using full exact diagonalization (ED) on $N=16$- and $18$-site square clusters with a single doped hole. We first provide a proof, treating degeneracies explicitly, that a constant dc $f_x$ function, defined as the sum of the squared current matrix elements connecting eigenstates at a given energy to eigenstates of nearly the same energy, averaged over degenerate initial states, is necessary and sufficient for strictly linear-in-$T$ resistivity: $ρ_x(T)=αT$ for all $T>0$ with a constant $α$. The numerically extrapolated dc $f_x$ is not exactly constant, but it is approximately flat over a broad region in the interior of the many-body spectrum and varies strongly within a scale $\varepsilon_{f_x}$ of the spectral edges. This scale $\varepsilon_{f_x}$ is related to a characteristic temperature $T_{\mathrm{cr}}$, which quantifies the leading departure of the resistivity from high-temperature linearity upon cooling. Within the studied temperature range $T/t \geq 0.05$, our ED resistivity results reveal a gradual crossover between high- and low-temperature approximately linear-in-$T$ regimes, with characteristic crossover temperatures $T_{\mathrm{cr}}/t\approx0.414$ and $0.467$ for $N=16$ and $18$, respectively. We also find a broad specific-heat peak associated with this gradual crossover, at $T/t \approx 0.70$ and $0.68$ for $N=16$ and $18$, respectively.

</details>


### [78] [On the nature of low-energy electronic states in twisted bilayer graphene: a first-principles study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10253)
*Albert Zhu,Daniel Bennett,Daniel T. Larson,Mohammed M. Al Ezzi,Efstratios Manousakis,Efthimios Kaxiras*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作以无额外近似的密度泛函理论（density functional theory）系统计算魔角（magic angle）附近扭角双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene, TBG）的低能电子态，并在每个 $K$ 谷构造十带有效模型（ten-band effective model），给出局域 Wannier 态的库仑与交换积分（Coulomb and exchange integrals），为多体理论提供第一性原理基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔角附近 TBG 低能电子态的性质此前多在定性层面讨论，而这些态是构建少带有效模型与多体理论的关键；因此需要系统的、无额外近似的第一性原理计算以提供定量依据。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory）进行大范围、系统性的第一性原理计算，不引入额外近似；在莫尔尺度（moiré scale）上为低能带构造局域 Wannier 态（Wannier states），并为每个 $K$ 谷发展十带有效模型；随后计算这些局域态的库仑积分与交换积分（Coulomb and exchange integrals），并对十带哈密顿量与基轨道进行对称化处理。

Result: 所构造的 Wannier 轨道同时包含原子尺度与莫尔尺度细节，且中心位于莫尔原胞中的高对称堆垛位置附近；计算得到的库仑与交换积分可与已有工作（此前获得或假设的取值）进行对比；对称化后的十带哈密顿量揭示了平带（flat-band）轨道间主导跃迁（dominant hoppings）随扭角（twist angle）的演化规律。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了魔角区间 TBG 低能态的系统第一性原理表征与十带有效模型，其局域态积分构成多体模型的basic ingredients，并给出平带轨道跃迁随扭角演化的定量认识。

Abstract: The nature of low-energy electronic states in twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) near the magic angle has been extensively discussed in the literature on qualitative grounds, since these states play a key role in the construction of effective few-band models toward a many-body theory. Here, we report extensive and systematic first-principles calculations of TBG electronic states in the magic-angle range, using density functional theory with no further approximations. We construct moiré-scale localized Wannier states for the low-energy bands and develop an effective ten-band model for each $K$ valley. The Wannier orbitals contain both atomic and moiré-scale details and are centered near the high-symmetry stackings in the moiré cell. We calculate Coulomb and exchange integrals of these localized states, quantities that are the basic ingredients in many-body models. We also compare the values of these integrals to those obtained or assumed in previous works. By symmetrizing the ten-band Hamiltonians and basis orbitals, we provide insight into how the dominant hoppings between the flat-band orbitals evolve as a function of twist angle.

</details>


### [79] [Discovery of Kondo-Kramers Nodal Line Topological State in strongly correlated material CeAuAl$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10368)
*Hari Bhandari,Kevin Allen,Kuan-Sen Li,Yuan Fang,Arushi,Karthik Rao,Mounica Mahankali,Sanu Mishra,Jian-Xin Zhu,Christopher Lane,Qimiao Si,Emilia Morosan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作在非中心对称、非手性近藤晶格（Kondo lattice）CeAuAl$_3$ 中，结合输运、热力学、密度泛函理论（DFT）与紧束缚模型，识别出由时间反演（time-reversal）与镜面对称性（mirror symmetry）保护的 Kramers 节点线（Kramers nodal lines, KNLs），并将其与近藤关联效应耦合，提出“近藤-Kramers 节点线”这一新拓扑态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KNL 虽被预言存在于所有非手性、非中心对称晶体中，但实验实现极少，且仅限于弱关联体系，强关联体系中的实现尚未被探索。作者因此寻求在近藤晶格中检验 KNL 拓扑及其可观测效应。

Method: 采用磁输运（magnetotransport）与热力学测量，结合 DFT 与紧束缚玩具模型（tight-binding toy model）计算，分析 CeAuAl$_3$ 的能带拓扑、对称性保护机制及贝里曲率（Berry curvature）来源。

Result: CeAuAl$_3$ 在 Γ-Z 与 X-P 方向存在 KNL；破坏晶体对称性或经自旋极化（spin polarization）破坏时间反演对称性可解除节点线简并并产生贝里曲率，从而产生本征反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect, AHE）。该 AHE 在低于近藤温度（Kondo temperature）时即显著出现，而非仅在磁有序温度 $T_N$ 以下，表明近藤关联将 KNL 推至费米面（Fermi surface）。

Abstract: Rare-earth magnets are often regarded as classical owing to their large angular momentum J, but this picture breaks down when exchange is smaller than the crystalline electric-field (CEF) energy scale, allowing the degeneracy of low-lying CEF manifolds to dictate the emergent quantum phases. Unlike quartets or higher multiplets, we find that the effective spin-1 triplet does not allow independent dipolar ordering without strong quadrupolar (nematic) interactions that act to break rotational symmetry, revealing an essential multipolar prerequisite for tilted magnetic ground states. Mapping the surrounding CEF parameter space further reveals competing magnetic, non-magnetic singlet, and potential quadrupolar phases, driven by multifold ground state degeneracies. These results establish CEF tuning as a versatile route to unconventional quantum phases across the broader RT6X6 (T = transition metal; X = Sn, Ge) family.

</details>


### [82] [Dipolar Exchange and Emergent Electric-Dipole-Carrying Quasiparticles in Electronic Ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10513)
*Ping Tang,Eiji Saitoh*

TL;DR: 该文为电子铁电性（electronic ferroelectricity）建立量子偶极-赝自旋交换模型，识别出携带本征电偶极矩的玻色型准粒子（bosonic quasiparticles），并预言其线性斯塔克位移（linear Stark shift）及太赫兹共振增强的二阶谐波产生（second-harmonic generation）与光整流（optical rectification）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋交换相互作用产生磁序及其磁振子（magnons）准激发已被充分理解，但电子铁电体中的对应准粒子激发仍缺乏认识，构成铁电物理与关联电子物理之间的概念空白。

Result: 所识别的偶极准粒子完全源自集体电子动力学（collective electronic dynamics），不涉及晶格振动，区别于传统晶格铁电体中的铁电振子（ferrons）；其激发谱在静电场下呈现线性斯塔克位移，与振荡电场耦合可实现相干激发，并在太赫兹波段产生巨大的共振增强非线性光学响应。

Conclusion: 偶极准粒子为电可调谐非线性光学（electrically tunable nonlinear optics）提供新途径，并将电子系统的输运范式从常规电荷流（charge currents）拓展至电偶极流（electric dipole currents）。

Abstract: Electronic correlations underpin diverse spontaneously ordered states in matter, whose collective excitations emerge as quasiparticles that encode the underlying order. While spin exchange interactions give rise to magnetic order and its quasiparticle excitations known as magnons, their counterparts in electronic ferroelectrics remain poorly understood. Here, we derive an analogous quantum dipolar-pseudospin exchange model for electronic ferroelectricity and identify the associated bosonic quasiparticles that carry intrinsic electric dipole moments. Unlike ferrons in conventional lattice-based ferroelectrics, these dipolar quasiparticles arise entirely from collective electronic dynamics without involving lattice vibrations. Their excitation spectrum exhibits a linear Stark shift under a static electric field, while coupling to an oscillating electric field enables their coherent excitation, leading to colossal, resonantly enhanced second-harmonic generation and optical rectification in the terahertz regime. These dipolar quasiparticles offer promising opportunities for electrically tunable nonlinear optics and open a route toward extending the paradigm of transport in electronic systems beyond conventional charge currents to electric dipole currents.

</details>


### [83] [Strong Collective Dynamics Hidden behind a Nearly Free Single-Particle Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10636)
*Zhixuan Zhao,Sicheng He,Hanxiang Di,Nayun Chen,Jiabai Xiong,Guyixue Gao,Yanling Wu,Jun Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出，单粒子谱（single-particle spectroscopy）与电子关联强度（electronic correlations）之间的传统对应关系可在同一微观轨道（orbital）上失效，其机制源于两个共振侧轨道（side orbitals）的局域干涉（local interference）。作者证明了一个与环境尺寸、相互作用强度无关且适用于任意定态（stationary states）的相对谱界（relative spectral bound），并给出可兼具可预测粒子谱与强集体灵敏度的量子探测设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，单粒子谱被视为衡量电子关联强度的判据：强集体运动（collective motion）预期会使谱峰位移、展宽并重整化色散（renormalize the dispersion）。该文旨在检验这一对应关系是否普遍成立，并揭示其可能失效的微观机制。

Method: 核心机制为两个共振侧轨道之间的局域干涉：一个抑制进入相互作用环境的低能实跃迁（real transitions），另一个抑制谱权重回流（spectral-weight backflow）至源轨道，而虚混合（virtual mixing）仍将环境自旋极化（spin polarization）传递至源轨道。作者证明了一个相对谱界，其与环境尺寸及相互作用强度无关；并结合全量子双胞器件（two-cell device）与量子蒙特卡洛（Quantum Monte Carlo）进行计算验证。

Result: 在全量子双胞器件中，集体模式软化（softens）达60%，而自由峰（free peak）处的粒子强度仅变化3.6 ppm；该保护随相对频率分辨率（relative frequency resolution）提高而增强。量子蒙特卡洛表明，转移的自旋响应随空间关联（spatial correlations）增强，并可由自由能带极化（free-band polarization）定量预测。

Conclusion: 研究结果重新诠释了简单粒子谱的物理含义，表明谱的不敏感性并不排除强集体响应；并据此提出一种量子探针（quantum probes）设计原则，使可预测的粒子谱与强集体灵敏度得以兼容。

Abstract: Single-particle spectroscopy has long served as a criterion for the strength of electronic correlations: strong collective motion is expected to shift and broaden spectral peaks and to renormalize the dispersion. Here we show that this correspondence can fail on the same microscopic orbital. The mechanism relies on local interference between two resonant side orbitals: one suppresses low-energy real transitions into the interacting environment, while the other suppresses spectral-weight backflow to the source orbital; virtual mixing nevertheless transfers the environmental spin polarization to the source. We prove a rigorous relative spectral bound that is uniform in environment size and interaction strength and holds for arbitrary stationary states; the protection becomes stronger as the relative frequency resolution improves. In a fully quantum two-cell device, a collective mode softens by 60%, whereas the particle intensity at the free peak changes by only 3.6 parts per million. Quantum Monte Carlo shows that the transferred spin response grows with spatial correlations and is quantitatively predicted by the free-band polarization. These results recast the meaning of a simple particle spectrum and provide a design principle for quantum probes that combine predictable particle spectra with strong collective sensitivity.

</details>


### [84] [Higher tensor network states in two spatial dimensions: fundamental theorem for open boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12658)
*Clement Delcamp,Boris Kjær*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过范畴化（categorification）矩阵乘积态（matrix product states）构造一类二维张量网络态（tensor network states），其键空间（bond space）由与格点 2-胞腔（2-cells）相关联的 2-向量空间（2-vector space）给出，并证明了表示等价性的基本定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵乘积态已有成熟的代数刻画，但将其提升到二维情形需要更高范畴结构；本文旨在通过范畴化方法为二维张量网络态建立范畴论框架，并推广单射性（injectivity）条件。

Method: 将张量网络表示定义为自由幺半范畴（free monoidal category）上的模范畴（module categories），该自由幺半范畴基于编码格点几何的幺半签名（monoidal signature）；键空间取为与格点 2-胞腔相关联的 2-向量空间。

Result: 证明了基本定理：在适当开边界条件（open boundary conditions）下生成相同态的两个表示作为模范畴是等价的；并引入 2-单射性（2-injectivity）概念，作为矩阵乘积态单射性条件的范畴化类比。

Conclusion: 该工作为二维张量网络态提供了范畴化的代数基础，其表示等价定理与 2-单射性概念为后续研究此类态的规范结构与可辨识性提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We introduce a class of two-dimensional tensor network states obtained by categorifying matrix product states. This procedure leads us to consider bond spaces provided by a 2-vector space associated with the 2-cells of the underlying lattice. Tensor network representations are then defined as module categories over the free monoidal category on a monoidal signature encoding the lattice geometry. We prove a fundamental theorem stating that two representations generating the same states with suitable open boundary conditions are equivalent as module categories. Finally, we introduce a notion of 2-injectivity, a categorified analogue of the injectivity condition for matrix product states.

</details>


### [85] [Momentum-dependent phonon effects on spin-fluctuation $d$-wave pairing in bulk La$_6$Ni$_5$O$_{12}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11453)
*Dexi Shao,Zhiming Pan,Ming Zhang,Yupeng Li,Chen Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究关注电声耦合（electron-phonon coupling）如何影响自旋涨落（spin-fluctuation）媒介的配对，针对 La$_6$Ni$_5$O$_{12}$ 中 $s$ 波与 $d$ 波配对竞争这一未决问题展开分析。

Method: 采用五层 Hubbard 模型（five-layer Hubbard model），在随机相近似（random-phase approximation）框架下处理，并引入两个由氧呼吸模式（oxygen breathing modes）启发的唯象耦合项，分别在有无电子电荷屏蔽（electronic charge screening）条件下比较 $s$ 波与 $d$ 波的配对强度，并进行动量分辨投影（momentum-resolved projections）分析。

Result: 无屏蔽时，电声耦合单独作用倾向 $s$ 波配对，且其在自旋涨落诱导能隙（spin-fluctuation-induced gap）正负号相反区域之间的吸引散射会抑制 $d$ 波；电子电荷屏蔽强烈减弱该抑制，使 $s$/$d$ 波强度边界向 $d$ 波移动，动量分辨投影定位了不利散射过程被削减的区域。

Conclusion: 在代表性电子相互作用（representative electronic interaction）下，$d$ 波配对仍占主导，说明电荷屏蔽是决定 La$_6$Ni$_5$O$_{12}$ 中配对对称性（pairing symmetry）竞争结果的关键因素。

Abstract: We study the effect of electron-phonon coupling on spin-fluctuation-mediated pairing in bulk La$_6$Ni$_5$O$_{12}$. A five-layer Hubbard model treated within the random-phase approximation yields a leading $d_{x^2-y^2}$ pairing state. Two phenomenological couplings motivated by oxygen breathing modes favor $s$-wave pairing when considered alone, while their attractive scattering between opposite-sign regions of the spin-fluctuation-induced gap suppresses $d$-wave pairing. Electronic charge screening strongly reduces this suppression. Comparing the $s$- and $d$-wave pairing strengths with and without screening shows that screening shifts their boundary in favor of $d$-wave pairing. Momentum-resolved projections locate the reduction of the unfavorable scattering processes. At the representative electronic interaction, $d$-wave pairing remains dominant.

</details>


### [86] [Frustrated Magnetism from Dimerized $S = 1/2$ Co$^{2+}$ in Rose-Phase Na$_2$CoP$_2$O$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11772)
*Shukun Li,Leon Ma,Xianghan Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为低对称性、由二聚体构成近似三角晶格的磁体，旨在探索有效自旋 S=1/2 体系中的阻挫量子磁性（frustrated quantum magnetism）以及自旋、晶格与介电自由度之间的耦合功能。

Method: 生长高质量单晶，并系统测量磁化率、热容（heat capacity）与介电（dielectric）性质，同时施加磁场考察磁介电响应（magnetodielectric response）与磁化强度的场依赖关系。

Result: 磁性测量表明以反铁磁相互作用（antiferromagnetic interactions）为主且各向异性显著；磁化率与热容在约 4 K 以下出现宽泛低温极大，指示短程磁关联（short-range magnetic correlations）。居里–外斯温度（Curie–Weiss temperature）绝对值与特征关联温度之间的大间隔表明强磁阻挫（magnetic frustration）。外场抑制上述异常并降低回收磁熵，磁介电响应与磁化场依赖高度一致。

Conclusion: 低晶格对称性允许多重非等价交换路径与各向异性相互作用，同时放松了对自旋—晶格—介电耦合的对称性限制；因此玫瑰相 Na₂CoP₂O₇ 可作为研究有效 S=1/2 磁体中阻挫量子磁性与耦合功能性的平台材料（platform）。

Abstract: We report the growth of high-quality single crystals and the magnetic, thermodynamic, and dielectric properties of rose-phase Na$_2$CoP$_2$O$_7$, a low-symmetry magnet in which corner-sharing Co$^{2+}$ dimers form an approximately triangular network. Magnetic measurements reveal dominant antiferromagnetic interactions and pronounced anisotropy among effective $S=1/2$ Co$^{2+}$ moments. Broad low-temperature maxima in the magnetic susceptibility and heat capacity suggest the development of short-range magnetic correlations below approximately 4 K. The large separation between the absolute Curie--Weiss temperatures and the characteristic correlation temperature indicates strong magnetic frustration arising from competing interactions within the coupled-dimer network. Applied magnetic fields suppress these anomalies and reduce the recovered magnetic entropy, suggesting further enhancement of frustration under field. A systematic magnetodielectric response closely follows the field dependence of the magnetization, indicating coupling between magnetic and dielectric degrees of freedom. The low lattice symmetry enables multiple inequivalent exchange pathways and anisotropic interactions while relaxing symmetry constraints on coupling among spin, lattice, and dielectric degrees of freedom. These results establish rose-phase Na$_2$CoP$_2$O$_7$ as a platform for exploring frustrated quantum magnetism and coupled functionalities in effective $S=1/2$ magnets.

</details>


### [87] [Positronium resonance of electric monopoles in quantum spin ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12277)
*José Maria Cruz,Hidemaro Suwa,Masafumi Udagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究量子自旋冰（quantum spin ice）中纯量子起源的“电单极子（electric monopole）”激发谱，在烧绿石晶格（pyrochlore lattice）量子 XXZ 模型的低能等价描述——量子二聚体模型（quantum dimer model）的 Rokhsar-Kivelson 点上，结合量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）与随机解析延拓（stochastic analytical continuation）进行数值研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋冰的低能物理由模拟量子电动力学（QED）的有效规范理论描述，其有效 QED 除磁单极子外还包含电单极子这一纯量子起源的能隙粒子；然而电单极子的性质尚未得到很好解析，因此需要厘清其激发谱特征。

Method: 以量子二聚体模型在 Rokhsar-Kivelson 点的低能描述为出发点（对应烧绿石晶格上的量子 XXZ 模型），将量子蒙特卡洛模拟与随机解析延拓相结合，提取电单极子的激发谱；并考察可翻转环路算符（flippable loop operator）作为局域物理可观测量激发电单极子对的可行性。

Result: 电单极子谱呈现清晰的双峰结构，分别对应电单极子的束缚态（bound states）与散射态（scattering states）；低能峰归因于电单极子对的激子型束缚态（exciton-like bound state），即量子自旋冰中“正电子素（positronium）”的信号。可翻转环路算符可作为激发一对电单极子的局域物理可观测量，暗示单极子可通过晶格或光学响应被探测。

Conclusion: 结果明确了量子自旋冰中电单极子激发谱的束缚态—散射态双重结构，并指出正电子素类似物与可探测算符，为通过晶格或光学响应实验探测单极子提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Quantum spin ice attracts considerable attention due to its remarkable low-energy properties, described by the effective gauge theory, which mimics the quantum electrodynamics (QED). The ``effective" QED in quantum spin ice is beyond the ``real" QED in our universe in many respects. Above all, the effective QED hosts two kinds of gapped particles, the electric monopole, in addition to the magnetic monopole. Among the two monopoles, the electric monopole is of purely quantum origin, and its character has not been well resolved. In this work, we focus on the Rokhsar Kivelson point of the quantum dimer model, as the low-energy description of the quantum XXZ model on a pyrochlore lattice, and address the excitation spectrum of the electric monopoles, by combining the quantum Monte Carlo simulation with the stochastic analytical continuation. Most strikingly, the electric monopole spectrum exhibits clear double-peak structure, corresponding to the bound states and scattering states of electric monopoles. The lower-energy peak is attributed to the exciton-like bound state of an electric monopole pair, a signal of positronium in quantum spin ice. We found that the flippable loop operator serves as a local physical observable to excite a pair of electric monopoles, implying that monopoles can be detected through lattice or optical response.

</details>


### [88] [Contrasting one- and two-particle excitations in cuprate strange metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12569)
*Falk Röder. Roman Schuster,Martin Knupfer,Axel Lubk,Jörg Fink*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过分析铜氧化物正常态中集体激发（等离激元）的能量与阻尼的动量依赖，并将两粒子激发数据与ARPES单粒子数据对比，发现等离激元行为可由空穴与电子自能的组合描述，且"奇异金属"的边缘费米液体（marginal Fermi liquid）模型能解释其阻尼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规超导（unconventional superconductivity）的配对机制仍是固态物理的核心未解问题，且其正常态显著偏离常规费米液体（Fermi-liquid）行为，尤其是散射率随能量和温度呈线性。作者希望借助集体激发的色散与阻尼来检验正常态电子相互作用的性质。

Method: 分析现有 Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8 的电子能量损失谱（EELS）数据、Bi2Sr2Ca2Cu3O10 的新光学等离激元数据，并重新分析 La1.84Sr0.16CuO4 与 La1.84Ce0.16CuO4 的共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）声学等离激元数据，将两粒子激发结果与角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）的一粒子激发结果进行对比。

Result: 多数等离激元行为可由空穴与电子的组合自能良好描述；高能光学等离激元的色散仅弱受关联效应影响，提示存在稳健的准粒子（resilient quasiparticles）；低能光学等离激元的阻尼强烈受边缘费米液体模型所描述的低散射率影响，同一模型也能解释声学等离激元的能量线性阻尼；在经典 Lindhard 临界波矢以下未见理论预测的超阻尼光学等离激元（overdamped optical plasmons）。

Conclusion: 集体激发的色散与阻尼为检验铜氧化物正常态电子相互作用提供了有效探针，结果支持边缘费米液体图像，同时未证实关于 Lindhard 临界波矢以下超阻尼光学等离激元的部分理论预测。

Abstract: In unconventional superconductivity, the pairing mechanism remains one of the central unresolved problems in solid-state physics. Moreover, the normal state shows strong deviations from conventional Fermi-liquid behavior, most notably a scattering rate that is linear in energy and temperature. Here we study the momentum dependence of the energy and damping of collective excitations in optimally doped cuprates in the normal state. In particular, we analyze existing Electron Energy Loss Spectroscopy (EELS) data of Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8 [Phys. Rev. B 44, 7155 (1991)] and new analogous optical plasmon data of Bi2Sr2Ca2Cu3O10. Furthermore, we reanalyze similar Resonant Inelastic Xray Scattering (RIXS) acoustic plasmon data [Phys. Rev. R 6, 043184 (2024)] of La1.84Sr0.16CuO4 and La1.84Ce0.16CuO4. We contrast these two-particle excitation results with one-particle excitations measured by Angle-Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES). Most of the plasmon behavior can be described well by combined self-energies of a hole and an electron. The dispersion of optical plasmons at high energy is only weakly determined by correlation effects, indicating resilient quasiparticles. The damping of the optical plasmons at low energy, however, is strongly influenced by the reduced scattering rates described in the marginal Fermi liquid model for "strange-metal" behavior. In addition, the same model describes the linear-in-energy damping of acoustic plasmons. Contrary to some theoretical predictions, we see no overdamped optical plasmons below the classical Lindhard critical wave vector.

</details>


### [89] [Ground-state phase diagram of the spin-1/2 Heisenberg zigzag ladder with anisotropic $Γ$ term revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12669)
*Toshiya Hikihara,Nobuya Maeshima,Kouichi Okunishi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过精确对角化、密度矩阵重整化群与低能有效理论，系统重审了含各向异性Γ项的自旋1/2 Heisenberg锯齿梯子模型基态相图，证实有限Γ下只有带能隙的Néel、二聚、铁磁与双Néel相，TLL相仅在Γ=0各向同性情形出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型可作为准一维磁性半导体YbCuS2的有效模型而受到关注，但已有相图（Saito与Hotta）需独立检验，尤其各向异性Γ项对基态相与临界行为的影响尚待系统澄清。

Method: 结合互补的数值与解析手段：精确对角化（exact diagonalization）、密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）以及低能有效理论（low-energy effective theories）。

Result: 存在各向异性Γ项时，基态为Néel、二聚（dimer）、铁磁（ferromagnetic）与双Néel（double-Néel）相，均具有有限激发能隙；无能隙的Tomonaga-Luttinger液体（TLL）相仅在Γ项缺失的各向同性情形出现。Néel–二聚与Néel–铁磁转变均为连续转变，由中心电荷c=1的高斯临界性（Gaussian criticality）支配，并数值估算了其特征TLL参数。

Conclusion: 修正并细化了原有相图：各向异性Γ项使系统普遍有隙并压制TLL相，临界线由c=1高斯不动点描述，其TLL参数为量化临界态性质提供了依据。

Abstract: We study the spin-1/2 Heisenberg antiferromagnet with an anisotropic $Γ$ term on a zigzag ladder, which can be exactly mapped to the spin-1/2 XYZ zigzag ladder by a spin rotation. This model has recently attracted considerable interest as an effective model for the quasi-one-dimensional magnetic semiconductor YbCuS$_2$. For the model, the ground-state phase diagram was previously proposed by Saito and Hotta [Phys. Rev. Lett. 132, 166701 (2024); Phys. Rev. B 110, 024409 (2024)]. We systematically reexamine the phase diagram through complementary numerical and analytical approaches, including exact diagonalization, the density-matrix renormalization group method, and low-energy effective theories. We thereby demonstrate that when the anisotropic $Γ$ term is present, the model exhibits the Néel, dimer, ferromagnetic, and double-Néel phases, all of which have a finite excitation gap. The gapless Tomonaga-Luttinger-liquid (TLL) phase appears only in the isotropic case where the $Γ$ term is absent. We also confirm that the transitions between the Néel and dimer phases and between the Néel and ferromagnetic phases are continuous transitions governed by Gaussian criticality with central charge $c=1$. The TLL parameter characterizing the critical states at the Gaussian transitions is estimated numerically.

</details>


### [90] [Tetragonal-to-orthorhombic distortion in CeRh$_{2}$As$_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12873)
*P. Khanenko,M. Pfeiffer,K. Semeniuk,E. Hassinger,P. Thalmeier,J. Wosnitza,A. M. León,G. Zwicknagl,C. Geibel,S. Khim,M. Brando*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在 CeRh₂As₂ 中，沿晶轴多个方向的纵向热膨胀测量显示 T₀≈0.46 K 以下出现面内四重对称性破缺，伴随约 10⁻⁷ 量级的四方—正交畸变，对相 I 与超导序参量形式构成约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CeRh₂As₂ 以其沿 c 轴磁场下的两个超导相闻名，但相 I（T₀≈0.46 K 出现的有序态）的序参量本质仍不清楚；虽有偶极有序（dipolar ordering）的证据，仍需检验 T₀ 处是否存在对称性破缺。

Method: 对切割成八边形（octagonal shape）的单晶样品，沿 [100]、[010]、[110]、[1-10] 四个晶体学方向开展纵向热膨胀（longitudinal thermal-expansion）测量，以探测面内对称性变化。

Result: 结果显示 T₀ 以下四重面内对称性（fourfold in-plane symmetry）发生破缺，表现为微小的四方—正交畸变（tetragonal-to-orthorhombic distortion），正交度（orthorhombicity）δ∼10⁻⁷。

Conclusion: T₀ 处四方对称性的破缺可能意味着相 I 内发展的超导序参量不能是多分量（multi-component）的；更一般地，该畸变对相 I 的可能序参量施加了限制。

Abstract: CeRh$_{2}$As$_{2}$ is best known for its two superconducting phases, which are observed when a magnetic field is applied along the $c$ axis of its tetragonal unit cell. The emergence of superconductivity, with $T_{c} \approx 0.31$K, is preceded by an ordered state (phase I), with an ordering temperature $T_{0} \approx 0.46$K. The precise nature of the order parameter in phase I is still unknown, although evidence of dipolar ordering was found. To investigate a possible symmetry breaking at $T_{\textrm{0}}$, we conducted longitudinal thermal-expansion measurements along the crystallographic [100], [010], [110], and [1$\overline{1}$0] directions on a single-crystalline sample cut into an octagonal shape. Our results demonstrate the breaking of the fourfold in-plane symmetry below $T_{\textrm{0}}$, evidencing a small tetragonal-to-orthorhombic distortion (orthorhombicity $δ\sim 10^{-7}$). The breaking of the tetragonal symmetry at $T_{\textrm{0}}$ might imply that the superconducting order parameter that develops within phase I cannot be multi-component. More generally, the distortion places restrictions on the possible order parameters for phase I.

</details>

### [91] [Anomalous charge conjugation and parity in (2+1)-dimensional relativistic hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11454)
*Sonali Patnaik,Rajeev Singh*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该文给出涉及 \(U(1)_Q\)、\(U(1)_F\)、电荷共轭（charge conjugation）与宇称（parity）的混合反常（mixed anomaly）在 2+1 维的流体静力学与一阶本构描述，其互陈-西蒙斯能级（mutual Chern-Simons level）为 \(k_{QF}=\tfrac12 \pmod{\mathbb Z}\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多荷反常（multi-charge anomaly）体系中混合反常的响应性质缺乏显式刻画的问题，论文旨在明确该反常项在 \(\mathsf{C}\)、\(\mathsf{P}\)、\(\mathsf{CP}\) 对称性下的行为及其可观测的平衡态交叉荷响应。

Method: 在已建立的多荷反常扇区（multi-charge anomaly sector）框架下，构造流体静力学与一阶本构描述，给出整体流入（global inflow）代表，并计算平衡态交叉荷响应；同时借助一个解析的无荷中性流体（neutral-fluid）例子进行验证。

Result: 该响应在 \(\mathsf{C}\) 与 \(\mathsf{P}\) 下分别表现为奇（odd），而在组合的 \(\mathsf{CP}\) 下为偶（even）；交叉荷响应为 \(\partial n_Q/\partial B_F=\partial n_F/\partial B_Q=1/4\pi \bmod 1/2\pi\)。

Conclusion: 解析的无荷中性流体例子表明该交叉荷响应不为零，而无源（source-free）流体力学极点保持不变，说明该反常响应可独立于谱结构而存在。

Abstract: We present a hydrostatic and first-order constitutive description of a mixed anomaly that involves $U(1)_Q$, $U(1)_F$, charge conjugation symmetry (acting only on $U(1)_Q$), and parity symmetry in $2+1$ dimensions. It is represented by a mutual Chern-Simons level $k_{QF}=\frac12 \,(\mathrm{mod}\ \mathbb Z)$. Adopting the already established multi-charge anomaly sector, we explicitly provide the information that this response is $odd$ under $\mathsf{C}$ and $\mathsf{P}$ symmetries separately, but $even$ under combined $\mathsf{CP}$ symmetry. We also provide a global inflow representative and compute the equilibrium cross-charge response which includes $\partial n_Q/\partial B_F=\partial n_F/\partial B_Q=1/4π\bmod 1/2π$. Using an analytic neutral-fluid example we show that this response is non-vanishing whereas the source-free hydrodynamic poles are invariant.

</details>


### [92] [Reabsorbed Urca neutrinos suppress bulk-viscous damping of neutron star merger oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12335)
*Prashant Thakur,Yeunhwan Lim*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该摘要指出，Urca中微子（Urca neutrinos）的重吸收（reabsorption）会显著抑制中子星并合振荡中预期的体粘滞阻尼（bulk-viscous damping），将阻尼时间尺度从19 ms延长至2.7 s量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型中子星并合振荡的体粘滞阻尼常基于中微子自由逃逸（freely escaping neutrinos）假设，可能高估阻尼效率，因此需检验Urca中微子被重吸收对阻尼的影响。

Method: 采用能量分辨的动理学处理（energy-resolved kinetic treatment），在核密度与4 MeV条件下考察1-kHz、10-km振荡，并跨多个取样状态、三种状态方程（equations of state）及反应率变化进行检验；同时对比全运动学反应率（full-kinematics rates）与弹性不透明度表（elastic opacity tables）。

Result: 在直接Urca阈值（direct-Urca threshold）以下，俘获中微子平均能量为19-46 MeV并在数米内被吸收；能量分辨处理给出阻尼时间为2.7 s，而自由逃逸情形仅为19 ms，且该结果在取样状态、三种状态方程与反应率变化下均成立，组分近乎冻结（nearly frozen composition）。

Conclusion: Urca中微子重吸收在中子星并合振荡中普遍存在，但其效应仅体现在全运动学反应率中、不见于弹性不透明度表，故基于弹性不透明度的阻尼预测需修正。

Abstract: Reabsorption of Urca neutrinos suppresses the bulk-viscous damping predicted for neutron star merger oscillations. Below the direct-Urca threshold the capture neutrinos have mean energies of 19-46 MeV and are absorbed within metres. An energy-resolved kinetic treatment gives 2.7 s for a 1-kHz, 10-km oscillation at nuclear density and 4 MeV, against 19 ms for freely escaping neutrinos. It holds across the sampled states, three equations of state and the rate variations, with nearly frozen composition. This absorption is present in full-kinematics rates and absent from elastic opacity tables.

</details>

### [93] [Dynamic Molecular Transformation Mediated Crystallization Regulation and Interface Passivation in Tin Based Perovskite Solar Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10420)
*Ying Tang,Zuhong Zhang,Hongzhuo Wu,Zhihao Li,Zhenhuang Su,Yonggang Yang,Hairui Liu,Feng Yang,Zhe Li,Luca Sapienza,Jorge Pascual,Shengnan Zuo,Bo Hou,Antonio Abate,Meng Li,Yufang Liu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作利用1-乙酰基哌嗪（1-acetylpiperazine, PX）的动态分子转化（dynamic molecular transformation），在成核生长阶段稳定Sn$^{2+}$前驱体并调控结晶，在退火中原位转化为脒基功能化物种钝化表面A位空位，实现体相到界面的协同优化，器件效率达15.18%并具良好光稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锡基钙钛矿（tin-based perovskites）是无铅光伏（lead-free photovoltaics）的有前景候选材料，但存在Sn$^{2+}$配位不稳定、结晶过快及严重界面缺陷等问题，需同时调控结晶过程与界面化学。

Method: 引入1-乙酰基哌嗪（PX）实现动态分子转化：成核与生长阶段羰基（carbonyl group）与Sn$^{2+}$配位以稳定前驱体络合物、缓和过饱和度（supersaturation）并实现可控生长；退火过程中PX原位转化，推测生成脒基功能化物种（amidinium-functionalized species）占据表面A位空位形成钝化层。

Result: 获得致密、高结晶度的钙钛矿薄膜并改善光电质量；器件功率转换效率（power conversion efficiency, PCE）达15.18%，在连续光照500小时后保持初始效率的94.1%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出将结晶控制与界面钝化（interface passivation）整合的动态分子工程（dynamic molecular engineering）策略，为高效稳定的无铅钙钛矿光伏提供可行路径。

Abstract: Tin-based perovskites are promising candidates for lead-free photovoltaics but suffer from labile Sn$^{2+}$ coordination, rapid crystallization, and severe interfacial defects. Here, we develop a dynamic molecular transformation using 1-acetylpiperazine (PX) to regulate crystallization and interface chemistry simultaneously. During nucleation and growth, the carbonyl group coordinates with Sn$^{2+}$ to stabilize precursor complexes, moderate supersaturation, and enable controlled crystal growth. Upon annealing, PX undergoes in situ molecular transformation, suggested to form amidinium-functionalized species that preferentially occupy surface A-site vacancies, forming a passivation layer and suppressing nonradiative recombination. This coupled bulk-to-interface evolution yields compact, highly crystalline films with improved optoelectronic quality. Devices achieve a power conversion efficiency of 15.18% and retain 94.1% of initial efficiency after 500 h of continuous illumination. This work demonstrates a dynamic molecular engineering approach integrating crystallization control and interface passivation, providing a pathway toward efficient and stable lead-free perovskite photovoltaics.

</details>


### [94] [2D polaritons for Squeezing and Shrinking Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10754)
*Eduardo Bede Barros,Niclas Sven Müller,Stephanie Reich,Christian Thomsen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作构建了一个无量纲标度的量子微观框架（quantum microscopic framework），用以描述二维材料中的极化激元（polaritons），并揭示极端波长压缩与电场正交分量内禀量子压缩（quantum squeezing）之间的协同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料可将光限制在深亚波长尺度，但材料厚度接近纳米极限时，宏观电动力学模型（macroscopic electrodynamic models）不再适用，导致对二维极化激元的理解存在明显空白。

Method: 从可极化二维表面（polarizable 2D surface）的量子性质出发，采用通用的无量纲哈密顿量（scale-less Hamiltonian）建立量子微观框架，推导集体激发（collective excitations），并识别出存在于光锥之外的横电磁模式（transverse electric magnetic modes）。

Result: 结果表明，二维体系可同时实现极端的波长收缩（wavelength shrinking）与电场正交分量的内禀量子压缩，二者形成强协同效应。

Conclusion: 该理论路线图为在纳米尺度控制光的非经典态（non-classical states of light）提供了基础架构，并为高精度量子传感（quantum sensing）与下一代光电子器件开辟新途径。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) materials have emerged as an extraordinary platform for polaritonics, given their remarkable ability to confine light to deep sub-wavelength scales. However, as material thicknesses reach the nanometer limit, macroscopic electrodynamic models become inadequate for the description of light-matter coupling in 2D materials, leaving a significant gap in our understanding of 2D polaritons. In this work, we develop a comprehensive quantum microscopic framework that describes 2D polaritons through a general, scale-less Hamiltonian. Starting from the quantum properties of a polarizable 2D surface, we derive collective excitations and we identify both transverse electric magnetic modes existing outside the light cone. Our results demonstrate that these 2D systems facilitate a powerful synergy between extreme wavelength shrinking and intrinsic quantum squeezing of electric field quadratures. By establishing this theoretical roadmap, our work provides the essential architecture for controlling non-classical states of light at the nanoscale, opening new avenues for high-precision quantum sensing and the development of next-generation optoelectronic devices

</details>

### [95] [Surface Enhanced Anisotropy and Its Impacts on Massive Neutron Star Configurations in Einstein Gravity : Stability, Structure, and Multimessenger Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09974)
*Ritabrata Biswas,Arpan Bhattacharya,Subhajit Pal*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在广义相对论（general relativity）框架下，采用三阶多项式状态方程（equation of state）与Buchdahl class-I度规拟设，构建了物理上可行且与观测相容的各向异性中子星（anisotropic neutron star）模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究在广义相对论中引入压力各向异性（pressure anisotropy）后，能否构造出满足因果性、稳定性等物理条件并与多信使观测一致的中子星内部解。

Method: 采用三阶多项式状态方程 p_r=Aρ+Bρ²+Cρ³ 与 Buchdahl class-I 度规拟设，通过 Darmois-Israel 衔接条件（junction conditions）将内部解与外部 Schwarzschild 时空光滑匹配；随后检验物质分布、各向异性、广义 Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff 方程、绝热指数、声速、紧致度（compactness）、表面红移与潮汐形变（tidal deformability），并进行马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov Chain Monte Carlo）贝叶斯分析。

Result: 能量密度与径向、切向压强有限、为正且单调递减，各向异性在中心为零并向表面平滑增大；广义TOV方程满足力平衡，绝热指数满足 Chandrasekhar 稳定判据，声速满足因果性（causality）与 Herrera cracking 条件；紧致度低于 Buchdahl 极限，最大质量超过对应观测质量并触及低质量间隙（mass gap）；潮汐形变与 GW170817、NICER 的多信使约束一致，质量-紧致度关系与观测估计吻合。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该框架在广义相对论下为描述各向异性中子星提供了物理可行且与观测相容的模型，贝叶斯分析亦给出潮汐 Love 数、紧致度与微扰参数的统计可靠约束。

Abstract: Anisotropic neutron-star configurations are investigated within Einstein's General Relativity using a third-order polynomial equation of state, (p_r=Aρ+Bρ^2+Cρ^3), and the Buchdahl class-I metric ansatz, (e^{q(r)}=\frac{L(1+τr^2)}{L-τr^2}). Physically viable interior solutions are constructed and smoothly matched to the exterior Schwarzschild spacetime using the Darmois-Israel junction conditions. The stellar properties are examined through the matter distribution, pressure anisotropy, hydrostatic equilibrium, compactness, surface redshift, tidal deformability, causality, and dynamical stability. The energy density and radial and tangential pressures remain finite, positive, and monotonically decreasing throughout the interior, while the anisotropy vanishes at the centre and increases smoothly toward the surface. Generalized Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equation confirms equilibrium through the balance of gravitational, hydrostatic, and anisotropic forces. Furthermore, adiabatic index satisfies the Chandrasekhar stability criterion, and both sound speeds obey the causality and Herrera cracking conditions. The compactness remains below the Buchdahl limit, with physically acceptable surface redshifts. The predicted maximum masses exceed the corresponding observed masses, with some configurations extending into the lower mass-gap region. The tidal deformabilities are consistent with multimessenger constraints from GW170817 and NICER. In addition, the theoretical mass-compactness relation and its prediction intervals show good agreement with observational estimates. Finally, Bayesian Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis provides statistically reliable constraints on the tidal Love number, compactness, and perturbation parameter. These results establish a physically viable and observationally compatible framework for describing anisotropic neutron stars within General Relativity.

</details>


### [96] [Periodic orbits and gravitational wave radiation of a phantom Dirac--Born--Infeld regular black hole Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09975)
*N. Heidari,A. A. Araújo Filho,Iarley P. Lobo,V. B. Bezerra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究由幽灵狄拉克—玻恩—因斯坦格尔标量场（phantom Dirac–Born–Infeld scalar field）支撑的渐近平坦正则黑洞（regular black hole）时空中的周期轨道（periodic orbits）与引力波辐射（gravitational wave radiation），通过有效势、闭合轨道构造、能量扰动敏感性及S2恒星近星点进动（periastron advance）分析，并结合数值kludge近似计算波形，评估正则核心参数的可约束性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从引力波与轨道动力学角度补充对幽灵狄拉克—玻恩—因斯坦格尔正则黑洞（phantom Dirac–Born–Infeld regular black hole）引力性质的认识，并探讨正则核心（regular core）参数能否通过轨道观测加以约束。

Method: 从类时测地线方程（timelike geodesic equations）出发，分析有效势（effective potential）与特征圆轨道，构造周期性的zoom–whirl轨迹，考察轨道闭合对微小能量扰动的敏感性；并采用基于四极矩的数值kludge近似（quadrupole based numerical kludge approximation）计算代表性周期轨道的双偏振模式引力波信号。

Result: 给出了有效势与特征圆轨道行为、周期zoom–whirl轨道及其对能量扰动的闭合敏感性，并计算了代表性周期轨迹的引力波波形（含两种偏振模式）；同时通过Sgr A*周围S2恒星近星点进动评估对正则核心参数的约束。

Conclusion: 正则核心对轨道构型与波形特征具有可辨识的影响，轨道动力学与引力波分析可为幽灵狄拉克—玻恩—因斯坦格尔正则黑洞的引力性质提供补充性视角与潜在观测约束。

Abstract: We investigate periodic orbits and gravitational wave radiation in the spacetime of an asymptotically flat regular black hole supported by a phantom Dirac--Born--Infeld scalar field. Starting from the timelike geodesic equations, we examine the effective potential and the characteristic circular orbits and construct periodic zoom--whirl trajectories. We further explore the sensitivity of orbital closure to small energy perturbations and examine the periastron advance of the $S2$ star around Sgr A$^*$ to assess possible constraints on the regular core parameter. The gravitational wave signals associated with representative periodic trajectories are then calculated within a quadrupole based numerical kludge approximation, considering both polarization modes. Particular attention is given to the influence of the regular core on orbital configurations and waveform characteristics, providing a complementary perspective on gravitational aspects of phantom Dirac--Born--Infeld regular black holes.

</details>


### [97] [Observational signatures of Bardeen black holes under nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09979)
*Haiyuan Feng,Dan-Dan Cui*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究非线性电动力学（NLED）下 Bardeen 黑洞（BH）的阴影与光学外观：将 NLED 中的光子传播等效为有效度规下的零测地线运动，推导有效光学几何，考察视界—光子球—阴影不等式链，并计算薄盘与球状吸积模型下的光子动力学与光学图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画非线性电动力学（NLED）效应对 Bardeen 黑洞强场光学特征的修正，并判断其是否能产生可与标准 Bardeen 黑洞区分的观测特征。

Method: 利用 NLED 中光子传播等效于有效度规（effective metric）中零测地线（null-geodesic）运动的性质，推导 NLED-Bardeen 黑洞的有效光学几何；检验视界-光子球-阴影不等式（horizon-photon sphere-shadow inequality）；并结合薄盘吸积（thin disk accretion）与球状吸积（spherical accretion）模型分析光子动力学与光学图像。

Result: NLED-Bardeen 黑洞不满足完整的不等式链；增大 g/M 会减小光子球半径与临界碰撞参数（critical impact parameter），使阴影更紧凑、发射峰向内移动。薄盘模型中光子轨迹按碰撞参数分为直接发射（direct emission）、透镜环（lensing ring）与光子环（photon ring），模型 1、2、3 分别呈现三个、两个与一个强度峰；静态球状吸积中强度在临界碰撞参数附近因光程增强而出现峰值，而内落球状吸积引入额外多普勒红移（Doppler redshift）压低观测强度。

Conclusion: 阴影尺度、环结构与强度分布可视为强场区（strong-field regime）中 NLED 效应的潜在观测特征，NLED-Bardeen 黑洞可能具有与 Bardeen 黑洞可区分的观测信号。

Abstract: We investigate the shadow and optical appearance of the Bardeen black hole (BH) within nonlinear electrodynamics (NLED). Since photon propagation in nonlinear electromagnetic field can be equivalently described as null-geodesic motion in the effective metric induced by NLED, we derive the corresponding effective optical geometry of the NLED-Bardeen BH. Furthermore, by examining the horizon-photon sphere-shadow inequality, we demonstrate that the NLED-Bardeen BH does not satisfy the complete inequality chain. In addition, we investigate photon dynamics and the corresponding optical images generated by both thin disk and spherical accretion models. Increasing $g/M$ reduces the photon sphere radius and critical impact parameter, resulting in a more compact shadow and an inward shift of the emission peak. For the thin disk accretion model, photon trajectories are classified into direct emission, lensing ring, and photon ring components according to the corresponding impact parameter. We find that Model 1 exhibits three distinct intensity peaks, whereas Models 2 and 3 display two and one peaks. The systematic dependence of these features on the magnetic charge indicates that the NLED-Bardeen BH may exhibit observational signatures distinguishable from those of the Bardeen BH. In static spherical accretion, the intensity peaks near the critical impact parameter because of the enhanced optical path length, while infalling spherical accretion introduces an additional Doppler redshift that suppresses the observed intensity. These results indicate that the shadow size, ring structure, and intensity distribution can serve as potential observational signatures of NLED effects in the strong-field regime.

</details>


### [98] [Analogue Gravity: the Subcritical Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10006)
*F. Belgiorno,S. L. Cacciatori,A. Viganò*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在弱色散介质中解析研究了亚临界白洞（subcritical white hole）情形下无视界时的粒子对产生（pair creation），发现该过程为非普朗克型（non-Planckian）且缺乏霍金粒子伙伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往类比引力（Analogue Gravity）中的对产生研究多集中于视界存在的情形；本文关注亚临界（subcritical）白洞几何中无视界条件下是否仍存在粒子对产生，并厘清亚临界与跨临界（transcritical）情形之间的过渡机制。

Method: 采用次光速色散关系（subluminal dispersion relation）与弱色散近似，选取简化模型即Corley模型，并借鉴量子力学中越垒散射（overbarrier scattering）的方法进行全解析计算。

Result: 复转折点（complex turning points）在模式转换（mode conversion）中起关键作用，使朴素WKB方法所缺失的模式转换得以出现；亚临界情形下仍存在对产生，但其谱为非普朗克型且不存在霍金伙伴（Hawking partner）。

Conclusion: 解析结果与现有文献在定性上一致，并确认在亚临界与跨临界区域之间存在一个不产生粒子对的临界区间（critical regime）。

Abstract: We focus on the subcritical case in Analogue Gravity for a subluminal dispersion relation, and investigate the problem of pair creation in the absence of an horizon analytically, for the subcritical white hole case. We limit our attention to weakly dispersive media. In particular, we take into account for simplicity the Corley model and adopt an approach inspired by quantum mechanics in the so-called overbarrier scattering. The presence of complex turning points is shown to play a fundamental role in allowing a mode conversion which would be absent in a naive WKB approach. We show that there is still a pair creation phenomenon, which, in the subcritical case, is non-Planckian and without a Hawking partner. Our fully analytical results are qualitatively in agreement with the literature at hand. Furthermore we confirm the existence of a critical regime betwen the subcritical and the transcritical ones where no pair creation occurs.

</details>


### [99] [Scalable early warning for binary neutron-star mergers in third-generation gravitational-wave detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10133)
*Martin Gerini,Rodrigo Tenorio,Davide Gerosa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于短时傅里叶变换（short-time Fourier transform）与卷积神经网络（convolutional neural network）的低延迟搜索方法，将第三代引力波（gravitational-wave）探测器的双中子星并合搜索隐式边缘化至啁啾质量（chirp mass），在保持相当探测灵敏度的同时将计算成本降低四个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三代引力波天文台可在并合前数小时观测双中子星并合，地球运动与长信号时长使标准搜索方法在低延迟检测场景下计算负担沉重。

Method: 方法以短时傅里叶变换为输入表示，用卷积神经网络学习信号的非线性时频形态，隐式边缘化外部参数（extrinsic parameters），从而将搜索有效约减为啁啾质量维度，并近似最优贝叶斯检测统计量（Bayesian detection statistic）；在 ETΔ 与 ET2L 两种爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope）配置及约 25 分钟至约 6 小时的信号时长下，与 GPU 优化的匹配滤波（matched filtering）基线进行对比。

Result: 在大规模模拟信号注入（simulated signal injection）测试中，网络取得与匹配滤波基线相当的探测灵敏度，同时计算成本降低四个数量级以上，处理时间由分钟级缩短至毫秒级。

Conclusion: 该高效方法为第三代探测器时代的快速预警（early-warning）与多信使天文学（multi-messenger astronomy）提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Third-generation gravitational-wave observatories will observe binary neutron-star coalescences for hours before merger. Over these extended time windows, Earth's motion and the long signal durations make standard search methods computationally demanding for low-latency detection. We propose an approach based on short-time Fourier transforms and convolutional neural networks that implicitly marginalizes over extrinsic parameters, effectively reducing the search to the chirp mass. The network learns the signals' nonlinear time-frequency morphology, approximating the optimal Bayesian detection statistic. Using a large-scale campaign of simulated signal injections, we compare the network's performance with a GPU-optimized matched-filtering baseline for both the ET$Δ$ and ET2L configurations of the Einstein Telescope and signal durations from approximately 25 minutes ($f_{\rm min} = 5$ Hz) to approximately 6 hours ($f_{\rm min} = 2$ Hz). The network achieves comparable detection sensitivity while reducing computational costs by over four orders of magnitude, bringing processing times from minutes to milliseconds. This efficiency paves the way for rapid early-warning alerts for multi-messenger astronomy in the third-generation detector era.

</details>


### [100] [Stealth scalars on prescribed dark-energy backgrounds: reconstruction, branch regularity, and perturbative bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10231)
*José Blanco,Cuauhtemoc Campuzano,Víctor H. Cárdenas,Giovany Cruz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在给定 FLRW 背景上重构齐次 stealth 标量场（stealth scalar field），通过消去自势（self-interaction potential）得到标量剖面的 Riccati 方程，并据此区分精确 stealthness、整体正则性与“扇区保持识别”三个层级。结果表明该识别在混合平坦常 w 宇宙中并非动力学不变量，而共形耦合（conformal coupling）情形可化为精确的共动距离求积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: stealth 场因标量应力张量（stress tensor）为零而不影响背景膨胀，需厘清其重构在何种意义上成立：是精确解、整体正则解，还是可将标量应力某部分等同于给定暗能量密度。作者旨在明确这些层级差异及其动力学稳健性。

Method: 在常 w 或演化暗能量状态方程的 FLRW 背景上重构齐次 stealth 标量；令标量应力张量为零、膨胀历史由独立背景扇区支撑，消去自势后得到标量剖面满足的 Riccati 方程（Riccati equation）。对无辐射 wCDM 背景给出超几何表示（hypergeometric representation），在共形耦合极限下约化为共动距离求积（comoving-distance quadrature）。

Result: 证明了扇区保持识别（sector-preserving identification）在混合平坦常 w 宇宙中不是动力学不变量。无辐射 wCDM 背景下，正则分支与奇异分支可由背景相关阈值区分；共形耦合时重构对任意正则平坦历史（含辐射）均成立。CPL 幻影穿越（phantom-divide crossing）不会使背景 stealth 方程奇异化，但暗能量扇区的扰动需独立处理。标量–张量描述在有效普朗克质量（effective Planck mass）为正时给出光速高频传播；若无屏蔽且场局部追随宇宙学值，Cassini 约束将强烈压低相应晚期引力响应。

Conclusion: stealth 重构的有效性依赖于层级定义与背景类型：扇区保持识别缺乏动力学不变性，CPL 穿越虽不破坏背景方程但扰动层面另需处理，且引力响应受 Cassini 约束强烈限制。

Abstract: We reconstruct homogeneous stealth scalar fields on prescribed Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker backgrounds with constant or evolving dark-energy equations of state. The scalar stress tensor vanishes, so the expansion history is supported by an independent background sector. Eliminating the self-interaction potential yields a Riccati equation for the scalar profile. We use it to distinguish exact stealthness from global regularity and from the stronger identification of a term in the scalar stress tensor with the prescribed dark-energy density. We prove that this sector-preserving identification is not dynamically invariant in a mixed flat constant-$w$ cosmology. For radiation-free $w$CDM backgrounds, a hypergeometric representation allows the regular and singular branches to be separated by a background-dependent threshold. At conformal coupling the reconstruction instead reduces to an exact comoving-distance quadrature, valid for arbitrary regular flat histories, including radiation. A CPL phantom-divide crossing does not singularize the background stealth equation, although the perturbations of the prescribed dark-energy sector require an independent treatment. The scalar--tensor description gives luminal high-frequency propagation under a positive effective Planck mass. If the field tracks its cosmological value locally without screening, Cassini constraints strongly suppress the corresponding late-time gravitational responses.

</details>


### [101] [Relativistic Simulations of Self-Gravitating Radiation Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10320)
*Bing-Jyun Tsao,Pablo Laguna,Octavio Guerrero*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作为纯辐射流体（pure radiation fluid）提供了一套在广义相对论流体动力学（general relativistic hydrodynamics）框架下的数值模拟方法，将其建模为静止质量为零的自引力理想流体（self-gravitating perfect fluid），并基于 Whisky 代码实现与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需在强引力时空（strong-gravity spacetimes）中数值模拟辐射流体，而现有相对论流体动力学代码缺乏对纯辐射流体的处理能力，故需在已有框架上扩展。

Method: 将辐射建模为静止质量为零的自引力理想流体；基于 Einstein Toolkit 框架中已有的 Whisky 相对论流体动力学代码，修改源项（source terms）、恢复方案（recovery scheme）与黎曼求解器（Riemann solvers）以纳入辐射流体，并用标准激波管测试（shock tube tests）进行验证。

Result: 修改通过标准激波管测试验证；进一步给出单个黑洞辐射吸积（radiation accretion）的结果，以及处于不稳定流体静力学平衡（unstable hydrostatic equilibrium）的光子星（photon star）解，并展示其在微扰下的坍缩（collapse）与弥散（dispersion）。

Conclusion: 该方案可将纯辐射流体纳入广义相对论流体动力学数值模拟，能够处理强引力场中的辐射问题，并再现光子星在不稳定平衡下的坍缩与弥散两种演化结果。

Abstract: We present an approach for numerical simulations of pure radiation fluids within the general relativistic hydrodynamics framework. The approach models radiation as a self-gravitating perfect fluid with vanishing rest mass, enabling simulations of radiation fluids in strong-gravity spacetimes. Building on the existing relativistic hydrodynamics code Whisky, within the Einstein Toolkit framework, we introduce modifications to incorporate radiation fluids, including updates to the source terms, recovery scheme, and Riemann solvers. Modifications are validated using standard shock tube tests. We present results for radiation accretion onto a single black hole, as well as a photon star solution for radiation in unstable hydrostatic equilibrium, demonstrating both collapse and dispersion under perturbations.

</details>


### [102] [Shallow-water black holes and lattice superradiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10539)
*Antonino Flachi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究在浅水涡旋构成的旋转类比黑洞中，用地形坎固定视界并测量超辐射（superradiance）作为能量提取，并通过三种黏度扫描分离视界与黏性耗散的作用。结果显示能量通量的方向符号由波型与视界转速关系决定，视界内禀无黏，黏性份额仅在能带边缘附近显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
