Motivation: 旋转黑洞对散射波的放大（超辐射）可由流体类比引入实验室，但如何将视界的作用与周围流体耗散区分开仍属困难，需要可分离两者的实验平台。

Method: 构建格点流体（lattice fluid）中的旋转类比黑洞：以地形坎（topographic sill）设定视界的浅水涡旋，使唯一汇（sink）位于不向外泄漏的超临界层之后；以能量提取方式测量超辐射，扫描三种格点黏度，并对比排水锚定视界（drain-anchored horizon）情形。

Result: 对图案旋转慢于视界的同向旋转波，视界处能流向外；对更快波、反向旋转波及非旋转孔，能流则向内。三种黏度扫描将无黏视界的提取与共转半径（corotation radius）内黏性流体的贡献分开：远低于能带边缘时视界为吸收者，近边缘时黏性份额在工作黏度下约一半、在最低黏度下消失；排水锚定视界给出相同符号。

Conclusion: 该分析有助于理解超辐射机制，并提供廉价、灵活的类比引力（analogue gravity）实验设计平台。

Abstract: Rotating black holes can amplify waves that scatter off them, a process known as superradiance that fluid analogues bring into the laboratory. Separating the horizon's role from dissipation in the surrounding fluid remains difficult. Here we realise a rotating analogue black hole in a lattice fluid: a shallow-water vortex whose horizon is set by a topographic sill, so that the only sink lies behind a supercritical layer that lets nothing out. We measure superradiance as energy extraction: the flux at the horizon is outward for co-rotating waves whose pattern rotates more slowly than the horizon, and inward for faster waves, counter-rotating waves and a non-rotating hole. A scan over three lattice viscosities separates the extraction at the horizon, which is inviscid, from that of the viscous fluid inside the corotation radius: well below the band edge the horizon is the absorber; near the edge the viscous share is half at the operating viscosity and vanishes at the lowest. A drain-anchored horizon, as in water-tank experiments, gives the same signs. Our analysis gives insight into the mechanism of superradiance and offers a cheap, flexible platform for designing analogue-gravity experiments.

</details>


### [103] [Engineering a digital acoustic black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10540)
*Antonino Flachi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在同步数字电路（synchronous digital circuit）中构造类比黑洞（analogue black hole），并在现场可编程门阵列（field-programmable gate array, FPGA）上实现与电学探测，验证了视界稳定性、因果锥（causal cone）、铃宕（ringdown）与旋转超辐射（rotational superradiance）等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将引力类比系统（gravitational analogues）拓展到可重构的数字电路平台，从而以电路自身逻辑定义声速、流动与视界，为类比黑洞研究提供一类新的实现途径。

Method: 以同步数字电路实现类比黑洞，依据电路自身逻辑定义声速、流动与视界；在 FPGA 上实现该电路，并用信号发生器（signal generator）与示波器（oscilloscope）进行电学探针测量；同时将电路视为元胞自动机（cellular automaton）。

Result: 实验测量了视界的稳定性、因果锥、铃宕与旋转超辐射；并发现视界内部单个翻转比特（flipped bit）向外泄漏的量随深度呈指数衰减。

Conclusion: 数字电路构成一类可重构的引力类比系统，并能以元胞自动机形式实现类比黑洞。

Abstract: We show how to design and build an analogue black hole as a synchronous digital circuit. We define the sound speed, the flow and the horizon of such a circuit in terms of its own logic, and realise one on a field-programmable gate array, where the black hole is probed electrically with a signal generator and an oscilloscope. We measure the stability of the horizon, its causal cone, the ringdown and rotational superradiance. A single flipped bit inside the horizon leaks out by an amount that falls exponentially with depth. Digital circuits thus form a reconfigurable class of gravitational analogues and realise an analogue black hole as a cellular automaton.

</details>


### [104] [ERG, axions, and a PT-symmetric accelerating Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10584)
*Nick E. Mavromatos,Sarben Sarkar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文基于弦论启发的场景提出猜想：具有反常Chern-Simons项并与轴子耦合的引力理论，可在红外能标经历从厄米相到宇称-时间（PT）对称相的转变，从而给出排斥性牛顿"常数"并解释晚期宇宙加速，无需暗能量成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规量子力学要求哈密顿量为厄米以保证实能谱，而PT对称的非厄米哈密顿量亦可具有全实谱；本文旨在探索此类机制能否在引力理论中解释宇宙晚期加速膨胀，从而避免引入暗能量。

Method: 在精确重整化群（Exact Renormalisation Group, ERG）框架下，采用修正的局域势近似（modified local potential approximation），分析轴子与电磁势的手征反常耦合的beta函数；以beta函数中出现的奇异性作为红外相变的判据。

Result: ERG beta函数中耦合的奇异性被认为可能指示红外相变；在这些奇异性对应的能标上，场论从厄米相转变为PT对称的引力理论，其特征是排斥性的牛顿"常数"（repulsive Newton's "constant"）。

Conclusion: 作者由此猜想PT对称引力相可诱导宇宙晚期的加速膨胀，而无需暗能量成分；该结论属猜想性论断，其论证依据为反常耦合的ERG行为，尚待进一步检验。

Abstract: In conventional quantum mechanics, the Hamiltonian is required to be Hermitian, ensuring real energy eigenvalues. Certain non-Hermitian Hamiltonians can also possess entirely real spectra when they are invariant under the combined action of parity ($\mathcal P$) and time reversal ($\mathcal T$). Such Hamiltonians are called unbroken parity--time ($\mathcal{PT}$)-symmetric. We conjecture that, in a string-inspired scenario, a repulsive phase of gravity may be due to a $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric gravitational theory, characterised by anomalous Chern-Simons terms, coupled to axion fields. The conjecture is based on the behaviour of the chiral anomalous couplings of these axions to electromagnetic potentials. Within the Exact Renormalisation Group (ERG) framework in the modified local potential approximation, we argue that singularities in the ERG beta functions for such couplings may indicate an infrared phase transition. At the energy scales of these singularities, we conjecture that the field theory transitions from the Hermitian phase to a ${\mathcal P}{\mathcal T}$-- symmetric theory, of gravity, characterised by a repulsive Netwon's ``constant''. This may induce the acceleration observed in the Universe at late eras, without the need for a dark--energy component.

</details>


### [105] [Generalized Second Law of Thermodynamics in $f(Q)$ Symmetric Teleparallel Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10604)
*Nadeem Azhar,Kayoomars Karami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在f(Q)对称远平行引力（symmetric teleparallel gravity）中，以Wald型熵前因子构建视界熵，并导出紧凑的总熵演化主公式，验证三个可行模型均满足广义热力学第二定律（GSL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义热力学第二定律（generalized second law, GSL）在f(Q)引力下宇宙学视界（cosmological apparent horizon）上的有效性，并厘清其成立是否依赖于具体模型。

Method: 采用Wald型熵前因子Af_Q/4G，结合由修正Friedmann方程导出的修正项，推导视界熵的闭式表达式，并用符号计算（symbolic computation）加以验证；进而计算总熵的时间导数，并应用于幂律（power-law）、指数（exponential）与对数（logarithmic）三个观测可行的f(Q)模型。

Result: 得到紧凑主公式T_A Ṡ_tot = 4π Ḣ(Ḣf_Q + Hḟ_Q)/H⁴（单位8πG=1），可正确退化为广义相对论（general-relativistic）极限；三个模型在整个宇宙历史中均满足GSL。几何部分（geometric sector）的能量条件（energy conditions）依赖模型：幂律与指数模型仅违反强能量条件，对数模型在z>-1时违反零、弱与主能量条件，在z<3.84时违反强能量条件。

Conclusion: GSL的有效性可归结为一个与理论无关的结构性条件：对满足零能量条件（null energy condition）的常压无压物质，其成立等价于Ḣ的符号，即总有效膨胀历史中不存在幽灵穿越（phantom crossing）。

Abstract: We investigate the validity of the generalized second law (GSL) of thermodynamics on the cosmological apparent horizon in $f(Q)$ symmetric teleparallel gravity, using the Wald-type entropy prefactor $Af_Q/4G$ together with a correction term derived directly from the modified Friedmann equations. We derive, and verify by explicit symbolic computation, a closed-form expression for the apparent horizon entropy in $f(Q)$ gravity and show that its time derivative combines with the matter entropy rate into a remarkably compact master formula, $T_A\dot S_{\rm tot} = 4π\dot H(\dot H f_Q + H\dot f_Q)/H^4$ (in units $8πG=1$), which correctly reduces to the known general-relativistic limit. We apply this formalism to three observationally viable $f(Q)$ models --- power-law, exponential, and logarithmic. We find that the GSL is satisfied throughout cosmic history for all three models, and relate this robustness to a general, theory-independent structural condition: for ordinary pressureless matter obeying the null energy condition, GSL validity reduces to the sign of $\dot H$, i.e., to the absence of a phantom crossing in the total effective expansion history. The geometric sector exhibits model-dependent energy conditions, power-law and exponential models violate only the strong condition, while the logarithmic model violates the null, weak, and dominant conditions for \(z>-1\), and the strong condition for \(z<3.84\).

</details>


### [106] [Scalar-tensor reconstruction of coherent quantum black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10616)
*R. Casadio,A. Giusti,R. da Rocha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文构造了球对称黑洞几何的标量-张量实现，其几何源自具有有限核心与有限尺度的量子相干态；重构结果显示有效动能项仅在有限区域内为正，且Horndeski表述亦受类似限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在考察由量子相干态产生的有效黑洞几何能否在半经典引力理论（semiclassical gravity）框架内被一致地重构，并厘清其适用边界。

Method: 采用标量-张量理论（scalar-tensor theory）重构：确定与Einstein-Hilbert拉格朗日量的非最小耦合（non-minimal coupling）、动能耦合（kinetic coupling）与标量势（scalar potential），并进一步用含导数耦合的Horndeski作用量（Horndeski action）复现同一几何、确定其有效域。

Result: 动能项仅在有限区域内为正；对固定黑洞质量，这些区域的边界给出将核心尺寸与空间延展同标量-张量动力学相联系的自然机制。两种重构均不包含事件视界（event horizon），且无法同时复现任意大的内部与外部区域。

Conclusion: 该黑洞几何的量子起源似乎部分地落在半经典引力理论适用范围之外。

Abstract: We construct a scalar-tensor realisation of spherically symmetric black hole geometries that effectively arise from quantum coherent states characterised by a finite core and finite extension. We determine the non-minimal coupling to the Einstein-Hilbert Lagrangian, kinetic coupling, and scalar potential and show that the kinetic term is positive only in finite domains. For fixed black hole mass, the boundaries of these domains provide a natural mechanism that relates the core size and spatial extension to the dynamics of the scalar-tensor realisation. We further show that the same geometry can also be obtained from a Horndeski action with derivative coupling and determine its domain of validity. Neither reconstruction includes the event horizon nor can it simultaneously reproduce arbitrarily large portions of the interior and exterior of the black hole, whose quantum origin therefore appears to remain partially outside the regime of applicability of semiclassical theories of gravity.

</details>


### [107] [Black-hole ringdown at an exceptional point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10661)
*Kyriakos Destounis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在旋转带电 de Sitter 黑洞的例外点（exceptional point）附近，光子球模式与近视界模式合并，其响应可获得线性时间因子；本文由约束 Einstein-Maxwell 数据计算该振幅，并表明可在固定几何下通过调节初始扰动抑制线性项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞以阻尼振荡形式“鸣响”（ringdown），而在例外点处模式合并会使响应出现线性时间因子，其振幅来源及对观测拟合的影响尚需定量刻画。

Method: 针对 de Sitter 空间（de Sitter space）中旋转且带电的黑洞，基于约束的 Einstein-Maxwell 数据计算例外点处线性时间项的振幅，并在固定七个竞争模式（competing modes）的条件下与指数拟合进行比较。

Result: 晚时曲率误差（late-time curvature error）在成对模式下为 0.00383%，在重新拟合的指数模型下为 0.01401%；线性项可在固定几何下通过调节初始扰动予以抑制。

Conclusion: 在模式接近合并（near coalescence）时，仅改变初始扰动即可改变由短时 ringdown 拟合（short ringdown fit）推断出的频率。

Abstract: Black holes ring in damped oscillations. At an exceptional point, photon-sphere and near-horizon modes merge, and their response can acquire a linear time factor. We compute its amplitude from constrained Einstein-Maxwell data for a rotating, charged black hole in de Sitter space. With seven competing modes fixed, the late-time curvature error is 0.00383$\%$ for the pair and 0.01401$\%$ for a refitted exponential. We suppress the linear term by tuning the initial disturbance at fixed geometry. Hence, near coalescence, changing only the initial disturbance can shift the frequency inferred from a short ringdown fit.

</details>


### [108] [The Energy-Momentum Tensor of a Compact Object in General Relativity to all Multipole Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10783)
*Trevor Scheopner,Felix Lichtner*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 采用Mathisson–Papapetrou–Dixon形式体系，构建了一组与各多极矩一一共轭的独立张量基，并给出致密天体能量-动量张量以约化多极矩表示的精确分布公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效场论（effective field theory）描述致密天体运动时，自旋与潮汐效应由黎曼张量及其导数构成的算符编码，但大量算符对可观测量贡献简并，且单个算符常同时影响多个多极矩，导致描述冗余。

Method: 基于Mathisson–Papapetrou–Dixon形式体系，构造一组相互独立且各自仅与唯一一个多极矩正则共轭（canonically conjugate）的张量基。

Result: 得到致密天体能量-动量张量（energy-momentum tensor）的新的精确分布公式，其以约化多极矩（reduced multipole moments）与该张量基共同表达。

Conclusion: 该张量基可消除多极矩间的简并，为有效场论中自旋与潮汐算符的组织及致密天体多极结构的描述提供自然且精确的表述框架。

Abstract: In effective field theory approaches to compact object motion in general relativity, spin and tidal effects are encoded by operators formed from the Riemann tensor and its derivatives. Many such operators contribute degenerately to observables, with a single one often affecting several multipole moments of the body. Using the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon formalism, we construct a natural basis of independent tensors which are each canonically conjugate to precisely one multipole. We find the novel exact distributional formula for the energy-momentum tensor of a compact object in terms of its reduced multipole moments and these tensors.

</details>


### [109] [Variational Approach to the Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon Equations of Motion for Extended Objects in General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10794)
*Trevor Scheopner,Felix Lichtner*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文系统综述描述弯曲时空中有限尺寸物体精确运动的 Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon 方程，并提出以显式规范不变的变分原理直接构造该理论，用于相对论致密天体（compact objects）的有效描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限尺寸物体在弯曲时空（curved spacetime）中的运动需超越点粒子近似，而 Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon 方程虽给出唯一位移、动量与自旋，但其理论构造与多极矩（multipole moments）结构缺乏统一、系统的梳理与原理性推导。

Method: 采用显式规范不变（manifestly gauge invariant）的变分原理（variational principle）直接构建该理论，使之适用于相对论致密天体的有效描述；并讨论协变自旋补充条件（covariant spin supplementary condition）的施加条件，识别与多极矩共轭的曲率张量（curvature tensors）自然基。

Result: 给出该理论的完整综述；确立由变分原理构建理论的路径；明确协变自旋补充条件可被施行的条件；辨识出与物体多极矩共轭的曲率张量自然基。

Conclusion: Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon 理论可借规范不变的变分原理系统建立，为相对论致密天体提供自洽的有效描述框架，其中多极矩与曲率张量基的对应关系是理论结构的核心要素。

Abstract: The exact motion of a finite size object in curved spacetime is captured by the Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon equations. They associate a unique worldline, momentum, and spin to that object and determine the evolution of such in terms of its fully independent multipole moments. We provide a thorough review of this theory and show how to build it directly via a manifestly gauge invariant variational principle well-suited for effective descriptions of relativistic compact objects. We discuss conditions under which the covariant spin supplementary condition can be enforced and identify the natural basis of curvature tensors conjugate to the object's multipole moments.

</details>


### [110] [A note on Positivity and Rigidity for an Expanding Quasi-Local Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10880)
*Eric Ling,Annachiara Piubello*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究扩张型准局部能量（quasi-local energy）$E_\lambda$ 的正性与刚性，证明端点情形下 $E_\lambda=0$ 时区域等距于半径不超过 $1/\lambda$ 的闭欧氏三维球，并给出反例否定先前猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对前期工作 [ALP26] 中引入的、类比 Brown-York 能量的扩张型准局部能量，其端点刚性结论较弱（仅推出边界为圆球），且存在一个待验证的正性与刚性猜想，需要加强端点刻画并检验猜想的有效性。

Method: 在非负标量曲率（nonnegative scalar curvature）与适当边界假设下，结合 Shi-Tam 关于 Brown-York 能量的刚性定理与严格凹性（strict concavity）论证，并构造显式反例。

Result: 将端点刚性强化为：若 $\lambda=\alpha$ 时 $E_\lambda=0$，则 $(\Omega,g)$ 等距于半径至多 $1/\lambda$ 的闭欧氏三维球；同时构造反例表明能量在阈值之外可取负值，且能量为零不必然蕴含刚性。

Conclusion: 端点刚性获得完全刻画，但 [ALP26] 中的正性与刚性猜想在阈值之外不成立，说明该阈值 $\alpha$ 是相应结论成立的自然界限。

Abstract: We study positivity and rigidity for an expanding quasi-local energy $E_λ$ associated with constantly umbilic initial data $(Ω,g,k=λg)$, introduced in earlier work [ALP26] as an analogue of the Brown-York energy adapted to an expanding flat de Sitter background. Under nonnegative scalar curvature and suitable boundary assumptions, our previous results established positivity for $λ$ below a geometrically determined threshold $α$, while vanishing $E_λ$ at $λ=α$ forced the boundary to be round. In this paper, we strengthen this endpoint rigidity statement by showing that, if $E_λ=0$ when $λ=α$, then $(Ω,g)$ is isometric to a closed Euclidean 3-ball of radius at most $1/λ$. The proof combines the rigidity theorem of Shi-Tam for the Brown-York energy with a strict concavity argument. We also construct an explicit counterexample to the positivity and rigidity conjecture formulated in [ALP26], showing that the energy can become negative beyond this threshold and that vanishing energy need not imply rigidity.

</details>


### [111] [Qubit representation of the mass observable in quantized Schwarzschild interior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10933)
*Babak Vakili*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文构建量子计算框架，用于研究Wheeler–DeWitt量子化Schwarzschild黑洞内区的质量可观测量；通过Pauli分解将质量算符映射为量子比特算符，并量化编码与测量所需的量子资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Wheeler–DeWitt量子引力与量子计算直接联系，为引力可观测量的量子模拟提供结构化路径。

Method: 在关联双模谐振子态张成的物理扇区中构造质量算符的有限维表示，经Pauli分解映射为量子比特算符；解析推导非零Pauli项数与独立测量设置数的标度，并对最小非平凡截断实现双量子比特变分量子本征求解器（variational quantum eigensolver）作基准测试，另提出独立于质量估计的Wheeler–DeWitt约束验证量子线路流程。

Result: 解析给出非零Pauli项数与独立测量设置数随截断尺寸增长的标度关系，定量刻画编码与测量黑洞质量可观测量的量子资源需求；最小截断下的变分量子本征求解器结果与精确有限维谱高度一致。

Conclusion: 研究建立了Wheeler–DeWitt量子引力与量子计算之间的直接联系，并为引力可观测量的量子模拟提供了结构化路线。

Abstract: We develop a quantum-computational framework for the mass observable of the Wheeler--DeWitt quantized Schwarzschild black-hole interior. Working in the physical sector spanned by correlated two-mode oscillator states, we construct finite-dimensional representations of the mass operator and map them onto qubit operators through Pauli decomposition. We analyze the resulting representations for increasing truncation sizes and establish analytically the scaling of the number of nonvanishing Pauli terms and independent measurement settings. These results provide a quantitative characterization of the quantum resources required to encode and measure the black-hole mass observable. As a representative benchmark, we implement a two-qubit variational quantum eigensolver for the smallest nontrivial truncation and find excellent agreement with the exact finite-dimensional spectrum. We further describe a quantum-circuit procedure for verifying the Wheeler--DeWitt constraint independently of the mass estimation. Our results provide a direct connection between Wheeler--DeWitt quantum gravity and quantum computation and establish a structured route toward the quantum simulation of gravitational observables.

</details>


### [112] [Thermodynamic, Optical, and Wave Signatures of Charged Letelier AdS Black Holes with PFDM and Quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10942)
*B. Eslam Panah,S. Gurtas Dogan,A. Guvendi,O. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了含弦云（cloud of strings）、PFDM 源与 Kiselev 型精质（quintessence）项的带电 Letelier–AdS 黑洞度规，计算了曲率不变量、视界热力学量、矩形循环效率（W=0.095000，η≃15.39%）、光子球与阴影角，以及标量场径向 Helmholtz 方程中的有效势垒。作者强调结果为特定参数模型下的自洽分析，而非普适效率提升或渐近质量结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将弦云、PFDM（完美流体暗物质）与 Kiselev 精质三种唯象源纳入同一带电 Letelier–AdS 黑洞背景，系统考察其热力学、几何光学与标量场响应，并明确区分代数恒等式与需额外计算才能成立的结论。

Method: 基于给定度规推导曲率不变量（curvature invariants）、视界温度、熵、电势与视界质量函数，分析局部热响应与扩展相空间（extended phase space）恒等式；构造矩形基准热力学循环求功与效率；由几何光学导出光子球条件、临界碰撞参数与有限半径阴影角；对无质量最小耦合标量场导出径向坐标 Helmholtz 方程，分析频率依赖势垒与倏逝区间。

Result: 矩形基准循环给出 W=0.095000 与 η≃15.39%（几何单位、指定参数），作者声明此为模型与循环特定值。几何光学得到光子球条件、临界碰撞参数及有限半径阴影角；标量场中识别出频率依赖势垒、倏逝区间与候选捕获区，且程函（eikonal）势垒顶部尺度趋近光子球捕获尺度。

Conclusion: 结果应视为所采用唯象几何在特定参数下的一致性分析：在给定 PFDM 应力张量约定下 ρ_PFDM=−β/(8πr³)，正 β 对应负密度有效源；视界质量函数若等同于守恒渐近 AdS 质量，需另行分析电荷与边界归一化；势阱分析亦不能确定复准正规模（quasinormal frequencies）。

Abstract: We study a four-dimensional charged Letelier--anti-de Sitter (AdS) black-hole metric containing a cloud of strings, a phenomenological perfect-fluid-dark-matter (PFDM) source, and a Kiselev-type quintessence term. We derive the curvature invariants, horizon temperature, entropy, electric potential, and horizon-based mass function, and analyze local thermal response and the associated extended-phase-space identities. A rectangular benchmark cycle gives $W=0.095000$ and $η\simeq15.39\%$ for the explicitly stated geometric-unit parameters; these values are model- and cycle-specific and are not presented as a universal efficiency enhancement. For geometric optics, we derive the photon-sphere condition, critical impact parameter, and finite-radius shadow angle. For a massless, minimally coupled scalar field, we obtain a radial-coordinate Helmholtz equation and identify frequency-dependent barriers, evanescent intervals, and candidate trapping regions; the eikonal barrier-top scale approaches the photon-sphere capture scale. Two qualifications delimit the interpretation. First, with the stated PFDM stress-tensor convention, $ρ_{\rm PFDM}=-β/(8πr^3)$, so positive $β$ describes a negative-density effective source. Second, the mass function follows from the horizon equation, but identifying it with a conserved asymptotic AdS mass requires a separate charge and boundary-normalization analysis. The potential-well analysis likewise does not determine complex quasinormal frequencies. We therefore present the results as a consistent, parameter-specific analysis of the adopted phenomenological geometry, while distinguishing algebraic thermodynamic identities from conclusions requiring asymptotic-charge or spectral calculations.

</details>


### [113] [Dynamical entropy on local causal horizons: the Einstein equation of state and canonical-energy flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11006)
*Roh-Suan Tung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析两类零超曲面上的局部几何泛函：HWZ 代表 $S_{\rm loc}$ 与对数型泛函 $S_{\rm L}$。前者在无需末端平衡截面的条件下导出爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equation），后者在零收敛下具有单调性，且其对所有小束成立的要求与爱因斯坦方程等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有热力学推导（Jacobson 条件性方案）通常依赖最终平衡截面与展开的线性项，需要寻找无需末端平衡、且能刻画局部平衡与熵单调性的表述。

Method: 对无扭曲零超曲面（twist-free null hypersurface）使用逐截面局部的 HWZ 代表（HWZ representative）与满足精确 Raychaudhuri 恒等式的 $S_{\rm L}$，结合熵密度 $1/4G$、局域 boost 的 Unruh 温度（Unruh temperature）$κ/2π$、局域平衡截面与应力张量守恒进行推导；并在两函数类内用截局部性、正性与一阶 HWZ 归一化做唯一性选择。

Result: 将 $S_{\rm loc}$ 的首阶平衡变化与物质 boost 热量等同，得到所有零方向上的 $R_{kk}=8πGT_{kk}$，即含未定宇宙学常数的爱因斯坦方程；在无焦散的生成元束上 $S_{\rm L}$ 的平衡关系含曲率、剪切与膨胀项且均为正，故 $V\geq0$ 时在首次焦散（caustic）前单调不减。反向地，要求每个小束与零方向在正仿射参数下满足该平衡，等价于爱因斯坦方程。密度 $1+\ln J-Vθ$ 在给定归一化下唯一。两泛函均依赖仿射原点，文中给出平移律并指出原点由平衡截面固定。近平衡下 $S_{\rm L}$ 与 HWZ 动力学熵（dynamical entropy）一阶一致，其 Hessian 的温度加权变化等于 HWZ 修正正则能量流（modified canonical-energy flux）。

Conclusion: 爱因斯坦方程在此框架下表现为一种状态方程（equation of state，Jacobson 意义下的条件性陈述），几何泛函的单调性与等价性构成其另一表述。有限振幅下 $S_{\rm L}$ 仅为单调几何泛函，将其解读为熵需额外引入粗粒化（coarse-graining）公设，该公设被陈述但未加推导。

Abstract: For a twist-free null hypersurface, the Hollands-Wald-Zhang (HWZ) representative $S_{\rm loc}=(4G)^{-1}\int(1-Vθ)dA$, local on each cross-section (cut), obeys an exact off-shell Raychaudhuri identity with no term linear in the expansion, so no final equilibrium cut is needed. Given an entropy density $1/4G$, the Unruh temperature $κ/2π$ of an approximate local boost, local equilibrium cuts, and stress-tensor conservation, equating the leading equilibrium change of $S_{\rm loc}$ to matter boost heat yields $R_{kk}=8πGT_{kk}$ for every null direction, hence the Einstein equation with undetermined cosmological constant, an equation of state in Jacobson's conditional sense. On a fixed caustic-free generator bundle with $dA=J\,dA_{\rm ref}$, the functional $S_{\rm L}=(4G)^{-1}\int(1+\ln J-Vθ)dA_{\rm ref}$ obeys an exact balance per reference cell with curvature, shear, and expansion terms all positive, so under null convergence $S_{\rm L}$ is nondecreasing for $V\geq0$ until the first caustic. Conversely, requiring this balance for positive affine parameter on every small bundle and null direction is equivalent to the Einstein equation, given conservation and the stated normalization. Cut-locality, positivity, and first-order HWZ normalization select the density $1+\ln J-Vθ$ uniquely within a two-function class. Both functionals depend on the affine origin; we give their shift laws, identify the anchoring-invariant content, and show the origin is physical data fixed by the equilibrium cut. Near equilibrium, $S_{\rm L}$ agrees with the HWZ dynamical entropy at first order, and the temperature-weighted change of its Hessian equals the HWZ modified canonical-energy flux for stationary-vacuum perturbations. At finite amplitude, $S_{\rm L}$ is only a monotone geometric functional; reading it as entropy requires a coarse-graining postulate, stated but not derived.

</details>


### [114] [Covariant Hamiltonian Gravity in Spherical Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11099)
*Cong Zhang,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在球对称协变哈密顿引力框架下构造有效爱因斯坦方程（effective Einstein equation），使任意给定单参数静态球对称度规族成为其真空解；应用于广义Vaidya解时，其能量-动量张量恰为标准Vaidya形式，表明零尘埃源可支撑超越广义相对论的动力学黑洞几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将球对称协变哈密顿引力（covariant Hamiltonian gravity）推广为可描述引力动力学的有效爱因斯坦方程，并检验标准物质源（如零尘埃）能否作为超越广义相对论（general relativity）的动力学黑洞几何的源。

Method: 基于球对称协变哈密顿引力构造有效爱因斯坦方程：对任意给定的单参数静态球对称度规族，构造方程使其成为真空解；随后以广义Vaidya解（generalized Vaidya solutions）为应用，计算对应的能量-动量张量（energy-momentum tensor）。

Result: 所构造的广义Vaidya解对应的能量-动量张量恰为标准Vaidya形式；标准零尘埃源（null dust sources）可作为超越广义相对论的动力学黑洞几何（含正规黑洞（regular black holes））的物质源。

Conclusion: 仅靠量子引力修正（quantum gravity corrections）可能不足以解释类Hawking蒸发（Hawking-like evaporation），因为在该有效框架内，入射Vaidya解中的质量损失必然要求违反零能量条件（null energy condition）。

Abstract: Building upon the covariant Hamiltonian gravity in spherical symmetry, we construct the effective Einstein equation governing gravitational dynamics. For any prescribed one-parameter family of static, spherically symmetric metrics, this equation can be constructed such that the prescribed family constitutes their vacuum solutions. As an application, we construct generalized Vaidya solutions to the effective Einstein equation and show that the corresponding energy-momentum tensor takes precisely the standard Vaidya form. The results demonstrate that standard null dust sources can serve as matter source for dynamical black-hole geometries beyond general relativity, including regular black holes. Furthermore, this work suggests that quantum gravity corrections alone may not account for Hawking-like evaporation, as mass loss in the ingoing Vaidya solution necessarily requires a violation of the null energy condition within our effective framework.

</details>


### [115] [Exceptional Points in Scalar Quasinormal Modes of Rastall String-Fluid Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11366)
*Ming Zhang,Xiao-Mei Wang,Ya-Nan Wang,Miao Wang,Lei Zhang,Xufen Zhang,De-Cheng Zou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在 Rastall 弦流体黑洞（Rastall string-fluid black hole）标量准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）中，通过加入局域 Pöschl–Teller 势扰动，研究由凸起位置与幅度两参数控制的例外点（exceptional points, EPs）。对正则分支给出分辨率稳定的二阶 EP，对对数分支则明确指出截断中的缺陷点不收敛、不作连续谱断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是在 Rastall 弦流体黑洞背景下考察准正规模谱中例外点这类非厄米（non-Hermitian）谱退化结构的存在性与稳健性，并检验其是否可在数值连续极限下被确认。

Method: 采用双域 Chebyshev 配点法（two-domain Chebyshev collocation）求解双曲化问题（hyperboloidal problem），配合任意精度线性代数（arbitrary-precision linear algebra）；以复判别式、本征矢合并、Jordan 链残差、平方根分裂（square-root splitting）与闭环模式交换作为缺陷性（defectiveness）的互补判据。

Result: 在正则 A=1、ε_R=-1 分支上，两参数搜索在 N=24 至 N=60 的分辨率范围内定位到稳定的二阶 EP，受约束半离散演化（semidiscrete evolution）给出 Jordan 型响应 (C+Dt)e^{iω_EP t}；在 A=2 的对数分支上，每个有限截断均出现缺陷点但其坐标不收敛。

Conclusion: 二阶例外点在正则分支上得到数值确认并可视为连续谱特征；对数分支的缺陷点缺乏收敛性，故不对该分支作连续谱层面的结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we study exceptional points (EPs) in scalar quasinormal modes(QNMs) of Rastall string-fluid black holes by adding a localized Pöschl--Teller perturbation to the scalar potential, with the bump position $a$ and amplitude $\eb$ serving as independent controls. We solve the hyperboloidal problem with two-domain Chebyshev collocation and arbitrary-precision linear algebra, and use the complex discriminant, eigenvector coalescence, Jordan-chain residuals, square-root splitting and closed-loop mode exchange as complementary tests of defectiveness. For the regular $A=1$, $\eR=-1$ branch, the two-parameter search locates a resolution-stable second-order EP from $N=24$ to $N=60$, while constrained semidiscrete evolution gives the Jordan response $(C+Dt)e^{\iiω_{\rm EP}t}$. For the logarithmic $A=2$ branch, defective points occur in each finite truncation but their coordinates do not converge, so we make no continuum claim for this branch.

</details>


### [116] [Negative Dynamical Friction in Dark Matter Spikes around Rotating Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11380)
*Wei Xiong,Peng-Cheng Li,Bin Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在旋转大质量黑洞周围的暗物质尖峰（dark matter spike）中可产生负动力学摩擦（negative dynamical friction），向处于稳定顺行圆轨道的次级黑洞输送轨道能量；当黑洞自旋超过模型依赖阈值时，能量增益区延伸至最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）之外，且环境效应与保守自力（conservative self-force）量级相当，须同时纳入高精度EMRI建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质可通过轨道能量交换与保守度规响应（conservative metric response）影响极端质量比旋近（extreme-mass-ratio inspirals, EMRIs），因此需明确这些环境效应在引力波波形精度上的实际量级与符号。

Method: 基于旋转大质量黑洞周围暗物质尖峰的动力学摩擦分析，考察次级黑洞在稳定顺行圆轨道上的能量交换；并以银河系型晕参数、质量比 q=10^-5、四年旋近作为示例算例，与真空情形及一阶保守自力修正进行对比。

Result: 暗物质尖峰可驱动负动力学摩擦，使次级黑洞获得轨道能量；自旋超过模型依赖阈值后，能量增益区越过ISCO。示例系统中动力学摩擦总贡献相对真空产生约10^2弧度的相位差（phase difference），而固定频率环境红移修正（environmental redshift correction）与一阶保守自力修正量级相当、符号相反。

Conclusion: 环境能量交换与保守度规响应均对EMRI精确建模不可或缺，二者在波形相位上的贡献不可忽略，需在EMRI波形模型中统一处理。

Abstract: Dark matter may affect extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) through orbital-energy exchange and a conservative metric response. Here we show that dark matter spikes around rotating massive black holes can drive negative dynamical friction, supplying orbital energy to secondary black holes on stable prograde circular orbits. The energy-gain region extends beyond the innermost stable circular orbit once the black-hole spin exceeds a model-dependent threshold. For an illustrative four-year inspiral with Milky Way--like halo parameters and mass ratio $q=10^{-5}$, the total dynamical-friction contribution produces a phase difference of order $10^2$ radians relative to vacuum. For the same system, the fixed-frequency environmental redshift correction is comparable in magnitude and opposite in sign to the first-order conservative self-force correction. Both environmental energy exchange and the conservative metric response are therefore essential to precision EMRI modeling.

</details>


### [117] [Pushing the accuracy of the quasi-circular baseline for the SEOBNR family of models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11449)
*Cheng Foo,Alessandra Buonanno,Héctor Estellés,Lorenzo Pompili*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出升级版引力波波形模型SEOBNRv5iHM，在有效单体（effective-one-body）框架下改进旋进末段与并合-铃宕（merger-ringdown）连接方式，并用591个数值相对论（numerical relativity）波形标定，使(2,2)模的最大失配中位数降至4.25×10⁻⁵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着探测器灵敏度提升，双黑洞信号的精确建模对源参数刻画与天体物理推断日益关键；既有模型在旋进末段与并合-铃宕阶段仍存在系统误差，需进一步降低波形失配（unfaithfulness）。

Method: 在SEOBNRv5HM基础上构建准圆轨道、对齐自旋（aligned-spin）、含高阶模式（higher-order modes）的模型：移除旧模型中的非准圆修正，引入旋进与铃宕之间的唯象中间区域，使各模式的铃宕衔接时间可独立选取（可置于该模式振幅峰值处），并用第三版Simulating eXtreme Spacetimes目录的591个波形进行标定与验证。

Result: (2,2)模在10-300M⊙总质量范围内相对数值相对论的最大失配中位数为4.25×10⁻⁵，比SEOBNRv5HM低4.5倍、比其他半解析模型低3-8倍，84%情形低于10⁻⁴；M≥150M⊙时中位数降至9.7×10⁻⁶，与代理模型NRHybSur3dq8相当。倾角π/3下包含至ℓmax=5模式的偏振失配中位数为4.2×10⁻⁴，分别比SEOBNRv5HM、IMRPhenomTHM及其他半解析模型低2.6、1.9和6-11倍。

Conclusion: 由于自旋进动（spin-precessing）与偏心（eccentric）模型均以准圆、对齐自旋基线为基础，该改进可提升未来SEOBNR系列模型的精度，减少后续分析中的波形系统误差。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave observations have opened a new era of astronomy, with accurate modeling of binary black hole signals becoming increasingly important for source characterization and astrophysical inference as detector sensitivities improve. We present SEOBNRv5iHM, an updated quasi-circular, aligned-spin inspiral-merger-ringdown gravitational-waveform model with higher-order modes, constructed within the effective-one-body formalism and building upon SEOBNRv5HM. The improvements mainly concern the late inspiral and the merger-ringdown: we remove the non-quasi-circular corrections used in previous models and instead introduce a phenomenological intermediate region between the inspiral and the ringdown, which allows the ringdown attachment time to be chosen independently for each mode, including at the peak of the mode's amplitude. The model is calibrated and validated against 591 quasi-circular aligned-spin waveforms from the third Simulating eXtreme Spacetimes catalog. For the $(2,2)$ mode, when considering the maximum unfaithfulness against numerical relativity over a total mass range of 10-300$M_\odot$, SEOBNRv5iHM achieves a median of $4.25\times10^{-5}$, a factor of 4.5 below SEOBNRv5HM, and a factor of 3-8 below other semi-analytical models, with $84\%$ of cases below $10^{-4}$. For $M\ge150 M_\odot$, the median drops to $9.7\times10^{-6}$, comparable to the numerical-relativity surrogate NRHybSur3dq8. For polarization mismatches at inclination $π/3$, with modes up to $\ell_\mathrm{max} = 5$, the median maximum unfaithfulness is $4.2\times10^{-4}$, a factor of 2.6 below SEOBNRv5HM, 1.9 below IMRPhenomTHM, and 6-11 below the other semi-analytical models. Since spin-precessing and eccentric models are built on top of the quasi-circular, aligned-spin baseline, these improvements will make future SEOBNR models more accurate, reducing waveform systematics in upcoming analyses.

</details>


### [118] [Smooth Rigidity Near Extremal Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11495)
*Ye Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明：在退化 Killing 视界（degenerate Killing horizon）上，若 Killing 轨道空间的极限度规（limiting metric）可在紧致截面（compact horizon section）上表示为类空图（spacelike graph），则具有相同横向射流（transverse jets）的光滑真空爱因斯坦胚（smooth vacuum Einstein germs）在 d≥4 时于整个截面的双侧邻域内局部等距，从而消除了泰勒级数无法确定时空的歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equations）只约束退化 Killing 视界处的横向泰勒级数（transverse Taylor series），但完整知道该级数未必决定一个光滑时空：非零差异可在视界处各阶消失。因而需要寻找消除这种歧义的条件，以建立视界数据到光滑时空的刚性（rigidity）分类。

Method: 证明结合终端波映射规范（terminal wave-map gauge）与轨道柱面（orbit cylinder）上的能量估计（energy estimates），得到有限加权匹配准则（finite weighted matching criterion）。对具有正爱因斯坦截面（positive-Einstein sections）的非翘曲视界（unwarped horizons），利用完整 Lichnerowicz 谱（Lichnerowicz spectrum）条件将光滑分类化归为平行的第一横向数据（parallel first transverse data）。对于圆截面（round sections）另给出有限正则性版本。

Result: 当极限度规在紧致截面上的类空图条件成立时，d≥4 的光滑真空爱因斯坦胚在相同标记横向射流下于整个截面双侧邻域局部等距。所有平行第一横向数据均有收敛的多重翘曲爱因斯坦实现（multiwarped Einstein realizations），包括截面含负截面曲率方向（negative sectional-curvature directions）的例子。连续延拓论证推广至四维爱因斯坦–麦克斯韦理论（Einstein–Maxwell theory），给出光滑带电 Nariai 刚性（charged-Nariai rigidity）。

Conclusion: 旋转 Nariai 数据在正则分支（regular branch）上满足所需的类空图条件；全阶张量估计（all-order tensor estimate）在 a²/r₀²≤1/8 时给出光滑 Kerr–de Sitter 或旋转 Nariai 刚性，且无需假设背景轴对称性（ambient axial symmetry）。

Abstract: The Einstein equations constrain the transverse Taylor series at a degenerate Killing horizon, but even complete knowledge of that series need not determine a smooth spacetime: a nonzero difference can vanish to every order at the horizon. We show that this ambiguity disappears when the limiting metric on the space of Killing orbits admits a spacelike graph over a compact horizon section. In dimensions $d\geq4$, smooth vacuum Einstein germs with the same marked transverse jets are then locally isometric on a two-sided neighborhood of the whole section. The proof combines a terminal wave-map gauge with energy estimates on the orbit cylinder and yields a finite weighted matching criterion. For unwarped horizons with positive-Einstein sections, a condition on the full Lichnerowicz spectrum reduces the smooth classification to parallel first transverse data. All such data have convergent multiwarped Einstein realizations, including examples whose sections have negative sectional-curvature directions. We also obtain a finite-regularity version for round sections. The continuation argument extends to four-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell theory and gives smooth charged-Nariai rigidity. Finally, rotating Nariai data admit the required spacelike graph throughout the regular branch; an all-order tensor estimate yields smooth Kerr--de Sitter or rotating-Nariai rigidity for $a^2/r_0^2\leq1/8$, without assuming ambient axial symmetry.

</details>


### [119] [Quasinormal modes of a scale-dependent black hole in 5D spacetime with cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11584)
*Ángel Rincón,Zdeněk Stuchlík*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究五维正宇宙学常数下尺度依赖黑洞的准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs），在标量、电磁与狄拉克微扰下求解精确解析解，并用WKB半解析方法计算QNM频率，发现各微扰情形均稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尺度依赖引力（scale-dependent gravity）作为量子修正引入的引力框架，其对黑洞动力学稳定性（由QNM刻画）的影响尚需系统考察，尤其在高维带正宇宙学常数情形下。

Method: 在五维尺度依赖引力框架下导出标量、电磁、狄拉克三类微扰的精确解析解，选取与已有文献不同的解族；对 lapse function（时滞函数）作一阶近似以匹配小 ε 情形，分析各类微扰的有效势（effective potentials），并采用WKB半解析方法计算QNM频率。

Result: 验证了WKB方法可用于该背景几何下QNM的计算；给出经典情形与 ε 非零情形的QNM频率，以表格与图形呈现，并表明小 ε 范围内一阶近似与精确结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: 无论何种微扰类型，所有QNM频率的虚部均为负，表明该尺度依赖黑洞解在考虑量子修正时仍是稳定的。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of a scale-dependent black hole in five dimensions with a positive cosmological constant, considering i) scalar, ii) electromagnetic, and iii) Dirac perturbations. After deriving exact analytical solutions within the framework of scale-dependent gravity in five dimensions and selecting a different family of solutions (cf. \cite{Contreras:2019fwu}), we analyze the corresponding effective potentials (given by the lapse function) for each perturbation type. We employ a first-order approximation for the lapse function, as it closely matches the exact case for small $\varepsilon$. Since quantum corrections are expected to be small, our focus is on a relatively low range of the scale-dependent parameter $\varepsilon$. We confirm that the WKB semi-analytical approach can be used to compute the QNMs for these perturbations to the background geometry. We calculate the QNM frequencies for the classical case (for comparison) and for cases where the scale-dependent parameter $\varepsilon$, which quantifies deviations from General Relativity, is non-zero. The results are summarized in a series of tables and figures. Finally, we find that, regardless of the perturbation type, the solutions are stable, as the imaginary part of each QNM frequency is negative.

</details>


### [120] [Nonlinear Scalarization of Reissner-Nordström Black Holes in Scalar-Torsion-Maxwell Teleparallel Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11686)
*Joaquín Robledo,Yerko Vásquez,P. A. González*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在标量-挠率-麦克斯韦引力（scalar-torsion-Maxwell teleparallel gravity）框架下研究 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞的标量化，发现六阶耦合项可实现由非线性效应驱动的标量场饱和，并数值构造出带标量毛的渐近平坦带电黑洞解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性层面可避免快子不稳定性（tachyonic instability），因此标准线性标量化机制失效，需要探索由非线性效应驱动的新型标量化机制。

Method: 考虑标量场与 Weitzenböck 不变量非最小耦合的多项式耦合函数；在退耦极限（decoupling limit）下研究有限振幅标量扰动的非线性演化，并借助有效势分析；进而数值求解完全耦合场方程。

Result: 六阶耦合项使标量场可动态演化至非平庸稳态，对应有效势出现非平庸局域极小值；构造出带标量毛的渐近平坦带电黑洞解，按标量场剖面节点数分类得到多支解，其结构与重数依赖耦合参数，高阶耦合逐步抑制高节点分支。

Conclusion: 标量毛的背反应（backreaction）体现为度规函数与挠率标量（torsion scalar）相对 Reissner-Nordström 背景的偏离；结果表明非线性效应可替代线性不稳定性成为标量化及饱和的驱动机制。

Abstract: We investigate the nonlinear scalarization of Reissner-Nordström black holes in scalar-torsion-Maxwell teleparallel gravity, in which the scalar field is nonminimally coupled to the Weitzenböck invariant. We consider polynomial coupling functions that avoid tachyonic instabilities at the linear level, thereby exploring a scalarization mechanism driven by nonlinear effects. In the decoupling limit, we study the nonlinear evolution of finite-amplitude scalar perturbations and show that the inclusion of a sixth-order coupling term allows the scalar field to dynamically evolve toward nontrivial stationary configurations. This behavior can be understood in terms of the effective potential, which develops nontrivial local minima that provide a mechanism for nonlinear saturation. By numerically solving the fully coupled field equations, we construct asymptotically flat charged black hole solutions endowed with scalar hair. We classify these solutions according to the number of nodes in the scalar field profile, identifying multiple branches whose structure and multiplicity depend on the coupling parameters. We further show that the higher-order coupling progressively suppresses higher-node branches and characterize the scalar backreaction through deviations of the metric functions and the torsion scalar from the Reissner-Nordström background.

</details>


### [121] [Horizon-penetrating rotating black-hole configurations in VCDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11775)
*Antonio De Felice*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该摘要指出正则黑洞度规（regular black-hole metric）未必对应 VCDM（变耦合常数暗物质模型？此处指代按原文）中的正则位形，因为其偏好叶状结构是引力解的一部分；作者在常辅助爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦分支上构造了稳态旋转、可能带电的位形，并通过 17 个普查位形与平均通量扫描进行了数值检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规观点将正则黑洞度规等同于正则引力位形，但在偏好叶状结构（preferred foliation）属于引力解本身的框架下，这一等价性并不成立，因此需要具体构造并检验旋转、带电情形下的正则稳态位形。

Method: 在常辅助爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦分支（constant-auxiliary Einstein-Maxwell branch）上构造稳态旋转且可能带电的位形，其 Kerr-Newman 与 Kerr-Newman-de Sitter 几何携带非零常平均曲率叶状结构；采用视界正则的通量表述确定内部数据与双端渐近结构，并以 17 个普查位形、固定四维几何的平均通量扫描、平坦/德西特配对比较及连续残差与网格对比进行数值验证。

Result: 通量变化会改变偏好叶状结构的剪切（shear），但不改变标准检验物质所感受的背景；配对局部时移（lapse）与剪切差异在计算范围内有效幂次接近二，时移比较需固定时间归一化。所得精确时移界排除了 Δ_r > 0 的正则稳态外部块中的零点，并区分了奇异有限固有距离端点与渐近喇叭状（trumpet）端点。

Conclusion: 局部宇宙学解释需额外的匹配假设；动力学选择与旋转内部端点无法由稳态构造确定。该工作总结了稳态正则位形的构造与数值检验框架，并明确了其适用边界。

Abstract: A regular black-hole metric need not define a regular configuration of VCDM, whose preferred foliation is part of the gravitational solution. We construct stationary rotating, possibly charged configurations on the constant-auxiliary Einstein-Maxwell branch. Their Kerr-Newman and Kerr-Newman-de Sitter geometries carry nonzero constant-mean-curvature foliations that cross the future black-hole horizon and, in the de Sitter case, the future cosmological horizon. A horizon-regular flux formulation specifies the inner data and the distinct hyperboloidal and expanding asymptotic ends. Seventeen survey configurations are supplemented by a mean-flux scan at fixed four-geometry and paired flat/de Sitter comparisons down to $κM = 0.01$, where $K_0 = 3κ$. Varying the flux changes the preferred-foliation shear without changing the background seen by standard test matter. The paired local lapse and shear differences have effective powers close to two over the computed range, with the lapse comparison requiring a fixed time normalization. Continuous residuals, geometric reconstructions, and selected grid comparisons test the numerical solutions. An exact lapse bound excludes zeros in the regular stationary exterior block with $Δ_r > 0$. We distinguish singular finite-proper-distance endpoints from asymptotic trumpet ends and give conditional bounds on a stationary spacelike inner limit. A local cosmological interpretation requires additional matching assumptions; dynamical selection and the rotating interior endpoint are not determined by the stationary construction.

</details>


### [122] [Quasinormal Modes of Black holes in the generalized Schwarzschild-de Sitter solution of $f(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11895)
*Dhruba Jyoti Gogoi,Himanshu Chaudhary,Tiberiu Harko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在修正 $f(R)$ 引力（$f(R)$ gravity）的 Schwarzschild 型静态球对称解中计算标量、矢量与轴张量扰动的准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs），发现合理参数下结果接近广义相对论（general relativity, GR）Schwarzschild 黑洞，且参数 $A$ 与 $\eta$ 呈现非线性甚至相反的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修正引力（modified gravity）中的黑洞时空偏离经典 Schwarzschild 度规，需通过扰动与准正规模检验其可观测特征及与广义相对论的偏离程度。

Method: 针对含线性增长项与对数修正、依赖四个积分常数的 $f(R)$ 静态球对称解，采用高阶 WKB 近似（higher-order Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin approximation）与解析方法计算三类扰动（标量、矢量、轴张量）的准正规模。

Result: 适当选取参数时 QNMs 与 Schwarzschild 黑洞高度吻合；参数 $A$ 使环down引力波（ringdown gravitational waves）的频率与阻尼率呈非线性增强，$A$ 较小时 $\eta$ 效应类似，$A$ 较大时效应反转，且标量扰动相对矢量与轴张量情形偏离更明显；参数空间等值线图与时间域演化曲线支持上述结论。

Conclusion: 该 $f(R)$ 黑洞解在参数合适区间内与广义相对论难以区分，但特定参数组合下扰动谱出现可辨识偏离，为区分修正引力与 GR 提供潜在观测判据。

Abstract: We analyze scalar, vector, and {axial} tensor perturbations in a Schwarzschild-type static spherically symmetric solution of modified $f(R)$ gravity. This solution differs from the Schwarzschild metric of general relativity through the presence of a linearly growing term and a logarithmic correction, and it depends on four integration constants. Using the higher-order Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB) approximation and an analytical method, we calculate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) for all three types of perturbations. Our results show that, for appropriately chosen parameter values, the QNMs closely match those of the Schwarzschild black hole. In particular, the model parameter $A$ produces a nonlinear increase in both the oscillation frequency and damping rate of ringdown gravitational waves. A similar effect arises for the parameter $η$ when $A$ is small, while for larger $A$ values the effects are opposite, where the scalar perturbations display a distinct deviation compared to the vector and {axial} tensor cases. Contour plots of QNMs in the parameter space support these findings. Finally, time-domain profiles are obtained to illustrate the dynamical evolution of the gravitational wave signal.

</details>


### [123] [Efficiency enhancement in the Unruh quantum Otto engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11917)
*Yongjie Pan,Baocheng Zhang,Qingyu Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究由两个初始无纠缠、加速度不同的 Unruh-DeWitt（UDW）探测器构成的 Unruh 量子奥托引擎（UQOE），指出效率提升源于固有时（proper time）相对因子对两探测器布居变化的差异化加权，而非相干项或纠缠；在热吸收阶段后态可分离的条件下仍可实现超越常规 UQOE 的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多将量子引擎的效率提升与纠缠或相干等量子资源相联系，但纠缠是否为效率提升的必要条件尚不明确；本文旨在检验在热吸收冲程后态为可分离态（separable state）时，UQOE 能否依然获得效率增强。

Method: 构建两个加速度不同、初始无纠缠的 UDW 探测器与闵可夫斯基真空（Minkowski vacuum）中无质量标量场相互作用的 UQOE，选取对角自由哈密顿量（diagonal free Hamiltonian），并以其中一个探测器的固有时定义功与热；随后筛选满足初始态被动性（passivity）、循环闭合（cycle closure）与能量守恒的循环工作点，并考察探测器间距对效率的影响。

Result: 由于采用对角自由哈密顿量，场相互作用产生的相干项不直接贡献功与热，效率提升来自对不同探测器布居变化赋予不同权重的固有时相对因子；所识别的循环工作点效率高于常规 UQOE，且热吸收阶段后引擎态为可分离态。增大探测器间距会减少相互作用期间产生的纠缠，但间距进入非局域相干项，从而限制满足循环闭合的参数范围并影响可实现的效率提升。

Conclusion: UQOE 在热吸收冲程后处于可分离态时仍能实现效率增强，表明纠缠产生并非效率提升的必要条件；效率增强的关键机制在于固有时相对因子及非局域相干项对参数空间的约束。

Abstract: We investigate the efficiency enhancement of an Unruh quantum Otto engine (UQOE) composed of two initially unentangled Unruh-DeWitt (UDW) detectors. The two detectors have different accelerations and interact with a massless scalar field in the Minkowski vacuum. We choose a diagonal free Hamiltonian for the UQOE and define its work and heat with respect to the proper time of one of the detectors. Consequently, the coherence terms generated through the interaction with the scalar field do not directly contribute to the work and heat of the UQOE. Instead, the efficiency enhancement arises from the relative proper-time factor, which assigns different weights to the population changes of the two detectors. We then identify cyclic operating points that satisfy the conditions of initial-state passivity, cycle closure, and energy conservation. Their efficiency is higher than that of a conventional UQOE, and the state of the UQOE after the heat-absorption stage is separable. Finally, we study the effect of the separation between the two detectors on the engine efficiency. During the interaction of the two detectors with the field, increasing their separation reduces the generated entanglement; however, this separation enters the nonlocal coherence terms, thereby restricting the range of parameter settings compatible with cycle closure and influencing the achievable efficiency enhancement. Our results demonstrate that the UQOE can achieve enhanced efficiency even when its state after the heat-absorption stroke is separable, showing that entanglement generation is not a necessary condition for efficiency enhancement.

</details>


### [124] [Deep-Learning-Assisted Bayesian Inference for Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11922)
*Jian-Jun Song,Zhong-Wu Xia,Wen-Hong Ruan,Zu-Cheng Chen,Hongwei Yu,Puxun Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种深度学习辅助的贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）框架，结合神经网络参数空间定位、粒子群优化（particle swarm optimization）与马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）采样，在无需真实源参数先验知识的条件下，成功恢复极端质量比旋近（extreme-mass-ratio inspirals, EMRI）信号的后验分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI 的似然面（likelihood surface）复杂且高度多峰（multimodal），在缺乏真实源参数信息（实际观测中不可得）时，难以定位狭窄的高似然区域，这构成贝叶斯参数推断（parameter inference）的主要挑战。

Method: 构建深度学习辅助的贝叶斯推断流程：先由神经网络引导参数空间定位，再以粒子群优化进行搜索，最后用 MCMC 采样完成后验推断。

Result: 在模拟的六个月 EMRI 信号上，该框架恢复的后验分布与以真实参数值附近为初始化的参考 MCMC 分析结果高度一致，且初始化过程无需此类真实参数知识；神经网络训练完成后，单个信号的完整推断流程约需 10 小时。

Conclusion: 神经网络引导的定位可替代对真实参数初始化的依赖，使 EMRI 贝叶斯参数推断在计算上可行，并与参考 MCMC 结果保持一致性。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) pose a major challenge to Bayesian parameter inference because of their complex, highly multimodal likelihood surfaces. Locating narrow high-likelihood regions is particularly difficult without knowledge of the true source parameters, which is unavailable in real observations. We develop a deep-learning-assisted Bayesian inference framework that combines neural-network-guided parameter-space localization, particle swarm optimization, and Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling. Applied to simulated six-month EMRI signals, the framework recovers posterior distributions in close agreement with reference MCMC analyses initialized near the true parameter values, without requiring such knowledge for initialization. Once the neural network has been trained, the complete inference pipeline takes approximately 10 hours per signal.

</details>


### [125] [Gravity Without Absolutes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12489)
*Julian Barbour,Maria I. R. Lourenço*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文以牛顿引力理论为案例，论证还原论（reductionism）框架在描述N体宇宙时的局限，并主张以整体论（holism）取而代之。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学近半个世纪缺乏重大进展，主流物理与宇宙学范式存在未解问题，作者由此怀疑以还原论为核心的研究路径是否需要被整体论替代。

Method: 以牛顿第二定律为核心，在绝对时空（absolute space and time）的还原论框架下分析N体宇宙的描述，并引入莱布尼茨的充足理由律（principle of sufficient reason, PSR）来约束解的任意性。

Result: 在还原论框架下描述N体宇宙会引入无穷多任意解；PSR可消除这种无穷任意性，并改变对N体宇宙性质的预测，使其呈现意外的有趣特征。若进一步追问决定宇宙粒子数的原则，PSR暗示粒子数只能为无穷。

Conclusion: 在无穷粒子宇宙中，相关性（correlation）可能取代因果性（causation），同时仍表现出各状态由过去动力学演化而来的表象。作者认为这些想法尚待发展为可行理论，但支持了整体论的立场。

Abstract: By formulating his theory of universal gravitation in absolute space and time, Newton demonstrated the power of reductionism; it has been behind every major advance in physics since then. However, the lack of significant advances over the last half-century, together with unresolved problems in the dominant paradigms of physics and cosmology, suggest that holism may need to replace reductionism as the way forward. The ontological simplicity and historical significance of Newtonian gravity makes it the ideal theory for examining this possibility. Taking Newton's second law of motion to be the core of his theory, we show that its use within the reductionist framework of absolute space and time introduces an infinity of arbitrary solutions if used to describe $N$-body universes rather than subsystems within them. We show that Leibniz's principle of sufficient reason (PSR) eliminates the infinite arbitrariness and transforms the predicted nature of $N$-body universes; they are all shown to be unexpectedly interesting. An even greater change is enforced if one asks what principle could determine the number of particles in the universe. The PSR suggests it can only be infinite. We suggest that, in such a universe, correlation could replace causation while still giving the appearance that each state has been created by dynamical evolution from the past, whereas its actual explanation may lie entirely within the present state. While much work remains to develop these ideas into a viable theory, we are confident that it advances the case for holism.

</details>


### [126] [Thermodynamics of Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson black holes from background subtraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12683)
*Chang-Xu Yan,Zili Mou,Yu Tian,Hongbao Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 针对沉浸于Bertotti-Robinson磁场中的带电转动黑洞（charged rotating black holes）非渐近平坦性所导致的时间生成元与视界电势规范选择（gauge choice）歧义，借助积分条件（integrability condition）与渐近平坦情形的规范作为初始条件，实现规范唯一固定，并通过背景减除法（background subtraction method）得到质量与角动量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bertotti-Robinson磁场中的带电转动黑洞具有非渐近平坦（non-asymptotically flat）结构，使时间生成元的规范选择与视界电势（horizon electric potential）存在歧义，进而影响质量、角动量等守恒量的定义。

Method: 采用新建立的积分条件（integrability condition）形式，并以渐近平坦带电转动黑洞的规范选择作为初始条件来唯一固定规范；随后以闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）为参考时空，用背景减除法（background subtraction method）计算质量与角动量。

Result: 在固定规范下得到的质量与角动量使得黑洞热力学第一定律（first law of black hole thermodynamics）自动满足，同时CR关系（CR relation）与Smarr公式（Smarr formula）亦成立。

Conclusion: 规范可通过积分条件与渐近平坦初始条件被唯一确定，且由此定义的守恒量自洽地满足黑洞热力学基本关系，说明该方案可消除非渐近平坦背景下的规范歧义。

Abstract: The non-asymptotic flatness of charged rotating black holes immersed in the Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field causes the ambiguity in the gauge choice for the time generator and the horizon electric potential. By using the newly established form of the integrability condition together with the gauge choice for the asymptotically flat charged rotating black holes as the initial condition, we have found that the gauge can be uniquely fixed. With this fixed gauge, we further obtain the mass and angular momentum by the background subtraction method with the Minkowski spacetime as the reference spacetime. As a result, not only is the first law of black hole thermodynamics automatically satisfied, but also both the CR relation and the Smarr formula are obeyed.

</details>


### [127] [Numerical-Relativity Particle-in-Cell Simulations: Self-Gravitating Collisionless Matter in Dynamical Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12831)
*Jiaxi Wu,Elias R. Most*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证一个三维数值相对论粒子模拟（NRPIC）框架，用于求解爱因斯坦–弗拉索夫系统，可模拟无碰撞物质的引力坍缩、黑洞形成与双星并合等强引力动力学过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力坍缩与不稳定性、大尺度分离系统中的黑洞动力学以及极端黑洞（extremal black hole）形成等问题，需要采用精确且适配的全非线性数值研究，而现有工具难以对物质与演化时空耦合的无碰撞相对论系统进行完全动理学（kinetic）描述。

Method: 构建数值相对论粒子网格（particle-in-cell, PIC）框架，在AthenaK代码上求解三维爱因斯坦–弗拉索夫（Einstein–Vlasov）系统：将粒子动力学与移动穿刺规范（moving puncture gauge）下的Z4c时空演化相耦合，在自适应加密网格（adaptively refined meshes）上通过守恒的应力–能量沉积实现耦合，并引入利用测地力电磁（洛伦兹力）形式的Boris型粒子推进器（particle pusher）。

Result: 耦合演化通过球对称尘埃坍缩得到验证；成功追踪长椭球坍缩经无碰撞焦散（caustics）形成黑洞的过程，并考察相对论星团的稳定性与非线性迁移；进一步模拟共同无碰撞包层内两个过密区的坍缩，完整跟随黑洞形成、双星旋近与并合以及并合后残余体的质量吸积。

Conclusion: 该框架将相对论等离子体的现代PIC建模方法通过等效电动力学表述引入广义相对论引力领域，为数值相对论中研究引力不稳定性（gravitational instability）提供了可行工具。

Abstract: Gravitational collapse and instability, black-hole dynamics in systems with large scale separations, and questions surrounding extremal black hole formation require full nonlinear studies using accurate and adapted numerical tools. To this end, we present a new algorithm to study collisionless relativistic systems using a fully kinetic description of matter coupled to the evolving spacetime. In detail, we present a numerical-relativity particle-in-cell (NRPIC) framework for solving the Einstein--Vlasov system in three spatial dimensions. Implemented on top of the performance-portable \texttt{AthenaK} code, the method couples particle dynamics to Z4c spacetime evolution in moving puncture gauge through conservative stress-energy deposition on adaptively refined meshes. Our implementation makes use of advances in the computational modeling of relativistic plasmas using modern PIC models, now made available to general-relativistic gravity via an equivalent electrodynamic formulation. We introduce a Boris-type particle pusher that exploits the electrodynamic (Lorentz force) form of the geodesic force. We validate the coupled evolution against spherical dust collapse, follow prolate collapse through collisionless caustics into black-hole formation, and investigate the stability and nonlinear migration of relativistic clusters. Highlighting its potential to study gravitational instability in NR, we simulate the collapse of two overdensities within a common collisionless envelope, following black-hole formation, binary inspiral and merger, and subsequent mass accretion onto the remnant.

</details>


### [128] [Scalar quasinormal modes of the exact spinning Morris--Thorne wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12838)
*Mark Sukaiti,Davide Batic,Denys Dutykh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在精确旋转 Morris-Thorne 虫洞（spinning Morris-Thorne wormhole）的双端几何上研究无质量标量场的准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs），通过二维 Chebyshev 配置法（Chebyshev collocation）构造二次特征值问题，解析地给出谱的镜像对称性与超辐射窗口（superradiant window）约束，并数值表明采样扇区内的所有收敛频率均为阻尼且在窗口之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确旋转虫洞背景的准正规模谱及其稳定性此前缺乏系统刻画；该几何两端渐近静止系（asymptotic rest frames）相互旋转，带来与 Kerr 黑洞不同的谱结构，需要专门处理。

Method: 在完整双端几何上求解最小耦合无质量标量场方程，提取轴行为与两端无穷远处的出射相位（outgoing phases）后紧化径向坐标，采用二维 Chebyshev 配置法构造二次特征值问题（quadratic eigenvalue problem）；利用解析背景给出谱的精确约束，并以独立积分与 Kerr 谱作为交叉验证。

Result: 固定方位角数 m 时，频率 ŵ=r0ω 关于 Re ŵ = 3πjm/2 镜像对称（j=J/r0^2 为无量纲角动量）；多极族仅通过 mj 依赖 m 与 j，三个角向 Chebyshev 多项式精确表示 L=|m|,|m|+1,|m|+2。守恒通量将非阈值实频限制在超辐射窗口 0<ω/(mΩ_-)<1（Ω_-=3πJ/r0^3）；在 m=1,2,3 且自旋至 j=19 的采样扇区，全部收敛频率均为阻尼且位于该窗口之外。大 mj 极限下频率以正比于 (mj)^{-1/2} 的偏移趋近超辐射窗口上边缘。

Conclusion: 所计算的标量准正规模在采样范围内表现稳定，但完整的稳定性判定仍需对未解析的标量谱及耦合度规-物质系统（coupled metric-matter system）的微扰作进一步分析。

Abstract: We study the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of a minimally coupled massless scalar field on the exact spinning Morris-Thorne wormhole. The problem is posed on the complete two-ended geometry, whose asymptotic rest frames rotate relative to each other. After extracting the axis behaviour and the outgoing phases at both infinities, we compactify the radial coordinate and construct a quadratic eigenvalue problem by two-dimensional Chebyshev collocation. The analytic background yields exact constraints on the spectrum. At fixed azimuthal number $m$, the frequencies $\widehatω=r_0ω$ are mirror symmetric about $\operatorname{Re}\widehatω=3πjm/2$, where $r_0$ is the throat radius and $j=J/r_0^2$ is the dimensionless angular momentum. Each multipole family depends on $m$ and $j$ only through $mj$ and three angular Chebyshev polynomials represent exactly the multipoles $L=|m|,|m|+1,|m|+2$. A conserved flux confines nonthreshold real quasinormal frequencies to the superradiant window $0<ω/(mΩ_-)<1$, where $Ω_-=3πJ/r_0^3$. For the sampled sectors $m=1,2,3$ and spins up to $j=19$, every converged frequency is damped and lies outside this window. Independent integration verifies the low-lying modes, while Kerr spectra provide a separate benchmark for the two-dimensional solver. A limiting radial equation explains why the computed mode frequencies approach the upper edge of the superradiant window with frequency offsets proportional to $(mj)^{-1/2}$ at large $mj$. A complete stability assessment requires further analysis of the unresolved scalar spectrum and of perturbations of the coupled metric-matter system.

</details>


### [129] [Beyond $Λ$CDM: Probing Structure Growth and Observational Viability of Generalized ${f\,(Q , \mathcal{L}_{m})}$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12839)
*Raj Kumar Das,Sivasish Paul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究广义幂律型加性最小耦合 $f(Q,\mathcal{L}_m)=-Q^\mu+\alpha\mathcal{L}_m^{\nu}+\beta$ 的宇宙学效应，结合背景数据与结构增长数据约束参数，发现加入 $f\sigma_8$ 数据后 $\nu\to1$，显著不利于额外自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作主要关注 $f(Q,\mathcal{L}_m)$ 引力（gravity）的背景层面对比（宇宙测像学，cosmography）与 $Λ$CDM 比较，尚缺乏对非守恒物质连续性方程下结构增长的系统检验，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用加性最小耦合（additive minimal coupling）的广义幂律形式，通过新参数化（parametrization）导出哈勃函数（Hubble function）$H(z)$；在 $\nu\neq1$ 的非守恒（non-conserved）情形下推导扰动场、Euler 与连续性方程，并联合 OHD、DESI DR2 BAO、三类 SNe Ia 汇编（Pantheon+、Union3、DES-SN5YR）及 $f\sigma_8$ 探针进行参数约束与贝叶斯证据（Bayesian evidence）计算。

Result: 背景数据在若干情形下支持该模型，其中一种实现获得中等支持（moderate support）；然而纳入 $f\sigma_8$ 增长数据后，参数被驱动至 $\nu\to1$，贝叶斯证据决定性地不利于额外自由度。

Conclusion: 背景层观测不足以区分该扩展模型与 $Λ$CDM，结构增长数据对检验此类扩展具有决定性作用；模型中 $\nu\neq1$ 对应的有效粒子产生（effective particle-creation）机制未被数据支持。

Abstract: $f(Q, \mathcal{L}_m)$ gravity is a well-known extension of $f(Q)$ gravity, obtained through non-minimal or additive minimal coupling in the action's Lagrangian density. Background-level cosmography within this framework, along with comparisons to $Λ$CDM, has been the focus of prior studies. The present work considers a generalized power-law form of the additive minimal coupling, $f(Q,\mathcal{L}_m) = -Q^μ+ α\mathcal{L}_m^{\,ν} + β$, from which the Hubble function $H(z)$ is derived through a novel parametrization. When $ν\neq 1$, the matter continuity equation is rendered non-conserved, a feature that can be interpreted as an effective particle-creation mechanism. Constraints on the model parameters are obtained from Observational Hubble Data, DESI DR2 BAO measurements, and three SNe Ia compilations (Pantheon+, Union3, DES-SN5YR), allowing their cosmographic effects to be examined. Beyond the background level, the perturbed field, Euler, and continuity equations are derived under non-conservation, and their impact on structure growth is studied through a joint analysis incorporating the $fσ_8$ probe. Bayesian evidence, computed relative to $Λ$CDM, shows that the model is favoured by background data in several cases, with moderate support for one realisation, whereas the inclusion of $fσ_8$ drives $ν\to1$ and decisively disfavours the additional freedom, highlighting the importance of growth data in testing such extensions.

</details>


### [130] [Mapping of Spacetimes with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12857)
*Estuti Shukla,David Radice,Rossella Gamba,Sebastiano Bernuzzi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的神经网络方法，从数值度规数据中学习坐标映射（coordinate map）到指定目标规范（target gauge），并在Schwarzschild、Kerr及动态穿孔演化中验证成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值相对论（numerical relativity）中的规范自由度（gauge freedom）使模拟坐标的解释及同一时空不同描述之间的比较变得困难，需要一个能将数值度规与解析背景相联系的坐标映射工具。

Method: 训练神经网络使变换后的源度规（source metric）匹配目标度规（target metric），不预设坐标变换的函数形式；先在解析Schwarzschild与Kerr时空上验证，再应用于移动穿孔规范（moving-puncture coordinates）下的黑洞数值演化。

Result: 解析情形下可恢复已知变换（至目标时空的等距变换（isometries））；非旋转黑洞学习到的映射将其与入射Eddington-Finkelstein坐标下的静态Schwarzschild度规相联系；旋转穿孔情形下与Kerr背景近似对应，并据此构造曲率微扰（curvature perturbations），用Teukolsky方程演化得到的波形与完全非线性模拟提取的波形高度吻合。

Conclusion: 学习到的坐标映射可将数值时空与解析背景联系起来，在跨代码验证（cross-code validation）与黑洞微扰理论（black hole perturbation theory）方面具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Gauge freedom in numerical relativity complicates the interpretation of simulation coordinates and the comparison of different descriptions of the same spacetime. We present a data-driven neural network method for learning coordinate maps from numerical metric data to a prescribed target gauge. The network is trained by matching the transformed source metric to the target metric, without assuming a functional form for the coordinate transformation. We first validate the method on analytical Schwarzschild and Kerr geometries, recovering known transformations up to the isometries of the target spacetime. We then apply it to numerical black hole evolutions in moving-puncture coordinates. For a non-spinning black hole, the learned map relates the time-dependent numerical metric to a stationary Schwarzschild metric in ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates. For a spinning puncture, the method provides an approximate identification with a Kerr background despite departures from Kerr in the numerical spacetime. We use this identification to construct curvature perturbations and evolve them with the Teukolsky equation, obtaining waveforms that closely match those extracted from the full nonlinear simulation. These results demonstrate that learned coordinate maps can relate numerical spacetimes to analytical backgrounds, with potential applications to cross-code validation and black hole perturbation theory.

</details>


### [131] [Direct product geometries in $n$-dimensional linear, quadratic, and conformal gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12483)
*Petr Jizba,Tereza Lehečková*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了线性引力（linear gravity）、纯二次曲率引力（pure quadratic-curvature gravity）及共形引力（conformal gravity）高维扩展中直积时空（direct-product spacetimes）的精确解结构，对 $(m_1+m_2)$、$(2+m)$、$(2+\ldots+2)$ 型时空进行分类，并考察其与电磁源、共形关系及广义Petrov型的相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越广义相对论（general relativity）的引力理论中精确解难以获得，而直积时空（direct-product spacetimes）提供了一条可处理的求解途径；已有研究对非恒定曲率二维子空间（nonconstant curvature）的情形尚缺乏系统分类。

Method: 在 $n$ 维直积时空框架下，允许二维子空间具有非恒定曲率，分别针对线性引力、一般纯二次曲率引力及共形引力的高维扩展推导解空间，并按引力理论与曲率耦合（curvature couplings）对所得几何进行分类；同时分析共形关系、广义Petrov型（generalized Petrov types）与电磁源（electromagnetic sources）相容性。

Result: 含非恒定曲率二维部分的度规由曲率剖面（curvature profile）的代数条件支配，某一特殊二次引力类别中则由微分方程支配；仅含恒定曲率 $m$ 维子空间的度规关系为纯代数。值得注意的是，在所考虑的共形引力形式中，$n>4$ 的直积扇区不包含标准静态黑洞几何（static black-hole geometries）。四维与六维情形得到详细分析并给出显式解析解。

Conclusion: 直积时空解空间的结构强依赖于引力理论及二次引力中的曲率耦合；共形引力高维扩展在直积扇区中排除了标准静态黑洞解，提示该理论的高维解结构与广义相对论存在本质差异。

Abstract: Direct-product spacetimes offer a tractable route to exact solutions in gravity theories beyond general relativity. We study $n$-dimensional direct-product spacetimes of $(m_1+m_2)$, $(2+m)$, and $(2+\ldots+2)$ type in linear gravity, general pure quadratic-curvature gravity, and one of the recently proposed higher-dimensional extensions of conformal gravity, allowing the two-dimensional subspaces to have nonconstant curvature. We classify the resulting solution spaces and determine how the allowed geometries depend on the gravitational theory and, in quadratic gravity, on the curvature couplings. Metrics containing two-dimensional part(s) of nonconstant curvature are governed either by algebraic conditions on a curvature profile or, in one special class of quadratic gravity, by a differential equation. Relations governing metrics with only constant-curvature $m$-dimensional subspaces are purely algebraic. We further examine conformal relations to linear-gravity solutions, generalized Petrov types, and compatibility with electromagnetic sources. Particularly noteworthy is that standard static black-hole geometries are absent from the direct-product sector of the considered form of conformal gravity for $n>4$. Four- and six-dimensional cases are analyzed in detail, and explicit analytic solutions illustrating the resulting structure are presented whenever possible.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [A Geometric Theory for Nonequilibrium Transformer Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10691)
*Aythami Bethencourt de León,Darryl D. Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个基于统计物理（statistical physics）的变分与几何理论，用自由能（free energy）与 Fisher–Rao 梯度流刻画固定参数下 Transformer 注意力权重与 token 表示的联合演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对固定模型参数下 Transformer 注意力（attention）与 token 表示的联合演化，缺乏统一的变分与几何分析框架，作者希望借助统计物理中的自由能与梯度流视角给出理论刻画。

Method: 为注意力引入自由能，并用 Fisher–Rao 梯度流（Fisher–Rao gradient flow）建模注意力权重演化；将表示的哈密顿动力学（Hamiltonian dynamics）及其动量与同一能量驱动的注意力弛豫耦合，并构造两种纳入注意力值映射（value map）的替代哈密顿量。

Result: 证明在固定 token 表示下 softmax 矩阵是唯一自由能极小元且任意正注意力轨迹收敛于它；对给定时间依赖表示，变换到 logits 变量后注意力动力学变为线性，给出相对瞬时 softmax 的跟踪界与收敛到固定矩阵的充分条件；耦合系统满足能量耗散律（energy-dissipation law），并证明注意力流及三个耦合系统的全局适定性（global well-posedness）。

Conclusion: 该工作以统计物理变分原理统一描述注意力弛豫与表示动力学，为 Transformer 内部动力学提供了具有收敛性、跟踪界与适定性保证的几何理论框架。

Abstract: We develop a variational and geometric theory based on statistical physics for the joint evolution of Transformer attention and token representations at fixed model parameters. To this end, we introduce a free energy for the attention and model the evolution of the attention weights by its Fisher--Rao gradient flow. At fixed token representations, we prove that the softmax matrix is the unique free-energy minimiser and that every positive attention trajectory converges to it. For prescribed time-dependent representations, we show that the attention dynamics becomes linear after a change to logit variables. This result yields tracking bounds relative to the instantaneous softmax and sufficient conditions for attention to converge to a fixed matrix. Hamiltonian dynamics for the representations and their momenta are then coupled to the attention relaxation driven by the same energy. This coupling results in an energy-dissipation law. We also develop two alternative Hamiltonians incorporating the attention value map. We prove global well-posedness of the attention flow and all three coupled systems.

</details>


### [133] [On the universal structure in deterministic generative diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10338)
*Defang Chen,Zhenyu Zhou,Can Wang,Zeyu Zheng,Siwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该摘要指出扩散生成模型（diffusion-based generative modeling）的确定性采样轨迹（deterministic sampling trajectories）具有与数据模态和模型实现无关的普适几何结构，并可由基于最优线性去噪器（optimal linear denoiser）的可精确求解模型解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成模型已成为跨领域模拟高维结构化数据的通用计算框架，但对其生成机制的理解仍不充分；作者认为理解生成机制对于以有原则的方式改进模型训练与推理至关重要。

Method: 研究通过考察确定性采样轨迹的几何性质展开，指出该结构具有跨数据模态（data modalities）和扩散模型实现的普适不变性，并构建基于最优线性去噪器（optimal linear denoiser）的可精确求解模型（exactly solvable model）对其进行解释。

Result: 发现确定性采样轨迹共享一种普适几何结构，该结构对数据模态与扩散模型实现均保持不变；这一结构可大部分由基于最优线性去噪器的精确可解模型所解释。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的确定性采样轨迹存在可用简单可解模型刻画的内在普适几何规律，为以原理化方式推进扩散模型的训练与推断提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative modeling has emerged as a general computational framework for simulating high-dimensional, structured data across various domains. Understanding its generative mechanisms is paramount for advancing model training and inference in a principled way. Here, we demonstrate that deterministic sampling trajectories share a universal geometric structure invariant to data modalities and diffusion model implementations. This structure can be largely explained by an exactly solvable model based on the optimal linear denoiser.

</details>


### [134] [Quantitative Assessment of Crystal Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10380)
*Sergio Rincón,Gabriel González,Nicolás Andrade,Rafael Velásquez,Sebastián Ojeda,Juanita Puentes,Nicolás Aparicio,Paula Cárdenas,Pablo Arbeláez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OPAL-Bench，一个面向晶体结构预测（Crystal Structure Prediction, CSP）的方向不变、多晶型感知（polymorph-aware）大规模基准，将 CSP 与对称性引导的（Symmetry-Informed CSP, SICSP）方法作为两类独立任务分别评估，并配套发布 SE(3) 等变流匹配基线 OPAL-CSP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架存在三方面局限：依赖阈值敏感或不可比的指标；偏重几何性能而忽视物理合理性（physical plausibility）；未考虑多晶型（polymorphism），即同一化学组成可结晶为多种稳定结构。此外，SICSP 方法在推理时接收晶体学模板（crystallographic templates），实质上是约束搜索空间的不同任务，却常与标准 CSP 基线并列报告。

Method: 构建 OPAL-Bench 基准，以方向不变且多晶型感知的协议将 CSP 与 SICSP 分开评估；在 4 个数据集上，采用无阈值（threshold-free）的几何与物理距离指标，评估 7 种 CSP 方法与 2 种 SICSP 方法；同时提出 SE(3) 等变流匹配（SE(3)-equivariant flow-matching）基线 OPAL-CSP，用于 CSP 赛道。

Result: 实验表明，没有任何方法在所有指标上占优；几何性能与物理合理性之间相关性不强。OPAL-CSP 在几何指标上具有竞争力，在物理指标上位列最佳方法之一。

Conclusion: 在多晶型感知的统一协议下整合几何与物理指标，OPAL-Bench 可为 CSP 生成质量提供更全面的评估；同时通过区分 CSP 与 SICSP 任务，提升评测的公平性与可比性。

Abstract: Crystal Structure Prediction (CSP) aims to predict a material's 3D structure from its chemical composition, with applications from energy storage to biomedical devices. Despite its importance, existing evaluation frameworks suffer from key limitations: they rely on threshold-sensitive or incomparable metrics, focus predominantly on geometric performance while overlooking physical plausibility, and fail to account for polymorphism, where a chemical composition can crystallize into multiple stable structures. In parallel, recent Symmetry-Informed CSP (SICSP) methods often report results alongside standard CSP baselines while receiving crystallographic templates at inference, which constrain the search space and make SICSP a fundamentally different task. To address these limitations, we introduce an Orientation-invariant, Polymorph-Aware, Large Benchmark (OPAL-Bench) that evaluates CSP and SICSP as separate tasks. We evaluate 7 CSP and 2 SICSP methods under OPAL-Bench across 4 datasets using threshold-free geometric and physical distance metrics. Our results show that no method dominates across all metrics, and that geometric performance is not strongly correlated with physical plausibility. We also release OPAL-CSP, an SE(3)-equivariant flow-matching baseline for the CSP track that performs competitively on geometric metrics and ranks among the best on physical ones. By unifying geometric and physical metrics under a polymorph-aware protocol, OPAL-Bench enables a more comprehensive assessment of generative quality in CSP.

</details>


### [135] [UniHam: A Large-Scale SOC-Complete Dataset and Benchmark for Hamiltonian Learning in Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12509)
*Yuewen Huang,Pin Chen,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniHam：构建含自旋-轨道耦合（SOC）的大规模复值厄米哈密顿量数据集与基准（10万+样本、72元素），并提出重构精度、OOD泛化、下游性质预测三维评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测电子哈密顿量可规避密度泛函理论（DFT）的高计算成本，但现有哈密顿量数据集规模小、结构多样性不足，且常缺失自旋-轨道耦合（SOC）等相对论物理效应，形成数据瓶颈。

Method: 构建UniHam大规模数据集与基准套件：包含10万+ DFT计算、具完整SOC的复值厄米哈密顿量，覆盖72种元素与多样晶格类型及空间群族；并在标准化协议下评测两个代表性先进模型，引入联合评估三维度的互补指标。

Result: 实验表明所提基准与指标能有效区分模型能力，揭示与SOC及元素相关的内在失效模式、组分分布外（OOD）鲁棒性的显著差异，以及谱级评估对于判断哈密顿量预测能否支撑下游电子结构性质的必要性。

Conclusion: UniHam提供了可复现、SOC完备的基准，有助于细化模型比较并加速下一代量子材料基础模型的发展。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of electronic Hamiltonians would enable broad property inference while avoiding the high computational cost of Density Functional Theory (DFT). However, progress toward general-purpose materials foundation models is limited by a data bottleneck: existing Hamiltonian datasets are typically small, lack structural diversity, and often omit essential relativistic physics such as spin--orbit coupling (SOC). We therefore construct UniHam, a large-scale Hamiltonian dataset and benchmark suite comprising 100,000+ DFT-computed complex-valued Hermitian Hamiltonians with full SOC, covering 72 elements and a wide range of crystal geometries and symmetries (spanning diverse lattice types and space-group families). Building on UniHam, we benchmark two representative state-of-the-art models under a standardized protocol and introduce complementary evaluation metrics that jointly assess three dimensions: (i) Hamiltonian reconstruction accuracy, (ii) out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization across composition/symmetry shifts, and (iii) the ability to support downstream property prediction from the predicted Hamiltonians. Experiments on UniHam demonstrate that the proposed benchmark and metrics effectively differentiate model capabilities, revealing intrinsic SOC- and element-dependent failure modes, large variations in compositional OOD robustness, and the necessity of spectral-level evaluation to assess whether Hamiltonian predictions reliably support downstream electronic-structure properties. Overall, UniHam provides a reproducible, SOC-complete benchmark that can sharpen model comparisons and accelerate the development of next-generation foundation models for quantum materials.

</details>

### [136] [Sensitivity to Sterile Neutrinos with Elastic Neutrino-Electron and Highly Elastic Neutrino-Nucleus Scattering in the DUNE Near Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10271)
*The DUNE Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: DUNE近探测器利用三类高排他性事例样本，对3+1框架下eV量级惰性中微子振荡开展灵敏度研究，在Δm²≈4–10 eV²区间给出90%置信度下|U_μ4|²≃0.01、|U_e4|²≃0.015的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DUNE近探测器除服务长基线三味振荡精确测量外，还对eV量级惰性中微子混合引起的短基线振荡敏感；高排他性事例样本可压低中微子相互作用与通量不确定性，从而提升惰性中微子搜寻灵敏度。

Method: 在模拟的DUNE近探测器中选取三类互补样本：高弹性ν_μ带电电流（CC）、高弹性ν_e CC以及中微子—电子弹性散射（ν+e）。低强子能量CC选择增强重建轻子能量与中微子能量的相关性，ν+e截面可精确计算并独立约束通量；统计分析考虑|U_e4|²、|U_μ4|²、|U_τ4|²与Δm²，以固定Δm²切片（取|U_τ4|²=0）呈现结果。

Result: 最强灵敏度出现在4≲Δm²≲10 eV²，此处振荡对重建能谱产生可分辨畸变。对一年轴上前馈电流运行，90%置信度灵敏度约为|U_μ4|²≃0.01与|U_e4|²≃0.015，具体取决于另一混合参数取值。

Conclusion: DUNE近探测器可对3+1框架下eV量级惰性中微子振荡提供有竞争力的灵敏度；CC样本中仍残留对相互作用模型的依赖，是后续需关注的不确定性来源。

Abstract: The Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment (DUNE) is a next-generation neutrino experiment designed for precision measurements of long-baseline three-flavor neutrino oscillations. The DUNE Near Detector (ND) is also sensitive to short-baseline oscillations arising from mixing with an eV-scale sterile neutrino. In this paper, we present a sensitivity study for sterile-neutrino oscillations in the $3+1$ framework using three complementary event samples in the simulated DUNE ND: highly elastic $ν_μ$ charged-current (CC), highly elastic $ν_e$ CC, and neutrino-electron elastic-scattering ($ν+e$) events. These highly exclusive samples are chosen to reduce neutrino-interaction and flux uncertainties: the low-hadronic-energy CC selections enhance the correlation between reconstructed lepton energy and neutrino energy, while the precisely calculable $ν+e$ cross section provides an independent constraint on the neutrino flux. Residual interaction-model dependence remains in the CC samples. The statistical analysis considers $|U_{e4}|^2$, $|U_{\mu4}|^2$, $|U_{\tau4}|^2$, and $Δm^2$, with sensitivity results presented as fixed-$Δm^2$ slices for $|U_{\tau4}|^2=0$. The strongest sensitivity occurs for $4 \lesssim Δm^2 \lesssim 10~\mathrm{eV}^2$, where oscillations produce resolvable distortions of the reconstructed-energy spectra. For one year of on-axis forward-horn-current running, the 90\% confidence-level sensitivity reaches approximately $|U_{\mu4}|^2 \simeq 0.01$ and $|U_{e4}|^2 \simeq 0.015$, depending on the value of the other mixing parameter.

</details>


### [137] [NuSTEC Review: Current Experimental and Theoretical Challenges of Neutrino-Nucleus Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10465)
*A. P. Furmanski,J. G. Morfín,L. Alvarez-Ruso,A. M. Ankowski,A. Ashkenazi,M. Sajjad Athar,S. Dolan,S. Dytman,S. Gardiner,R. González-Jiménez,R. J. Hill,M. Hostert,J. Isaacson,N. Jachowicz,M. Kabirnezhad,T. Katori,S. R. Klein,F. Kling,M. Martini,G. D. Megias,B. A. Messerly,K. Niewczas,A. Nikolakopoulos,J. Nowak,J. M. Paley,V. Pandey,A. Papadopoulou,L. Pickering,N. Rocco,J. T. Sobczyk,J. E. Sobczyk,C. Thorpe,C. Wret*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该综述指出，GeV能区中微子-原子核相互作用（neutrino-nucleus interactions）的建模是精确测量中微子性质（neutrino properties）的关键瓶颈，系统梳理了从准弹性到深度非弹性散射各物理过程的开放问题及其对振荡参数（oscillation parameters）测定的潜在影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子性质的精确测量是基础粒子物理的最高优先事项之一，而实验依赖中微子在核环境中的相互作用，因此实验范围与精度的提升需要同步加强对强子与核物理的建模及理论不确定度（theoretical uncertainties）的理解。

Method: 采用综述形式，围绕GeV能区中微子-原子核相互作用的开放问题展开，以振荡参数测定为核心案例讨论未知因素的影响，并评述中微子事件产生器（neutrino event generators）如何处理这些挑战、电子-原子核相互作用（electron-nucleus interactions）如何提供辅助理解，逐一分析各底层物理过程。

Result: 综述发现，从准弹性散射（quasi-elastic scattering）到深度非弹性散射（deep-inelastic scattering）的每个物理过程中均存在重要且未解决的问题，这些未知量会通过建模与理论不确定度传导至实验结果的解释。

Conclusion: 上述挑战只能通过强相互作用（strong interactions）与电弱物理（electroweak physics）专家的积极支持与协作来应对，需要核物理与高能物理界的理论家和实验家共同参与。

Abstract: The precise measurement of neutrino properties is among the highest priorities in fundamental particle physics, involving many experimental and theoretical efforts worldwide. Since the experiments rely on the interactions of neutrinos in the nuclear environment, the planned advances in the scope and precision of these experiments require a commensurate effort in understanding and modeling the hadronic and nuclear physics of these interactions, as incorporated in neutrino event generators. This modeling is essential to every phase of the experimental analyses, and the theoretical uncertainties play an important role in interpreting experimental results.
  In this Review of current challenges, we summarize the open questions in neutrino-nucleus interactions at GeV-scale energies, and discuss in detail the potential impacts of these unknowns on the measurement of neutrino properties, using the determination of oscillation parameters as a central example. After an Executive Summary and a concise Overview of the issues, we address how the neutrino event generators treat these challenges, how electron-nucleus interactions can help understanding these challenges and the important unanswered questions for each underlying physics process---from quasi-elastic to deep-inelastic scattering. Across every topic, we find that current challenges can be met only through active support and collaboration among specialists in strong interactions and electroweak physics, including theorists and experimentalists from both the nuclear and high-energy physics communities.

</details>
