Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集的约2712.4×10⁶个ψ(2S)事例，对ψ(2S)→γχ_{c1}、χ_{c1}→K⁺K⁻η′过程进行分波分析，首次在Kη′系统中观测到J^P=1⁻共振态K₁*(1870)，显著度为11.0σ，并测量其质量、宽度及相关分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在通过ψ(2S)辐射跃迁到χ_{c1}的衰变链，探索Kη′系统中的共振结构，为奇异介子谱学（strange-meson spectroscopy）及可能的混杂态（hybrid-state）候选者研究提供实验依据。

Method: 采用分波分析（partial wave analysis）方法，利用BESIII探测器收集的(2712.4±14.3)×10⁶个ψ(2S)事例，η′介子通过π⁺π⁻γ和π⁺π⁻η两种衰变模式重建，并在拟合中引入多个标量、矢量与张量中间态。

Result: 首次在Kη′系统中观测到J^P=1⁻共振态K₁*(1870)，统计显著度为11.0σ，测得质量M=(1869.0±14.6±18.6) MeV/c²、宽度Γ=(167.3±34.0±24.2) MeV；χ_{c1}→K^∓K₁*(1870)^±→K⁺K⁻η′衰变链分支比为(1.48±0.30±0.33)×10⁻⁵，χ_{c1}→K⁺K⁻η′衰变分支比为(7.90±0.48)×10⁻⁴。

Conclusion: 结果为理解奇异介子谱学及探究可能的混杂态候选者提供了新的实验输入，并表明描述数据需要多个标量、矢量与张量中间态的贡献。

Abstract: A partial wave analysis of the process $ψ(2S)\to γχ_{c1}$, $χ_{c1}\to K^{+}K^{-}η^{\prime}$, with the $η^{\prime}$ meson reconstructed in the $π^+π^-γ$ and $π^+π^-η$ decay modes, is performed using a sample of $(2712.4 \pm 14.3)\times10^{6}$ $ψ(2S)$ events collected with the BESIII detector. In the $Kη^{\prime}$ system, a resonant contribution with quantum numbers $J^{P}=1^{-}$ is observed for the first time and is denoted as the $K_1^*(1870)$ meson. Its statistical significance is $11.0σ$, and its mass and width are measured to be $M=(1869.0 \pm 14.6\pm 18.6$) MeV/$c^{2}$ and $Γ=(167.3 \pm 34.0\pm 24.2$) MeV, respectively. The branching fraction of the $χ_{c1}\to K^{\mp}K_1^*(1870)^{\pm}\to K^{+}K^{-}η^{\prime}$ decay chain is determined to be $(1.48 \pm 0.30\pm 0.33)\times10^{-5}$. The branching fraction of the $χ_{c1}\to K^{+}K^{-}η^{\prime}$ decay is measured to be $(7.90 \pm 0.48)\times10^{-4}$. Several intermediate scalar, vector, and tensor states are required to provide a good description of the data. These results provide new input for understanding strange-meson spectroscopy and investigating possible hybrid-state candidates.

</details>


### [139] [Machine learning assisted $ν/\barν$ discrimination in atmospheric neutrino events for CPV sensitivity in a 20-kton liquid scintillator detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11474)
*Dek Hao Liew,Mohammad Nizam,Shu Tian Eu,Siew Yan Hoh*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究利用机器学习（machine learning）对无磁场液闪探测器中的中微子（neutrino）与反中微子（antineutrino）进行统计分离，以部分恢复大气中微子测量中损失的轻子CP破坏（leptonic CP violation）灵敏度，AUC约0.83，峰值灵敏度由1.49σ提升至1.96σ（正常质量序）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气中微子能量与基线范围广，可互补探测轻子CP破坏；但类JUNO液闪探测器无磁场，无法直接区分中微子与反中微子相互作用，导致CP破坏灵敏度下降。研究旨在检验基于机器学习的统计分离能否弥补这一损失。

Method: 用GENIE（Honda通量）在类JUNO几何中生成νμ与νe带电流事件，并以OscProb三味振荡概率（含地球物质效应、NuFIT参数）重加权；探测器响应以能量、方向与中子多重数（neutron multiplicity）的涂抹近似。训练逻辑回归（logistic regression）、XGBoost与多层感知机（multilayer perceptron）分类器，最终选用XGBoost；以泊松似然（Poisson likelihood）含系统 nuisance 参数并对五个振荡参数在3σ范围内边缘化，评估200 kton-year曝光的CP破坏灵敏度。

Result: XGBoost在两个味道样本上的ROC曲线下面积（AUC）均约0.83。在平方根近似下，峰值CP破坏灵敏度在正常质量序（normal mass ordering）下由1.49σ提升至1.96σ，反转质量序（inverted mass ordering）下由1.14σ提升至1.61σ。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的统计式中微子-反中微子分离可部分挽回电荷盲（charge blind）探测器损失的CP破坏灵敏度；结果为初步结果，需结合经验证的探测器响应与系统不确定性进一步研究。

Abstract: Atmospheric neutrinos span a broad range of energies and baselines, offering a complementary probe of leptonic CP violation. However, a JUNO-like liquid scintillator detector without a magnetic field cannot directly separate neutrino and antineutrino interactions, reducing CP violation sensitivity. This study investigates whether machine learning based statistical separation can improve CP violation sensitivity in a 20-kton detector. $ν_μ$ and $ν_e$ charged current events are generated with GENIE (using Honda flux) in a JUNO-like detector geometry and are reweighted using OscProb three flavor oscillation probabilities incorporating Earth matter effects and NuFIT parameters. Detector response is approximated by smearing lepton and hadronic energies, neutrino direction, and neutron multiplicity. Logistic regression, XGBoost, and multilayer perceptron (MLP) classifiers are trained on inelasticity, neutron multiplicity, and charged lepton energy, with XGBoost selected for the analysis. CP violation sensitivity is evaluated for a 200 kton-year exposure using a Poisson likelihood with systematic nuisance parameters and marginalization over five oscillation parameters within their respective $3σ$ ranges. XGBoost achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of approximately 0.83 for both flavor samples. Under the square root approximation, peak CP violation sensitivity improves from 1.49 $σ$ to 1.96 $σ$ for normal mass ordering and from 1.14 $σ$ to 1.61 $σ$ for inverted mass ordering. Machine learning based statistical neutrino-antineutrino separation can recover part of the CP violation sensitivity lost in charge blind detectors. These preliminary results motivate further studies incorporating validated detector response and systematic uncertainties.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的约27.12亿ψ(3686)事例，首次以超过5σ的统计显著性观测并测量了χ_{cJ}→φωη与χ_{cJ}→φωη′（J=0,1,2）六个衰变道的分支比（branching fraction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅指出该工作是对χ_{cJ}→φωη与χ_{cJ}→φωη′衰变的首次数值观测与分支比测量，具体物理背景未在摘要中给出。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器（BESIII detector）在BEPCII对撞机上采集的样本量为(2712.4±14.3)×10^6的ψ(3686)事例，通过分析χ_{cJ}（J=0,1,2）到φωη与φωη′的衰变，测定其分支比（branching fraction），并分别给出统计误差与系统误差。

Result: 所有六个衰变道均以大于5σ的显著性被首次观测。η模式分支比为：χ_{c0}→φωη为(5.43±0.23±0.52)×10^-4、χ_{c1}→φωη为(2.51±0.14±0.24)×10^-4、χ_{c2}→φωη为(3.03±0.15±0.28)×10^-4；η′模式为：χ_{c0}→φωη′为(1.23±0.23±0.15)×10^-4、χ_{c1}→φωη′为(1.09±0.17±0.13)×10^-4、χ_{c2}→φωη′为(0.98±0.17±0.11)×10^-4，其中第一项为统计不确定度，第二项为系统不确定度。

Conclusion: 该研究首次以高显著性（>5σ）确立了χ_{cJ}→φωη与χ_{cJ}→φωη′共六个衰变道的存在，并给出了各道精确的分支比测量值，为粲偶素（charmonium）χ_{cJ}衰变的实验研究提供了新的数据。

Abstract: Using a sample of $(2712.4 \pm 14.3) \times 10^{6}$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, we report the first observation and measurement of the branching fractions for the decays $χ_{cJ}\to φωη$ and $χ_{cJ} \to φωη^{\prime}$ ($J = 0,1,2$) with statistical significance greater than $5σ$. The branching fractions are determined to be $\mathcal B(χ_{c0} \to φωη) = (5.43 \pm 0.23 \pm 0.52) \times 10^{-4}$, $\mathcal B(χ_{c1} \to φωη) = (2.51 \pm 0.14 \pm 0.24) \times 10^{-4}$ and $\mathcal B(χ_{c2} \to φωη) = (3.03 \pm 0.15 \pm 0.28) \times 10^{-4}$ for the $η$ mode, and $\mathcal B(χ_{c0} \to φωη^{\prime}) = (1.23 \pm 0.23 \pm 0.15) \times 10^{-4}$, $\mathcal B(χ_{c1} \to φωη^{\prime}) = (1.09 \pm 0.17 \pm 0.13) \times 10^{-4}$ and $\mathcal B(χ_{c2} \to φωη^{\prime}) = (0.98 \pm 0.17 \pm 0.11) \times 10^{-4}$ for the $η^{\prime}$ mode, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic.

</details>


### [141] [Measurements of $C\!P$ violation with charm hadrons at Belle and Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11957)
*Jaeyoung Kim*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该报告汇总了Belle与Belle II实验在粲强子衰变中寻找CP破坏（CP violation）的最新结果，涵盖多种D介子与单卡比博压低（singly Cabibbo-suppressed）粲重子三体衰变模式，所有测得的直接CP不对称性在现有精度内均与CP对称性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准模型（Standard Model）框架下，粲夸克（charm quark）扇区的CP破坏预期极小，任何显著偏离都可能暗示新物理（new physics）贡献；因此需在更多衰变道中开展高精度检验，尤其是此前测量较少的粲重子（charmed baryon）三体衰变与同位旋（isospin）相关模式。

Method: 基于Belle II Run 1 的 428 fb⁻¹ 数据，并对 K_S^0K_S^0 测量补充 980 fb⁻¹ 的 Belle 数据，分析 D⁺_(s)→ηh⁺、D→ππ、D⁰→K_S^0K_S^0、D⁰→π⁺π⁻π⁰ 及 Ξ_c⁺→Σ⁺h⁺h⁻、Λ_c⁺→ph⁺h⁻（h=π,K）等衰变道；采用对 D→ππ 同位旋结构的互补检验与包容性粲味标记（inclusive charm-flavor tagging）方法，并首次给出这些粲重子三体模式的单一衰变率不对称性。

Result: 报告了 ηh⁺ 模式的初步结果、D→ππ 同位旋结构的互补检验以及包容性粲味标记的应用；首次获得三种粲重子三体衰变模式的单独衰变率不对称性，并借此提供 U-spin 对称性（U-spin symmetry）检验。所有不对称性在现有统计与系统精度内均与CP对称性相符。

Conclusion: 目前尚未在所列粲强子衰变道中观测到CP破坏信号；结果对标准模型预期及 U-spin、同位旋对称性假设给出了实验约束，并显示随 Belle II 数据量增长，此类检验的灵敏度有望进一步提升。

Abstract: Recent searches for $C\!P$ violation in charm-hadron decays at Belle and Belle II are presented. The measurements cover $D^+_{(s)}\toηh^+$, $D\toππ$, $D^0\to K_S^0K_S^0$, and $D^0\toπ^+π^-π^0$, together with the singly Cabibbo-suppressed baryon decays $Ξ_c^+\toΣ^+h^+h^-$ and $Λ_c^+\to ph^+h^-$, where $h=π,K$. The Belle II analyses use the Run~1 data sample of $428\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, supplemented by $980\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of Belle data for the $K_S^0K_S^0$ measurements. We report preliminary results for the $ηh^+$ modes, complementary tests of the isospin structure of $D\toππ$, and an application of inclusive charm-flavor tagging. The first individual decay-rate asymmetries in these three-body charmed-baryon modes also provide tests of $U$-spin symmetry. All reported asymmetries are consistent with $C\!P$ symmetry within the present precision.

</details>


### [142] [Beam-beam effects on measurements at electron-positron colliders: LEP neutrino counting and Higgs factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12265)
*Brendon Madison*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 介绍束-束电磁相互作用模拟框架FeeBL，复现LEP Bhabha散射场积分计算，发现既有束偏转模拟约2%偏差，修正后中微子数N_ν=2.9965±0.0040（原2.9963±0.0074）；并纠正三种对产生建模错误，研究ILC、HALHF、FCC-ee的退极化、亮度谱与单色化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 束-束电磁相互作用影响正负电子对撞机的亮度、碰撞能量、极化与粒子本底，进而关系到精密测量，因此需要一个可靠的束-束模拟框架并系统检验既有结果。

Method: 采用新开发的FeeBL（Fast e±e± Beam and Luminosity）模拟框架，与LEP及未来对撞机的已发表结果对比；复现LEP Bhabha散射的解析场积分计算，检查Breit-Wheeler、Bethe-Heitler与Landau-Lifshitz对产生建模，并建立刚性高斯束重叠模型估算单色化所需色散。

Result: 发现既有束偏转模拟约2%偏差，修正亮度并重估不确定度后得N_ν=2.9965±0.0040（此前2.9963±0.0074，新不确定度以实验特定误差统一重标定为条件）；识别并纠正三种对产生建模的实现错误；探索性直线对撞机配置可在显著亮度代价下获得亚MeV碰撞能峰宽，FCC-ee收窄明显较弱，估计需约10 m纯水平色散达到约1 MeV宽度。

Conclusion: FeeBL可作为检验束-束效应的有效工具；结果提示需进一步审查原始不确定度预算，修正后的对产生建模对希格斯工厂本底评估有影响，并为碰撞能量单色化提供定量参考。

Abstract: Beam-beam electromagnetic interactions affect the luminosity, collision energy, polarization, and particle backgrounds relevant to precision measurements at electron-positron colliders. We investigate these effects using Fast $e^{\pm}e^{\pm}$ Beam and Luminosity (FeeBL), a new beam-beam simulation framework, and compare its predictions with published results from LEP and proposed future colliders. We reproduce the analytical field-integration calculations of LEP Bhabha scattering and identify an approximately 2% bias in previously published beam-deflection simulations. Applying the corresponding luminosity correction and a revised uncertainty assessment motivated by the observed agreement among the four LEP experiments yields ($N_ν= 2.9965 \pm 0.0040$), compared with the previously reported ($2.9963 \pm 0.0074$). The revised uncertainty is conditional on an assumed common rescaling of experiment specific uncertainties and motivates further investigation of the original uncertainty budgets. We identify and correct implementation errors in the modeling of Breit-Wheeler, Bethe-Heitler, and Landau-Lifshitz pair production, with implications for detector backgrounds at Higgs factories. Simulated beam depolarization and luminosity spectra are compared with existing ILC and HALHF predictions. Finally, we investigate collision energy monochromatization for direct Higgs production, obtaining sub-MeV collision energy peak widths in exploratory linear collider configurations at substantial luminosity cost, while finding significantly weaker narrowing for the FCC-ee configurations studied. We derive a rigid Gaussian beam overlap model and estimate that FCC-ee needs roughly 10 m of horizontal only dispersion to reach widths of $\approx1~\mathrm{MeV}$.

</details>


### [143] [Search for Dark Photon Dark Matter at 74--159 $μ$eV/$c^2$ Using Broadband Cryogenic Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12774)
*Hiroki Takeuchi,Shunsuke Adachi,Shunki Endo,Keigo Hanimura,Michinori Hirose,Shunsuke Honda,Yuki Sakurai,Junya Suzuki,Osamu Tajima*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用低温K/Ka波段接收机与宽带频谱仪搜寻74–159 μeV/c²质量范围的暗光子暗物质（dark photon dark matter, DPDM）转化为光子的信号，未发现显著信号，并给出动力学混合系数（kinetic mixing）χ的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗光子（dark photon）是经动力学混合与普通光子耦合的假想有质量矢量玻色子，其作为暗物质候选者需通过实验检验；此前在数十至逾百μeV/c²质量区间缺乏直接限制。

Method: 采用低温K波段与Ka波段接收机配合宽带频谱仪，探测DPDM在金属表面转化为光子（频率由其质量决定）的信号，在74–159 μeV/c²（18–38.5 GHz）范围内扫描，每个频率bin积分时间为4.8×10⁵ s。

Result: 未发现统计显著信号；在假设DPDM构成全部本地暗物质的前提下，于95%置信水平对99.8%的搜索范围给出χ < (3.1–17)×10⁻¹²的上限。

Conclusion: 结果在74–110 μeV/c²大部分区间将此前上限改善一个数量级以上，并在110–159 μeV/c²大部分区间给出首个直接限制，约低于宇宙学界限两个数量级。

Abstract: Dark photons are hypothetical massive vector bosons that couple to ordinary photons through kinetic mixing $χ$. Dark photon dark matter (DPDM) converts into photons at a metal surface, with a frequency set by its mass. Using cryogenic K- and Ka-band receivers with a broadband spectrometer, we searched for this signal over 74--159 $μ$eV/$c^2$ (18--38.5 GHz) with an integration time of $4.8\times10^5$ s per frequency bin. We found no statistically significant signal and set upper limits of $χ<(3.1$--$17)\times10^{-12}$ at the 95% confidence level over 99.8% of the search range, assuming DPDM constitutes all local dark matter. These results improve on previous limits by more than an order of magnitude over most of 74--110 $μ$eV/$c^2$ and provide the first direct limits over most of 110--159 $μ$eV/$c^2$, approximately two orders of magnitude below cosmological bounds.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

### [144] [Operator Learning of Microstructure-Dependent Internal-Variable Models for Inelastic Homogenization: Approximation and Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11805)
*Lianghao Cao,George Stepaniants,Andrew M. Stuart*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文针对非弹性异质材料的均匀化本构律学习，提出并理论化了一个循环神经算子（recurrent neural operator）框架，在二维与三维周期Kelvin–Voigt黏弹性情形下证明了关于均匀化本构律的一致通用逼近定理，并给出内部变量维数的显式上界，辅以数值实验与Lean 4形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接进行单胞（unit-cell）并发模拟代价高昂，亟需能泛化到不同加载历史与微结构的替代模型（surrogate）；现有理论仅限一维且依赖分段常值材料的显式解，缺乏多维情形的通用逼近保证。

Method: 采用循环神经算子框架：以内部变量（internal variables）实现加载历史依赖的马尔可夫近似（Markovian approximation），以神经算子（neural operator）表示依赖微结构的本构响应；在二维与三维中基于均匀化产生的记忆核（memory kernel）谱表示构造逼近，并证明通用逼近定理及内部变量维数的显式界。

Result: 证明该模型可在时间上一致、并在广泛的容许应变轨迹与微结构属性场上，以任意精度逼近均匀化本构律；内部变量维数的上界随逆相对应力误差与材料对比度呈对数增长。黏弹性与晶体塑性（crystal plasticity）数值实验展示了内部变量维数与样本复杂度（sample complexity）的作用，理论结果在Lean 4中得到形式化验证。

Conclusion: 该工作将均匀化本构律的通用逼近理论从一维推广至二维与三维，为基于单胞数据的可泛化替代模型提供了理论支撑与可证验证的保证。

Abstract: We study the learning of homogenized constitutive laws for inelastic heterogeneous materials, aiming to replace costly concurrent unit-cell simulations with surrogates that generalize across loading histories and microstructures. We focus on the recurrent neural operator framework, motivated by the explicit form of homogenized Kelvin--Voigt linear viscoelasticity. In this framework, internal variables enable a Markovian approximation of loading-history dependence, and neural operators represent the microstructure-dependent constitutive response. For periodic Kelvin--Voigt viscoelasticity, we prove a universal approximation theorem showing that the model can approximate the homogenized constitutive law to any prescribed accuracy, uniformly in time and over a wide set of admissible strain trajectories and microstructural property fields, in two and three dimensions. This comprises a significant extension of the existing, somewhat limited, one-dimensional theory that uses explicit solutions for piecewise constant materials. Instead, in two and three dimensions, we adopt a more versatile approximation based on the spectral representation of the memory kernel that arises from homogenization. This also enables us to give an explicit bound on the internal-variable dimension, sufficient for approximation, exhibiting logarithmic growth in the inverse relative stress error and in material contrast. Numerical experiments on viscoelasticity and crystal plasticity complement the theory, illustrating the roles of internal-variable dimension and sample complexity in learning homogenized responses from unit-cell data. Theoretical results are formally verified in Lean 4.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Exceptional Topological Bound States in the Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10077)
*Luohong Liu,Qicheng Zhang,Shuaishuai Tong,Chunyin Qiu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出并实验演示了一种构建“异常拓扑连续域束缚态（exceptional topological BICs, ET-BICs）”的通用方案，将拓扑相、异常点与BIC三重融合，并在可电调损耗的声学平台上完整验证了本征能量与本征态的同时合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑相（topological phases）、异常点（exceptional points）与连续域束缚态（bound states in the continuum, BICs）两两组合已产生丰富的奇异模式，但三者合一的三重融合结构（ET-BICs）尚未被探索。

Method: 系统由四条耦合拓扑链构成：y方向镜像堆叠产生拓扑BIC对，z方向堆叠并引入偏置损耗形成广义镜像对称，使位于不同子空间中的BIC对在临界损耗下合并；实验上采用损耗可电调谐的声学平台进行验证。

Result: 实验不仅直接观测到本征能量（eigenenergies）的合并，也观测到本征态（eigenstates）的合并，从而对ET-BIC的形成给出了完整验证。

Conclusion: 三重协同的ET-BIC有望为同时需求鲁棒性、灵敏度与BIC局域约束的波动器件（wave devices）提供新途径。

Abstract: Topological phases, exceptional points, and bound states in the continuum (BICs) are three key concepts in condensed matter and classical wave physics. Their pairwise combinations have yielded a rich variety of intriguing singular modes, but the triple merger--exceptional topological BICs (ET-BICs)--has yet to be explored. In this Letter, we theoretically propose and experimentally demonstrate a universal approach for constructing such elusive exceptional structures. The system consists of four coupled topological chains: y-mirror stacking generates pairs of topological BICs, while z-stacking with biased loss yields a generalized mirror symmetry under which each pair--separated in different subspaces until their collapse--merges into an ET-BIC at a critical loss. Experimentally, using an acoustic platform with electrically tunable loss, we not only directly observe the coalescence of eigenenergies but also the coalescence of eigenstates, offering a complete verification of the ET-BIC formation. The triply synergistic ET-BICs may pave the way for wave devices that simultaneously demand robustness, sensitivity, and BIC confinement.

</details>


### [146] [Optimizing one-dimensional lithographically defined nanowires in semiconducting heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10177)
*Leah Tom,Yik Kheng Lee,Karina L. Hudson,Krittika Kumar,Jackson S. Smith,Alex R. Hamilton,S. N. Coppersmith,Jared H. Cole,Mark Friesen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过理论与计算模型预测，优化半导体异质结构中分裂栅（split gates）的几何形状，可提升一维纳米线输运通道的质量，并证明该优化对于分辨Rashba诱导的自旋能隙（spin gap）等关键特征不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维纳米线（one-dimensional nanowires）是研究量子输运（quantum transport）的重要平台，但光刻定义的器件中无序（disorder）会严重干扰新效应的实现与解释，使实验表现出非理想行为，因此需要降低无序影响、提高通道质量。

Method: 采用理论（theoretical）与计算（computational）模型，对半导体异质结构中分裂栅（split gates）的几何形状进行优化设计，以改善光刻定义的导电通道。

Result: 预测几何优化能获得更高质量的一维输运通道，并表明该优化是分辨Rashba诱导自旋能隙（Rashba-induced spin gap）等感兴趣特征的必要条件，同时带来更好的能量分辨率（energy resolution）且对无序更不敏感。

Conclusion: 优化分裂栅几何是一种改进光刻定义导电通道的新方法，可提升能量分辨率并降低对无序的敏感性，为高质量一维量子输运测量提供可行途径。

Abstract: One-dimensional nanowires can host remarkable electronic properties and are central to studying quantum transport. Although heterostructure-defined confinement combined with gate-defined potentials can greatly reduce the effects of impurities as measured via electron mobilities, the realization and interpretation of novel effects at this scale are complicated by disorder. Experiments on lithographically defined one-dimensional nanowires therefore do not exhibit ideal behavior. Using theoretical and computational models, we predict that optimizing the geometry of split gates in semiconducting heterostructures will lead to higher-quality channels for transport measurements. Further, we show that this optimization is essential for resolving features of interest such as the Rashba-induced spin gap. This new approach to lithographically defined conduction channels provides better energy resolution and is less susceptible to disorder.

</details>


### [147] [Coherent Electrometer Using Hybridised Double Quantum Dot - Resonator States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10274)
*Samuel Andersson,Pierre Glidic,Andrea Cicovic,Harald Havir,Sebastian Lehmann,Kimberly A. Dick,Ville F. Maisi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出基于相干相互作用（coherent interaction）的电荷探测器概念，利用微波谐振腔与双量子点（double quantum dot）的相干耦合实现快速、高灵敏的单电荷静电计（electrometer）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统快速单电荷静电测量依赖射频腔探测电荷敏感系统的电导（conductance）或电抗（reactance）变化；本文旨在探索一种基于相干相互作用的替代探测器方案。

Method: 构建微波谐振腔（microwave resonator）与双量子点之间的相干相互作用，据此实现相干静电计。

Result: 该相干静电计达到当前最优读出速度：在16 μs内以信噪比（SNR）11.7分辨两个电荷态，且读出信号功率比此前达到该速度所需低两个数量级，样品加热可忽略。

Conclusion: 得益于高灵敏度与低耗散，该相干静电计为纳秒级、低反作用（back-action）的量子极限电荷探测（quantum limited charge detection）开辟了途径。

Abstract: Probing the change in conductance or reactance of a charge sensitive system using an rf cavity is a standard way of doing fast single-charge electrometry. In this article, we present a detector concept which is instead based on coherent interaction. We show that the coherent interaction between a microwave resonator and double quantum dot gives rise to a fast, highly sensitive electrometer. The coherent electrometer achieves a state-of-the-art readout speed, resolving two charge states with SNR of 11.7 in 16 $μ$s, with a readout signal two orders of magnitude lower in power than previously required to reach this speed. In this way, heating of the sample becomes negligible. Thanks to its sensitivity and low dissipation, the coherent electrometer opens up the avenue for quantum limited charge detection with nanosecond speed and minimal back-action.

</details>


### [148] [The Photonic Bath at Infinity: Foundations, Derivations, and Consistency Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11816)
*Jian-Sheng Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文澄清了光子非平衡格林函数（NEGF）框架中"无穷远光子浴"自能的推导问题，提出四种互补方法并给出其一维、平面、球面几何的显式结果，验证其能重现普朗克谱、斯特藩–玻尔兹曼定律与辐射压。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无穷远光子浴用于描述非平衡格林函数（nonequilibrium Green's function, NEGF）框架中无界电磁环境作为有效热库，需同时处理出射辐射与入射电磁涨落，但须避免与自由推迟格林函数中已含的出射响应重复计数，此一致性问题是本文动机。

Method: 发展四种互补方法推导浴自能（bath self-energy）：无穷小环境吸收、半无限引线（semi-infinite leads）、自由格林函数求逆，以及带出射辐射边界条件的半双线性形式（sesquilinear form）；并给出平面几何下显式的三格点矩阵求逆，引入将浴自能与Silver–Müller辐射条件相联系的形式。

Result: 建立四种方法之间的相互联系，并得到一维、平面与球面几何下的显式浴自能；所得结果可重现普朗克谱（Planck spectrum）、斯特藩–玻尔兹曼定律（Stefan–Boltzmann law）与辐射压（radiation pressure）。

Conclusion: 所导出的浴自能为光子NEGF提供能量守恒所需的辐射贡献，从而在避免重复计数的前提下自洽描述辐射输运。

Abstract: The photonic bath at infinity represents the unbounded electromagnetic environment as an effective reservoir in the nonequilibrium Green's function (NEGF) formalism. It accounts for outgoing radiation and incoming electromagnetic fluctuations, allowing radiative transport to be described consistently. We clarify how environmental fluctuations are incorporated without double-counting the outgoing radiation response already contained in the free retarded Green's function. We develop four complementary approaches to deriving the bath self-energy: infinitesimal environmental absorption, semi-infinite leads, inversion of the free Green's function, and sesquilinear forms with outgoing radiation boundary conditions. In particular, we present an explicit three-site matrix inversion for a planar geometry and introduce a sesquilinear form that relates the bath self-energy to the Silver--Müller radiation condition. We establish the connections among these approaches and obtain explicit bath self-energies for one-dimensional, planar, and spherical geometries. The resulting bath self-energies reproduce the Planck spectrum, the Stefan--Boltzmann law, and radiation pressure, and provide the radiative contribution required for energy conservation in photon NEGF.

</details>


### [149] [Probing Chern Topology through Hall-Sensitive Photonic Spin Hall Shifts in a d-wave Altermagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10706)
*Mahnoor Sajid,Maha Siddiqui,Muzamil Shah*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了外加垂直交换场下二维Rashba耦合d波交变磁性Chern体系的光子自旋霍尔效应（photonic spin Hall effect），通过能带结构与Kubo形式的光电导计算反射高斯光束的螺旋度相关位移，发现纵向自旋分裂在相反Chern相间变号，可作为探测Hall活性拓扑相的光学探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将偏振分辨的光子束位移作为探测Hall活性拓扑相及其特征光谱的灵敏手段，考察d波交变磁性交换与Rashba耦合共同作用下的拓扑相与光学响应。

Method: 先分析高对称点能带结构与Dirac质量分布（m_Γ=m_M=Δ_x，m_X=Δ_x−4t_J，m_Y=Δ_x+4t_J），识别平庸相（C=0）与Chern绝缘相（C=±1）；再采用Kubo形式计算光电导，结合衬底支撑导电薄层的反射矩阵，计算反射高斯光束的螺旋度相关有限束腰位移（finite-waist displacement）。

Result: 光学响应呈现最小带间吸收阈值、Van Hove型谱特征，以及因交换场动量依赖而在X、Y点出现的不同跃迁。在给定Hall张量与偏振符号约定下，纵向螺旋度半分离对光学Hall电导反演为奇，在相反Chern相间变号；横向半分离对Hall响应为偶。自旋相关分裂在赝布儒斯特反射极小附近达到最大，纵向自旋分裂的显著谱变化反映d波交变磁性交换导致的非等价带间跃迁。

Conclusion: 偏振分辨的光子束位移可作为Hall活性拓扑相及其特征光学谱的灵敏探针，纵向自旋分裂的符号反转体现了交换场控制的光学Hall响应。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the photonic spin Hall effect in a two-dimensional Rashba-coupled $d$-wave altermagnetic Chern system subjected to a uniform out-of-plane exchange field. We first analyze the energy-band structure and the Dirac mass distribution at the high-symmetry points, where $m_Γ=m_M=Δ_x, m_X=Δ_x-4 t_J$, and $m_Y=Δ_x+4 t_J$. Band inversions at high-symmetry points generate both a topologically trivial regime $(C=0)$ and Chern-insulating phases $(C= \pm 1)$. Employing the Kubo formalism for the optical conductivity together with the reflection matrix for a substrate-supported conducting sheet, we compute helicity-dependent finite-waist displacements of a reflected Gaussian beam. The resulting optical response exhibits a minimum interband absorption threshold, a Van Hove-type spectral feature, and distinct transitions at the $X$ and $Y$ points arising from the momentum dependence of the exchange field. Under the specified Hall-tensor and polarization sign conventions, the longitudinal helicity half-separation is odd upon reversal of the optical Hall conductivity and correspondingly changes sign between opposite-Chern phases, whereas the transverse half-separation is even in the Hall response. The maximal spin-dependent splitting occur in the vicinity of the pseudo-Brewster reflectance minimum. The longitudinal spin splitting reverses sign between opposite Chern phases, reflecting the exchange-controlled optical Hall response, while its pronounced spectral variations reveal the influence of inequivalent interband transitions induced by the $d$-wave altermagnetic exchange. These findings establish polarization-resolved photonic beam shifts as a sensitive probe of Hall-active topological phases and their characteristic optical spectra.

</details>


### [150] [2D coherent spectroscopy of topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10905)
*Thaís V. Trevisan,Michał Papaj*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立了绝缘体系中多维光学磁化率的量子几何（quantum geometric）表述，聚焦双脉冲设置下的三阶二维相干光谱（2DCS），揭示其回波信号可探测四重带间贝里联络（Berry connection）矩阵元的乘积，并可反映能带拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维相干光谱在多维频谱中通常能解耦系统的不同自由度信息，但其量子几何内涵此前主要限于低阶响应；本文旨在将量子几何解释推广到更高阶（三阶）响应，并刻画中心对称体系中的ABB响应。

Method: 在速度规范（velocity gauge）下针对双脉冲方案推导二阶与三阶二维相干光谱的解析表达式，重点分析三阶ABB响应；采用二维陈绝缘体（Chern insulator）的连续模型与方晶格模型，考察xxyx响应。

Result: 三阶ABB响应的顺磁（paramagnetic）贡献产生回波信号，涉及四个带间贝里联络矩阵元之积，在两能带体系中约化为占据带量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor）的双线性组合；数值上，回波信号能够分辨这些几何量在动量空间的符号结构，且xxyx响应的回波节点数与能带拓扑相关。

Conclusion: 研究将多维光谱的量子几何解释扩展至高阶响应，表明三阶2DCS可获取低阶响应所无法探测的几何结构。

Abstract: Multidimensional coherent spectroscopy probes the response of a system to a sequence of time-separated light pulses, and has attracted significant interest in recent years. In the frequency space, it generates multidimensional spectra, in which information about different degrees of freedom of the system is generically disentangled. In this work, we develop a quantum geometric formulation of multidimensional optical susceptibilities in insulating systems. Focusing on a two-pulse setup and working in the velocity gauge, we derive analytical expressions for second-order and third-order two-dimensional coherent spectra (2DCS). We focus on the third-order ABB response, which remains finite in centrosymmetric systems where the second-order susceptibility vanishes by inversion symmetry. Its paramagnetic contribution supports an echo signal that probes products of four interband Berry-connection matrix elements, which reduce in two-band systems to bilinear combinations of the quantum geometric tensor of the occupied band. Using continuum and square-lattice models of two-dimensional Chern insulators, we show how the echo resolves the momentum-space sign structure of these geometric quantities. For the $xxyx$ response of the models considered, the number of nodes in the echo signal is related to the band topology. Our results extend the quantum-geometric interpretation of multidimensional spectroscopy to higher-order responses, showing that third-order 2DCS provides access to geometric structures beyond those probed at lower orders.

</details>


### [151] [Imaging spin entanglement with a scanning tunneling microscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10946)
*J. C. G. Henriques,B. Viña-Bausá,I. Brihuega,J. Fernández-Rossier*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作建立自旋非弹性电子隧道谱（inelastic electron tunneling spectroscopy, IETS）与自旋-1/2体系纠缠之间的联系层级，表明谱矩可用于探测、量化并空间成像表面自旋阵列中的量子关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面组装的人工自旋阵列可用于原子尺度研究量子关联，但缺乏将实验可测的IETS谱学特征与纠缠度量系统联系起来的框架。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先论证自旋-1/2二聚体中谱学活性跃迁数目可作为纠缠见证（entanglement witness）；再利用针尖位于自旋i处的一阶隧穿电导dI(i)/dV，定义谱矩M^(n)(i)并逐一关联到不同量子关联：零阶矩探测有能隙非简并基态体系中的单缠结（one-tangle），一阶矩在旋转不变Heisenberg体系中给出热自旋关联函数，二阶矩与反矩分别对应长程关联与局域自旋磁化率；对三聚体则联系多重态间非弹性谱权重与由concurrence fill量化的三体纠缠。

Result: 在旋转不变Heisenberg体系中，一阶矩可重构最近邻concurrence并实现自旋链纠缠的空间成像，同时给出内能与磁比热作为纠缠见证；应用于纳米石墨烯（nanographene）自旋链的实验IETS数据时与理论符合良好。

Conclusion: 通过与数字量子电路（digital quantum circuit）的类比，调和了IETS的局域性与对非局域纠缠的敏感性；由此确立IETS作为量化与成像工程化表面自旋体系纠缠的工具。

Abstract: Artificial spin arrays assembled on surfaces enable atomic-scale studies of quantum correlations. Here we establish a hierarchy of connections between spin inelastic electron tunneling spectroscopy (IETS) and entanglement in spin-$1/2$ systems. We first show that the number of spectroscopically active transitions in a spin-$1/2$ dimer serves as an entanglement witness. Using the leading-order tunneling conductance for the tip placed on spin $i$, $dI(i)/dV$, we then relate spectral moments $\mathcal{M}^{(n)}(i)=\int V^n(d^2I(i)/dV^2)\,dV$ to quantum correlations. The zeroth moment probes the one-tangle in systems with a gapped, nondegenerate ground state. For rotationally invariant Heisenberg systems, the first moment yields thermal spin correlators $\langle\boldsymbol S(i)\cdot\boldsymbol S(j)\rangle$, enabling reconstruction of nearest-neighbor concurrence and spatial imaging of entanglement in spin chains. It also provides access to the internal energy and magnetic specific heat, both usable as entanglement witnesses. Applying this approach to experimental IETS data from nanographene spin chains, we find good agreement with theory. We further discuss how the second moment probes longer-range correlations and the inverse moment relates to local spin susceptibility. For spin-$1/2$ trimers, we connect inter-multiplet inelastic spectral weight to genuine tripartite entanglement quantified by the concurrence fill. Finally, we reconcile the local character of IETS with its sensitivity to nonlocal entanglement through an analogy with digital quantum circuits, where Hamiltonian evolution implements multiqubit operations. These results establish IETS as a tool for quantifying and imaging entanglement in engineered on-surface spin systems.

</details>


### [152] [Electron cooling mediated by transverse optical phonons in doped SrTiO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11225)
*A. A. Zyuzin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究推导了三维电子气（three-dimensional electron gas）与掺杂 SrTiO$_3$ 中软横向光学声子（soft transverse optical phonon, TO）之间的热流，揭示其温度依赖在铁电（polar）相变附近呈现交叉与单调性反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掺杂 SrTiO$_3$ 中软 TO 声子通过极化场与电子耦合，其耦合形式在顺电（paraelectric）相为二次、在极性相额外含线性项；然而该耦合对电子–声子热流（heat flow）温度依赖的影响尚需厘清。

Method: 基于双 TO 声子（two-TO-phonon）散射与极性相新增的单 TO 声子（one-TO-phonon）散射通道，解析计算热电子向软 TO 声子的热流随温度的变化规律，并考察 Bloch-Grüneisen 温度与自发极化（spontaneous polarization）的作用。

Result: 双声子通道的热流在 Bloch-Grüneisen 温度处由 $T^5$ 过渡到 $T^3$；极性相的单声子通道给出更软的依赖，低温为 $T^3$、高温为 $T$，其谱权重（spectral weight）由自发极化设定。

Conclusion: 单 TO 声子通道可在低温占主导，导致热流在极性相变（polar transition）附近呈现非单调的温度依赖，为调控该体系电子–声子热输运提供机制性判据。

Abstract: Soft transverse optical (TO) phonons in doped SrTiO$_3$ couple to electrons through the polarization field, quadratically in the paraelectric phase and, additionally, linearly in the polar phase due to the onset of spontaneous polarization. We investigate heat flow between a three-dimensional electron gas and soft TO phonons. For two-TO-phonon scattering, the heat flow of hot electrons crosses over from $T^5$ to $T^3$ at the Bloch-Gruneisen temperature. In the polar phase, an additional one-TO-phonon channel emerges, yielding a softer temperature dependence of the heat flow, $T^3$ at low and $T$ at high temperatures, with the spectral weight set by the spontaneous polarization. This channel may dominate at low temperatures and leads to a nonmonotonic temperature dependence of the heat flow in the vicinity of the polar transition.

</details>


### [153] [Theory of spin-polarized scanning tunneling microscopy with topological insulator tips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11267)
*Zhuo-Yuan Shi,Chuang Li,Lin Jiao,Huiqiu Yuan,Zhenyu Wang,Xianhui Chen,Lun-Hui Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator）探针的自旋极化扫描隧道显微镜（spin-polarized STM）理论，指出非对称隧穿接触几何与自旋退相干（spin dephasing）使螺旋边界态产生净自旋极化隧穿电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于螺旋边界态的拓扑绝缘体探针已用于自旋敏感STM，但其产生净自旋极化隧穿电流的机制尚不清楚，本文旨在阐明该机制。

Method: 在标准Landauer-Büttiker形式体系内，针对量子自旋霍尔（quantum spin Hall, QSH）绝缘体探针建模：非对称接触使两条携带相反自旋的反向传播螺旋边缘态路径长度相差ΔL，自旋退相干诱导背散射。

Result: 对代表性非对称接触与弱自旋退相干，QSH探针在正偏压下给出隧穿自旋极化P_s=-17.2%，增大ΔL时幅度可达50%，且对Rashba自旋轨道耦合具鲁棒性；该极化由螺旋边缘输运主导，并因自旋-动量锁定（spin-momentum locking）随偏压极性自然反号。

Conclusion: 结果与近期使用拓扑探针的STM实验一致，为拓扑绝缘体探针的自旋极化隧穿电流提供了机制解释。

Abstract: Topological insulators have enabled a new class of spin-sensitive scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) probes based on helical boundary states. However, how these probes generate a net spin-polarized tunneling current remains unclear. We present a theory of spin-polarized STM with topological insulator tips and show that asymmetric tunneling-contact geometry and spin dephasing enable helical boundary states to generate a spin-polarized tunneling current. Within the standard Landauer-Büttiker formalism, we demonstrate this mechanism for a quantum spin Hall (QSH) insulator tip: asymmetric contacts make the two counter-propagating helical edge states, which carry opposite spins, traverse path lengths that differ by $ΔL$, and spin dephasing induces backscattering that reduces the conductance more strongly for the longer path. For a representative asymmetric contact and weak spin dephasing, the QSH tip exhibits a tunneling spin polarization of $P_s=-17.2\%$ under positive bias, whose magnitude can reach $50\%$ for larger $ΔL$, and which remains robust against Rashba spin-orbit coupling. Crucially, this polarization, dominated by helical edge transport rather than by bulk states, naturally reverses sign with the bias polarity owing to spin-momentum locking. Our findings are consistent with recent STM experiments using topological tips.

</details>


### [154] [Industrial RPCVD growth of high-purity 28Si epilayers with 29Si content below 30 ppm for high-fidelity electron spin qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11405)
*Zhenzhen Kong,Weizhu Liao,Ning Chu,Yiwen Zhang,Ying Zhang,Jixuan Li,Changyang Xu,Yongliang Hao,Yi Zhou,Yanpeng Song,Xiaomeng Liu,Hailing Wang,Xiangsheng Wang,Jinbiao Liu,Chao Zhao,Hao Meng,Gang Cao,Guo-Ping Guo,Hai-Ou Li,Guilei Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用工业级减压化学气相沉积（RPCVD）在区熔硅上外延生长高纯度28Si（同位素纯度99.997%，29Si低于30 ppm），并确定低于950 °C的热预算窗口以保持薄层结构完整；基于该平台制备的双量子点实现T2* = 0.62 μs、Hahn回波相干时间28.8 μs及最高99.9%的单比特门保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天然硅中残余的29Si核自旋通过超精细相互作用（hyperfine interaction）导致退相干（dephasing），限制了硅量子点自旋量子比特的相干性，必须借助同位素纯化（isotopic purification）加以克服，因此需要验证工业化途径制备高纯度28Si的可行性。

Method: 采用工业减压化学气相沉积系统在区熔硅（float-zone silicon）上外延生长28Si；以二次离子质谱（SIMS）表征同位素纯度，结合原子力显微镜与SIMS开展850–1150 °C真空退火矩阵实验，考察原生氧化物的缺陷介导分解（defect-mediated decomposition）与非平衡空位注入；并在1.5 K下进行磁输运测量与双量子点器件测试。

Result: SIMS确认28Si纯度达99.997%、29Si浓度低于30 ppm；在1.5 K下霍尔迁移率（Hall mobility）峰值达12,858 cm² V⁻¹ s⁻¹，量子散射寿命0.659 ps，对应单粒子能级展宽499 μeV；双量子点器件测得T2* = 0.62 μs、Hahn回波相干时间28.8 μs，随机基准测试（randomized benchmarking）给出的单比特门保真度最高达99.9%。

Conclusion: 研究确立了低于950 °C的热预算窗口，证明工业RPCVD是通往高相干、高保真硅自旋量子比特（silicon spin qubits）的可行技术路线。

Abstract: Silicon quantum-dot spin qubits are among the most promising routes to large-scale quantum computation, but the residual 29Si nuclear spins in natural silicon impose a hyperfine dephasing limit that must be overcome by isotopic purification. Here, we report the epitaxial growth of high-purity 28Si on float-zone silicon using an industrial reduced-pressure chemical vapor deposition (RPCVD) system. Secondary-ion mass spectrometry (SIMS) confirms an 28Si isotopic purity of 99.997%, with the 29Si concentration below 30 ppm. Through a systematic vacuum annealing matrix (850-1150 C), combined with atomic force microscopy and SIMS, we investigate the defect-mediated decomposition of the native oxide and the injection of non-equilibrium vacancies. We identify a thermal-budget window below 950 C within which the thin-layer structure of 28Si remains intact. At 1.5 K, magnetotransport measurements reveal a peak Hall mobility of 12,858 cm^2 V^-1 s^-1 and a quantum scattering lifetime of 0.659 ps, corresponding to a single-particle level broadening of 499 micro-eV. A double quantum dot fabricated on this platform exhibits a T2* of 0.62 us, a Hahn-echo coherence time of 28.8 us, and single-qubit gate fidelities of up to 99.9% measured by randomized benchmarking. Our results establish industrial RPCVD as a promising route toward high-coherence, high-fidelity silicon spin qubits.

</details>


### [155] [Ballistic transport through 3D flat bands in rhombohedral graphene in an in-plane magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11603)
*Eran Sela,Rafi Bistritzer,Yuval Oreg*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究面内磁场如何重构菱面体多层石墨烯有限薄片的输运通道，并沿厚度方向重新分配电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究面内磁场（in-plane magnetic field）对菱面体多层石墨烯（rhombohedral multilayer graphene）有限薄片中传输通道（transmitting channels）的重塑作用，以及电流沿厚度方向的再分布机制。

Method: 以两端口电导（two-terminal conductance）计数传输通道；以各表面电压反映被接触层上右行与左行态（right- and left-moving states）的相对谱权重（spectral weights）。

Result: 在足够厚的薄片或足够强的磁场下，近平坦的体态第零朗道能级（zeroth Landau level）导致三维霍尔效应（three-dimensional Hall effect），并在四端口霍尔电阻（four-terminal Hall resistance）中出现平台。

Conclusion: 面内磁场通过重构通道与重新分配电流，可在厚样品或强场条件下产生具有霍尔电阻平台特征的三维霍尔效应。

Abstract: We explore how an in-plane magnetic field reshapes the transmitting channels of a finite slab of rhombohedral multilayer graphene and redistributes the current across its thickness. The two-terminal conductance counts these channels, while the voltage at each surface reflects the relative spectral weights of right- and left-moving states on the contacted layer. In sufficiently thick slabs or at sufficiently strong fields, the nearly flat bulk zeroth Landau level gives rise to a three-dimensional Hall effect with a plateau in the four-terminal Hall resistance.

</details>


### [156] [Coherent Control of High-Order Lightwave Currents in Gate-Tunable Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11616)
*Samuel Haeuser,Martin Mootz,Dasol Kim,Avinash Khatri,Joong-Mok Park,Randall K. Chan,Richard H. J. Kim,Yongxing Yao,Ilias E. Perakis,Hou-Tong Chen,Thomas Koschny,Long Ju,Jigang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用相位锁定的单周期太赫兹（THz）脉冲对，在双栅双层石墨烯（bilayer graphene, BLG）器件中实现对高阶、非微扰光电流的振幅、相位翻转与过零点的栅压可调相干控制，并揭示带间多光子相干与带内光波加速之间的强场交叉机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以定制太赫兹电场波形（而非传统脉冲偏压）在亚周期（sub-cycle）尺度加速载流子，是超越千兆赫兹电子技术缩放极限的光波电子学（lightwave electronics）关键方向，但多阶、非微扰电流的栅极可调相干控制此前仍未实现。

Method: 采用相位锁定的单周期 THz 电场对驱动双栅 BLG 器件，通过原位调控带隙（bandgap）与电荷掺杂（charge doping），并结合干涉式非线性 THz 光电流光谱（interferometric nonlinear THz photocurrent spectroscopy）与量子动理学模拟（quantum-kinetic simulations）进行表征与机理映射。

Result: 观测到极端的 THz 光电流整流（rectification）与亚 THz 周期电流产生，其行为受带间多光子相干向带内光波加速的强场交叉支配，并对脉冲对驱动表现出极高敏感性；非微扰多量子光电流表现为极高阶 THz 频率梳（frequency comb）。

Conclusion: 亚 THz 周期的弹道输运（ballistic transport）是栅压与 THz 场驱动功能的物理基础；该按需相干控制非微扰光电流输运区间的方案可推广至多种原子级薄器件结构，为皮赫兹（petahertz）时间尺度、原子长度尺度上可编程的逻辑、存储与量子模拟器提供新途径。

Abstract: The sub-cycle acceleration of charge carriers in an electronic circuit by tailored terahertz (THz) electric-field waveforms, rather than conventional pulsed bias voltages, opens a transformative frontier for lightwave electronics operating far beyond the scaling limits of gigahertz electronic technologies. Yet gate-tunable coherent control of multi-order and non-perturbative currents has remained elusive. Here, we demonstrate lightwave control over the amplitude, phase flips, and zero-crossings of THz field-driven high-order currents by utilizing phase-locked pairs of single-cycle THz fields in a dual-gated bilayer graphene (BLG) device. By leveraging the in-situ tuning of both bandgap and charge doping, we reveal extreme THz photocurrent rectification and sub-THz-cycle current generation that are governed by a strong field-driven crossover from interband multi-photon coherence to intraband lightwave acceleration, whose competition provides an exceptional sensitivity to the THz-pulse pair drive. Our interferometric nonlinear THz photocurrent spectroscopy of sub-cycle electronic excitations accesses and controls non-perturbative, multi-quantum photocurrents, which manifest as an extreme-order THz frequency comb. Quantum-kinetic simulations map these lightwave-controlled electron trajectories, revealing that sub-THz-cycle ballistic transport underpins gate- and THz-field-driven functionality enabled by electrostatic doping and bandgap tuning within the atomically thin device architecture. The demonstrated on-demand coherent control of nonperturbative photocurrent transport regimes can be extended to a broad range of atomically thin device architectures, opening new avenues for gate- and field-programmable logic, memory, and quantum simulators in which electronic pathways are steered on sub-cycle, petahertz timescales and atomic length scales.

</details>


### [157] [Giant magnetoresistance rectification by Néel vector dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11654)
*Zhiyi Che,Yifang Hao,Aakanksha Sud,Murat Cubukcu,Charlie W. F. Freeman,Chenghao Yang,Daniel Prestwood,Troy Dion,Shunsuke Fukami,Shigemi Mizukam,Hidekazu Kurebayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在人工反铁磁体（synthetic antiferromagnets）中观测到由奈尔矢量（Néel vector）动力学驱动的室温巨磁阻（giant magnetoresistance, GMR）整流效应，其电压符号可经直流电流非易失地翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索反铁磁体中奈尔矢量动力学与磁阻效应之间的耦合机制，并寻找可从各向异性磁阻（anisotropic magnetoresistance）和自旋霍尔磁阻（spin Hall magnetoresistance）中区分出来的GMR整流信号。

Method: 利用自旋力矩铁磁共振（spin-torque ferromagnetic resonance）激发人工反铁磁体的反相（光学）模式，使两个子磁矩相对夹角振荡，从而产生GMR整流电压；并通过角对称性与微波功率线性依赖关系将该分量与其他磁阻贡献分离。

Result: 提取的GMR系数为0.18–0.20%，与独立测量的直流磁阻结果一致；用直流电流翻转奈尔矢量可非易失且可控地改变GMR整流电压的符号。

Conclusion: GMR整流可作为探测反铁磁奈尔矢量动力学的有效手段，且奈尔矢量的电学翻转可实现非易失的整流电压符号调控，为反铁磁自旋电子学器件提供潜在途径。

Abstract: We observe room-temperature giant magnetoresistance (GMR) rectification driven by Néel vector dynamics in synthetic antiferromagnets. During spin-torque ferromagnetic resonance, the out-of-phase (optical) mode makes the relative angle between the two sub-moments oscillate, producing a rectified voltage due to GMR. This component is isolated from anisotropic and spin Hall magnetoresistance contributions by its angular symmetry, with its voltages scaling linearly with microwave power. We extract a GMR coefficient of 0.18-0.20 % from the rectification, which agrees with independently measured dc magnetoresistance. Reversing the Néel vector with a dc current changes the sign of the GMR rectification voltage in a non-volatile and controlled manner.

</details>


### [158] [Phonon emission due to dc-driven electron heating in a two-dimensional electron gas containing artificial scatterers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12185)
*Frederik Bartels,Mihai Cerchez,Jan Lange,Dominique Mailly,Klaus Pierz,Hans W. Schumacher,Thomas Heinzel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在含人工散射体的二维电子气中，借助dc电流电子加热激发声子发射，观测到高达8阶的声子诱导电阻振荡（PIROs），并通过与霍尔场诱导电阻振荡（HIROs）的双共振调制大幅提升其可见度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡条件下声子诱导电阻振荡（PIROs）信号微弱、仅两个极大值可测，需寻找增强其可观测性的手段并厘清相关声子模式与电子-声子相互作用行为。

Method: 施加dc电流使电子加热以激活声子发射，同时产生霍尔场诱导电阻振荡（HIROs）；通过精确分析被PIROs双共振调制的HIROs振幅来显现PIROs，并在Dmitriev等人理论框架下分析亚声速区（ε_dc < ε_ph）的振荡振幅调制。

Result: 观测到至ε_ph = 8阶的PIROs；识别出声速s = 3.4 km/s的横声学声子为相关模式；在声屏障（ε_dc = ε_ph）处观察到振荡型电子-声子相互作用的相移。

Conclusion: dc电流驱动的双共振方法可显著增强PIROs可见度，声屏障处的相移独立验证了近期实验发现。

Abstract: We report the observation of phonon-induced resistance oscillations (PIROs) up to order $\varepsilon_\mathrm{ph} = 8$ in a two-dimensional electron gas containing artificial scatterers, activated by electron heating due to an applied dc current which enables phonon emission. Under equilibrium conditions, only two weak PIRO maxima are detectable. The dc current simultaneously generates Hall field induced resistance oscillations (HIROs). A precise analysis of their amplitude, which is modulated by the double resonance with PIROs, dramatically enhances the visibility of the latter compared to the equilibrium case. We identify transverse acoustic phonons with a sound velocity of $s = 3.4$\,km/s as the relevant mode and analyze the oscillatory amplitude modulation in the subsonic regime ($\varepsilon_{\mathrm{dc}} < \varepsilon_{\mathrm{ph}}$) within the theoretical framework of Dmitriev \textit{et al.} At the sound barrier ($\varepsilon_{\mathrm{dc}} = \varepsilon_{\mathrm{ph}}$), where the electron drift velocity reaches the speed of sound, we observe a phase shift in the oscillatory electron-phonon interaction that independently confirms recent experimental findings.

</details>


### [159] [Lattice Commensurability and Majorana sectors of Pfaffian--Anti-Pfaffian interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12195)
*Feng Liu,Xue-Yang Song*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究在Half-filled莫尔晶格体系中给出Pfaffian与anti-Pfaffian非阿贝尔分数量子反常霍尔态的耦合线（coupled-wire）构造，并分析二者界面的边缘动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往耦合线构造非阿贝尔态常需借助空间变化的磁场引入动量转移；本文指出晶格动量模倒格矢守恒可免除该要求，从而在莫尔体系中更自然地实现这两类态。

Method: 利用晶格动量模倒格矢（reciprocal lattice vector）守恒，构造半满莫尔晶格的耦合线模型，得到Pfaffian与anti-Pfaffian相，并研究二者形成的界面（interface）。

Result: 在Pfaffian–anti-Pfaffian界面上，Umklapp过程可使反向传播的电荷扇区（charge sector）能隙化，而剩余四个同向传播的马约拉纳模（Majorana modes）按平移对称性归并为两个扇区。

Conclusion: 晶格公度性（lattice commensurability）定性改变了非阿贝尔态的耦合线可达性，并提供直接约束其边缘与界面动力学的微观动量结构。

Abstract: We study Pfaffian and anti-Pfaffian non-Abelian fractional quantum anomalous Hall states in half-filled moiré lattice systems. Owing to crystal-momentum conservation modulo a reciprocal lattice vector, these lattice systems admit coupled-wire constructions of the two phases without requiring spatially varying magnetic-field-induced momentum shifts. For the resulting Pfaffian--anti-Pfaffian interface, a Umklapp process can gap the counter-propagating charge sector, while the remaining four co-propagating Majorana modes naturally group into two sectors organized by translation symmetries. Our results show that lattice commensurability qualitatively changes the coupled-wire accessibility of non-Abelian states while providing a microscopic momentum structure that directly constrains their edge and interface dynamics.

</details>


### [160] [Plasmon excitations in monolayer stanene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12212)
*Jia-Hui Qin,Yunhua Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作系统研究了单层锡烯（stanene）的高能等离激元结构与低温集体激发，涵盖温度、静电掺杂和垂直电场调控。研究发现除已有石墨烯/硅烯的π与π+σ等离激元外，锡烯还存在两条新等离激元支，且低能等离激元在THz至中红外波段可调控，具备纳米光子学应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层锡烯具有强内禀自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）及Γ与K谷共存、强电子-空穴不对称的低能能带特征，但其等离激元性质与外部场可调性尚不明确，因此需要系统厘清其等离激元结构及调控机制。

Method: 采用含SOC与亚晶格交错势的sp³紧束缚模型（tight-binding model），并结合随机相近似（random-phase approximation, RPA）下的有限温度多带Lindhard函数进行计算。

Result: 除石墨烯/硅烯已知的π和π+σ等离激元外，锡烯还支持两条新的高能等离激元支。低能方面：热激发拓宽可及能-动量范围并保持高质量因子与集体相干性；中等掺杂给出K谷主导的√q狄拉克等离激元，重电子掺杂经Γ与K谷Drude贡献保持√q色散，重空穴掺杂则在Γ点附近触发带间跃迁，产生新的σ-σ与σ-σ*带间等离激元模式及复杂多支谱；垂直电场在0.3 V/Å以下通过能带重构连续调控√q等离激元，并在带隙闭合附近诱导额外模式。

Conclusion: 锡烯的可调低能等离激元覆盖太赫兹至中红外频段，具备纳米光子学（nanophotonics）应用潜力，且其等离激元行为对温度、掺杂类型与垂直电场高度敏感，可作为多参数调控的等离激元平台。

Abstract: Monolayer stanene exhibits strong intrinsic spin-orbit coupling (SOC) and notably distinct low-energy band features involving both $Γ$ and K valleys together with strong electron-hole asymmetry among group-IV monolayers. Its plasmonic properties and external-field tunability remain largely unclarified. Here, we systematically investigate its high-energy plasmon structures and low-energy collective excitation modulated by temperature, electrostatic doping and perpendicular electric field. Calculations use a $sp^3$ tight-binding model with SOC and sublattice staggered potentials, combined with the finite-temperature multiband Lindhard function under the random-phase approximation. Besides the $π$ and $π+σ$ plasmon known in graphene/silicene, stanene supports two new high-energy plasmon branches. For low-energy plasmons, thermal excitation extends the accessible energy-momentum range while preserving high quality factors and collective coherence. Electrostatic doping yields distinct plasmon behaviors: moderate doping gives K-valley dominated intraband $\sqrt{q}$ Dirac plasmons; heavy electron doping retains $\sqrt{q}$ dispersion via Drude contributions from $Γ$ and K valleys; heavy hole doping triggers interband transitions near the $Γ$ point, producing new $σ$-$σ$ and $σ$-$σ^*$ interband plasmon modes and complex multi-branch spectra. Furthermore, the perpendicular electric field enables continuous tuning of the $\sqrt{q}$ plasmon via band reconstruction below 0.3 V/Å and induces extra plasmon modes near band-gap closure. These tunable low-energy plasmons span terahertz-to-mid-infrared frequencies, promising for nanophotonic applications.

</details>


### [161] [Impact of multi-dot configurations on electron temperature and double quantum dot latching in gate-controlled qubit devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12217)
*Jakob Walsh,Miriam Schweinböck,Jhih-Sian Tu,Yuji Yamamoto,Felix Reichmann,Lars R. Schreiber,Dominique Bougeard*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在Si/SiGe自旋量子比特器件中，若单电子晶体管（SET）同时充当电荷传感器与储库，其能谱会影响量子点（QD）的脉冲栅极光谱，且其电子温度随偏压显著升高，即便在50 μV偏压时也高于参考实验100 mK。结果提示应选择不依赖储库耦合的自旋量子比特初始化方案，并进一步在少电子稳定图中系统映射了双量子点与单一储库连接时的锁存行为可调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展的Si/SiGe自旋量子比特架构常采用栅控多量子点结构，并依赖库仑阻塞（Coulomb blockade）物理，其中常以同一单电子晶体管（SET）兼顾电荷传感与邻近量子点（QD）的储库，然而这一复用方式可能引入未被充分认识的测量与物理效应，需要加以检验。

Method: 实验通过脉冲栅极光谱（pulsed gate spectroscopy）研究SET同时在位储库与传感器时对QD测量的影响，并以量子点作为温度探针，量化SET电子温度对施加偏压的依赖关系；此外采用双量子点（double quantum dot）连接单一储库的配置，在少电子稳定图（stability diagram）中系统映射锁存行为（latching behavior）。

Result: SET的能谱会显著影响QD的脉冲栅极光谱；以量子点探测得到，SET电子温度强烈依赖偏压，在仅50 μV偏压下即比参考实验高出100 mK。双量子点—单一储库配置中，特征锁存行为在少电子区域的可调性得到系统展示。

Conclusion: 对于SET兼具传感器与储库功能的器件，应优先采用不依赖与储库耦合的自旋量子比特初始化方案；同时，双量子点与单储库连接的读出配置中，锁存行为可通过少电子稳定图进行系统调控。

Abstract: Many currently envisaged scalable architectures for Si/SiGe spin qubit devices utilize gate-controlled multi-dot configurations characterized by their peculiar Coulomb blockade physics. One example is the concurrent use of a single electron transistor (SET) as a charge sensor and as a reservoir for adjacent qubit quantum dots (QD). We find that the SET energy spectrum impacts pulsed gate spectroscopy of the QD when the SET simultaneously acts as the reservoir and the sensor. Then, using the qubit QD as a probe, we quantify how strongly the electron temperature of the SET depends on the applied bias. It surpasses a reference experiment by 100 $mK$ even at 50 $μV$ bias. Our results provide a strong incentive for choosing spin qubit initialization schemes which do not rely on coupling to the reservoir in such devices. Switching to a double quantum dot connected to a single reservoir, a frequent configuration for qubit readout, we finally demonstrate the tunability of the characteristic latching behavior by systematically mapping it in the few-electron stability diagram.

</details>


### [162] [Topological phases and scattering protected by projective chiral symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12227)
*Théo Torres,Renaud Côte,Marc Pachebat,Antonin Coutant*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文在同时具有平移对称性与手征（chiral，或称子格 sublattice）对称性的时间反演不变经典系统中，证明当两种对称性反对易时可出现与D类拓扑超导体（type D topological superconductor）同一位势保护（same invariant）的拓扑相，并在声学网络（acoustic network）中实验观测到相应边缘态（edge modes）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体-边对应（bulk-boundary correspondence）表明体材料的拓扑性质可保证受保护边缘态存在，并常伴随背散射免疫（backscattering immunity）这一有吸引力的特性；本文旨在将该机制推广到经典波系统，探索对称性条件如何决定拓扑相与边缘态保护。

Method: 考虑一个时间反演不变（time reversal invariant）且兼具平移与手征（子格）对称性的经典系统，分析两种对称性反对易（anti-commute）时的拓扑分类；并以离散格点模型（discrete lattice models）作理论预测，在声学网络（acoustic network）中进行实验实现与测量对照。

Result: 实验观测到与理论预测相符的受保护边缘态，测量结果与离散格点模型预言高度一致；并进一步表明这些边缘态对于由两个相继相同缺陷（two subsequent identical defects）构成的扰动具有背散射免疫性。

Conclusion: 经典声学系统可在特定对称性（平移与手征对称性反对易、时间反演不变）条件下实现与D类拓扑超导体同类的拓扑相及其受保护边缘态，为在经典波动平台中研究拓扑保护与背散射免疫提供依据。

Abstract: The bulk-boundary correspondence states that topological properties of a bulk material guarantee the existence of protected edge modes, oftentimes associated with the attractive property of backscattering immunity. In this Letter, we consider a time reversal invariant classical system with both translation and chiral, or sublattice, symmetries. We show that when these two symmetries anti-commute, the material exhibits a topological phase protected by the same invariant as type D topological superconductors. We realize such a system experimentally and observe the existence of the associated edge modes in an acoustic network. Our measurements are in excellent agreement with theoretical predictions from discrete lattice models. Finally, we show that these edge modes are immune to backscattering for perturbations consisting in two subsequent identical defects.

</details>


### [163] [Tuning correlated states in rhombohedral pentalayer graphene with magnetic encapsulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12281)
*Cuiju Yu,Yitao Sun,Jose L. Lado*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作表明，借助CrGeTe₃铁磁衬底的磁封装可切换交换耦合，在菱面五层石墨烯中实现对关联自旋对称性破缺与非常规超导的可控调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱面石墨烯多层体系虽能呈现多种关联态，但如何选择性地促进特定关联味对称破缺态仍是挑战；本文旨在通过磁性封装手段实现这一控制目标。

Method: 采用CrGeTe₃磁性封装菱面五层石墨烯，利用铁磁衬底诱导的可切换交换耦合；分析电荷中性附近关联效应对交换邻近劈裂的重整化及掺杂平带后的超导解。

Result: 电荷中性下关联效应强烈重整交换邻近劈裂，产生集体自旋模式，其性质随封装平行或反平行排列而切换；掺杂平带后出现自旋三重态f波超导解，层与自旋选择性受封装控制，并在边缘具有能隙内激发。

Conclusion: 磁排列可作为调控菱面五层石墨烯中自旋对称性破缺与非常规超导的手段，确立了磁性封装在该关联体系中的调控作用。

Abstract: Rhombohedral graphene multilayers provide a flexible platform for correlated physics associated with their flat-band electronic structure.While a variety of correlated states appear in these systems as a function of bias and electrostatic doping, promoting specific correlated flavor symmetry breaking states remains a challenge. Here we show that magnetic encapsulation by CrGeTe$_3$ achieves this in rhombohedral pentalayer graphene, by using switchable exchange couplings induced by ferromagnetic substrates. We show that correlations in rhombohedral multilayers at charge neutrality strongly renormalize the exchange proximity splitting,giving rise to collective spin modes whose character is switched by the parallel or antiparallel alignment of the encapsulation. We further show that upon doping of the flat bands, spin triplet $f$-wave superconducting solutions emerge, whose layer and spin selectivity is controlled by the magnetic encapsulation, hosting in-gap excitations at the edges. Our results establish magnetic alignment as a means to control spin symmetry breaking and unconventional superconductivity in rhombohedral pentalayer graphene.

</details>


### [164] [Gate-tunable supercurrent switching in a three-terminal quantum dot transistor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12415)
*David Novák,Kacper Wrześniewski,Filip Krizek,Wolfgang Belzig,Peter Zalom*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析一种三端超导晶体管开关（three-terminal superconducting transistor switch），其栅极定义的量子点（gate-defined quantum dot）区域截断一个磁通控制的环路；当两环路臂与量子点等隧穿耦合且环路穿过半磁通量子时，输出超电流被完全关断，器件等效为半磁通下的对称SQUID。该关断条件由单一几何因子χ导出，可将三端问题约化为等效两端Anderson杂质模型，并与库仑相互作用、点能级位置、总杂化和温度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是寻求一种开关机制不依赖库仑相互作用、点能级位置、杂化强度与温度等微观参数的超导晶体管开关，从而提高器件对势垒调谐不理想的容忍度。

Method: 引入单一几何因子χ，将三端问题约化为等效两端Anderson杂质模型（Anderson impurity model）；并在全耦合参数空间进行数值重整化群（numerical renormalization group）计算，另在零带宽两能级模型（zero-bandwidth two-level model）中考察第二个点能级的影响。

Result: 关断条件独立于库仑相互作用、点能级位置、总杂化与温度，这些量仅通过前置因子影响器件对势垒调谐误差的容忍度。数值重整化群显示共振嵌于中间杂化Γ≈Δ的宽开关比抬升区域，更弱或更强耦合则使其退化为孤立细线；有限温度平滑单态-双态转变（singlet-doublet transition）并降低超电流幅值，但开关比基本保持。

Conclusion: 第二个点能级会开启泄漏通道（leakage channel），且随两能级能量接近而增强；提高输出势垒及能级间库仑排斥（inter-level Coulomb repulsion）的现实存在可抑制该泄漏。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a three-terminal superconducting transistor switch in which a gate-defined quantum dot region interrupts a flux-controlled loop. The output supercurrent is switched off completely when the two loop arms couple to the dot with equal tunneling strengths and half a flux quantum threads the loop. The device then operates as a symmetric SQUID at half flux. The condition follows from a single geometric factor $χ$, which reduces the three-terminal problem to an equivalent two-terminal Anderson impurity model and makes the switching mechanism independent of the Coulomb interaction, the dot level position, the total hybridization and temperature. These enter only through a prefactor and thus decide how tolerant the device is to imperfect barrier tuning. Numerical renormalization group calculations across the full coupling parameter space show that the resonance is embedded in a broad region of elevated switching ratio at intermediate hybridization $Γ\approx Δ$, while both weaker and stronger coupling reduce it to a thin isolated line. Finite temperature smooths the underlying singlet-doublet transition and lowers the supercurrent amplitude, but affects the open and closed states comparably and leaves the switching ratio largely intact. Within a zero-bandwidth two-level model, a second dot level is shown to open a leakage channel that grows as the two levels approach in energy. Raising the output barrier and the realistic presence of inter-level Coulomb repulsion, however, suppress the leakage.

</details>


### [165] [Topological states in fractals through a self-similar tensor network encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12546)
*Zebedeus F. Osseweijer,Jagoda Malag,Jose L. Lado,Tiago V. C. Antão,Anouar Moustaj*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用张量网络（tensor network）方法的尺度分离特性，精确紧凑地编码Sierpiński垫片等自相似分形晶格上的紧束缚哈密顿量（tight-binding Hamiltonian），并在超百万格点的深层分形上研究Chern绝缘体（Chern insulator）拓扑态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络方法已被推广到单粒子紧束缚哈密顿量并可在指数大晶格上计算可观测量，但如何高效处理自相似、尺度不变的分形结构（如Hausdorff维数约1.58的Sierpiński垫片），以及拓扑与非整数维度（non-integer dimensionality）如何相互作用，仍缺乏可行手段。

Method: 利用张量网络方法的尺度分离（scale-separated）特性对自相似结构进行自然编码，得到分形晶格哈密顿量的精确紧凑表示；并将通量kagome模型（flux kagome model）限制在该分形几何上，发展在张量网络框架内考察其拓扑态的方法。

Result: 相较稠密矩阵（dense-matrix）方法可达到更高的分形代数（fractal generation），解析了超过1亿格点的深层分形的态密度（density of states）；更大的结构还使在更小的能隙（gap）中分辨拓扑成为可能。

Conclusion: 结果表明张量网络编码可显著扩展分形体系的可计算规模，并有望成为理解拓扑与非整数维度相互作用的有效工具。

Abstract: Tensor network methods have been instrumental in addressing exponentially large many-body problems. Most recently, these techniques have also been applied to single-particle tight-binding Hamiltonians, allowing observables to be evaluated on exponentially large lattices. Here, we show that the scale-separated nature of these methods allows for a natural encoding of self-similar structures, such as the Sierpiński gasket, a scale-invariant fractal with Hausdorff dimension $\log 3/\log 2 \approx 1.58$, yielding an exact and compact representation of Hamiltonians on such lattices. Using this encoding, we study a Chern insulator, the flux kagome model, confined to this fractal geometry, and develop methods to investigate its topological states within a tensor network framework. This allows us to reach much higher fractal generations than would otherwise be possible with dense-matrix methods, resolving the density of states of a deep fractal with over 100 million sites. Furthermore, the larger structures allow us to resolve topology in smaller gaps, suggesting that these methods could be instrumental in understanding the interplay between topology and non-integer dimensionality.

</details>


### [166] [Quotient Index for Symmetry-Protected Zeros and the Tenfold Way in Mackey's Construction of Super Corepresentations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12561)
*Zheng Zhang,Y. X. Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文将“与哈密顿量反对易的对称性保护零模”这一障碍表述为对称性表示加法群模去“忘却相容分级映射”像后的商群，并用 Mackey 方法对有限分级对称群的不可约超余表示进行分类，得到 2 个复型与 10 个实型结构类型；该商在有限半单情形下完全消失当且仅当所有零模可被对称相容微扰消除，并在一维端态与无序边界 Dirac 理论插值柱面上恢复受保护多重态计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反对易（anticommute）对称性通过阻止对称相容微扰（symmetry-compatible perturbation）移除零模来保护零模，但缺乏统一的代数框架来刻画“哪些零模受保护、哪些可被消除”这一障碍本身；本文旨在把该障碍形式化为可计算的群论商结构。

Method: 把障碍构造为对称性表示加法群对“忘却相容表示分级（forgetful map）”像的商（quotient）；借助 Mackey 方法构造有限分级对称群的全部不可约超余表示（irreducible super corepresentations），允许反幺正操作（antiunitary operations）与投影因子系统（projective factor systems）；从保持哈密顿量的幺正正规子群出发做诱导，并对限制关系做穷举枚举与特征标判别（character criteria）。

Result: 构造给出 2 个复型与 10 个实型结构类型，由残余 Altland–Zirnbauer 对称代数刻画；残余与诱导分级模具有同构的分级交换子（graded commutant），其表示对称代数与交换子共享一个超相反（super opposite）的分级 Morita 类；限制关系的穷举枚举给出独立的扇区贡献 0、Z 或 Z_2，纯幺正情形下只出现整数因子。

Conclusion: 在有限半单（finite semisimple）情形下，完全指标（complete index）消失当且仅当所有零模都能被对称相容微扰提升消除，从而给出零模可消除性的判别准则；应用于一维端态与无序边界 Dirac 理论的插值柱面（interpolation cylinder）时，恢复受保护多重态计数，并借助体–边对应（bulk–boundary correspondence）、织构–指标定理（texture-index theorems）与费米子相位计算，把该商与体拓扑及 sigma 模型拓扑项联系起来。

Abstract: Symmetries that anticommute with a Hamiltonian can protect zero modes by obstructing their removal through symmetry-compatible perturbations. We formulate this obstruction as a quotient of the additive group of symmetry representations by the image of the map that forgets a compatible representation grading. To compute the quotient, we use Mackey's method to construct all irreducible super corepresentations of finite graded symmetry groups, allowing antiunitary operations and projective factor systems. Starting from the unitary Hamiltonian-preserving normal subgroup, this construction yields two complex and ten real structural types, characterized by residual Altland--Zirnbauer symmetry algebras. The residual and induced graded modules have isomorphic graded commutants, and their represented symmetry algebras share a graded Morita class that is super opposite to that of the commutant. We derive character criteria that identify these types. Exhaustive enumeration of the restriction relations gives independent sector contributions $0$, $Z$, or $Z_2$, with only integer factors in the purely unitary case. In the finite semisimple setting, the complete index vanishes if and only if all zero modes can be lifted by a symmetry-compatible perturbation. Applications to one-dimensional end states and interpolation cylinders of disordered boundary Dirac theories recover protected multiplet counts and connect the quotient to bulk topology and sigma-model topological terms using bulk--boundary correspondence, texture-index theorems, and fermion-phase calculations.

</details>


### [167] [Spin-valve effect through electrostatic coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12588)
*Jan Phillips,Joaquín Fernández-Rossier*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出一种自旋阀效应（spin-valve effect）：绝缘磁性范德华多层通过静电耦合调控相邻非磁导体的电导，而输运电子不进入磁性层；该耦合源于磁化相对取向变化引起的电子结构变化。基于密度泛函计算，预测电荷转移器件磁阻比约 1.14–1.20，单电子晶体管中可达 10³。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋阀依赖自旋极化电流在磁性层中的输运，本文动机在于探索一种无需输运电子进入磁性层的替代机制，利用绝缘磁性范德华多层的静电耦合实现电导调控。

Method: 采用密度泛函（density functional）计算，考察 CrI₃、CrBr₃、CrCl₃ 与 NiBr₂ 等多种材料，评估两类耦合与器件概念：向石墨烯单层的电荷转移（charge transfer）与单电子晶体管（single-electron transistor）中的电容耦合。

Result: 相邻单层磁化由平行转为反平行时，介电常数（dielectric constant）与能带对齐（band alignment）同时改变；预测电荷转移器件磁阻比（magnetoresistance ratio）为 1.14–1.20，单电子晶体管中可达 10³。

Conclusion: 绝缘磁性范德华多层可通过静电耦合调控非磁导体电导，构成不依赖磁性层内自旋输运的自旋阀机制，并在所考察材料中给出可观的磁阻响应，其中单电子晶体管构型的效应最为显著。

Abstract: We propose a spin-valve effect in which an insulating magnetic van der Waals multilayer controls the conductance of an adjacent non-magnetic conductor by electrostatic coupling, without transport-electrons entering the magnetic layers. The coupling is induced by the variations of the electronic structure when the relative orientation of the magnetization of adjacent monolayers changes from parallel to antiparallel, modifying both the dielectric constant and band alignment. We consider two types of coupling and device concepts: charge transfer to a graphene monolayer and capacitive coupling in a single-electron transistor. We use density functional calculations to assess the magnitude of the effect in various materials, including CrI$_3$, CrBr$_3$, CrCl$_3$ and NiBr$_2$. We predict magnetoresistance ratios in the range of 1.14-1.20 for the charge-transfer device and up to $10^3$ in the single-electron transistor.

</details>


### [168] [Compensation Domain Walls in Ferrimagnets as Spin-Torque Oscillator Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12652)
*Matteo Fettizio,Can Onur Avci*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过微磁学模拟与集体坐标建模，研究亚铁磁系统中补偿畴壁（compensation domain walls）在自旋转移矩驱动下的稳态旋转，并将其识别为可调谐自旋力矩纳米振荡器（spin-torque nano-oscillator）的候选平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋力矩纳米振荡器作为纳米尺度微波源具有潜力，但存在工作范围窄、对器件参数敏感等性能限制，因此需要探索新的物理平台以拓展其可调谐性与稳定性。

Method: 采用数值（微磁学）模拟研究不同补偿特性相邻区域中补偿畴壁的动力学；并建立集体坐标模型（collective-coordinate model），推导振荡频率的解析表达式，与模拟结果进行定量对比。

Result: 在所建模型中，补偿畴壁可在较宽的电流密度范围内实现稳态的电流驱动旋转；解析表达式定量再现了微磁学模拟中振荡频率对电流与磁场的依赖关系；外场下交换能与塞曼能（Zeeman energy）的竞争稳定了该磁结构，形成内禀的场致局域化（field-induced confinement）。

Conclusion: 补偿畴壁构成一类具有内禀场致局域化的可调谐自旋力矩纳米振荡器候选平台，为突破传统器件工作范围窄与参数敏感性问题提供了新的物理途径。

Abstract: Spin-torque nano-oscillators are promising nanoscale microwave sources, yet their performance is often limited by narrow operational ranges and sensitivity to device parameters. Here, we numerically investigate the dynamics of compensation domain walls in ferrimagnetic systems driven by spin-transfer torque as a microwave source candidate. These walls arise in structures with adjacent regions of different compensation character, where the competition between exchange and Zeeman energies under an applied magnetic field stabilizes a distinct magnetic texture. We demonstrate steady current-driven rotation of compensation domain walls over a broad range of current densities within the model. To elucidate the underlying mechanism, we develop a collective-coordinate model and derive an analytical expression for the oscillation frequency, which quantitatively reproduces the dependence on current and magnetic field observed in micromagnetic simulations. Our results identify compensation domain walls as a candidate platform for tunable spin-torque nano-oscillators with intrinsic field-induced confinement.

</details>


### [169] [Spin Shuttling Through Quantum-Dot Based Chiral Molecular Bridges: Gauge Constraints, Holonomy, and Quantum Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12720)
*Amitoj S. Miglani,Rajdeep R. Dwivedi,Balsant S. Tiwari,Vishvendra S. Poonia*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文以 Makhlin 不变量为工具，评估手性分子桥在硅量子点间通过手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）效应产生两比特纠缠的能力，结论是单根手性键经 KSEA 规范论证等价于各向同性交换、不构成新的纠缠类别，而破坏该规范对称性后可获得更快的类 CNOT 传播子、非阿贝尔 SU(2) 和乐以及抑制动力学相位噪声的几何门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性分子桥被认为可通过手性诱导自旋选择性（chirality-induced spin-selectivity, CISS）为硅量子点提供新型两比特纠缠资源，但单根手性键能否构成新的纠缠类别、以及 CISS 的微观起源尚不明确，需要定量评判。

Method: 将相位相干桥建模为两自旋间的 SU(2) 值跃迁，经 Schrieffer-Wolff 约化得到扭曲交换相互作用 J S1·R S2，其 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 与对称各向异性部分由单一桥角 α 确定；使用 Makhlin 不变量刻画两比特传播子，并借助 Kaplan-Shekhtman-Entin-Wohlman-Aharony（KSEA）规范论证判定其等价性。另构建由相干旋转、方向反转自旋滤波与指针基退相干组成的统一输运通道模型。

Result: KSEA 论证证明单根手性键的扭曲交换在局域上等价于各向同性交换，故不构成新纠缠类；破坏该规范对称性后，手性交换将均匀横向场转为合成梯度，使两比特传播子在不变空间中以等效正交梯度 4.7 倍的速度进入距 CNOT 类 7×10⁻⁴ 的范围。自旋穿行非共线桥轴可实现非阿贝尔 SU(2) 和乐，产生旋转非互易输运与二阶抑制动力学相位噪声的闭环几何门。输运模型给出泵浦极化的精确 tanh 合成律与闭式横向衰减律，提供四个可实验超定相干分数 F_c 的观测量。硅器件约束方面，确立了 Landau-Zener 谷绝热窗口（E_v ≥ 2.1√(ħv)）与电荷噪声极限（σ_J/J ≤ 3%），以满足 99% 门保真度。

Conclusion: 单根手性键本身不提供新的纠缠类别，手性交换的价值在于破坏规范对称性后带来的合成梯度加速、非互易和乐与几何门抗噪能力；所提统一输运模型可通过四个观测量实验检验 CISS 的相干分数，并给出了硅基硬件实现 99% 保真度所需的谷绝热性与电荷噪声边界。

Abstract: Chiral molecular bridges between silicon quantum dots can provide novel two-qubit entangling resources via the chirality-induced spin-selectivity (CISS) effect. We evaluate this using Makhlin invariants. Modeling a phase-coherent bridge as an $\mathrm{SU}(2)$-valued hopping between two spins, a Schrieffer-Wolff reduction yields a twisted exchange interaction $J \mathbf{S}_1 \cdot \mathbf{R} \mathbf{S}_2$, whose Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya and symmetric anisotropic parts are fixed by a single bridge angle $α$. Using the Kaplan-Shekhtman-Entin-Wohlman-Aharony (KSEA) gauge argument, we prove this interaction is locally equivalent to isotropic exchange; thus, a single chiral bond does not constitute a new entangling class. Breaking this gauge symmetry reveals genuine chiral utility: chiral exchange converts a uniform transverse field into a synthetic gradient, bringing the two-qubit propagator within $7\times10^{-4}$ of the CNOT class in invariant space 4.7 times faster than an equivalent orthogonal gradient. Shuttling a spin through bridges with non-collinear axes implements a non-Abelian $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ holonomy, yielding rotationally non-reciprocal transport and closed-loop geometric gates that suppress dynamical-phase noise to second order. To address the unsettled microscopic origin of CISS, we introduce a unified transport channel combining coherent rotation, a direction-reversing spin filter, and pointer-basis dephasing. This model obeys an exact $\tanh$ composition law for pumped polarization and a closed-form transverse decay law, yielding four observables that experimentally over-determine the bridge's coherent fraction $F_c$. Finally, we quantify silicon-specific hardware constraints, establishing the Landau-Zener valley-adiabaticity window ($E_v \geq 2.1\sqrt{\hbar v}$) and charge-noise limit ($σ_J/J \leq 3\%$) required for $99\%$ gate fidelity.

</details>


### [170] [Universal semiclassical drift velocity of magnetic edge channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12783)
*Ekaterina R. Sleptsova,Alexey A. Sokolik*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用边缘跳跃轨道的半经典量子化，证明在费米能级穿过大量边缘通道的极限下，硬边界处的漂移速度恒等于电子费米速度的二分之一，该结果对硬边界与各向同性色散具有普适性，并在有质量电子与石墨烯无质量电子中获数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性边缘通道（magnetic edge channels）的漂移速度（drift velocity）与边缘态密度、量子电容（quantum capacitance）及边缘磁等离激元（edge magnetoplasmons）速度直接相关，但其微观取值规律尚缺乏统一刻画，尤其缺乏对边界形状与色散依赖性的系统判据。

Method: 采用电子跳跃轨道（skipping orbits）的半经典量子化（semiclassical quantization）方法，对费米能级处群速度作调和平均以得到漂移速度，并分别处理硬边界（hard edge）与平滑静电定义边界、各向同性（isotropic）与各向异性（anisotropic）色散；同时对有质量电子与石墨烯（graphene）无质量电子开展数值检验。

Result: 在费米能级穿过大量边缘通道的极限下，硬边界漂移速度为费米速度的 1/2，且该结果对硬边界与各向同性色散具有普适性，数值计算在有质量与无质量电子体系中均得到验证。平滑静电定义边界（smooth electrostatically defined edges）则不具备此种普适性；各向异性色散情形下，硬边界半经典漂移速度化为沿回旋轨道（cyclotron orbit）的坐标—速度关联。

Conclusion: 硬边界漂移速度与费米速度的 1/2 关系是普适结果，其成立条件为大量边缘通道、硬边界与各向同性色散；边界平滑度与色散各向异性是破坏该普适性的关键因素。

Abstract: Magnetic edge channels, arising at the edge of a two-dimensional electron gas in a magnetic field, are topologically protected and propagate unidirectionally along the edge. Their group velocities at the Fermi level, being averaged harmonically, result in drift velocity, which is related to the edge density of states, quantum capacitance, and the velocity of edge magnetoplasmons. Using semiclassical quantization of electron skipping orbits, we show that the drift velocity equals 1/2 of the electron Fermi velocity in the limit of a large number of edge channels crossing the Fermi level. This result, being universal for a hard edge and for isotropic dispersion, is checked numerically for massive electrons and for massless electrons in graphene. The drift velocity at smooth electrostatically defined edges lacks such universality. In the case of anisotropic dispersion, the semiclassical drift velocity at the hard edge reduces to a coordinate-velocity correlation along the cyclotron orbit.

</details>


### [171] [Enhancing valley splitting in Si/SiGe Wiggle Well quantum dots through integrated stressor-gate architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12850)
*E. S. Joseph,Talise Oh,D. E. Savage,D. S. Stone,DeAnna Campbell,Benjamin D. Woods,Mark Friesen,M. A. Eriksson*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出以图形化应力薄膜（patterned stressed thin films）在Si/SiGe量子阱中产生剪切应变，有限元模拟显示压应力TiN薄膜可产生超过0.1%的剪切应变，足以实现数百μeV的确定性谷分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅自旋量子比特（silicon spin qubits）的规模化操作需要大且可复现的谷分裂（valley splitting），但Si/SiGe量子点中的谷分裂主要源于原子级无序（atomic-scale disorder），本质上是随机的，因此需要引入大幅度的确定性贡献。

Method: 应变辅助Wiggle Wells通过Si阱中振荡的Ge浓度结合剪切应变（shear strain）实现确定性谷分裂，但兼容大规模架构的应变产生方式仍是挑战；本文提出用图形化应力薄膜作为可扩展方案，并采用有限元模拟（finite-element simulations）评估压应力TiN薄膜在量子阱处的剪切应变。

Result: 模拟表明压应力TiN薄膜可在量子阱处产生超过0.1%的剪切应变，在应变辅助Wiggle Wells中足以带来数百μeV的确定性谷分裂；该应变可延伸跨越多个量子点，并在实际栅极几何结构中得以保持。

Conclusion: 图形化应力薄膜提供了一种与制造工艺兼容的方法，可将确定性谷分裂扩展至量子点阵列（quantum dot arrays）。

Abstract: Engineering large and reproducible valley splittings is essential for the scalable operation of silicon spin qubits. In Si/SiGe quantum dot devices, valley splitting arises predominantly from atomic-scale disorder and is therefore inherently random, motivating approaches that introduce a large deterministic contribution. Strain-assisted Wiggle Wells provide a route to deterministic valley splitting by combining an oscillatory Ge concentration in the Si well with shear strain; however, generating the required strain in a manner compatible with large-scale architectures remains a challenge. Here, we propose patterned stressed thin films as a scalable approach for generating shear strain in the quantum well of Si/SiGe quantum dot devices. Finite-element simulations show that compressively stressed TiN films can generate shear strains exceeding 0.1\% at the quantum well, sufficient for deterministic valley splittings of hundreds of $μ\text{eV}$ in strain-assisted Wiggle Wells. We show that this strain can extend across multiple quantum dots and is maintained in realistic gate geometries, providing a fabrication-compatible approach for scaling deterministic valley splitting to quantum dot arrays.

</details>


<div id='math.CA'></div>

# math.CA [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [The solution of the Bochner--Krall problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11742)
*K. Castillo,B. Shapiro*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该论文解决了 Krall 于 1938 年提出的 Bochner–Krall 问题，在放弃正交性假设的一般框架下，对所有满足三项递推关系且为某固定非标量有限阶变系数微分算子特征多项式的首一多项式序列进行了完整分类，并在正定情形下确定了表示测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Bochner–Krall 问题的分类长期局限于正规正交多项式序列（regular orthogonal polynomial sequences），Krall 在 1938 年提出的问题要求突破这一限制；本文旨在给出不依赖正交性假设的完整分类。

Method: 不假设正交性，也不对递推系数施加渐近条件或对支撑集施加有界性假设；分类对象为首一多项式序列，要求乘以自变量后为该项及其前后相邻次数多项式的线性组合（即三项递推结构），且序列成员为同一固定非标量、任意有限阶、多项式系数微分算子的特征多项式；一般表述允许序列从任意固定次数开始。

Result: 在超过某一固定阈值的每个次数上，每个序列在可逆仿射变量变换与首一规范化意义下，必属于 Hermite、Laguerre、Jacobi 或 Bessel 族、单项式基（monomial basis），或 Laguerre/Jacobi 族的有限有理 Darboux 变换（rational Darboux transform）。在正定（positive-definite）情形下，进一步确定了表示测度、证明其唯一性，并给出可容许的端点质量（endpoint masses）。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Bochner–Krall 分类从正规正交序列推广至无正交性假设的一般首一多项式序列，并给出正定情形下测度的唯一性与端点质量刻画，从而完整回答了 Krall 1938 年提出的问题。

Abstract: We solve the Bochner--Krall problem posed by Krall in 1938, extending the classification beyond regular orthogonal sequences. We classify monic polynomial sequences for which multiplying each polynomial by the variable gives a linear combination of that polynomial and the polynomials of the immediately preceding and following degrees, and whose members are eigenpolynomials of one fixed nonscalar differential operator of arbitrary finite order with polynomial coefficients. Orthogonality is not assumed. The general formulation allows sequences to start at any fixed degree. In every degree beyond a fixed threshold, each sequence agrees, up to an invertible affine change of variable and monic normalization, with a Hermite, Laguerre, Jacobi or Bessel family, the monomial basis, or a finite rational Darboux transform of a Laguerre or Jacobi family. In the positive-definite case, we determine the representing measures, prove their uniqueness and specify the admissible endpoint masses. No boundedness assumption on the support or asymptotic conditions on the recurrence coefficients are imposed.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Evidence for quark+quark pairing in proton formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10259)
*Hui-Yu Xing,Dan-Dan Cheng,Craig D. Roberts,Jose Rodriguez-Quintero*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过计算描述高能碰撞中核子产生的碎裂函数（fragmentation functions, FFs），并与唯象全局拟合及强子多重数数据比较，发现重子内夸克配对（diquark correlations，双夸克关联）存在强经验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重子内部夸克之间是否形成配对（diquark pairing）这一问题尚未定论，需要一种可观测、可与数据直接比对的判据来检验。

Method: 利用 Drell-Levy-Yan 关系从核子部分子分布函数（parton distribution functions, PDFs）获得碎裂函数，再求解π介子、K介子和核子喷注级联方程（jet cascade equations）构成的耦合方程组，并将预言与全局拟合及强子多重数数据比较。

Result: 全局拟合比较中，下夸克到质子的碎裂（d→p）给出最清晰的信号；与动量依赖的质子多重数分布比较时，含双夸克关联的质子波函数给出的 z 分布与数据完全一致，而无配对波函数的预言在 9σ 水平被数据排除。

Conclusion: 碎裂函数分析提供了双夸克关联存在的强经验证据，尤其体现于 d→p 碎裂及质子多重数的 z 分布。

Abstract: The question of pairing between quarks inside baryons is addressed by calculating the fragmentation functions that describe production of nucleons (protons and neutrons) in high-energy collisions and comparing attendant predictions with phenomenological global fits to available data and directly with data on hadron multiplicities. The fragmentation functions are obtained from nucleon parton distribution functions using the Drell-Levy-Yan relation and subsequently solving a coupled set of pion, kaon, and nucleon jet cascade equations. Strong empirical evidence is found for the presence of diquark correlations. In comparison with global fits, $d$ (down) quark to proton fragmentation ($d\to p$) exhibits the clearest signals. The strongest evidence is found in comparisons with the momentum-dependent proton multiplicity distribution: FFs built from a proton wave function that contains diquark correlations deliver a $z$-profile that is fully consistent with available data, whereas that obtained from a no-pairing wave function is disfavoured by data at the $9σ$ level.

</details>


### [174] [Untangling flow-harmonic correlations in OO and Pb--Pb collisions at the LHC using event-shape engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10914)
*Suraj Prasad*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用 EPOS4 在 OO 与 Pb–Pb 碰撞中研究 q₂、q₃ 选择对对称累积量（symmetric cumulants）的影响，并借助协方差分解区分选择部分与残余部分。结果显示轻离子碰撞中关联主要局限于 q₂ 类别内部，而 Pb–Pb 中 q₂ 与 q₃ 选择可分别作用于 v₅ 非线性耦合的两个因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称累积量 SC(m,n) 度量流谐波（flow harmonics）v_m 与 v_n 事件逐事件涨落的关联，高次谐波受椭圆流 v₂ 与三角流 v₃ 非线性响应影响；事件形状工程（event-shape engineering）本可控制该贡献，但在轻离子碰撞中受前向多重数（multiplicity）较小的限制，其效力受限，故需定量评估。

Method: 采用 EPOS4 模型，在 √s_NN = 5.36 TeV 的 OO 与 Pb–Pb 碰撞中，以类 ATLAS 接受度模拟按约化流矢量（reduced flow vector）q₂、q₃ 的选择效应；并运用全协方差定律（law of total covariance）将对称累积量分解为选择部分（随 q_n 类别间差异变化）与类别内残余部分。

Result: 在 OO 碰撞中，v₂ 与 v₃、v₄ 的关联几乎完全保持在 q₂ 类别内部，与 Pb–Pb 情形不同；但中心 OO 碰撞中 q₂ 最小的事件显示出 v₂ 与 v₃、v₄ 的关联减弱，归因于 v₄ 趋近其线性响应（linear response）。在 Pb–Pb 碰撞中，q₂ 与 q₃ 选择分别作用于 v₅ 非线性耦合的两个因子。
