Conclusion: 事件形状选择在轻离子碰撞中对对称累积量的调控能力有限，但在 Pb–Pb 碰撞中 q₂ 与 q₃ 选择可用于实验上分离 v₅ 非线性耦合的两个因子，提供了一种可操作的测量途径。

Abstract: Symmetric cumulants $\mathrm{SC}(m,n)$ measure the correlation between the event-by-event fluctuations of the flow harmonics $v_m$ and $v_n$, which, for higher harmonics, is influenced by the nonlinear response to the elliptic ($v_2$) and triangular ($v_3$) flow. Event-shape engineering, which selects events by the reduced flow vector $q_n$, is expected to control this contribution. However, its power is limited by the small forward multiplicity in light-ion collisions. In this work, we study the effect of the $q_2$ and $q_3$ selections on the symmetric cumulants in OO and Pb--Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=5.36$~TeV using EPOS4 with an ATLAS-like acceptance. Using the law of total covariance, the symmetric cumulant is separated into a selected part, which follows the differences between the $q_n$ classes, and a residual part within them. It is observed that in OO collisions the correlations of $v_2$ with $v_3$ and $v_4$ remain almost entirely within the $q_2$ classes, unlike in Pb--Pb collisions. Nevertheless, events with the smallest $q_2$ in central OO collisions show weaker correlations of $v_2$ with $v_3$ and $v_4$, which is attributed to $v_4$ approaching its linear response. In Pb--Pb collisions, the $q_2$ and $q_3$ selections act separately on the two factors of the nonlinear coupling of $v_5$, which establishes a way to isolate them experimentally.

</details>


### [175] [Precision Fragmentation Functions from Modern COMPASS SIDIS Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11237)
*Maryam Soleymaninia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对带电π介子、带电K介子与未鉴别带电强子的共线碎裂函数（collinear fragmentation functions, FFs）三组整体确定进行了比较分析，整合单举e⁺e⁻湮灭与电荷分离的SIDIS数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子和同位旋标量靶样本的新数据（2025 COMPASS质子靶与修订的2026同位旋标量靶结果）可提升SIDIS的味敏感性，因此需系统比较三组碎裂函数确定并评估其约束状况。

Method: 采用HAPS-PiFF1.0、HAPS-KaFF1.0与HAPS-hFF1.0三套分析，结合单举e⁺e⁻湮灭与电荷分离的半单举深度非弹性散射（semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering, SIDIS），使用灵活的神经网络（neural-network）参数化、Monte Carlo复制样本（replicas）以及NLO/NNLO微扰设置。

Result: 质子与同位旋标量样本增强了SIDIS的味敏感性，而胶子碎裂（gluon fragmentation）的约束相对较弱；文中并讨论了微扰精度（perturbative accuracy）的影响。

Conclusion: 三组整体确定各有主要局限，摘要中讨论了这些局限及未来测量（future measurements）的前景。

Abstract: We present a comparative account of three global determinations of collinear fragmentation functions (FFs) for charged pions, charged kaons, and unidentified charged hadrons. The HAPS-PiFF1.0, HAPS-KaFF1.0, and HAPS-hFF1.0 analyses combine single-inclusive $e^+e^-$ annihilation with charge-separated semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering (SIDIS), including the 2025 COMPASS proton-target measurements and the revised 2026 isoscalar-target results. Flexible neural-network parametrizations, Monte Carlo replicas, and NLO/NNLO perturbative setups are employed. The proton and isoscalar samples sharpen the flavor sensitivity of SIDIS, while gluon fragmentation remains comparatively weakly constrained. We discuss the effects of perturbative accuracy, the principal limitations, and prospects for future measurements.

</details>


### [176] [Probing Exotic Electron-Electron Interactions via Adiabatic Spin Inversion in MRFM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11367)
*Chunlong Li,Shien Yang,Yiwei Huang,Yu Zhang,Fei Xue*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于振荡悬臂驱动绝热反转（OSCAR）的磁共振力显微镜（MRFM）方案，将超越标准模型轻玻色子介导的奇异自旋相关电子间相互作用转换为可测机械信号，并评估其对V2、V3、V8耦合的探测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）的轻玻色子可在电子间介导奇异自旋相关相互作用，因而需要在短距离尺度开展高灵敏搜寻；现有约束仍有改进空间。

Method: 采用磁共振力显微镜（magnetic resonance force microscopy, MRFM），利用悬臂振荡驱动的电子自旋绝热反转（OSCAR）；铁磁针尖同时充当致密极化自旋源与磁场梯度源，无需在源与传感器间加磁屏蔽。在线性化耦合自旋-力学动力学框架下推导V2、V3对应的共振频率移动及速度依赖V8产生的二次谐波力信号，并研究中断OSCAR差分探测与距离依赖测量以区分奇异信号与本底。

Result: 在给定噪声与统计假设下，结合系综与单自旋MRFM实验的代表性参数给出了投影灵敏度；结果表明该方案可对短程自旋相关相互作用提供互补灵敏度，并有望在部分亚微米相互作用范围内改进对V2的现有约束。

Conclusion: 该研究将绝热自旋反转MRFM确立为探测奇异电子-电子相互作用的一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Light bosons beyond the Standard Model can mediate exotic spin-dependent interactions between electrons, motivating sensitive searches at short distances. We propose a magnetic resonance force microscopy (MRFM) scheme that exploits oscillating-cantilever-driven adiabatic reversals (OSCAR) of electron spins to convert these interactions into measurable mechanical signals. The ferromagnetic tip serves as both a dense polarized-spin source and a source of magnetic-field gradients, enabling force detection without magnetic shielding between the source and sensor. Within a linearized treatment of the coupled spin-mechanical dynamics, we derive resonance-frequency shifts associated with the $V_2$ and $V_3$ interactions and a second-harmonic force signal arising from the velocity-dependent $V_8$ interaction. We further investigate interrupted-OSCAR differential detection and distance-dependent measurements to discriminate exotic signals from conventional magnetic backgrounds. Using representative parameters from ensemble and single-spin MRFM experiments, we estimate the projected sensitivity under specified noise and statistical assumptions. The results suggest that the proposed scheme could provide complementary sensitivity to short-range spin-dependent interactions, with the potential to improve existing constraints on $V_2$ over selected submicrometer interaction ranges. Our study establishes adiabatic spin-inversion MRFM as a promising approach to probing exotic electron-electron forces.

</details>


### [177] [How to isolate charm production mechanisms using charm-anticharm rapidity and azimuthal angle differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11422)
*Marcus O'Riordan,Peter Christiansen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用PYTHIA模拟研究粲（charm）与反粲强子的关联，发现硬过程、末态劈裂与初态簇射产生的粲在快度（rapidity）和方位角差上分布显著不同，可用Δy选择增强或压低末态劈裂贡献，为实验区分不同产生机制提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强子碰撞中的粲产生涉及多种过程，难以从第一性原理计算，需寻找可实验观测的判别量以区分不同产生机制。

Method: 采用PYTHIA模拟，研究粲与反粲强子之间的关联，比较硬过程粲、末态劈裂（final-state splitting）粲与初态簇射（initial-state shower）粲在快度及方位角差（azimuthal-angle difference）上的分布，并施加对快度间隔Δy的选择。

Result: 三类产生机制占据快度与方位角差空间中明显不同的区域；对Δy的选择可强烈增强或压低近侧（near-side）末态劈裂贡献，从而赋予实验对不同产生过程的灵敏度。与LHCb Run 1的D0-D0bar关联测量比较，支持利用更大LHC数据集开展更微分的研究。

Conclusion: 将此类测量推广至重离子（heavy-ion）碰撞，可进一步区分硬散射直接产生的粲与来自母胶子（parent gluon）的粲在介质中的修正，为重夸克（heavy quark）与胶子同夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）的不同相互作用提供新探针。

Abstract: Charm production in hadronic collisions proceeds via many different processes, which makes it hard to calculate from first principles. Using PYTHIA simulations, we study correlations between charm and anticharm hadrons and show that hard-process charm, final-state splitting charm, and initial-state-shower charm populate markedly different regions of rapidity and azimuthal-angle difference. In particular, selections on the pair rapidity separation, $Δy$, can strongly enrich or suppress the near-side final-state splitting contribution and thereby provide experimental sensitivity to the different production processes. A comparison with existing LHCb $D^0\bar{D}^0$ correlation measurements from Run 1 motivates more differential studies using the substantially larger LHC data sets now available. Extending such measurements to heavy-ion collisions could further distinguish medium modifications of charm produced directly in the hard scattering from charm originating from a parent gluon, providing a new handle on the different interactions of heavy quarks and gluons with the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [178] [Spin entanglement in $pp$ and $pA$ collisions: high-energy TMD effects and fragmentation probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11568)
*Michael Fucilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在冲击波（shockwave）形式的 k_T 因子化（k_T-factorization）框架下，研究质子-质子或质子-离子碰撞中夸克-反夸克对的旋量关联（spin-spin correlations），构建包含性与衍射（diffractive）通道的自旋密度矩阵，并揭示线性极化胶子诱导的量子信息量调制及衍射通道的纠缠性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能强子碰撞中产生的夸克对具有自旋关联，可用量子信息量（如纠缠）刻画；但包含与衍射通道中这些关联的来源、以及在饱和（saturation）效应下的行为尚需系统的统一描述。

Method: 在 k_T 因子化（k_T-factorization）与冲击波（shockwave）形式下，取两夸克背对背（back-to-back）极限，构建包含与衍射通道的完整自旋密度矩阵（spin-density matrix）；以 concurrence、stabilizer Rényi 熵（stabilizer Rényi entropy）与量子失协（quantum discord）为量子信息度量进行分析。

Result: 包含通道中，线性极化胶子（linearly polarized gluons）导致上述三个量出现 cos(2φ) 调制，调制依赖六个小 x 胶子 TMD（transverse-momentum-dependent distributions）的相互竞争；从稀薄（BFKL）图像看调制最大且与横向动量失配 q 无关，而饱和效应显著压低该调制。衍射通道中，夸克对始终纠缠，性质与衍射深度非弹性散射（diffractive DIS）一致，源于 Pomeron 交换的排他性与程函（eikonal）性质，对应秩至多 2 且边际最大混合的自旋密度矩阵。

Conclusion: 对这类秩至多 2、边际最大混合的态，纠缠与非定域性（Bell nonlocality）重合，且由光前螺旋度振幅（light-front helicity amplitudes）的显式形式保证其在整个相空间持续存在。文中建议通过 Collins 与双强子碎裂函数（dihadron fragmentation functions）测量非极化强子碰撞中的自旋关联，并指出无质量夸克的包含产生中，适当选取的双 Collins 不对称性（double-Collins asymmetries）可作为有前景的纠缠见证（entanglement witnesses）。

Abstract: Spin-spin correlations of a quark-antiquark pair produced in proton-proton or proton-Ion collisions are investigated in $k_T$-factorization within the shockwave formalism. We construct the full spin-density matrix of both inclusive and diffractive channels in the limit in which the two quarks are back-to-back. In the inclusive channel, due to linearly polarized gluons, we show that a $\cos (2 φ)$-modulation of the concurrence, stabilizer Rényi entropy, and quantum discord emerges and depends on the interplay between six small-$x$ gluon TMDs. The modulation is predicted to be maximally strong and independent of the transverse momentum imbalance, $q$, from a dilute (BFKL) perspective, while it is considerably suppressed by saturation effects. In diffraction, we observe that the pair is always entangled and exhibits the same properties found in diffractive DIS. We explain how these properties follow directly from the exclusive and eikonal nature of the Pomeron exchange. We trace this behavior to a spin-density matrix of rank at most two with maximally mixed marginals. For this class of states, entanglement and Bell nonlocality coincide even when the state is mixed, while the explicit form of the light-front helicity amplitudes ensures that both persist in the whole phase space. We suggest accessing the spin correlations in unpolarized hadron collisions through the Collins and dihadron fragmentation functions. We point out that, in inclusive production, for massless quarks, suitably chosen double-Collins asymmetries provide promising entanglement witnesses.

</details>


### [179] [HEART: a resonance-cascade model of soft hadron production in p+p collisions at 158 GeV/c](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11570)
*Ondrej Chvala*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 HEART 模型（Hadronic Excitations And Resonance Transitions），将 p+p 碰撞的已识别强子谱解释为靶碎裂（target）与弹碎裂（projectile）两个激发强子系统衰变之和，并在 NA49 数据上以 14 个参数实现事件级拟合，显著优于 EPOS-LHC、UrQMD 与 PYTHIA 8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fischer 等将 158 GeV/c p+p 碰撞的 NA49 强子谱解读为靶与弹两个激发的强子系统衰变之和，但该图像缺乏事件级的具体实现；本文旨在补足这一实现并加以检验。

Method: 单衍射（single diffraction）、双衍射（double diffraction）与非衍射交换产生色单态（color-singlet）激发系统；该系统在 1.7–2.2 GeV 的对偶边界（duality boundary）以下取目录共振态、以上取共振连续谱，通过严格守恒定律的连续两体跃迁（two-body transitions）退激，并由单π交换（one-pion exchange）产生领头中子。14 个参数拟合 1,254 个 π±、K±、p、p̄ 双微分截面点与 8 个中子点。

Result: 在考虑相关系统误差（correlated systematic uncertainties）并设定 5% 模型下限（model floor）的误差模型下，拟合给出 χ² = 2263 / 1248 自由度（d.o.f.）；同样数据点上 EPOS-LHC、UrQMD 与 PYTHIA 8 的 χ² 高出 4.2–14.4 倍。模型无需中心源（central source），并复现前向 K⁺ 亏损、前向 K*(892)⁰ 过量、平坦的 Λ x_F 分布，Ξ⁻ 产额达 NA61/SHINE 的 0.61。

Conclusion: 重激发系统的带电多重数随质量线性增长，快于 e⁺e⁻ 湮灭中的行为；区分级联（cascade）模型与弦（string）、火球（fireball）模型的观测量集中于碎裂区（fragmentation region），可由大接受度固定靶谱仪（fixed-target spectrometer）测量。

Abstract: Fischer and co-workers interpret the NA49 identified-hadron spectra in p+p collisions at 158 GeV/c as the sum of a target and a projectile component, each the decay of an excited hadronic system. This paper supplies an event-level realization of that picture, the HEART model (Hadronic Excitations And Resonance Transitions), and tests it on the published NA49 data. Single diffraction, double diffraction, and non-diffractive exchange produce color-singlet excited systems. Each system is a catalogued resonance below a duality boundary of 1.7 to 2.2 GeV or a member of a resonant continuum above it. It de-excites by successive two-body transitions with exact conservation laws, and one-pion exchange produces the leading neutrons. Fourteen parameters are fitted to 1,254 points of the $π^\pm$, $K^\pm$, $p$, and $\bar{p}$ double-differential cross sections and eight neutron points. Under an error model that profiles the correlated systematic uncertainties, with a declared 5% model floor, the fit yields $χ^2 = 2263$ on 1248 degrees of freedom. On the same points, EPOS-LHC, UrQMD, and PYTHIA 8 at their published tunes give $χ^2$ values 4.2 to 14.4 times higher. The model needs no central source. The forward $K^+$ deficit, the forward $K^*(892)^0$ excess, and the flat $Λ$ $x_F$ distribution point to the momentum sharing at the vertex where the leading baryon turns into a hyperon. The $Ξ^-$ reaches 0.61 of the NA61/SHINE yield. The charged multiplicity of a heavy excited system grows linearly with its mass, faster than in $e^+e^-$ annihilation. The observables that distinguish a cascade from string and fireball models lie in the fragmentation region, where wide-acceptance fixed-target spectrometers can measure them.

</details>


### [180] [Heavy-quark spin symmetry and Pauli exclusion in pentaquarks with three to five heavy quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12318)
*Ulaş Özdem*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在 QCD 求和规则（QCD sum rules）框架下系统计算了含 3–5 个重夸克（heavy quark）的负宇称隐味五夸克态（hidden-flavor pentaquarks）$QQ\bar Qqq$、$QQQ\bar Qq$、$QQQQ\bar Q$ 的质量分裂，发现特定组态的分裂随重夸克质量按 $\bar m_c/\bar m_b$ 减小，而全重系统的 $5/2^-$ 分裂不随味减小，指向泡利原理而非超精细效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对质量对重夸克质量方案（heavy-quark mass scheme）和有限宽度（finite width）敏感，故转而研究同一重子—介子组态内部的能级分裂，以获取更稳健、更具判别力的理论预言，并检验重夸克自旋对称性（heavy-quark spin symmetry）与泡利原理的作用。

Method: 采用 QCD 求和规则，构建 $J^P=1/2^-,3/2^-,5/2^-$ 的完整 S 波重子—介子流（baryon–meson currents）集合，算符乘积展开（operator product expansion, OPE）分别保留至维度 10、9、6；并将关联函数（correlators）分解为可因子化的双强子（two-hadron）部分与连通的夸克交换（quark-exchange）贡献。

Result: 轻自由度自旋唯一的组态近乎简并，或其分裂从粲到底按与 $\bar m_c/\bar m_b$ 相容的因子减小；两个轻夸克分处不同强子的组态则分裂不被压低。关联函数以双强子部分为主导，且该模式在两部分中分别成立；交换重夸克时交换贡献从粲到底减小，交换轻夸克时不减小。全重系统中 $1/2^-$ 与 $3/2^-$ 在约 20 MeV 内简并，$5/2^-$ 分别高出 $3/2^-$ 约 126–139 MeV（$cccc\bar c$）与 183–228 MeV（$bbbb\bar b$）。

Conclusion: 全重系统的 $5/2^-$ 分裂由全同重夸克间的交换项产生且不随味减小，表明其来源为泡利原理（Pauli principle）而非超精细（hyperfine）效应；此外，重子与介子不共享夸克味的流在本阶严格可因子化，无法区分束缚态与双强子态。

Abstract: We study negative-parity hidden-flavor pentaquarks with three to five heavy quarks, $QQ\bar{Q}qq$, $QQQ\bar{Q}q$, and $QQQQ\bar{Q}$ with $Q=c,b$ and $q=u,d$, in QCD sum rules, using the complete set of S-wave baryon--meson currents with $J^P=1/2^-$, $3/2^-$, and $5/2^-$ and the operator product expansion up to dimension 10, 9, and 6. We focus on the splittings between states of the same baryon--meson configuration, which, unlike the absolute masses, are only weakly affected by the choice of the heavy-quark mass scheme and by a finite width. Configurations whose light degrees of freedom have a unique spin are nearly degenerate or show splittings that decrease from charm to bottom by a factor compatible with $\bar m_c/\bar m_b$, whereas configurations with two light quarks in different hadrons show splittings that are not reduced, as expected from heavy-quark spin symmetry. A decomposition of the correlators into factorizable two-hadron and connected quark-exchange contributions shows that the correlators are dominated by the two-hadron part and that this pattern holds separately for both parts: the quark-exchange contribution decreases from charm to bottom when a heavy quark is exchanged and is not reduced when a light quark is exchanged. In the fully heavy systems the $1/2^-$ and $3/2^-$ states are degenerate within about $20$~MeV, while the $5/2^-$ state lies $126$--$139$~MeV ($cccc\bar c$) and $183$--$228$~MeV ($bbbb\bar b$) above the $3/2^-$ state. This splitting is generated by the exchange terms between the identical heavy quarks and does not decrease from charm to bottom, which points to the Pauli principle rather than to a hyperfine effect. Currents whose baryon and meson share no quark flavor are exactly factorizable at this order and cannot distinguish bound states from two-hadron states.

</details>


### [181] [Magnetic moments of the $Σ_c$ and $Σ_b$ baryons from external-field QCD sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12373)
*Halil Mutuk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用外场量子色动力学求和规则（external-field QCD sum rules）计算含粲与含底同位旋三重态重子 Σc、Σb 的磁矩，并以同一插值场的质量求和规则归一化以消除残留；质子磁矩用于标定夸克凝聚磁化率，最终给出各成员磁矩及各不确定度来源的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重味重子（heavy baryon）磁矩是检验夸克模型与量子色动力学（QCD）非微扰图像的重要观测量，而 Σc、Σb 同位旋三重态的实验与理论信息相对有限，需要基于第一性原理方法给出可靠预言并厘清不确定度来源。

Method: 采用外场 QCD 求和规则：重夸克传播子保留完整质量依赖，并同时包含与光子、软胶子（soft gluons）及两者的耦合；磁矩求和规则由同一插值场（interpolating field）的质量求和规则归一化以消除残留（residue）；夸克凝聚磁化率（quark-condensate magnetic susceptibility）χ 由同一插值场计算的质子磁矩标定；不确定度通过蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）分析并叠加插值场、Borel 窗口、重夸克质量标度、标定与归一化等误差。

Result: 取 MS-bar 重夸克质量时（以核磁子（nuclear magneton）为单位）：μ(Σc++)=2.37±0.65、μ(Σc+)=0.50±0.15、μ(Σc0)=−1.39±0.37、μ(Σb+)=2.16±0.65、μ(Σb0)=0.55±0.16、μ(Σb−)=−1.06±0.32；该阶结果对重夸克质量方案（mass scheme）有显著依赖。磁矩主要由轻双夸克（light diquark）贡献，重夸克部分在 Σc 中为 −0.13±0.02 μN、在 Σb 中为 +0.013±0.003 μN，其分离方式依赖于插值场。

Conclusion: 该方法在不引入额外自由参数（χ 由质子标定）的前提下自洽给出 Σc、Σb 三重态磁矩及量化误差，并显示三重态内磁矩呈近似等间距的电荷依赖性；重夸克质量方案与插值场选择是当前精度的主要限制，重夸克自身对磁矩的贡献较小且符号随味改变。

Abstract: We compute the magnetic moments of the $Σ_c$ and $Σ_b$ isotriplets with external-field QCD sum rules. The heavy-quark propagator is kept with its full mass dependence and with its couplings to the photon, to soft gluons and to both simultaneously, and the magnetic sum rule is normalized by the mass sum rule of the same interpolating field, which removes the residue and gives the baryon masses within a few percent. The quark-condensate magnetic susceptibility $χ$, which otherwise dominates the uncertainty, is fixed by the proton magnetic moment computed with the same interpolating field; the neutron moment then follows to about $5\%$. Uncertainties from the QCD inputs are evaluated with a Monte Carlo analysis, and those from the interpolating field, the Borel window, the scale of the heavy-quark mass, the calibration and the normalization are added. With $\overline{\rm MS}$ heavy-quark masses we obtain $μ_{Σ_c^{++}}=2.37\pm0.65$, $μ_{Σ_c^{+}}=0.50\pm0.15$, $μ_{Σ_c^{0}}=-1.39\pm0.37$, $μ_{Σ_b^{+}}=2.16\pm0.65$, $μ_{Σ_b^{0}}=0.55\pm0.16$ and $μ_{Σ_b^{-}}=-1.06\pm0.32$ in nuclear magnetons; at this order the results depend sizably on the heavy-quark mass scheme. The moments are carried by the light diquark; the heavy-quark contribution, whose separation depends on the interpolating field, is $-0.13\pm0.02\,μ_N$ for $Σ_c$ and $+0.013\pm0.003\,μ_N$ for $Σ_b$.

</details>


### [182] [A possible signature of neutrino spin precession in the J-PARC muon $g-2$ experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12749)
*S. Carneiro,F. C. Sobrinho*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出中微子对携带的角动量可能在缪子磁异常测量中引入不确定性，并计算了J-PARC E34与费米实验室测量之间可观测的修正差异（约0.415±0.176 ppm），用于独立于强子贡献检验该效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作指出未被探测的中微子对携带的角动量可能使由正电子时间调制推断的磁异常（magnetic anomaly）存在不确定性；费米实验室（Fermilab）的高相对论缪子可用前向发射近似（forward-emission approximation），而J-PARC较低缪子动量要求显式处理衰变运动学（decay kinematics），故需重新定量估计。

Method: 利用理论弱衰变谱（weak-decay spectrum）给出的正电子能谱与不对称度，对中微子对的纵向动量（longitudinal momentum）进行积分，并在两个实验中使用相同的事件计数权重 N(E)A(E)；比较费米实验室（1.5–2.9 GeV）与J-PARC（200–275 MeV）正电子能量区间内的修正大小。

Result: 在假定E34最乐观精度下，J-PARC处的修正比费米实验室小 0.415±0.176 ppm；若未来测量以该值为中心，预期差异达 2.36 个标准差（standard deviations）。

Conclusion: 该计算给出一个可检验所提效应的实验比较方案，且不依赖标准模型预测中的强子贡献（hadronic contribution）；在固定200 MeV正电子能量阈下，最大灵敏度出现在245 MeV缪子束能量处，相对于J-PARC标称配置的预测差异在最乐观实验灵敏度下显著性为 4.88σ。

Abstract: We examine a possible signature of the neutrino angular-momentum contribution to the muon magnetic anomaly measurement in the J-PARC E34 experiment. In previous papers we argued that the angular momentum carried by the unobserved neutrino pair may leave an uncertainty in the anomaly inferred from the time modulation of detected positrons. At Fermilab the highly relativistic muons make the forward-emission approximation suitable for this estimate. The lower muon momentum at J-PARC requires the decay kinematics to be treated explicitly. We integrate the longitudinal momentum of the neutrino pair using the positron energy distribution and asymmetry derived from the theoretical weak-decay spectrum, and the same event-counting weight $N(E)A(E)$ is employed for both experiments. For positron energies between $1.5$ and $2.9$ GeV at Fermilab and between $200$ and $275$ MeV at J-PARC, the proposed correction is smaller at J-PARC by $0.415\pm0.176$ ppm, when the most optimistic E34 precision is assumed. The expected difference amounts to $2.36$ standard deviations if the future measurement is centred on this value. The calculation thus identifies an experimental comparison which may test the proposed effect independently of the hadronic contribution to the Standard Model prediction. We also examine the possibility of testing the effect by comparing measurements at two different muon momenta. With a fixed positron energy threshold of 200 MeV, the maximum sensitivity is obtained at a muon beam energy of 245 MeV. The predicted difference relative to the nominal J-PARC configuration has significance of $4.88σ$ for the most optimistic experimental sensitivity.

</details>


### [183] [Quantum Open Effective Field Theory: anomalous decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10287)
*Gabriela Barenboim,Daniel Boyanovsky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Schwinger-Keldysh形式与二阶量子主方程，研究标量场与具有临界行为的"反常"环境耦合下的退相干（decoherence）。结果表明退相干函数Φ(t)呈幂律（∝t^{-μ}，μ>0）或对数（∝ln t）形式，其指数由谱密度阈值行为与关联函数反常标度维度决定；临界环境还引入新的动力学标度指数，并在特定共振处强烈增强退相干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于S矩阵（S-matrix）与费米黄金规则（Fermi's golden rule）的速率描述无法刻画非马尔可夫（non-Markov）瞬态动力学，尤其当环境具有反常标度或临界行为时。因此需要借助Schwinger-Keldysh与含时速率的主方程，系统研究此类环境下的退相干过程。

Method: 采用Schwinger-Keldysh形式并引入影响泛函（influence functional），推导到耦合二阶的朗之万方程（Langevin equation）及含时速率（time dependent rates）的Lindblad型量子主方程（quantum master equation, QME）。以三个非马尔可夫实例为对象：非粒子物理（unparticle physics）、阈值与红外增强、临界环境（critical environments）。

Result: 探测器约化密度矩阵（reduced density matrix）在能量基下退相干，非对角元按e^{-Φ(t)}衰减，退相干率Γ(t)=Φ̇(t)。瞬态或渐近行为为Φ(t)∝t^{-μ}（μ>0），μ由谱密度阈值行为与关联函数反常标度维度（anomalous scaling dimensions）决定；红外情形为Φ(t)∝ln(t)；临界环境存在新的动力学标度指数。频率ω_q的探针在q_*∝ω_q^{1/z}处与"临界慢"（critically slow）准粒子共振，退相干被强烈增强。

Conclusion: 具有反常标度与临界行为的环境导致明显偏离马尔可夫速率描述的非马尔可夫退相干动力学，其退相干函数形式由谱密度阈值与反常标度维度共同控制。临界环境引入新的动力学标度指数，且共振条件可显著增强退相干，为探测反常环境与临界浴提供潜在手段。

Abstract: We study decoherence of a scalar field coupled to ``anomalous'' environments with critical behavior with the Schwinger-Keldysh including the influence functional, and the quantum master equation (QME) up to second order in the coupling. The Schwinger-Keldysh formulation yields a Langevin equation and the Lindblad (QME) includes \emph{time dependent rates} allowing to study transient dynamics. We focus on three examples featuring non-Markov dynamics that cannot be described with S-matrix/Fermi's golden rule rates \textbf{i) Unparticle physics:} a conformal sector beyond the Standard Model at an infrared fixed point featuring correlation functions with anomalous scaling dimensions, \textbf{ii) Threshold and infrared enhancement:} when the mass shell of the field coincides with or is near to a kinematic threshold of the spectral density of the bath, \textbf{iii) Critical environments:} the bath is at a critical point, correlation functions scale with anomalous dimensions and long-wavelength fluctuations feature critical slowing down. In all cases the reduced density matrix undergoes decoherence in the energy basis of the probe, and off-diagonal matrix elements decay as $e^{-Φ(t)}$ with $Φ(t)>0 $ the \emph{decoherence function} and $Γ(t) = \dotΦ(t)$ the decoherence rate. In all cases we find that either transient or asymptotically $Φ(t) \propto t^{-μ}~;~ μ>0$ with $μ$, determined by the behavior of the spectral density at threshold and the anomalous scaling dimensions of the correlation functions, or $Φ(t) \propto \ln(t)$ for the infrared case. For a critical environment there is a new dynamical scaling exponent. A probe of frequency $ω_q$ is resonant with a \emph{critically slow} quasiparticle for $q_*\propto ω^{1/z}_q$ at which decoherence is strongly \emph{enhanced}.

</details>


### [184] [Quark-level CPT violation in neutral-meson systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11033)
*Ralf Lehnert,Agnes Roberts*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在中性介子干涉测量（neutral-meson interferometry）框架下研究 CPT 与洛伦兹对称性破缺（Lorentz breakdown），基于非最小标准模型扩展（nonminimal Standard-Model Extension, SME），为 d=5 夸克算符与介子有效拉氏量建立联系并给出首批约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将中性介子振荡中的 CPT 检验与 d=5 夸克层级的 SME 系数联系起来，使实验观测可在夸克层面得到解释，从而拓展对 CPT 与洛伦兹破缺的检验能力。

Method: 采用手征微扰理论（chiral perturbation theory）方法处理质量量纲 d=5 情形，建立夸克层级与介子有效伪标量拉氏量（effective pseudoscalar Lagrangian）之间的对应关系，并结合探测器几何（detector geometry）分析中性介子振荡中的现象学特征。

Result: 利用现有数据首次提取出两个系数的约束（first constraints），并估计相对已发表界限最高可达 18 个数量级的灵敏度提升。

Conclusion: 该分析使得此类 CPT 检验可在 SME 的 d=5 夸克扇区（quark sector）内进行解释，并显示中性介子干涉测量在未来具有显著的灵敏度潜力。

Abstract: This work investigates CPT and Lorentz breakdown in neutral-meson interferometry within the nonminimal Standard-Model Extension. Employing methods of chiral perturbation theory for mass dimension d=5, a connection between the quark-level and the meson effective pseudoscalar Lagrangian is established. The resulting phenomenological signatures in neutral-meson oscillations with particular focus on the detector geometry are explored. This analysis permits the interpretation of such CPT tests within the d=5 quark sector of the Standard-Model Extension. Existing data are used to extract first constraints on two coefficients and to estimate achievable sensitivity gains over published bounds of up to 18 orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [185] [The first-order phase transitions and gravitational waves from an effective theory inspired by four-stack intersecting D6-brane models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11211)
*Haotian Huangfu,Tianjun Li,Qi Sun,Rui Sun,Wenxing Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在一类由四叠相交 D6 膜模型启发的 Pati-Salam 有效理论中，研究右手规范对称性破缺 $SU(2)_{R_1}\times SU(2)_{R_2}\to SU(2)_R$ 产生的一阶相变引力波信号；快壁参考情形下谱峰位于 $f_{\rm peak}=0.611$ Hz、$h^2\Omega_{\rm GW,peak}=1.232\times10^{-11}$，落入 DECIGO 与 BBO 目标的分赫兹（deci-Hz）频段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期宇宙一阶相变（first-order phase transition）可产生随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational-wave background），其频段正对应拟议的分赫兹观测站；本文旨在考察受紫外完备构造约束的 Pati-Salam 有效理论中右手规范对称性破缺相变的引力波可探测性，从而将弦论膜构造与引力波观测联系起来。

Method: 紫外构造固定规范结构与粒子多重数，标量质量与四次耦合（quartic coupling）作为超对称破缺标度以下的重整化输入；采用含环形重求和（ring resummation）的一阶有限温度势（finite-temperature potential），追踪少数 TeV 标度下强过冷（strongly supercooled）相变的成核（nucleation）、逾渗（percolation）、完成与再加热过程，并以领头阶摩擦诊断判断壁面是否失控，评估声波与湍流贡献。

Result: 领头阶摩擦诊断不支持失控壁（runaway wall），故引力波信号由声波（sound waves）主导、湍流为次主导贡献，有限声学寿命压低谱峰；快壁参考情形下谱峰为 $f_{\rm peak}=0.611$ Hz、$h^2\Omega_{\rm GW,peak}=1.232\times10^{-11}$，位于分赫兹频段。文中进一步给出谱对壁速、重整化标度与热屏蔽（thermal screening）的依赖，并对若干拟议探测器配置作了仅含噪声的比较。

Conclusion: 该右手规范对称性破缺相变可在 DECIGO 与 BBO 所瞄准的分赫兹频段产生可观的引力波背景，且其谱形与幅度对标量扇区输入及壁速等参数敏感；膜构造所固定的规范结构与多重数为该类预言提供了受约束的理论基础。

Abstract: First-order phase transitions in the early Universe can generate stochastic gravitational-wave backgrounds in frequency bands targeted by proposed deci-Hz observatories. We study the gravitational-wave consequences of the right-handed gauge symmetry breaking $SU(2)_{R_1}\times SU(2)_{R_2}\to SU(2)_R$ in a Pati-Salam effective theory inspired by four-stack intersecting D6-brane models. The ultraviolet construction fixes the gauge structure and particle multiplicities, while the scalar masses and quartic couplings are treated as renormalized inputs below the supersymmetry-breaking scale. Using a one-loop finite-temperature potential with ring resummation, we follow a strongly supercooled transition at the few-TeV scale through nucleation, percolation, completion, and reheating. The leading-order friction diagnostic does not support a runaway wall, so sound waves dominate the gravitational-wave signal, with turbulence as a sub-leading contribution; the finite acoustic lifetime suppresses the peak. For the fast-wall reference case, the spectrum peaks at $f_{\rm peak}=0.611$ Hz with $h^2Ω_{\rm GW,peak}=1.232\times10^{-11}$, in the deci-Hz band targeted by DECIGO and BBO. We present its dependence on the wall speed, renormalization scale, and thermal screening, together with a noise-only comparison for several proposed detector configurations.

</details>


### [186] [A Self-Interacting Dark-Matter Route to Supermassive Kerr Black Holes via Super-Eddington Bondi Accretion: Mass-Spin Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12282)
*Chian-Shu Chen,Feng-Li Lin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 将此前史瓦西（Schwarzschild）情形下的临界相对论邦迪吸积（critical relativistic Bondi accretion）推广至旋转的克尔黑洞（Kerr black hole），表明排斥性自相互作用暗物质（repulsively self-interacting dark matter）仍可提供高效的超爱丁顿（super-Eddington）增长通道，并同时实现质量增长与自旋加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST 早期观测结果需要解释早期宇宙中黑洞的快速生长机制；既有工作仅处理非旋转的史瓦西黑洞，缺少对旋转吸积流与克尔度规的考虑。

Method: 在稳态赤道准球对称（stationary equatorial quasi-spherical）框架下求解克尔黑洞的临界相对论邦迪吸积，流入角动量取 Kerr 边际束缚捕获值（marginally bound capture）所限定的有限区间，并分析声速结构、吸积效率与暗物质晕匹配。

Result: 在该角动量范围内存在光滑临界解；旋转改变声速结构与吸积效率，而临界密度相消（critical density cancellation）与暗区—晕匹配保持史瓦西形式。快速生长可持续至有限的晕供给成为限制；示意性星族演化显示恒星质量种子可同时增长质量与自旋。

Conclusion: 旋转吸积流为早期超大质量克尔黑洞提供了一条质量—自旋联合的增长路径（joint mass–spin route），但最终受限于暗物质晕的供给上限。

Abstract: Recent JWST observations motivate mechanisms for rapid black-hole growth in the early Universe. Extending our previous Schwarzschild study, we show that critical relativistic Bondi accretion of repulsively self-interacting dark matter remains an efficient super-Eddington channel for Kerr black holes with rotating inflow. Within a stationary equatorial quasi-spherical framework, smooth critical solutions exist over a finite range of inflow angular momentum set by the Kerr marginally bound capture values. Rotation changes the sonic structure and accretion efficiency, while the critical density cancellation and dark-sector/halo matching retain their Schwarzschild form. The resulting rapid growth persists until the finite halo supply becomes limiting. Because the inflow also carries angular momentum, stellar-mass seeds can grow and spin up simultaneously; illustrative population evolutions therefore provide a joint mass--spin route to early supermassive Kerr black holes.

</details>


### [187] [Naturalness in the Past from a Time-Dependent Supersymmetry Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12363)
*Tony Gherghetta,Kunio Kaneta,Wenqi Ke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在无标度超引力（no-scale supergravity）框架下，电弱自然性（electroweak naturalness）可具有时代依赖性：滚动的第五元素模量（quintessence modulus）可控制超对称破缺标度，使超伴子（superpartners）在早期宇宙中较轻而现今较重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于考察电弱自然性问题是否可能依赖于宇宙演化时期，即超对称破缺标度是否随宇宙时代变化，从而缓解现今观测与自然性期望之间的张力。

Method: 采用无标度超引力框架，引入滚动的第五元素模量控制超对称破缺标度；通过可见区Kähler度规（Kähler metric）中相关的模权重（modular weights）与模量依赖的超势汤川耦合（superpotential Yukawa couplings）抑制第五力（fifth-force）与常数变化效应。

Result: 该机制可使超伴子在早期宇宙中显著轻于现今；但第五元素势（quintessence potential）的辐射稳定性（radiative stability）需要对场依赖的真空能（field-dependent vacuum energy）进行额外调节。所得有效理论对早期宇宙相变（phase transitions）、遗迹丰度（relic abundances）与引力波（gravitational waves）具有独特预言。

Conclusion: 电弱自然性可能并非固定属性，而是随宇宙时代演化的；该时代依赖的自然性方案在满足现有第五力与常数变化约束的同时，给出可检验的早期宇宙与引力波信号。

Abstract: We consider the possibility that electroweak naturalness may be epoch dependent. In no-scale supergravity, a rolling quintessence modulus can control the supersymmetry-breaking scale, allowing superpartners to be much lighter in the early universe while remaining heavy today. Fifth-force and varying-constant effects can be suppressed via correlated modular weights in the visible-sector Kähler metric and modulus-dependent superpotential Yukawa couplings, while radiative stability of the quintessence potential requires additional tuning of the field-dependent vacuum energy. The resulting effective theory has distinctive implications for early-universe phase transitions, relic abundances, and gravitational waves.

</details>


### [188] [Pentagon Functions for Two-Loop Leading-Colour $pp\to t\bar{t}H$ Amplitudes with a Light-Quark Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12661)
*Simone Devoto,Manfred Kraus,Ben Page*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文为进入 $pp\to t\bar{t}H$（含闭合轻夸克圈、领头色振幅）的平面两圈主积分（master integrals）构建了一套特殊函数基与高效数值实现，并集成到 \texttt{PentagonFunctions++} 库中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在 LHC 上开展 $t\bar{t}H$ 产生的 NNLO 研究，需要 $pp\to t\bar{t}H$（含闭合轻夸克圈）领头色振幅所涉及的平面两圈主积分的可靠计算方法。

Method: 对超超越权重（transcendental weight）一、二者以对数与双对数（dilogarithms）给出解析结果，更高权重以一重积分表示；重点研究嵌套根式（nested roots）在物理区域的解析结构，构造含根式字母（letters）与 $\mathrm{d}\log$ 形式的数值稳定表示，并对无法用单一对数原函数表示的 $\mathrm{d}\log$ 形式采用自适应 Chebyshev 逼近（adaptive Chebyshev approximation）进行数值处理；以 \texttt{C++} 扩展 \texttt{PentagonFunctions++} 实现。

Result: 发现部分 $\mathrm{d}\log$ 形式的原函数在物理区域无法用单一对数表示；在物理相空间点上测试表明，该实现的求值精度与速度均满足唯象应用需求。

Conclusion: 该工作为 LHC 上 $t\bar{t}H$ 产生的 NNLO 研究提供了重要构件（building block）。

Abstract: We present a basis of special functions and an efficient numerical implementation for the planar two-loop master integrals that enter the leading-colour amplitudes for $pp\to t\bar{t}H$ with a closed light-quark loop. We provide analytic results for the special functions at transcendental weight one and two in terms of logarithms and dilogarithms, while higher weights are evaluated as one-fold integrals. We focus on the nested roots that arise, studying their analytic structure on the physical region and constructing numerically stable representations of letters and $\mathrm{d}\log$ forms that involve them. We find that a number of $\mathrm{d}\log$ forms have an antiderivative that cannot be represented by a single logarithm over the physical region and tackle them numerically with adaptive Chebyshev approximation. We implement the functions in \texttt{C++} as an extension of the \texttt{PentagonFunctions++} library. We study the performance of our implementation over physical phase-space points and find that the evaluation is accurate and fast enough for phenomenological application. This provides an important building block for NNLO studies of $t\bar{t}H$ production at the LHC.

</details>


### [189] [Quality time in extra dimensions: axion quality and radion cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12765)
*Falguni Biswas,Yeshan Juberi,Ahmed Rakin Kamal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文在五维圆紧致化中研究由阿贝尔规范场产生的轴子及动力学半径（radion）的稳定机制，并评估残余强CP破坏、radion衰变与重加热对轴子暗物质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高形式对称性（higher-form symmetries）虽可保护额外维轴子，但额外对称性破缺仍可能破坏强CP问题（strong CP problem）的解，因此需考察带电物质与磁规范化（magnetic gauging）带来的残余破缺及其宇宙学后果。

Method: 采用卡西米尔能量（Casimir energy）稳定半径/radion，分含与不含绕紧致圆的周期标量缠绕两种机制；将磁规范化的额外自由度纳入卡西米尔谱，计算残余强CP角、radion衰变通道与重加热，并分析radion衰变熵注入对轴子丰度的稀释。

Result: 两种机制均存在闵可夫斯基（Minkowski）与德西特（de Sitter）极小值；在Minkowski极小值处radion质量约100–170 TeV，带电物质诱导的残余强CP角量级为10^-17且半径位移可忽略，磁规范化在耦合足够小时仍相容。半径增大抑制带电物质破缺而增强磁破缺贡献。对ξ=7，可见区重加热温度约85 MeV，暗辐射贡献ΔN_eff≈0.02。

Conclusion: radion衰变释放的熵可稀释轴子丰度，使f_a=10^14 GeV、初始错位角ϑ_i≈0.43的轴子可解释全部观测暗物质；若在辐射主导（radiation domination）时期产生同样丰度，则需ϑ_i≈0.065。

Abstract: Higher-form symmetries can protect extra-dimensional axions, but additional symmetry breaking can still spoil the solution to the strong CP problem. We focus on electrically charged matter and magnetic gauging in a five-dimensional circle compactification where the axion arises from an Abelian gauge field and the radius is dynamical. We discuss two mechanisms for stabilising the radius/radion using Casimir energy from fields neutral under the Abelian gauge field, with and without an additional periodic scalar winding around the compact circle. Both admit Minkowski and de Sitter minima. At the Minkowski minima (with radion masses of $100$--$170\,\mathrm{TeV}$), electrically charged matter induces a residual strong CP angle of order $10^{-17}$ and shifts the radius negligibly. Magnetic gauging remains compatible after including its additional degree of freedom in the Casimir spectrum, provided its coupling is sufficiently small. We observe that increasing the radius suppresses charged-matter breaking relative to QCD but increases magnetic breaking contribution. We also determine the radion decay channels, including the graviphoton, and study reheating from a possible radion-dominated era without scalar winding or magnetic gauging. For a non-minimal Higgs-curvature coupling $ξ=7$, the visible-sector reheating temperature is about $85\,\mathrm{MeV}$ with a dark-radiation contribution of $ΔN_{\rm eff}\approx0.02$. The entropy released during radion decay dilutes the axion abundance, allowing axions with $f_a=10^{14}\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and an initial misalignment angle $\vartheta_i\approx0.43$ to account for the entire observed dark matter abundance. For the same decay constant, reproducing this abundance in radiation domination requires $\vartheta_i\approx0.065$.

</details>

### [190] [Test beam results for machine-learning-based reconstruction of resistive silicon sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10194)
*Don C. Wong,Leena Diehl,Spencer Ellis,Marcos Fernández García,Gabriele Giacomini,Vagelis Gkougkousis,Ben Kilminster,Jingyu Luo,Anna Macchiolo,Yajna Mahabeer,Yevhenii Padniuk,Daria Senina,Samantha Sunnarborg,Jessica Tang,Alessandro Tricoli,Lixing Wang,Gaetano Barone*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文在CERN SPS的120 GeV π介子束流（pion beam）条件下，配合MIMOSA-26望远镜，对500 μm像素间距（pitch）的AC-LGAD进行全波形（full-waveform）位置重建，并比较三种估计器：无参数电荷失衡公式（charge-imbalance formula）、线性矩阵反演（linear matrix inversion）与拓扑无关的transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低增益雪崩二极管（LGAD）与交流耦合低增益雪崩二极管（AC-LGAD）将部分重建负担转移到传感器模拟波形上，电极间电荷共享（charge sharing）可在远小于电极间距（pitch）的尺度上编码入射位置；恢复该位置是一个连续、非线性反问题。瞬态电流技术（TCT）测量仅在固定注入电荷与高信噪比下刻画该问题，而最小电离粒子（MIP）束流会引入朗道涨落（Landau fluctuations），更接近探测器实际工作条件。

Method: 对2×2 pad矩阵读出的500 μm间距AC-LGAD在120 GeV π介子束流下进行全波形重建，并配以六层MIMOSA-26望远镜进行径迹参考；望远镜经对准并在原位校准各层分辨率（per-plane resolutions），在器件处给出约2.5 μm每轴的径迹指向分辨率，使测量残差反映传感器响应而非与参考的卷积。随后比较无参数电荷失衡公式、拟合式线性矩阵反演，以及为阻性硅传感器（resistive silicon sensors）开发的拓扑无关transformer。

Result: 在整个有源pad范围内，transformer取得最佳中位总位置精度，相对其他方法提升达3倍；在电荷共享核心区（charge-sharing core），在已达到的信噪比下三种方法收敛至该传感器的性能下限（floor）。

Conclusion: 在束流条件下，基于全波形的机器学习重建（transformer）在整体有源区域优于解析估计器，而在电荷共享核心区解析方法已足够、三者受限于传感器本身的信噪比；重建精度上限由传感器响应与信噪比共同决定。

Abstract: The advent of Low-Gain Avalanche Diodes (LGADs) and AC-coupled Low-Gain Avalanche Diodes (AC-LGADs) as candidate technologies for four-dimensional tracking has moved part of the reconstruction burden onto the analog waveforms of the sensor, in which the charge shared between electrodes encodes the impact position well below the electrode pitch. Recovering that position is a continuous, non-linear inverse problem. Transient Current Technique measurements characterize this problem at fixed injected charge and high signal-to-noise, whereas a minimum-ionizing beam adds Landau fluctuations in the deposited charge under detector operating conditions. We report test beam results for full-waveform reconstruction of an AC-LGAD of 500 $μ$m pitch read out on a $2\times2$ pad matrix, exposed to the 120 GeV pion beam of the CERN Super Proton Synchrotron (SPS) and read out alongside a six-plane MIMOSA-26 telescope. The telescope is aligned, and its per-plane resolutions are calibrated in situ, giving a track pointing resolution of about 2.5 $μ$m per axis at the device, so that the measured residual is the sensor response rather than a convolution with the reference. We then compare two analytic estimators, the parameter-free charge-imbalance formula and a fitted linear matrix inversion, with the topology-agnostic transformer developed for resistive silicon sensors. Over the full active pad, the transformer reaches a median total position with up to factor 3 improvement. Within the charge-sharing core, the three methods converge to the floor of this sensor at the achieved signal-to-noise.

</details>


### [191] [First demonstration of long-distance electron drift in liquid xenon with the Xenoscope time projection chamber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12808)
*N. Angelides,M. Babicz,E. E. Barillier,L. Baudis,H. Birch,C. Capelli,J. J. Cuenca-García,S. George,R. N. Hampp,M. A. Hernandez,L. Hötzsch,F. Jörg,K. Morå,M. Noia,S. Ouahada,B. Penning,F. Piastra*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: Xenoscope 双相氙时间投影室在液氙中实现 2.6 m 电子漂移的实测演示，并测得两个电场下的电子漂移速度，为下一代 XLZD 大型液氙探测器提供实验基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向下一代 XLZD（XENON-LUX-ZEPLIN-DARWIN）天文台，需要在与未来探测器相当的长距离上验证液氙中的电子输运（electron transport）可行性。现有实验缺乏米级漂移距离下的电荷输运实测基准。

Method: 利用苏黎世大学运行的 Xenoscope 双相氙时间投影室（dual-phase xenon time projection chamber），借助宇宙 μ 子（cosmic muon）诱导的电离信号；通过外部 μ 子标记（muon tagging）为已知深度处产生的电荷提供时间参考，并以平均电致发光（electroluminescence）波形的终点探测全漂移长度上的输运。

Result: 在液氙中演示了 2.6 m 的电子漂移（electron drift）；测得电子漂移速度（drift velocity）为 (1.320 ± 0.006) mm/μs（80.92 ± 0.36 V/cm）与 (1.436 ± 0.001) mm/μs（96.00 ± 0.42 V/cm）。

Conclusion: 结果确立了米级距离上可探测的电荷输运，并为未来大型液氙时间投影室的研发提供实验基准（experimental benchmark）。

Abstract: Xenoscope is a dual-phase xenon time projection chamber operated at the University of Zurich to investigate electron transport over distances relevant to the next-generation XLZD (XENON-LUX-ZEPLIN-DARWIN) observatory. We demonstrate electron drift over $2.6$ m in liquid xenon using cosmic-muon-induced ionization signals. External muon tagging provides a timing reference for charge produced at known depths, while the endpoint of the average electroluminescence waveform probes transport over the full drift length. We measure electron drift velocities of $(1.320 \pm 0.006)$ mm/$μ$s at $(80.92 \pm 0.36)$ V/cm and $(1.436 \pm 0.001)$ mm/$μ$s at $(96.00 \pm 0.42)$ V/cm. These results establish detectable charge transport over meter-scale distances and provide an experimental benchmark for the development of future large liquid-xenon time projection chambers.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [A Free-Field Realization of Boundary Symmetries in Discrete Jackiw--Teitelboim Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10074)
*H. T. Özer,Aytül Filiz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在有限周期（finite-periodic）格点上构造了与 sl(2,R) Jackiw–Teitelboim（JT）引力相关的经典 Virasoro–dilaton 扇区的 Miura 自由场实现，通过离散 Miura 映射导出格点自旋二流与复合 dilaton 场，并显式计算了诱导的混合 Poisson 括号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为 JT 引力的边界变换规律提供自由场对应物，探索能否在不额外假设独立 dilaton 括号的前提下，由单一对数 Cartan 场统一实现 Virasoro–dilaton 结构。

Method: 从单个对数 Cartan 场出发，利用已有离散 Miura 映射（discrete Miura map）及其自由场 Poisson 括号构造格点自旋二流；dilaton 由同一 Cartan 变量的周期复合实现，由非齐次差分方程及其单值性条件（monodromy condition）选取；随后考察相关的转移问题与二次伴随不变量，并用有限 Fourier 代数检验连续 Virasoro–dilaton 变换（其中 dilaton 共形权重为 −1）。

Result: 该构造给出显式的诱导混合 Poisson 括号（mixed Poisson bracket），无需假设独立的 dilaton 括号；有限 Fourier 代数与 dilaton 权重为 −1 的连续 Virasoro–dilaton 变换进行了对照检验。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了 JT 边界变换规律的自由场对应实现，但本身并未将 Miura Poisson 结构与正则 BF 边界荷代数（canonical BF boundary-charge algebra）等同起来。

Abstract: We investigate a finite-periodic Miura free-field realization of the classical Virasoro--dilaton sector relevant to sl(2,R) Jackiw--Teitelboim (JT) gravity. Starting from a single logarithmic Cartan field, we use the established discrete Miura map and its free-field Poisson bracket to construct the lattice spin-two current. The dilaton is then realized as a periodic composite of the same Cartan variables, selected by an inhomogeneous difference equation and its monodromy condition. This construction yields an explicit induced mixed Poisson bracket without postulating an independent dilaton bracket. We examine the associated transfer problem and quadratic adjoint invariant, and test the finite Fourier algebra against the continuous Virasoro--dilaton transformation, in which the dilaton has conformal weight minus one. The construction supplies a free-field counterpart of the JT boundary transformation laws; it does not by itself identify the Miura Poisson structure with the canonical BF boundary-charge algebra.

</details>


### [193] [Baby Universes Nucleate by Hiding Observers from the Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10112)
*Sergio E. Aguilar-Gutierrez,Takato Mori*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究两个由四参数相互作用耦合的双标度Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（double-scaled SYK）模型，在含观测者（以粒子表示）的态中，虫洞（wormhole）先单向增长，继而经罕见哈密顿量弦（Hamiltonian strings）乘积抵消粒子弦，形成携带观测者的闭合婴儿宇宙（baby universe），空虫洞恢复有限长度的离散谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 描述闭合宇宙（closed universes）时观测者被认为不可或缺，但在常规全息理论（holographic theory）框架内刻画观测者仍具挑战性，本文旨在为此提供可计算的模型设定。

Method: 构建两个双标度Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev模型，以含广义Maldacena-Qi耦合（generalized Maldacena-Qi coupling）的四参数相互作用相连，并在含粒子型观测者的态中分析其动力学；以Krylov复杂度（Krylov complexity）度量虫洞长度，以Holevo信息刻画边界对观测者微观态的可知性。

Result: 重粒子存在时虫洞只能增长；罕见哈密顿量弦乘积抵消粒子弦后，婴儿宇宙携带观测者离开，空虫洞恢复有限长度的离散态。该过程为一阶相变（first-order transition），潜热等于相变温度乘以观测者熵，由空虫洞获得的能量支付；Krylov复杂度在相变处下降，边界亦丧失关于观测者微观态的Holevo信息。

Conclusion: Krylov复杂度的下降可在边界上探测婴儿宇宙（其边界描述仍有争议）的出现，表明观测者熵、虫洞几何与边界信息量之间存在定量联系。

Abstract: Observers are expected to be essential to describe closed universes, but their description within a conventional holographic theory remains challenging. We study two double-scaled Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev models coupled by a four-parameter interaction, which includes a generalized Maldacena-Qi coupling, in a state that contains an observer, represented as a particle. With a heavy particle inside, the wormhole connecting the two systems can only grow. Rare products of Hamiltonian strings cancel the string of the particle: a closed baby universe then carries the observer, and the wormhole, now empty, recovers discrete states of finite length. The energy gained by the empty wormhole pays for the entropy of the observer, in a first-order transition whose latent heat is the transition temperature times this entropy. Krylov complexity, which measures the wormhole length, drops at the transition and thus detects from the boundary the emergence of the baby universe, whose boundary description is actively debated. The boundary also loses the Holevo information about the microstates of the observer, which the baby universe carries.

</details>


### [194] [Small Defects at Large Charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10113)
*Jesse Woods*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在半经典大电荷极限下，计算了含杂质尖点线缺陷（cusped line defects with impurities）在阿贝尔希格斯模型（abelian Higgs model）中的共形数据（conformal data），包括杂质标度维数至 O(q^3) 的全程耦合重求和、临界规范耦合附近的奇异行为重求和，以及轫致辐射函数（Bremsstrahlung function）等物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统获取含杂质尖点线缺陷的共形数据，并检验其与已知微扰结果、数值解及近期尖点（cusps）界限的一致性，同时探讨大快度极限（large rapidity limit）与半经典近似（semiclassics）之间的相互作用。

Method: 采用半经典大电荷极限，并以杂质电荷 q 作微扰展开；在 't Hooft 四次耦合（'t Hooft quartic coupling）与规范耦合（gauge coupling）的所有阶上计算，每一项对应无穷多费曼图（Feynman diagrams）的重求和（resummation）；在共形性丧失的临界规范耦合附近，将主导奇异行为对 q 全阶重求和。

Result: 得到杂质标度维数（scaling dimension）至 O(q^3)；给出 O(q) 阶激发尖点态（excited cusp states）谱的评述；计算轫致辐射函数的主导贡献及聚变极限（fusion limit）中主导无关算符（leading irrelevant operator）的系数，并论证 Casimir 能量在半经典层面无修正；结果与已知微扰数据和数值解一致，并与近期尖点界限进行了比较。

Conclusion: 半经典大电荷框架可自洽地给出含杂质尖点线缺陷的共形数据，且与既有微扰与数值结果相符；临界耦合附近的奇异重求和拓展了微扰适用范围，并为理解大快度极限与半经典近似的相互关系提供了依据。

Abstract: We compute conformal data of cusped line defects with impurities for the abelian Higgs model in the semiclassical large-charge limit. By working perturbatively in the impurity charge $q$, we obtain the scaling dimension of the impurity to $\mathcal{O}(q^3)$ to all orders in the 't Hooft quartic and gauge couplings. Each term represents a resummation of infinitely many Feynman diagrams. Near the critical gauge coupling where conformality is lost, we resum the leading singular behaviour to all orders in $q$. We also comment on the spectrum of excited cusp states at $\mathcal{O}(q)$. Additionally, we compute the leading contributions to the Bremsstrahlung function, the coefficient of the leading irrelevant operator in the fusion limit, and argue that the Casimir energy receives no corrections semiclassically. Our results agree with known perturbative data and numerical solutions. We compare them with recent bounds on cusps. We comment on the interplay between the large rapidity limit and semiclassics.

</details>


### [195] [Fast, training-free simplification of spinor-helicity amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10115)
*Yifan Fei,Zhen Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练（training-free）方法，通过在壳恒等式（on-shell identities）上的贪心最佳优先搜索（greedy best-first search）化简无质量旋量-螺旋度（spinor-helicity）表达式，在四、五、六点情形测试中高效求解并优于既有机器学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无质量旋量-螺旋度表达式（尤其来自费曼图（Feynman diagram）计算或恒等式混淆的长表达式）冗长且难以直接提取物理信息，需要一种无需训练的自动化化简手段，并作为从振幅（amplitude）中提取物理的初步探索。

Method: 在在壳恒等式上执行贪心最佳优先搜索，对候选表达式进行排序与搜索；方法无需训练。以比色序五胶子 MHV 树级振幅（color-ordered five-gluon MHV tree-level amplitude）为典型算例，并采用有限域（finite fields）在在壳运动学下独立校验结果。

Result: 从全部 1024 个费曼图表达式（含不同极化参考旋量、分子项多达 228 项）推导出 Parke-Taylor 公式；在先前机器学习方法使用的公开测试集上全部求解且求解率总体更优，单 CPU 核平均耗时 0.02-0.3 秒；延长搜索并改用候选排序后，亦求解新构建的 21,600 个更大测试表达式。

Conclusion: 对本文研究的表达式类别，该方法可将恒等式混淆或费曼图计算产生的长表达式有效压缩为紧凑形式，每个结果均附带显式推导并可独立校验；这是通过关联不同紧凑表达式、筛选具偏好因子与极点的表示、并引导发现新振幅结构来从振幅中提取物理的第一步。

Abstract: We present a training-free method that simplifies massless spinor-helicity expressions by a greedy best-first search over on-shell identities, and test it at four, five, and six points. Applied to the color-ordered five-gluon MHV tree-level amplitude, the search derives the Parke-Taylor formula from all 1024 Feynman-diagram expressions with different polarization reference spinors and up to 228 numerator terms. On public test sets used by previous machine-learning methods, the search solves every case and generally outperforms these methods in solve rate. Its mean wall-clock time per case is 0.02-0.3 s on one CPU core. With longer searches and an alternative ranking of candidate expressions, it also solves all 21,600 cases of a new suite of larger test expressions. Each result comes with an explicit derivation and is independently checked over finite fields at on-shell kinematics. For the classes studied here, the method effectively compresses long expressions produced by identity scrambling or Feynman-diagram calculations into compact forms. This is a first step toward extracting physics from amplitudes by relating distinct compact expressions, selecting representations with preferred factors and poles, and guiding the discovery of new amplitude structures.

</details>


### [196] [Scale-separated quasi-dS and the Swampland](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10116)
*Rafael Álvarez-García,Ivano Basile,Shi Chen,Cumrun Vafa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 M 理论紧化中检验 Swampland 猜想（dS 猜想与 TCC）关于弱耦合区无法实现尺度分离准 de Sitter 真空的预期，结论支持该预期：在 EFT 控制下不存在满足条件的 de Sitter 临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Swampland 纲领（Swampland program）中的 de Sitter 猜想（dS conjecture）与弱引力猜想变体 TCC（Trans-Planckian Censorship Conjecture）暗示，弦论/M 理论难以在弱耦合区给出描述当前宇宙的 V>0、‖∇V‖/V < 2/√(d−2) 且 V ≪ m² 的尺度分离“准 de Sitter（quasi-dS）”解。本文旨在检验该预期。

Method: 在 M 理论紧化（M-theory compactifications）框架下，针对双曲流形（hyperbolic manifolds）与（刚性）Riemann 平坦流形（Riemann-flat manifolds），在包含 Casimir 能量（Casimir energy）量子效应的情况下分析标量势的斜率；并在超引力（supergravity）一环水平（one-loop level）做参数化讨论，以标量场 Casimir 能量作为代理量给出显式数值界限。

Result: 即使纳入 Casimir 能量，EFT 控制下仍排除 de Sitter 临界点（de Sitter critical points）：势的斜率无法低于 TCC 预言的渐近值 ‖∇V‖/V ≳ 2/√(d−2)，除非在模空间（moduli space）体内部某个 order-one 区域，而该区域 EFT 控制失效。参数化层面该结论在一环超引力水平成立，并给出控制程度的量级估计。

Conclusion: 结果支持 Swampland 关于弱耦合区不存在尺度分离准 de Sitter 真空的预期，表明 EFT 可控性与满足 TCC 斜率界之间存在张力；Casimir 能量作为代理量所提供的数值界限可用于估计此类情形的可控性量级。

Abstract: Ideas from the Swampland program (dS conjecture and TCC) suggest that it is impossible to obtain solutions of string theory/M-theory in the weak-coupling regions describing our current universe, with $V>0$, $\|\nabla V\|/V < 2/\sqrt{d-2}$ and $V \ll m^2$ (scale-separated "quasi-dS") where $m$ is the mass of the light tower. We test this expectation in M-theory compactifications and find that even after including the quantum effect of Casimir energy, for hyperbolic and (rigid) Riemann-flat manifolds, de Sitter critical points under EFT control are excluded: the slope of the potential cannot drop below the asymptotic value predicted by the TCC $\|\nabla V\|/V\gtrsim 2/\sqrt{d-2}$ except perhaps in an order-one region in the bulk of moduli space, where EFT control is lost. At the parametric level, our results hold at one-loop level in supergravity; using the scalar-field Casimir energy as a proxy, we also obtain explicit numerical bounds to provide a ballpark estimate of how much control one can expect to achieve in such settings.

</details>


### [197] [Irregularization of punctures: From $T_N$ to Argyres--Douglas via $\mathcal{N}=1$ flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10118)
*Ki-Hong Lee,Shlomo S. Razamat,Jaewon Song,Thomas Waddleton*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了 $D_p(\mathfrak{g})$ Argyres--Douglas 理论与带正则穿刺的 6d $(2,0)$ 球面紧致化之间的联系，并给出 $D_p(\mathfrak{su}_3)$ 的新拉格朗日构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在直接将两类 $4d$ 强耦合超共形场论（SCFT）构造联系起来：一类由带非正则穿刺（irregular punctures）的球面紧致化给出，另一类由带正则穿刺（regular punctures）的球面紧致化给出。并借此为 Argyres--Douglas 理论的 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 构造提供几何解释。

Method: 从 $\mathfrak{g}$ 型 6d $(2,0)$ 理论在带两个极大穿刺的球面上的 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 紧致化出发（通量线性依赖于 $p$），通过对其中一个极大穿刺对称性做规范化（gauging），可能附加额外物质扇区，从而得到 $\mathcal{N}=2$ 的 $D_p(\mathfrak{g})$ 理论；其中部分 $\mathcal{N}=2$ R-对称性来自双穿刺球面的 R-对称性与 $\mathfrak{u}_1$ 几何等距对称性。

Result: 该构造给出了部分 Argyres--Douglas 理论 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 构造的几何解释，并为 $D_p(\mathfrak{su}_3)$ 理论提供了显式的新拉格朗日构造（一般涉及对涌现对称性（emergent symmetries）的规范化）。

Conclusion: 寻找 $D_p(\mathfrak{g})$ Argyres--Douglas 理论（及其通过 Higgs 分支重整化群流（Higgs branch RG flow）相连的大类理论）的拉格朗日表述问题，可约化为寻找 $T_{\mathfrak{g}}$ 理论拉格朗日表述的问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we directly relate constructions of two large classes of $4d$ strongly coupled SCFTs: 1) compactifications of type $\mathfrak{g}$ $6d$ $(2,0)$ theories on spheres with irregular punctures leading to the $D_p(\mathfrak{g})$ Argyres--Douglas theories, and 2) the compactifications of the same $6d$ $(2,0)$ theories on spheres with regular punctures. In particular, we show that the $\mathcal{N}=2$ $D_p(\mathfrak{g})$ Argyres--Douglas theories can be obtained starting from $\mathcal{N}=1$ compactifications of 6d $(2,0)$ theories of type $\mathfrak{g}$ on a sphere with two maximal punctures with flux linearly dependent on $p$ by gauging one of the maximal puncture symmetries, possibly with additional matter sectors. Importantly, part of the $\mathcal{N}=2$ R-symmetry of $D_p(\mathfrak{g})$ theories arises from the R-symmetry of the two-punctured sphere and the $\mathfrak{u}_1$ geometric isometry symmetry. This construction gives in particular a geometric explanation of some of the $\mathcal{N}=1$ constructions of Argyres--Douglas theories, and also provides explicit new Lagrangian constructions (involving gauging of emergent symmetries in general) for $D_p({\mathfrak{su}_3})$ theories. More generally, we relate the problem of finding a Lagrangian for $D_p(\mathfrak{g})$ Argyres--Douglas theories (and thereby a large class of theories that are connected by Higgs branch RG flow) to the problem of finding Lagrangians for the $T_{\mathfrak{g}}$ theories.

</details>


### [198] [Integrable AdS$_2$ branes in mixed flux AdS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10120)
*Davide Bonomi,Volker Schomerus*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明，在 AdS₃×S³×T⁴ 中混合 NSNS 与 RR 通量背景下包裹 AdS₂×S² 的半 BPS D3-膜，为 Green–Schwarz 超弦的超陪集 sigma 模型提供了可积边界条件；通过构造依赖场的反射矩阵并得到双行单值矩阵，证明任意通量混合下开弦的经典可积性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有可积 D-膜（integrable D-branes）结果（Dekel–Oz）未覆盖含 Wess–Zumino 项的背景，而混合通量情形此前仅有玻色子层面的结论；因此需要把可积边界条件推广到完整超弦。

Method: 利用 D3-膜作用量确定稳定膜位形的电、磁世界体（worldvolume）场，发现其可由纯 NSNS 背景下 WZW 模型 AdS₂×S² 膜的结果经同一 SL(2,ℝ) 旋转（在 D3-膜上表现为电磁对偶（electric–magnetic duality））得到；在 worldsheet 上引入特定闭二形式以抵消费米边界项，并构造依赖场的反射矩阵，将其提升为 Cagnazzo–Zarembo Lax 联络（Lax connection）的粘合条件（gluing conditions）。

Result: 费米边界项抵消所需的闭二形式对玻色边界条件的贡献精确重现了上述 worldvolume 场；反射矩阵描述沿垂直于膜的测地线（geodesics）的位移，所得双行单值矩阵（double-row monodromy matrix）产生无穷多守恒荷。

Conclusion: 对任意通量混合，开超弦的经典可积性（classical integrability）得以确立；在 AdS₃/CFT₂ 对应中，这些膜预期对偶于 T⁴ 对称积轨道流形（symmetric product orbifold）形变中的共形界面（conformal interfaces）。

Abstract: We show that half-BPS D3-branes wrapping AdS$_2\times$S$^2$ in AdS$_3\times$S$^3\times$T$^4$ with mixed NSNS and RR flux define integrable boundary conditions for the supercoset sigma model of the Green--Schwarz superstring. This extends the integrable D-branes of Dekel and Oz to backgrounds with a Wess--Zumino term, and previous bosonic results for mixed flux to the full superstring. Using the D3-brane action, we find that the electric and magnetic worldvolume fields that stabilise the branes at generic positions are obtained from those of the AdS$_2\times$S$^2$ branes of the WZW model by the same $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ rotation that maps the pure NSNS background to the mixed flux one, acting on the D3-brane as electric--magnetic duality. On the worldsheet, cancelling the fermionic boundary terms requires introducing a specific closed two-form in the boundary action, and its contribution to the bosonic boundary conditions reproduces precisely these worldvolume fields. We then construct field-dependent reflection matrices that lift the boundary conditions to gluing conditions for the Cagnazzo--Zarembo Lax connection and describe displacements along geodesics normal to the brane. The resulting double-row monodromy matrix generates infinitely many conserved charges, which establishes the classical integrability of the open superstring for any mixture of fluxes. In AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$, these branes are expected to be dual to conformal interfaces in a deformation of the symmetric product orbifold of T$^4$.

</details>


### [199] [Emergent de Sitter Universes in Non-Hermitian Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10121)
*Daniel Areán,Suman Das,David Garcia-Fariña,Juan F. Pedraza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一类渐近反德西特（AdS）正则黑洞，其内部平滑过渡为无奇点、膨胀的德西特（dS）宇宙，并借助非厄米全息（non-Hermitian holography）为统一AdS/CFT、dS/CFT与静态补丁全息提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏在成熟AdS/CFT框架内系统探讨dS全息（含未来边界与静态补丁描述）的具体构造，作者希望建立一个可计算的“全息实验室”以连接三种互补的全息视角。

Method: 基于非厄米全息（non-Hermitian holography），利用零能量条件（null energy condition）的违反使内部几何发生平滑反弹并趋近晚期dS吸引子；构造在单一事件视界（event horizon）之后动态涌现宇宙，并延伸至类空未来边界（spacelike future boundary）$\mathcal I^+$，无需引入辅助膜（branes）或拼接不同几何。

Result: 所构造的黑洞内部演化为无奇点、膨胀的dS宇宙；锚定在AdS边界的可观测量（observables）与dS全息的互补概念（边界非厄米CFT、$\mathcal I^+$处的欧几里得CFT及以观测者为中心的静态补丁全息）之间展现出具体联系。

Conclusion: 该工作为在AdS/CFT既定框架内系统研究dS量子引力的未来边界描述与静态补丁描述如何涌现提供了新的全息平台与理论工具。

Abstract: We construct a class of asymptotically anti-de Sitter (AdS), regular black holes whose interiors evolve into nonsingular, expanding de Sitter (dS) cosmologies. Our construction relies on non-Hermitian holography, where violations of the null energy condition allow the interior geometry to undergo a smooth bounce and approach a late-time dS attractor. The cosmology emerges dynamically behind a single event horizon, extending to a spacelike future boundary, $\mathcal I^+$, without introducing auxiliary branes or gluing distinct geometries. This provides a novel setting to explore the connections between three complementary holographic perspectives: a non-Hermitian conformal field theory (CFT) at the AdS boundaries, a putative Euclidean CFT at $\mathcal I^+$ (dS/CFT), and observer-centered static-patch holography. Remarkably, observables anchored at the AdS boundaries reveal concrete connections with these complementary notions of dS holography. Our construction thus offers a new holographic laboratory for systematically exploring how future-boundary and static-patch descriptions of dS quantum gravity may emerge within the established setting of AdS/CFT.

</details>


### [200] [Holographic warped CFTs on AdS$_3$, parity violation and unitarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10126)
*Kristiansen Lara,Ricardo Troncoso*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究AdS₃广义相对论在warped共形代数边界条件下、引入宇称奇（parity-odd）项后的正则生成元代数与全息性质，发现中心扩展发生移动，熵与幺正性要求将参数逼至临界点并指向手征点拓扑大质量引力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期提出的AdS₃边界条件使正则生成元构成warped共形代数（warped conformal algebra）。本文旨在考察在爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量中加入不改变真空场方程的宇称奇项后，该代数结构与全息自洽性（幺正性、黑洞熵非负）会受到何种影响。

Method: 在既有边界条件下计算含宇称奇项时的正则生成元及其代数，分析非线性贡献与中心扩展（central extensions）的移动；随后结合Kac-Moody能级（Kac-Moody level）符号约束，讨论幺正表示与黑洞熵非负条件，并在退化情形下施加零挠率（vanishing torsion）约束。

Result: 生成元代数仍为Virasoro与u(1) Kac-Moody的半直和（semidirect sum），但中心扩展被移动；适当选取宇称奇耦合可使 k_KМ ≥ 0 从而允许幺正表示，而熵非负要求 k_KМ ≤ 0，两者兼容只剩 k_KМ = 0。该情形下场方程退化，施加零挠率约束后理论恰为手征点（chiral point）的拓扑大质量引力（Topologically Massive Gravity, TMG）。

Conclusion: 宇称奇项通过移动中心扩展影响warped CFT的幺正性与热力学自洽性，二者的联合约束唯一挑出 k_KМ = 0 的临界情形，其等价于手征点TMG，提示需将边界条件推广以容纳非平凡对数分支（logarithmic branches）。

Abstract: We consider recently introduced boundary conditions for General Relativity on AdS$_3$, whose canonical generators span the warped conformal algebra, in the case of the Einstein-Hilbert action supplemented by parity-odd terms that do not modify the field equations in vacuum. The canonical generators are shown to acquire nonlinear contributions, and their algebra remains being the semidirect sum of Virasoro and $u(1)$ Kac-Moody, but with shifted central extensions. Thus, for suitable values of the parity-odd coupling, the Kac-Moody level can be nonnegative ($k_\text{KM}\geq 0$), admitting unitary representations.
  Nevertheless, requiring the black hole entropy to be nonnegative, implies $k_\text{KM}\leq 0$, in agreement with the established understanding of holographic warped CFTs. Compatibility of both requirements (entropy and unitarity) then singles out the case $k_\text{KM}=0$. However, the field equations degenerate in this case, which can be amended by imposing vanishing torsion as a constraint. The theory then precisely becomes Topologically Massive Gravity (TMG) at the chiral point, suggesting the extension of our boundary conditions to accommodate nontrivial logarithmic branches.

</details>


### [201] [The Ghost and Its Secret Partner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10128)
*Luca Buoninfante*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该研究表明，鬼场传播子在多粒子阈值之上出现一对第一叶复共轭极点，揭示存在一个渐近耦合的隐藏复合场（“鬼场的秘密伙伴”），导致负范数单粒子态与正范数复合粒子态之间持续干涉，从而使自旋二鬼场无法作为孤立渐近激发出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有不定范数矢量空间（indefinite-norm vector space）的理论中，负范数鬼态通常被要求不出现为渐近态，相关处理（如替代振幅处方或超选择规则）多为人为设定或特设引入，缺乏动力学依据。本文旨在为这类做法寻找动力学基础。

Method: 从鬼场传播子（propagator）的极点结构入手，分析其在多粒子阈值（multi-particle threshold）之上出现的第一叶复共轭极点（first-sheet complex conjugate poles），并据此论证隐藏渐近复合场（asymptotic composite field）的存在及其与鬼场的渐近耦合；具体在四导数理论（four-derivative theories）特别是二次引力（quadratic gravity）中展开分析。

Result: 鬼场传播子的极点结构显示存在一个渐近仍与鬼场耦合的隐藏复合场，由此在负范数单粒子态与正范数复合粒子态之间产生持续干涉；在二次引力中，量子动力学阻止自旋二鬼场（spin-two ghost）表现为孤立的渐近激发。

Conclusion: 研究结果尚未排除可观测的负概率（negative probabilities），但可为不定范数理论中通常被特设选择或施加的替代振幅处方或超选择规则（superselection rules）提供动力学基础。

Abstract: The propagator of a ghost field acquires a pair of first-sheet complex conjugate poles above the multi-particle threshold. We show that this pole structure reveals the existence of a hidden asymptotic composite field -- the ghost's secret partner -- that remains coupled to the ghost asymptotically. This coupling induces persistent interference between the negative-norm one-particle state and the positive-norm composite-particle state. We analyze this phenomenon in four-derivative theories, in particular quadratic gravity, where the quantum dynamics prevents the spin-two ghost from appearing as an isolated asymptotic excitation. Although our results do not yet rule out observable negative probabilities, they may provide a dynamical basis for alternative amplitude prescriptions or superselection rules usually chosen or imposed ad hoc in theories with indefinite-norm vector spaces.

</details>


### [202] [Large-D approach to nearly critical holographic matter and Yang-Mills deconfinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10203)
*Matti Järvinen,Leonardus B. Putra,Dorin Weissman*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在五维爱因斯坦-膨胀子（Einstein-dilaton）理论中构建动力学黑洞解的系统性展开，该展开由纯引力的大D极限（large-D limit）经维度约化（dimensional reduction）所启发；领头阶给出四维非相对论流体力学方程，并用于分析相共存畴壁与QCD禁闭-去禁闭相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机来自全息对偶（holographic duality）下近临界强耦合四维场论对应的动力学黑洞，并借助纯引力大D极限的维度约化获得系统性展开框架，以推广黑洞膜（black branes）的大D有效理论。

Method: 采用系统性展开求解五维爱因斯坦-膨胀子方程；在领头非平凡阶，爱因斯坦方程约化为封闭的四维非相对论流体力学方程组；结合黑洞热力学（black hole thermodynamics）与全息字典（holographic dictionary）导出热力学与流体力学，并分析平面、柱面、球面对称的畴壁（domain walls）及具有非零动量流的非平衡稳态壁，同时用全息流（holographic flow）将近视界几何补全至渐近AdS5区域。

Result: 由近边界几何提取的能量-动量张量（energy-momentum tensor）在有效方程成立时严格守恒；给出了各类畴壁（含非平衡稳态壁）的分析；并在改进全息QCD（improved holographic QCD）中构造禁闭与去禁闭相之间的畴壁，计算其表面张力（surface tension）并与近期格点（lattice）结果比较。

Conclusion: 该展开为近临界强耦合场论对应的动力学黑洞提供了一个自洽的有效流体力学描述，其守恒律与全息字典一致，并可应用于Yang-Mills理论禁闭-去禁闭相变等具体问题。

Abstract: We study a systematic expansion of dynamical black hole solutions in a class of five-dimensional Einstein-dilaton theories that are holographically dual to nearly critical, strongly coupled four-dimensional field theories. The expansion is motivated, through dimensional reduction, by the large-D limit of pure gravity, where D is the number of spacetime dimensions. At leading nontrivial order, the Einstein equations reduce to a closed set of non-relativistic hydrodynamic equations in four dimensions, as first shown in our previous letter; these equations generalize the large-D effective theory of black branes. We derive the thermodynamics and hydrodynamics of the system from black hole thermodynamics and the holographic dictionary, and show that the energy-momentum tensor extracted from the near-boundary geometry is conserved precisely when the effective equations hold. We analyze planar, cylindrical, and spherical domain walls between coexisting phases, as well as non-equilibrium steady-state walls with nonzero momentum flux. We also show how the near-horizon geometry can be completed by a holographic flow to an asymptotically AdS5 region. Finally, as an application to the confinement-deconfinement transition of Yang-Mills theory, we construct the domain wall between the confined and deconfined phases in improved holographic QCD, compute its surface tension, and compare it with recent lattice results.

</details>


### [203] [The Gribov problem on the horizon: A short guide to the Gribov-Zwanziger framework and its refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10303)
*Antonio D. Pereira*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该综述文章介绍欧几里得杨-米尔斯理论中的 Gribov 问题，并梳理 Gribov-Zwanziger 作用量及其通过引入量纲二凝聚（dimension-two condensates）的改进，讨论红外传播子、与格点结果的比较以及 BRST 对称性的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杨-米尔斯理论中的规范固定（gauge fixing）存在非微扰障碍：规范等价的组态可同时满足同一规范条件，即 Gribov 拷贝（Gribov copies），其存在威胁 Faddeev-Popov 方案的全局有效性；解决这一歧义需要将规范场空间几何与红外动力学联系起来的框架。

Method: 以小型综述（mini-review）形式，在欧几里得杨-米尔斯理论框架内引入 Gribov 问题，追溯 Gribov-Zwanziger 作用量的构造，并说明通过引入量纲二凝聚对其进行的改进。

Result: 给出由该框架导出的红外传播子（infrared propagators），并将其与格点（lattice）结果进行比较，同时讨论 BRST 对称性所扮演的角色。

Conclusion: 综述若干选定应用与开放问题，指出该方向仍存在待解决的问题。

Abstract: Gauge fixing in Yang-Mills theories encounters a non-perturbative obstruction: distinct gauge-equivalent configurations can satisfy the same gauge condition. These are the so-called Gribov copies and their existence challenges the global validity of the Faddeev-Popov prescription. Addressing this ambiguity motivates a framework that connects the geometry of gauge-field space to infrared dynamics. This mini-review introduces the Gribov problem in Euclidean Yang-Mills theories and traces the construction of the Gribov-Zwanziger action and its refinement through the introduction of dimension-two condensates. We discuss the resulting infrared propagators, their comparison to lattice results, and the role of BRST symmetry, before surveying selected applications and open questions.

</details>


### [204] [An RG perspective on the (un)naturalness of Starobinsky inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10326)
*Francesca Campani,Francesco Ferrarin,Serena Giardino,Alessia Platania*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文以渐近安全引力（asymptotically safe gravity）为平台，通过第一性原理重整化群（renormalization group）计算检验引力有效场论中 Wilson 系数同阶的期望，发现紫外区耦合同阶、亚普朗克能区高阶耦合被压低，从而使 $R^2$ 主导的 Starobinsky 暴胀（inflation）成为自然的低能近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Starobinsky 暴胀虽被视为成功的最小早期宇宙模型，但依赖较大的 $R^2$ 耦合，与引力有效场论（effective field theory）中 Wilson 系数应同阶的预期存在表面张力，需从第一性原理加以检验。

Method: 以渐近安全引力作为紫外完备的重整化群轨迹定义框架，用宇宙学常数与普朗克单位下的 $R^2$ 耦合作为自由参数唯一预言高阶耦合；计算纳入至四阶的高曲率算符（higher-curvature operators）。

Result: 紫外区确实生成量级相当的无量纲耦合，且非相关耦合（irrelevant couplings）通过线性关系与相关耦合（relevant couplings）相联系；但在亚普朗克能区，类正则标度（canonical-like scaling）接管，非相关耦合被暴胀尺度与普朗克尺度之比幂次压低，形成所需的高阶算符抑制。

Conclusion: $R^2$ 主导的动力学可作为自然的低能近似而浮现；同时基本物理所要求的高阶项会对 Starobinsky 预言产生微小但可计算的偏离，值得结合最新及未来 DESI 与 ACT 结果加以考虑。

Abstract: Starobinsky inflation is often regarded as a successful minimal model of the early universe, yet it relies on a large $R^2$-coupling, in apparent tension with the expectation that Wilson coefficients in a gravitational effective field theory are of the same order. We test this expectation with a first-principles renormalization group calculation. We use asymptotically safe gravity as a testing ground to define ultraviolet-complete renormalization group trajectories and to uniquely predict higher-order couplings in terms of the free parameters of the theory---the cosmological constant and the $R^2$-coupling in Planck units. Including higher-curvature operators up to the fourth order, we find that indeed the flow generates dimensionless couplings of comparable size in the ultraviolet, with the irrelevant couplings tied to the relevant ones by linear relations. However, at sub-Planckian energies a canonical-like scaling takes over, and the irrelevant couplings get suppressed by powers of the ratio between the inflationary and Planck scales. This hierarchy yields the suppression of higher-order operators needed for consistency with cosmological data, so that the $R^2$-dominated dynamics emerges as a natural low-energy approximation. At the same time, the higher-order terms required by fundamental physics induce small but calculable deviations from the Starobinsky predictions, which may be worth considering in light of the latest and future DESI and ACT results.

</details>


### [205] [Non-Relativistic Supersymmetric Gauge Theories and Soliton Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10479)
*Yoonbai Kim,O-Kab Kwon,D. D. Tolla,Hanwool Song*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过零约化（null reduction）从四维 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 相对论超对称规范理论构造 $2+1$ 维伽利略不变的非相对论超对称理论，研究超对称阿贝尔希格斯模型及其涡旋解，并揭示其真空结构（加权射影空间）与超导金兹堡-朗道理论的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统研究由零约化得到的非相对论超对称规范理论，考察其超对称代数结构，并探讨超对称阿贝尔希格斯模型中孤子解（soliton）的存在性及其物理意义。

Method: 以四维 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 相对论超对称理论为起点施加零约化，保持规范对称性与超对称性，得到对称结构为超伽利略代数（super-Galilean algebra）的巴格曼扩张（Bargmann extension）的非相对论理论；分别分析有质量与无质量的超对称阿贝尔希格斯模型，求解 BPS 方程，并在径向对称条件下解析与数值研究涡旋方程。

Result: 有质量模型中质量项阻碍非平凡 BPS 涡旋解的存在，尽管其具有非平凡真空位形；无质量多带电标量场模型中，在适当电荷与绕数条件下 BPS 方程存在涡旋型孤子解，真空模空间为加权射影空间（weighted projective space），自然给出整数与分数涡旋（fractional vortex）位形。

Conclusion: 非相对论超对称规范框架的真空结构可与超导的朗道-金兹堡理论（Ginzburg-Landau theory）相联系，表明熟悉的超导行为可在保持对称性的非相对论超对称理论中涌现，凸显零约化方法的构造价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates non-relativistic supersymmetric gauge theories obtained through null reduction, with particular focus on supersymmetric Abelian Higgs models and their soliton solutions. Starting from four-dimensional $\mathcal{N} = 1$ relativistic supersymmetric theories, we apply null reduction to derive Galilean-invariant non-relativistic theories in $2 + 1$ dimensions while preserving gauge symmetry and supersymmetry. The resulting symmetry structure is shown to correspond to the Bargmann extension of the super-Galilean algebra. We first analyze the massive supersymmetric Abelian Higgs model and show that, although it possesses nontrivial vacuum configurations, the mass term obstructs the existence of nontrivial BPS vortex solutions. We then study the massless supersymmetric Abelian Higgs model with multiple charged scalar fields, for which the BPS equations admit vortex-type soliton solutions under suitable charge and winding number conditions. The vacuum moduli space is identified as a weighted projective space, leading naturally to both integer and fractional vortex configurations. By imposing radial symmetry, we derive the corresponding vortex equations and examine their solutions analytically and numerically. Finally, we relate the vacuum structure of the model to the Ginzburg-Landau theory of superconductivity, highlighting how familiar superconducting behavior can emerge from a symmetry-preserving non-relativistic supersymmetric framework.

</details>


### [206] [Configurational Information and Hadron Spectroscopy in Holographic QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10741)
*P. H. O. Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文将构型信息测度（Configurational Information Measures, CIMs）与唯象 AdS/QCD 模型结合，提出一种混合方法用于强子谱（hadron spectroscopy）研究，并通过质量外推估计高激发态质量、探讨未识别粒子的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIMs 基于熵（entropy）与复杂性（complexity）概念，能从空间局域能量密度中提取稳定性极限、相变等物理信息；论文旨在将这一工具引入强子谱研究，以辅助识别实验已探测但尚未明确的粒子性质。

Method: 采用唯象 AdS/QCD 模型与 CIMs 结合的混合方法，将已编目的介子与重子共振态（meson and baryon resonances）内插为类 Regge 轨迹（Regge trajectories）结构，并由此推导解析表达式以估计更高阶激发态质量；应用对象包括动力学模型中的轻介子、考虑组分夸克质量（constituent quark masses）框架下的奇异矢量 kaon，以及有限温度下的轻味重子。

Result: 由类 Regge 轨迹导出的解析表达式可给出高阶激发态的质量估计，这些估计与实验数据进行比较，用以寻找可能阐明未识别粒子性质的对应关系；该方法在轻介子、奇异矢量 kaon 与有限温度轻味重子三类体系中得到应用分析。

Conclusion: 基于 CIM 的 AdS/QCD 混合方法可作为强子谱研究的可行工具，通过质量外推与实验数据对照，有助于解释实验已探测但尚未识别的粒子属性。

Abstract: Based on the concepts of entropy and complexity, Configurational Information Measures (CIMs) are tools capable of extracting information regarding physical systems from their spatially localized energy density, determining properties such as stability limits and phase transitions. In this thesis, CIMs are employed to investigate hadron spectroscopy. By utilizing phenomenological AdS/QCD models in conjunction with CIMs, cataloged meson and baryon resonances are interpolated into structures analogous to Regge trajectories, from which analytical expressions are derived to estimate the masses of higher-order excited states. These estimates are compared with experimental data to search for possible correspondences that may elucidate the properties of particles that have been experimentally detected but remain unidentified. Using this CIM-based AdS/QCD hybrid method, this work analyzes light mesons within a dynamical model, strange vector kaons in a framework that accounts for constituent quark masses, and light-flavor baryons at finite temperature.

</details>


### [207] [Monodromy Defects and Bulk Renormalization Group Flows on the Fuzzy Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10815)
*Kimyeong Lee,Joydeep Naskar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在模糊球（fuzzy sphere）上研究缺陷（defect）的低能性质如何依赖其与体自由度及自身的相互作用，系统分析了各向异性流下 O(3) 半扭转（half-twist）向 O(2) 半扭转的演化、大 N 极限下未扭转旁观者（spectator）对缺陷熵的正修正，以及受钉扎（pinning）单值性（monodromy）线上畴壁（wall）维数的稳定性判据，并推广构造了模糊四球上的规则半扭转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绕缺陷的单值性（monodromy）虽指定了一个对称变换，但缺陷的低能性质还取决于它与体自由度以及自身的相互作用；摘要旨在阐明这一依赖关系，并以此判断候选有序线（ordered line）的稳定性。

Method: 采用模糊球（fuzzy sphere）框架：沿各向异性流追踪 O(3)→O(2) 半扭转的演化；在大 N 下计算未扭转旁观者对缺陷熵的贡献；引入带电相关微扰实现钉扎（pinning）；将畴壁维数分解为倾斜刚度（tilt stiffness）与角向形状，并与 Ward 恒等式（Ward identity）、两圈展开（two-loop expansion）及大 N 鞍点（large-N saddle）比较；最后在模糊四球上构造规则半扭转。

Result: 各向异性流中，O(3) 半扭转在扭转分量保持轻时趋近 O(2) 半扭转，在分量变重时消失；大 N 下未扭转旁观者给出由自由缺陷共形权重（conformal weight）固定的缺陷熵正修正。剩余的 O(2) 单值性允许相关带电微扰将其钉扎，钉扎在易面体流之前或之后给出匹配的红外结点数据。畴壁在已研究的钉扎 O(2) 线上为相关，在普通 O(2) 线上以较小裕度倾向于相关，而普通 O(3)、O(4) 畴壁接近边缘（marginality）。完全扭转的大 N 模型中绕数（winding）削弱凝聚并降低畴壁维数与钉扎释放的熵。

Conclusion: 熵比较表明，凡领先阶畴壁估计为相关之处，均可无序化至未钉扎线；未扭转分量的钉扎仅在相反的易轴流下存活。该工作以模糊四球上的规则半扭转构造作为五维类似问题的起点。

Abstract: A monodromy around a defect specifies a symmetry transformation, but the low energy properties of the defect depend on its interaction with bulk degrees of freedom and also itself. We study this dependence on the fuzzy sphere. Along an anisotropy flow, an $O(3)$ half-twist approaches the $O(2)$ half-twist when its twisted components stay light and disappears when they become massive. At large $N$, untwisted spectators give a positive correction to the defect entropy, fixed by the conformal weight of the free defect. The surviving $O(2)$ monodromy admits a relevant charged perturbation that pins the line. Pinning before or after the easy-plane bulk flow gives matching infrared junction data. A pin of the untwisted component survives only the opposite, easy-axis flow. Walls between opposite pinnings test the stability of the candidate ordered line. They are relevant on the pinned $O(2)$ monodromy lines studied and favoured to be relevant, with a smaller margin, on the ordinary $O(2)$ line, while the ordinary $O(3)$ and $O(4)$ walls lie close to marginality. We separate the wall dimension into a tilt stiffness and an angular shape, comparing them with a Ward identity, a two-loop expansion and a large-$N$ saddle. In the fully twisted large-$N$ model, winding weakens the condensate and lowers the wall dimension and the entropy released by pinning. The resulting entropy comparison permits disordering into the unpinned line wherever the leading wall estimate is relevant. Finally, we construct the regular half-twist on the fuzzy four-sphere as a starting point for analogous questions in five dimensions.

</details>


### [208] [eAdS- embedded AdS, tensionless strings and contracted Carroll-Weyl symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10907)
*Ulf Lindström,Bo Sundborg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文将 Dirac 共形 d+2 维嵌入构造用于定义 AdS_{d+1} 中的零弦（null string），引入在 AdS 中标量、在嵌入空间局域标度变换下平移的 Stückelberg 场 φ，使模型获得局域标度不变性，称为嵌入反德西特（eAdS）零弦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往 AdS 背景下的规范作用量中，局域标度对称性与 AdS 的 so(d,2) 对称性不相容；本文旨在构造一种使局域标度对称性与 so(d,2) 对称性对易的规范表述。

Method: 基于 Dirac 共形 d+2 维嵌入构造，在 AdS_{d+1} 中定义零弦，并引入额外 Stückelberg 场 φ（AdS 下标量、嵌入空间局域标度下平移），从而赋予模型局域标度不变性；随后在嵌入空间与直接在 AdS 中分别给出对称性与约束代数（constraint algebras）。

Result: 新规范表述的关键性质是局域标度对称性与 AdS 的 so(d,2) 对称性对易；此外，eAdS 零弦对应于嵌入空间中张紧弦（tensile string）的无张力极限（tensionless limit），其约化到 AdS 内禀描述后同样如此。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间与 AdS 两种描述下的对称性和约束代数均被展示，表明该规范化的 eAdS 零弦表述可自洽地联系嵌入空间张紧弦的无张力极限与其 AdS 内禀形式。

Abstract: We adapt the Dirac conformal $d+2$ dimensional embedding construction to define a null string in $AdS_{d+1}$. Introducing an extra Stückelberg field $\varphi$, which is a scalar under $AdS$ and shifts under local scaling of embedding space, the model acquires a local scaling invariance. We refer to this string as the embedded anti-De Sitter, $eAdS$, null string. A very useful property of this new gauged formulation is that, unlike previous gauged actions in $AdS$ background, the local scaling symmetry commutes with the $so(d,2)$ symmetry of $AdS$. Interestingly, the $eAdS$ null string is the tensionless limit of a tensile string in embedding space and the same holds for its reduction to an intrinsic description of AdS. We display the symmetries and constraint algebras both in embedding space and directly in AdS.

</details>


### [209] [Spectral function, OPE, and QNMs detect different singular points of black branes with unequal R-charges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10998)
*Hyunwoo Oh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在STU模型黑膜（black brane）的R-电荷（R-charge）不相等时，几何中存在三个奇点，仅$R=-q_3$为物理奇点；其“光学时间”（optical time）$T_i$在复平面构成直角三角形，而谱函数（spectral function）、OPE系数（OPE coefficients）与准正规模（QNMs）三种观测量分别对$T_1$、$T_2$、$T_3$敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是厘清黑膜几何中多个曲率奇点（curvature singular points）中哪一个对边界场论的可观测量起主导作用，并建立奇点结构与该全息（holographic）可观测量之间的对应关系。
