Method: 以STU模型中三R-电荷参数$q_1,q_2,q_3$的黑膜解为对象，定义复平面中边界到奇点往返零射线（null rays）的复时间$T_i$；并基于零空间动量下$T_{xy}$推迟关联函数（retarded correlator）构造三种观测量，通过WKB分析推导结果并以数值方法校验。

Result: 大实频率下谱函数的指数小修正由${\rm Im}\,T_i$最小的$T_1$与$T_2$主导；OPE收敛半径由$|T_i|$最小的$T_1$决定；一条QNM线沿$1/T_2$方向（因$\arg T_i$最小），另一条沿负虚轴，携带物理奇点$T_3$的信息。

Conclusion: 三种观测量分别对不同奇点敏感，说明几何中的多重奇点结构可在边界关联函数的不同方面被独立探测量，且WKB分析与数值结果一致。

Abstract: We consider black brane solutions in the STU model, with three R-charges labeled by the parameters $q_1,q_2,q_3$. For equal R-charges $q_1=q_2=q_3$, the solution becomes the Reissner-Nordström black brane. For unequal R-charges $q_1>q_2>q_3\geq0$, there are three curvature singular points at $R\equiv r^2=-q_i$. Only $R=-q_3$ is the physical singularity, and the other two lie beyond it. Their optical times $T_i$, the complex times of null rays from the boundary to these singular points and back, form a right triangle in the complex $T$-plane. We probe these singular points using three observables, all built from the retarded correlator of $T_{xy}$ at zero spatial momentum: spectral function, OPE coefficients, and QNMs. The exponentially small correction to the spectral function at large real frequency is dominated by $T_1$ and $T_2$, which have the smallest ${\rm Im}\,T_i$. The OPE convergence radius is determined by $T_1$, which has the smallest $|T_i|$. A line of QNMs lies along the direction of $1/T_2$, since $T_2$ has the smallest phase $\arg T_i$. Furthermore, there is a second line of QNMs along the negative imaginary axis, which carries information about $T_3$ corresponding to the physical singularity. We derive these results from a WKB analysis and check them numerically. The three observables are therefore sensitive to different singular points of the geometry.

</details>


### [210] [Kinematically Unrestricted Flat Space Scattering from AdS$_{d+1} \times$ S$^p$/CFT$_d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11028)
*Bhanu Narra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在 AdS/CFT 的平直空间极限下，由 CFT 算符构造具有一般运动学的标量场散射态，并将 Mellin 空间形式推广以纳入紧致动量，为从 CFT 关联函数构造无限制 S-矩阵及平直空间全息提供途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有从 CFT 关联函数提取平直空间 S-矩阵（S-matrix）的构造通常受运动学限制，难以覆盖沿紧致球面方向的动量。本文旨在突破该限制，构造具有更一般运动学的散射态。

Method: 在 AdS_{d+1}×S^p 背景下，通过将 R-对称荷（R-symmetry charge）标度至大值并选取特定的 R-对称极化参数化，使态获得沿 S^p 紧致方向的动量；同时推广 Mellin 空间（Mellin space）形式以纳入紧致动量。

Result: 所构造的态可从 CFT 关联函数的平直空间极限得到无限制的 S-矩阵，并在多种树图级相互作用（tree level interactions）中得到验证。

Conclusion: 紧致动量的纳入扩展了平直空间极限下 S-矩阵的构造范围，作者并讨论了其对平直空间全息（flat space holography）的可能启示。

Abstract: We construct scattering states for scalar fields with generic kinematics from CFT operators in the flat space limit of AdS/CFT. The states can have momentum along the compact $S^p$ directions of $AdS_{d+1}\times S^p$, which is achieved by scaling to large R-symmetry charge and choosing a particular parameterization for the R-symmetry polarization. These states allow for the construction of an unrestricted S-matrix from the flat space limit of CFT correlation functions, which we check for various tree level interactions. We also describe a generalization of the Mellin space formalism which incorporates the compact momentum, and we discuss some possible implications for flat space holography.

</details>


### [211] [Stanley Deser 19th March 1931 - 21st April 2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11323)
*M. J. Duff,K. S. Stelle*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 斯坦利·德塞尔（Stanley Deser）是一位世界著名的理论物理学家，其成就包括广义相对论（general relativity）的ADM表述、与Zumino的超引力（supergravity）研究、与Jackiw和't Hooft的平坦空间动力学，以及对量子引力理论（quantum theory of gravity）的诸多贡献；他还推动了LIGO引力波实验的立项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该文本为一份纪念性学者简介（obituary/profile），动机在于系统记录德塞尔的学术地位、代表性成果、科学政策贡献及所获荣誉，以彰显其对理论物理尤其是引力物理的持久影响。

Method: 文本采用生平概述式叙述：先交代其任职机构与总体学术身份，再列举核心科学贡献（ADM表述、超引力、平坦空间动力学等），随后说明其在LIGO（Laser Interferometer Gravitational-Wave Observatory）获批中的推动作用，最后罗列奖项与院士荣誉。

Result: 所列成果显示其在广义相对论与量子引力领域具有奠基性贡献，并以爱因斯坦奖章（Einstein Medal, 2015）尤为突出，明确表彰其ADM形式体系的发展；此外还获Heineman奖（1994）、当选美国国家科学院（National Academy of Sciences）院士（1994）等多项荣誉。

Conclusion: 德塞尔在理论物理的多个方向及科学政策层面均有重要建树，其学术遗产与所获荣誉共同确立了其在现代引力物理史上的关键地位。

Abstract: Stanley Deser was Ancell Professor Emeritus at Brandeis University and Visiting Professor at the California Institute of Technology. He was a world-renowned theoretical physicist whose accomplishments include the Arnowitt-Deser-Misner (ADM) formulation of general relativity, supergravity with Zumino, the dynamics of flat space with Jackiw and 't Hooft and many other contributions to the quantum theory of gravity. In the 1980s he was instrumental in securing National Science Foundation approval of the LIGO gravitational wave experiment against much opposition. He was awarded the Einstein Medal in 2015 for important contributions to general relativity, in particular the development of the ADM formalism. Other awards include: The Dannie Heineman Prize 1994; Fellow of the National Academy of Sciences 1994; Honorary Member of the Torino Academy of Sciences 2001; Member of the American Academy of Arts and Sciences 1979, Fellow of the American Institute of Physics.

</details>


### [212] [Liberation in the Heavy Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11594)
*Sridip Pal,Gordon Rogelberg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究大维度 $\\Delta$ 标量主算符的四点函数，提出“解放（liberated）”概念（即与广义自由场（GFF）具有相同指数增长行为），并将其与轻谱稀疏性、低扭度（low twist）稀疏性及共形交叉对称性联系起来，给出解放成立的 $z,\\bar z$ 区域判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重自由粒子沿 AdS 测地线传播时四点函数与 GFF 具有相同指数增长率，本文希望刻画何种 CFT 在大 $\\Delta$ 极限下呈现这种“解放”行为，从而厘清其与轻谱结构的关系。

Method: 以相同标量主算符的四点函数为对象，在固定 $0<z,\\bar z<1$ 下取 $\\Delta\\to\\infty$ 极限；通过交叉对称点处的分析并结合轻谱、低扭度谱的稀疏性假设，逐区域判定解放是否成立，并考察二维 Virasoro 情形。

Result: 交叉对称点处解放当且仅当轻谱稀疏；此时在 $z,\\bar z$ 位于 $1/2$ 同侧的区域解放成立。若低扭度态亦稀疏，则除 $z+\\bar z=1$ 附近显式带外处处解放；若主算符扭度间隙（twist gap）高于 $\\sqrt{2}\\Delta$，则处处解放。扭度截断低于 $\\Delta$ 时，两个自旋 GFF 的复合态在双光锥极限（double lightcone limit）附近带内不解放。二维中 Virasoro 主算符除非中心荷（central charge）增长快于 $\\Delta$ 否则不能解放。

Conclusion: “解放”这一判据把大 $\\Delta$ 四点函数的普适行为与谱稀疏性定量对应，并推广至二维大 $c$ 环面配分函数（torus partition function）的普适性表述。

Abstract: We study CFT four-point functions of identical scalar primaries as their dimension $Δ$ grows for fixed $0<z, \bar z<1$, and call them liberated if they grow at the same exponential rate as those of a generalized free field (GFF), as is the case for heavy free particles propagating along geodesics in AdS. We show that the correlator is liberated at the crossing symmetric point if and only if the light spectrum is sparse, and that liberation then holds wherever $z$ and $\bar z$ lie on the same side of $1/2$. If the low twist states are also sparse, liberation holds everywhere except in an explicit band around $z+\bar z=1$. In particular, if the primary twist gap lies above $\sqrt{2}Δ$, then the correlator is liberated everywhere. For twist cutoffs below $Δ$, composites of two spinning generalized free fields fail to be liberated in a region that fills the band near the double lightcone limit. In two dimensions, Virasoro primaries cannot be liberated unless the central charge grows faster than $Δ$. The same framework sharpens the universality of large-$c$ torus partition functions of two-dimensional CFTs.

</details>


### [213] [Decoupling Across Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11687)
*Param Gogia,Caius Gordon-Bruno,Emily Nardoni,Peter Weck*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种判别由膜紧致化所得四维超共形场论（4d SCFT）中解耦部门的方法，核心手段是对比场论约化与反常流入（anomaly inflow）两种途径给出的反常多项式（anomaly polynomial）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四维超共形场论经紧致化后常出现相互作用部门与解耦部门的混合，需要一种系统性判据来识别解耦部门并刻画其贡献来源。

Method: 以M5膜探测 $\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{Z}_2$ 轨道折叠奇点所得六维 $(1,0)$ 理论在带流环面（torus with flux）上紧致化为旗舰例子；比较场论约化与反常流入所得反常多项式，把翻转场（flip fields）部门的贡献表示为奇异与分辨几何中流入反常多项式之差，并用等变局域化（equivariant localization）高效计算；同时在 $Y^{p,q}$ 箭图（quiver）与由三价顶点（trinions）得到的 class $\mathcal{S}_2$ 项链箭图两类对偶描述上研究相互作用与解耦部门，构建手征环（chiral ring）的显式映射。

Result: 相互作用不动点的反常多项式可分解为六维反常多项式的 $T^2$ 约化加上翻转场贡献；两类对偶箭图描述的手征环之间建立了显式映射，并识别出一段参数空间使箭图流向表观上不同的相互作用不动点；每个手征环生成元对应 M 理论分辨几何中缠绕一个 2-圈（2-cycle）的 M2 膜。

Conclusion: 反常多项式对比（场论约化 vs. 反常流入）为识别膜紧致化所得 4d SCFT 的解耦部门提供了可行方案，并借由对偶箭图与 M 理论几何实现手征环生成元的统一对应。

Abstract: We present a prescription for identifying decoupled sectors of 4d SCFTs obtained from compactifications of branes. Central to our analysis is the comparison of anomaly polynomials obtained via field-theoretic reduction versus anomaly inflow. Our flagship examples are the 4d $\mathcal{N}=1$ SCFTs obtained by compactifying the 6d $(1,0)$ theory from M5-branes probing a $\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{Z}_2$ orbifold singularity on a torus with flux. The anomaly polynomial of the interacting fixed point is known to decompose into the $T^2$ reduction of the 6d anomaly polynomial, plus a contribution from flip fields introduced to remove operators from the spectrum. Adopting the inflow perspective, the contribution from the flipped sector is obtained as the difference between inflow anomaly polynomials in the singular and resolved geometries, which we efficiently compute using equivariant localization. Helpfully, the 4d SCFTs admit two dual quiver gauge theory descriptions: $Y^{p,q}$ quiver gauge theories, and class $\mathcal{S}_2$ necklace quivers obtained from trinions. We study the interacting and decoupled sectors on both sides of the duality and construct an explicit map between their chiral rings, identifying a range of parameter space for which the quivers flow to apparently different interacting fixed points. Each chiral ring generator is furthermore matched to an M2-brane wrapping a 2-cycle in the resolved M-theory geometry.

</details>


### [214] [Landau-Ginzburg Theory of a Holographic Superconductor: A Horizon Perspective on the Zero-Temperature Ground State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11706)
*Sejin Kim,Yunseok Seo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究了四次标量自相互作用（quartic scalar self-interaction）λ|φ|⁴ 对含非线性电动力学耦合、具有扩展 Lifshitz 区域的全息超导体（holographic superconductor）的影响。随着 λ 增大，穹顶内的解依次经历 Lifshitz 区域收缩、无 AdS 区域出现、以及全为渐近 AdS 毛发黑洞三个阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有全息超导模型中非线性电动力学耦合可生成包含扩展 Lifshitz 区域（Lifshitz regime）的超导穹顶，但标量场自身四次自相互作用对穹顶内部非线性结构的影响尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在正常相周围线性化标量动力学不受 λ 影响的前提下，通过求解标量场较大时的非线性解，考察 λ 增大时穹顶内解的结构演化，并借助视界处有效势（horizon effective potential）的 Landau-Ginzburg 形式进行分析。

Result: 随 λ 增大，Lifshitz 区域收缩并在 λ* 处其标度解消失，留下无 AdS 区域（no-AdS region），其边缘之外的流动在到达任何渐近区域前终止；当 λ 超过 λ_c 时穹顶完全由渐近 AdS 毛发黑洞（hairy black holes）组成。穹顶底部存在标量场视界值最大的临界点，该值位于 Landau-Ginzburg 型视界有效势最深的对称破缺极小值处。

Conclusion: 该 Landau-Ginzburg 解释基于视界处的标量场而非边界序参量（boundary order parameter），与常规描述不同。当 λ 低于 λ* 时，Lifshitz 不动点的常标量场则位于其渐近标度区域有效势的极小值处。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of a quartic scalar self-interaction, $λ|φ|^4$, on a holographic superconductor in which non-linear electrodynamic couplings generate a superconducting dome containing an extended Lifshitz regime. The quartic interaction leaves the linearized scalar dynamics around the normal phase unchanged while modifying the non-linear solutions where the scalar field is large. As $λ$ increases, the solutions within the dome pass through three stages. The Lifshitz region shrinks until its scaling solution disappears at $λ^*$, leaving a no-AdS region, beyond whose edge the flows end before reaching any asymptotic region, and above $λ_c$ the dome consists entirely of asymptotically AdS hairy black holes. At the bottom of the dome we identify the point where the horizon value of the scalar field is largest, which we refer to as the critical point. There this value lies at the deepest symmetry-breaking minimum that the horizon effective potential, of Landau-Ginzburg form, can have. Unlike a conventional Landau-Ginzburg description, this interpretation rests on the scalar field at the horizon rather than on the boundary order parameter. Below $λ^*$, the constant scalar of the Lifshitz fixed point sits instead at the minimum of the effective potential of its asymptotic scaling region.

</details>


### [215] [The tertiary invariant and discrete Green-Schwarz couplings in heterotic string theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11731)
*Hiroki Wada,Kazuya Yonekura*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从定义出发直接计算了二维 𝒩=(0,1) 超对称量子场论之间新近引入的三次不变量（tertiary invariant），在单位 H-通量下的 Sp(2) 与 SU(3) sigma 模型上分别得到 3 阶与 2 阶的非零值，并据此确定相应理论（𝔰𝔬(16)⊕𝔰𝔬(16) 杂化弦与非超对称杂化 6-膜近视界区）的 Green-Schwarz 耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三次不变量是由拓扑模形式（topological modular forms）动机引入的二维 𝒩=(0,1) 超对称场论间新配对，其物理意义与可计算性有待检验。已有工作通过反常流入（anomaly inflow）至 NS5-膜得到 𝔰𝔬(16)⊕𝔰𝔬(16) 理论的 ℤ₃ 离散拓扑项，亟需用世界面理论独立验证。

Method: 将目标空间视为球面上的 S³ 丛（S³ bundle over sphere），用携带规范瞬子（gauge instanton）的四维圆盘填充每个纤维，从而把不变量约化为底流形上 Dirac 算子的指标（index of a Dirac operator）。对 Sp(2) 与 SU(3) 情形分别与 𝔰𝔬(16)⊕𝔰𝔬(16) 杂化弦内部理论、非超对称杂化 6-膜近视界区内部理论配对计算。

Result: 计算得到阶为 3 与 2 的非零不变量值：前者对应 𝔰𝔬(16)⊕𝔰𝔬(16) 理论的 ℤ₃ 值离散拓扑项，后者对应 SU(3) 情形的 Green-Schwarz 耦合。Sp(2) 情形的结果与既有反常流入计算完全一致。

Conclusion: 仅使用世界面理论的三次不变量计算精确复现了反常流入所得的 ℤ₃ 离散拓扑项，从而确认了该反常流入结果，并表明三次不变量在确定 Green-Schwarz 耦合方面是有力的世界面工具。

Abstract: The tertiary invariant is a recently introduced pairing between two-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=(0,1)$ supersymmetric quantum field theories, motivated by topological modular forms. We evaluate it directly from its definition for the sigma models on $\mathrm{Sp}(2)$ and $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ with unit $H$-flux, paired with the internal theories of the non-supersymmetric $\mathfrak{so}(16)\oplus\mathfrak{so}(16)$ heterotic string and of the near-horizon region of the non-supersymmetric heterotic 6-brane, respectively. Regarding the target spaces as $S^3$ bundles over spheres, we fill in every fiber with a four-disk carrying a gauge instanton and reduce the invariant to the index of a Dirac operator on the base. We find nonzero values of order three and two, which determine the Green-Schwarz couplings of the two theories on $\mathrm{Sp}(2)$ and $\mathrm{SU}(3)$, respectively. The former, a $\mathbb{Z}_{3}$-valued discrete topological term of the $\mathfrak{so}(16)\oplus\mathfrak{so}(16)$ theory, was previously studied through anomaly inflow to NS5-branes. Our computation, which uses only the worldsheet theories, agrees exactly with that result and thus confirms the anomaly inflow.

</details>


### [216] [Proof of the Rényi QNEC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11760)
*Thomas Faulkner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对所有 $p>2$ 给出了基于 Araki-Masuda 加权 $L_p$ 范数表述的 Rényi 量子零能条件（Rényi QNEC）的一般性证明，推广了 $p=2$ 时的代数证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 QNEC 的代数证明仅适用于 $p=2$，作者希望将其推广到任意 $p>2$ 的 Rényi 版本，以拓展该不等式在量子信息与量子场论中的适用范围。

Method: 采用加权条件 $L_p$ 范数（weighted conditional $L_p$ norm）来外推量子展开量/反对易量（quantum expansion/anti-quantity），并分析半侧模包含（half-sided modular inclusion）及具有共同循环分离矢量的 von Neumann 子代数下的加权条件熵性质。

Result: 成功证明了对所有 $p>2$ 的 Rényi QNEC，并阐明了相应加权条件熵在两类代数结构下的若干性质；$p=2$ 极限对应于黑洞动力学背景下定义的条件动力学熵（conditional dynamical entropy）。

Conclusion: 该工作将 QNEC 的代数证明从 $p=2$ 推广到全参数 $p>2$，并建立了加权条件熵与黑洞动力学中条件动力学熵之间的联系。

Abstract: We give a general proof of the Rényi QNEC formulated using the Araki-Masuda weighted $L_p$ norms for all $p > 2$. This generalizes the algebraic proof of the QNEC applicable at $p=2$. Our approach utilizes a weighted version of a conditional $L_p$ norm which extrapolates the quantum expansion/ant-quantity. We elucidate some properties of the corresponding weighted conditional entropy both for half-sided modular inclusions and for general von Neumann subalgebras with a common cyclic and separating vector. The $p=2$ limit computes a conditional dynamical entropy originally defined in the context of black hole dynamics.

</details>


### [217] [Discretising 2D Fermionic CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11807)
*Dazhuo Cheng,Hexuan Li,Gong Cheng,Ling-Yan Hung*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将 Cheng 等人的精确不动点张量构造推广到费米子极小模型，给出费米子有理共形场论不动点张量的解析构造，并验证其在自由 Majorana 费米子上的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有精确不动点张量构造主要针对玻色型有理共形场论，费米子情形尚缺乏解析工具；而费米子临界现象的张量网络模拟需要可靠的解析起点。

Method: 将费米子不动点张量定义为三角形上边界改变算符（boundary-changing operators）的关联函数，谨慎处理费米子宇称（fermionic parity）与自旋结构（spin structure）；构造边界张量并与三角形张量组合成条带以提取标度维数；同时基于有理玻色共形场论的费米化（fermionisation）及模张量范畴（modular tensor categories）给出通用离散化方案。

Result: 构造涵盖可收缩边界条件的修正权重与自旋结构的系统描述，恢复 Neveu-Schwarz 与 Ramond 两个扇区；在自由 Majorana 费米子（c=1/2）上，最小后代层级截断即收敛到众多低能态的精确体标度维数；离散化自旋结构自然重现 R/NS 扇区所需符号。

Conclusion: 离散化二维共形场论的 OPE 系数集合可自然解释为自旋拓扑量子场论（spin-TQFT）的状态和，类比玻色情形；该工作为费米子临界现象的张量网络模拟提供了解析计算的起点。

Abstract: We extend the exact fixed-point tensor construction of Cheng et al. to fermionic minimal models, providing an analytic construction of fixed-point tensors for fermionic rational conformal field theories. The fermionic fixed-point tensor is defined as a correlation function of boundary-changing operators on a triangle, with careful treatment of fermionic parity and spin structure. Boundary tensors are also constructed and combined with triangle tensors to form strips, enabling the extraction of scaling dimensions for boundary-changing operators. The construction incorporates modified weights for shrinkable boundary conditions, systematic depiction of spin structures, recovering both the Neveu-Schwarz and Ramond sectors. As a benchmark, our tensor with a minimal truncation of descendant levels yields convergence to the exact bulk scaling dimensions of many low energy states for the free Majorana fermion ($c=1/2$), demonstrating the efficiency of approximate fixed point tensors from boundary changing operators. We also make use of a more general description of fermionic CFTs based on fermionisation of rational bosonic CFTs, and make a generic prescription for discretisation making use of their connections to modular tensor categories. We show that the discretised spin structures on a spin surface naturally recover the signs needed to recover the R/NS sectors. We propose that the collection of OPE coefficients of the discretised 2d CFT can be naturally interpreted as a state-sum of spin-TQFTs, analogous to the bosonic case. This work provides analytically computed starting points for tensor network simulations of fermionic critical phenomena.

</details>


### [218] [Critical Slowing Down and the Crossover to Quantum Scrambling in Holographic CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11889)
*Mozib Bin Awal,Prabwal Phukon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在全息框架下研究RN-AdS黑洞对偶共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）的临界弛豫动力学及其与量子信息扰乱（quantum information scrambling）的联系，发现普适的临界指数Δ≈2/3，并在大中心荷极限下给出热力学弛豫与量子扰乱的交叉尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将临界动力学标度、有限中心荷效应（finite-central-charge effects）与量子信息扰乱统一置于全息CFT框架中，超越常规单参数分析，理解临界弛豫与信息扰乱的关系。

Method: 采用全息字典（holographic dictionary）以边界CFT变量表述热力学，用Langevin与Fokker-Planck形式研究约化视界半径的随机演化，并引入中心荷变化构造温度与中心荷的组合标度变量；通过对比弛豫时标与快扰乱时标（fast-scrambling timescale）考察二者相互作用，并用完整非线性自由能势垒进行数值验证。

Result: 对偶CFT中呈现显著的临界减慢（critical slowing down）：自关联时间与随机涨落增强，最低非零Fokker-Planck本征值被压低；自关联时间呈普适幂律发散τ∼|ε|^{-Δ}，Δ≈2/3，与解析预言及既有引力学结果一致；沿二维参数空间不同轨迹的主导弛豫保持相同临界指数；热力学弛豫与量子扰乱之间存在交叉，交叉尺度在大中心荷极限下为ε̂∼(C ln C)^{-3/2}，并获数值验证。

Conclusion: 研究结果在全息CFT框架内将临界动力学标度、有限中心荷效应与量子信息扰乱联系起来，给出普适临界指数及弛豫—扰乱交叉尺度的解析与数值刻画。

Abstract: We investigate critical relaxation dynamics and their connection to quantum information scrambling in the conformal field theory dual to a Reissner-Nordström anti-de Sitter (RN-AdS) black hole. Employing the holographic dictionary, we formulate the thermodynamics in terms of boundary CFT variables and study the stochastic evolution of the reduced horizon radius using Langevin and Fokker-Planck formalisms. We demonstrate pronounced critical slowing down in the dual CFT, characterized by an enhancement of the autocorrelation time and stochastic fluctuations, accompanied by a suppression of the lowest nonzero Fokker-Planck eigenvalue. The autocorrelation time exhibits a universal power-law divergence, $τ\sim|ε|^{-Δ}$, with $Δ\simeq 2/3$, consistent with the analytical prediction and previous bulk gravitational results. Going beyond the conventional single-parameter analysis, we incorporate variations of the CFT central charge and identify a combined scaling variable involving temperature and central charge, demonstrating that the leading relaxation dynamics retain the same critical exponent along different trajectories in the two-dimensional parameter space. Finally, we explore the interplay between thermodynamic relaxation and quantum information scrambling by comparing the relaxation timescale with the fast-scrambling timescale. The comparison reveals a crossover between thermodynamic relaxation and quantum scrambling for which we derive the corresponding crossover scale analytically and find that, in the large-central-charge limit, it behaves as $\hatε\sim(C\ln C)^{-3/2}$. We also verify this numerically using the full nonlinear free-energy landscape. Our results connect critical dynamical scaling, finite-central-charge effects, and quantum scrambling within the holographic CFT framework.

</details>


### [219] [Proof of the QNEC, without assumptions and under a weight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11920)
*Thomas Faulkner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在半边平移与模包含（HST/HSMI）的数学框架下补全了量子零能条件（QNEC）的证明，去除了此前证明所依赖的一项假设，并给出相对熵关于视界切片的凸性定理及面向蒸发黑洞的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 QNEC 的证明依赖“存在有限零能的纯化（purification with finite null energy）”这一假设，适用范围受限；本文旨在移除该假设，从而在更一般情形下完成证明。

Method: 在半边平移与模包含（Half-Sided Translations and Modular Inclusions, HST/HSMI）的数学框架内建立一条定理，并将其翻译到物理设定中；同时以模论中的权重（weight）推广 Hartle-Hawking 希尔伯特空间。

Result: 所证定理在物理上意味着：比较静止黑洞外部场态与 Hartle-Hawking（HH）态的相对熵（relative entropy）恒为视界切片（horizon cut）的凸函数；并得到 HH 希尔伯特空间被某权重替代的 QNEC 推广。

Conclusion: 该工作在不依赖有限零能纯化假设的情况下完成了 QNEC 证明，且所得推广可能与平直空间中的蒸发黑洞相关（relevant to evaporating black holes）。

Abstract: We complete a proof of the quantum null energy condition (QNEC) in the mathematical framework of Half-Sided Translations and Modular Inclusions (HST/HSMI) removing an assumption that applied to previous proofs. Here we establish a theorem, which when translated to a physical setting implies that the relative entropy comparing the state of fields outside a stationary black hole with a Hartle-Hawking (HH) state is always a convex function of the horizon cut. Previous proofs only applied when a purification with finite null energy was available. We also obtain a QNEC generalization in which the HH Hilbert space is replaced with one based on a weight which is plausibly of relevance to evaporating black holes in flat space.

</details>


### [220] [Semiclassical Asymptotics of Entanglement in Torus-Link States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11961)
*Vikash Ganwariya,Simran Sain,Siddharth Dwivedi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究(2+1)维SU(2) Chern–Simons理论中与$T_{d,\,dn}$环链相关的拓扑量子态的纠缠熵，在半经典极限$k\to\infty$下通过约化密度矩阵矩的渐近分析给出极限纠缠熵的显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $T_{d,\,dn}$环链的链补态为拓扑量子场论（topological quantum field theory）中的多体纠缠（multipartite entanglement）研究提供了自然框架，但此类态纠缠熵在半经典极限下的渐近行为尚需系统刻画。

Method: 将可积表示空间（integrable-representation space）分解为左边缘、右边缘与体（bulk）三个区域，利用驻相法（stationary-phase method）分别计算各区域对约化密度矩阵矩的贡献，并据此推导纠缠熵的极限。

Result: 主导渐近行为完全由两个边缘区域决定，体贡献渐近受抑；极限纠缠熵以Hurwitz zeta函数表示，并依赖于$k$模$n$的剩余类（residue class）；在大$d$极限下熵约化为由左右边缘两个归一化权重决定的二元熵（binary entropy）。

Conclusion: 极限纠缠熵对缠绕参数$n$与链组分数$d$的依赖得到分析；两个迭代极限$k\to\infty$与$d\to\infty$可交换，从而重现此前先取大参与方极限（large-party limit）所得结果。

Abstract: We investigate the entanglement entropy of topological quantum states associated with $T_{d,\,dn}$ torus links in (2+1)-dimensional SU(2) Chern--Simons theory at level $k$. These links consist of $d$ components with pairwise linking number $n$, and the corresponding link-complement states provide a natural setting to study multipartite entanglement in topological quantum field theories. We analyze the semiclassical limit $k\to\infty$ of the entanglement entropy by deriving the asymptotic behavior of the moments of the reduced density matrix. To achieve this, we decompose the integrable-representation space into left-edge, right-edge, and bulk regions and employ the stationary-phase method to evaluate their respective contributions. We find that the leading asymptotic behavior is governed entirely by the two edge regions, while the bulk contribution is asymptotically suppressed. The resulting expression for the limiting entanglement entropy is given in terms of Hurwitz zeta functions and exhibits a dependence on the residue class of $k$ modulo $n$. We further investigate the dependence of the limiting entanglement entropy on the winding parameter $n$ and the number of link components $d$. In the large-$d$ limit, the entropy reduces to a binary entropy determined by two normalized weights associated with the left and right edges. Finally, we show that the two iterated limits, $k\to\infty$ and $d\to\infty$, commute, reproducing the result obtained previously by taking the large-party limit first.

</details>


### [221] [Matrix-vector models for the 3D Ising CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12071)
*Kaichi Mizohata,Tatsuya Seko,Asato Tsuchiya*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究球面上最低朗道能级相互作用费米子构造的三维共形场论，提出一类由角动量阻尼函数参数化的形变哈密顿量，并将其重写为矩阵模型；以高斯阻尼为例，数值结果表明在一段参数范围内低能谱与三维伊辛共形场论一致，且未出现紫外/红外混合迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面上最低朗道能级（lowest Landau level）相互作用费米子方法在构造三维共形场论（3D CFTs）方面表现高效，但其与模糊球（fuzzy sphere）上常规场论构造方式存在差异，需要更系统、与基底无关的表述来理解此类模型。

Method: 引入由控制高角动量相互作用阻尼方式的函数所指定的一类形变哈密顿量（包含原始模型），将其以基底无关方式重写为矩阵—向量量子力学模型，并在积掉费米子向量后得到矩阵模型；重点分析高斯选择，此时玻色矩阵获得宽度由参数 ξ 控制的热核传播子（heat-kernel propagator），并通过最多 16 个费米子的精确对角化（exact diagonalization）进行计算。

Result: 在临界点处，形变哈密顿量的低能谱在一段 ξ 范围内与三维伊辛共形场论（3D Ising CFT）的谱一致，从而给出三维伊辛共形场论的矩阵模型实现；与常规模糊球场论不同，其低能谱未显示紫外/红外混合（UV/IR mixing）的迹象。

Conclusion: 球面最低朗道能级费米子模型可统一表述为矩阵模型，其中高斯阻尼情形可复现三维伊辛共形场论低能谱且避免紫外/红外混合，为构造三维共形场论提供了有别于模糊球途径的可行框架。

Abstract: The construction of three-dimensional conformal field theories (3D CFTs) using interacting fermions in the lowest Landau level on a sphere has recently proven remarkably efficient, while it differs from that of conventional field theories on the fuzzy sphere. Focusing on the 3D Ising CFT, we consider a class of models that are defined by deformed Hamiltonians specified by a function which controls how the interaction is damped at high angular momenta, and this class includes the original model. We show that each model in the class can be reformulated in a basis-independent manner as a matrix--vector quantum-mechanical model, and, upon integrating out the fermionic vectors, as a matrix model. We then focus on a Gaussian choice, for which the bosonic matrices acquire a heat-kernel propagator whose width is controlled by a parameter $ξ$. Through exact diagonalization with the number of fermions up to $16$, we find that the low-energy spectra of the deformed Hamiltonians at criticality agree with those in the 3D Ising CFT for a range of $ξ$. We thus obtain matrix-model realizations of the 3D Ising CFT that, in contrast to conventional fuzzy-sphere field theories, show no sign of UV/IR mixing in the low-lying spectrum.

</details>


### [222] [On a soliton with a non-normalisable zero mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12387)
*Guido Franchetti,Stefano Bolognesi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全纯 baby Skyrme 模型（holomorphic baby Skyrme model），将其作为具有不可归一化零模（non-normalisable zero mode）的孤立子（soliton）的可解析范例，并提出用半平面上的径向 (1+1) 维有效作用量处理此类零模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望构造一个简单、可处理的模型来刻画带有不可归一化零模的孤立子，因为此类零模难以用通常的模空间（moduli space）方法处理，而该现象可能与 3+1 维某些单极子（monopole）相关。

Method: 论文提出全纯 baby Skyrme 模型，分析 1-孤立子（1-soliton）结构；指出 sigma 模型（sigma model）的尺寸模（size modulus）获得质量而相位模（phase modulus）保持无质量，并因孤立子尾部导致在 (0+1) 维意义下不可归一化；随后采用半平面上径向 (1+1) 维有效作用量（effective action），由孤立子核心设定边界条件，并用 WKB 近似（WKB approximation）与数值方法求解。

Result: 1-孤立子与固定尺寸的 lump 具有相同结构；尺寸模变为有质量、相位模保持无质量且不可归一化。有效模型可通过 WKB 近似与数值计算评估，并讨论了在特定截断尺度（cutoff scale）下带边界项的 (1+1) 维有效模空间近似（effective moduli space approximation）的类比形式。

Conclusion: 该模型可作为更复杂系统的原型（prototype），用于研究具有不可归一化零模的情形，例如 3+1 维中的某些单极子。

Abstract: We consider the holomorphic baby Skyrme model, which we propose as a simple tractable example of a soliton with a non-normalisable zero mode. The $1$-soliton has the same structure as a lump of fixed size. The size modulus of the sigma model becomes massive, while the phase modulus remains massless. The latter is non-normalisable in the $(0+1)$-dimensional sense because of the soliton tail. A suitable way to treat it is to consider the radial $(1+1)$-dimensional effective action on the half-plane. The soliton core sets the boundary condition for the effective model, which we evaluate with the WKB approximation and also numerically. We discuss the analogue of the effective moduli space approximation with a $1+1$ model and a boundary term at a certain cutoff scale. This may serve as a prototype for more complex systems featuring non-normalisable zero modes such as certain kinds of monopoles in $3+1$ dimensions.

</details>


### [223] [First-order origins of second-order supersymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12418)
*Stevan Cebrian,Mikhail S. Plyushchay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在具有循环坐标的曲面上，由守恒一阶算子构造一维二阶超对称；通过中心扩展的 N=4 代数与循环约化导出二阶超荷及电荷与伴侣哈密顿量中的关联量子修正，并将修正表示为 Schwarzian 导数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从守恒的一阶算子出发构造一维二阶超对称（second-order supersymmetry），并厘清二阶超荷、量子修正与二次不变量 C 的符号结构及其几何来源。

Method: 采用 Witten 力学（Witten's mechanics）中双费米子模式下的中心扩展 N=4 代数，取零费米子与双费米子扇区的守恒乘积，经循环约化（cyclic reduction）得到二阶超荷；用等温坐标（isothermal coordinates）将辅助 Hill 方程与分离的 Laplace/波方程对应；另以 Pauli 约化与 Hermitian 双分量中间体构造一阶链。

Result: 中心动量固定二次不变量 C：Riemannian 构造给出 C≤0，不定度规对应给出 C≥0；对角化单费米子中间体后，C≤0 对应实标量分解、C>0 对应复分解。双曲平面上出现曲率依赖的不变量与额外电荷；一维中形式 Hermitian 双分量中间体给出任意 C 的局部一阶链；常数超势下平坦柱面上的实横向形变实现具有正内积的 C>0 一阶曲面实现。

Conclusion: 二阶超对称的量子修正可统一表述为 Schwarzian 导数，且 C 的符号与几何框架（Riemannian 或不定度规）及分解的实/复性质紧密相关；一阶机制（Pauli 约化、双分量中间体、柱面形变）为 C>0 情形提供了可行实现。

Abstract: One-dimensional second-order supersymmetry is constructed from conserved first-order operators on a surface with a cyclic coordinate. In Witten's mechanics with two fermionic modes, a centrally extended $N=4$ algebra permits a conserved product connecting the zero- and two-fermion sectors. Cyclic reduction gives the second-order supercharge and the correlated quantum corrections in the charge and partner Hamiltonians. Isothermal coordinates identify the auxiliary Hill equation with the separated Laplace or wave equation for the gauge-transformed charge kernel, and express the correction as a Schwarzian derivative. The central momentum fixes the quadratic invariant $C$: the Riemannian construction gives $C\leq0$, and its indefinite counterpart gives $C\geq0$, with a separately chosen positive adjoint after reduction. Diagonalizing the one-fermion intermediate recovers real scalar factorizations for $C\leq0$ and complex ones for $C>0$. Pauli reductions provide a second first-order mechanism; on the hyperbolic plane they reveal a curvature-dependent invariant and additional charges of a regular free-particle--Pöschl--Teller pair in a selected momentum sector. In one dimension, a formally Hermitian two-component intermediate with a positive inner product yields local first-order chains for arbitrary $C$. For a constant superpotential, a real transverse deformation on a flat cylinder also gives a first-order surface realization of $C>0$ with a positive inner product.

</details>


### [224] [Cubic Curvature Corrections to Taub-NUT-AdS Black Holes and Their Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12482)
*Yu-Qi Chen,Hai-Shan Liu,Hong Lu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造四维Taub-NUT-AdS黑洞在三阶曲率（cubic curvature）修正下的一阶解析解，验证其热力学在微扰修正下依然自洽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Taub-NUT-AdS黑洞（Taub-NUT-AdS black hole）的热力学（thermodynamics）能否在微扰修正（higher-derivative correction）下保持自洽，尤其是近边（NUT）势与NUT荷（NUT charge）等变量的确定性问题。

Method: 构造高阶导数耦合（higher-derivative couplings）一阶下的完整解析解，导出修正的霍金温度（Hawking temperature）与Wald熵（Wald entropy）；再借助两种改进的Reall-Santos方法——场重定义（field redefinition）与背景扣除（background subtraction）——独立计算吉布斯自由能（Gibbs free energy）。

Result: NUT势保持解析不变；通过麦克斯韦关系（Maxwell relation）重构NUT荷与吉布斯自由能后，仅余一个NUT参数的未定函数。两种Reall-Santos方法在匹配热力学变量后给出相同结果，并确定该未定函数，从而自洽地定出质量（mass）与NUT荷。

Conclusion: 结果表明Taub-NUT-AdS黑洞的热力学能够承受高阶导数修正（higher-derivative correction），即修正后的热力学结构保持一致。

Abstract: We investigate cubic curvature corrections to four-dimensional Taub-NUT-AdS black holes and their thermodynamics. We construct the complete analytic solution to the first order in the higher-derivative couplings, and derive the corrected Hawking temperature and Wald entropy. The NUT potential remains unchanged, while reconstructing the NUT charge and Gibbs free energy through the Maxwell relation leaves just one undetermined function of the NUT parameter. We then independently evaluate the Gibbs free energy using two improved Reall-Santos methods, based on either the field redefinition or the background subtraction. After matching the thermodynamic variables, both methods yield identical results and fix the undetermined function, providing a consistent determination of the mass and NUT charge. Our work therefore establishes that the thermodynamics of Taub-NUT-AdS black hole withstands the higher-derivative corrections.

</details>


### [225] [Deep Inelastic Scattering and Proton Structure Functions in AdS/QCD models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12693)
*Rafael A. Costa-Silva,Henrique Boschi-Filho*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在全息（holographic）框架下研究形变AdS模型中的费米子深度非弹性散射（deep inelastic scattering, DIS）。通过要求左右手征费米子势为SUSY伙伴势并作系统截断，作者证明二阶近似下结果与软墙（soft wall）模型一致，且高阶近似在Bjorken参数x=0.85、0.75、0.65处与实验数据吻合更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是在形变AdS模型中建立可计算的费米子DIS描述，并借助超对称（SUSY）伙伴势保证左右手征模式的同谱性（isospectrality），从而对势函数进行系统化截断与近似展开。

Method: 方法为：由类薛定谔（Schrödinger-like）方程导出费米子左、右手征模式对应的势，要求其为SUSY伙伴势以保持同谱性，并据此系统截断这些势；随后在形变AdS模型的二次近似下计算费米子DIS与质子结构函数（structure functions）F_{1,2}(q^2,x)，并与软墙模型及完整解的高阶近似进行比较。

Result: 结果显示，二次近似下得到的F_{1,2}(q^2,x)与软墙模型结果完全相同；高阶近似与形变AdS模型完整解的对比表明，近似阶数越高，F_{1,2}(q^2,x)与实验数据（特别是在x=0.85、0.75、0.65处）的符合程度越好，符合预期。

Conclusion: 结论是形变AdS模型中的SUSY伙伴势截断方案能为费米子DIS提供可控的近似序列，其二阶项退化为软墙模型，而高阶修正提升了与实验数据的符合度。该结果支持以高阶形变近似描述质子结构函数。

Abstract: In this work we study the holographical fermionic deep inelastic scattering in a deformed AdS model. Imposing that the potentials that emerge from the Schrödinger-like equations for the fermionic chiral left and right modes are SUSY partners to preserve isospectrality, we implement a systematical truncation of such potentials. Then, we show that in the quadratic approximation of the deformed AdS model, the fermionic DIS and the proton structure functions $F_{1,2}(q^2,x)$ are identical with the results obtained from the soft wall model. Further, we obtain and compare high-order approximations for the fermionic potentials with the full solution of the deformed AdS model and show, as expected, that as greater the order of approximation, the better are the results for $F_{1,2}(q^2,x)$ when compared with experimental data explicitly for the Bjorken parameter values $x=0.85, 0.75$ and $0.65$.

</details>


### [226] [On Thermodynamic Role of the Gauss-Bonnet Topological Term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12698)
*Marek Liška,David Kubiznak*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文重新审视四维时空中Gauss-Bonnet拓扑项的热力学效应，指出此前关于黑洞熵获得正比于视界欧拉示性数修正的结论依赖于视界上边界作用量的选取；作者找到一种使Wald熵自然重现Jacobson-Myers熵的取法，并证明该拓扑项会改变Hawking-Page相变的临界温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中已有主张认为：四维Gauss-Bonnet项虽不改变运动方程，却会给黑洞熵带来正比于视界欧拉示性数（Euler characteristic）的贡献。作者质疑该结论的稳健性，尤其是其对视界上边界作用量（boundary action）选取的依赖性，并希望厘清该拓扑项是否具有可观测的热力学印记。

Method: 采用解析论证：考察视界处不同边界作用量的选择对熵结论的影响，识别出使Wald熵（Wald entropy）表达式自然给出Jacobson-Myers熵（Jacobson-Myers entropy）的特定选取（且适用于非稳态黑洞）；同时分析光滑外边界对守恒量（conserved quantities）的贡献，并计算Gauss-Bonnet项对Hawking-Page相变临界温度的影响。

Result: 熵修正的结论确实敏感于视界边界作用量的选取；在选定的边界作用量下，Wald熵对非稳态黑洞也自然重现Jacobson-Myers熵。光滑外边界不产生对守恒量的额外贡献，且这一结论与边界条件选取及时空渐近行为无关。此外，Gauss-Bonnet项改变Hawking-Page相变的临界温度；在Gauss-Bonnet耦合常数为正时，于高温高压下出现零阶（zeroth-order）Hawking相变。

Conclusion: 四维Gauss-Bonnet拓扑项的热力学效应取决于视界边界作用量的选择，原则上提供了在引力作用量中探测其存在的途径。尽管讨论集中于四维Gauss-Bonnet情形，相关结果更具一般性，可定性推广至其他Lovelock拉格朗日量（Lovelock Lagrangians）在其相应临界维度（critical dimensions）的情形。

Abstract: We revisit the thermodynamic implications of the Gauss-Bonnet topological term in four spacetime dimensions. It has been previously claimed in the literature that this term, although it does not modify the equations of motion, shifts the black hole entropy by a contribution proportional to the Euler characteristic of the horizon. We argue that this conclusion is sensitive to the choice of the boundary action on the horizon. We then identify a choice for which the Wald entropy expression naturally reproduces the Jacobson-Myers entropy, even for non-stationary black holes. We also show that there are no further contributions to conserved quantities from smooth outer boundaries, regardless of the choice of boundary conditions there and of the asymptotic behaviour of the spacetime. Moreover, we demonstrate that the topological Gauss-Bonnet term changes the critical temperature of the Hawking-Page phase transition, opening, in principle, a way for detecting its presence in the gravitational action. In fact, for a positive Gauss-Bonnet coupling constant, we observe a zeroth-order Hawking phase transition at high temperatures and pressures. While our discussion focuses on the case of the Gauss-Bonnet term in 4D, the results are more general and qualitatively apply to other Lovelock Lagrangians in their respective critical dimensions.

</details>


### [227] [Boundary Formula for Volume Response of Entanglement Wedge Polygons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12715)
*Chen-Te Ma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究 AdS₃ 中纠缠楔多边形（entanglement wedge polygon）真空体积的拓扑性，并确定其对时间对称相干标量物质的半经典二次响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠楔体积的真空部分具有拓扑性，而物质扰动如何在其上产生半经典修正尚需刻画；同时需厘清体积响应与熵响应之间的对应关系。

Method: 在 AdS₃ 中考察六段区间熵（six interval entropies）的对比以构造非负加权能量积分，并与体积所依赖的非加权积分比较；辅以对 dimension-3/2 主算符（primary）的独立 CFT 计算。

Result: 六段熵对比度量的是非负加权能量积分，而体积依赖于非加权积分；在稳定性界之上取负质量平方时，具有相同全局能量二次系数的扰动可通过二阶匹配全部六个真空熵，却给出相反的体积响应；连续统的收窄四元组（quartets）能以受控噪声重构体积响应，且 CFT 计算精确复现多边形棱边公式。

Conclusion: 体积响应不能由熵数据唯一确定，熵与体积对物质扰动的敏感方式存在本质差异；体积响应可通过受控方式重构，并得到独立 CFT 计算的精确支持。

Abstract: The vacuum volume of an entanglement wedge polygon is topological. We determine its semiclassical quadratic response to time-symmetric coherent scalar matter in AdS$_3$. A contrast of six interval entropies measures a nonnegatively weighted energy integral, whereas volume depends on the unweighted integral. For negative mass squared above the stability bound, perturbations with the same quadratic coefficient of global energy can match all six vacuum entropies through second order yet have opposite volume responses. A continuum of narrowing quartets reconstructs the volume response with controlled noise. An independent CFT calculation for a dimension-$3/2$ primary reproduces an exact polygon edge formula.

</details>


### [228] [Kasner Exponents from the Bouncing Singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31699)
*Hyunwoo Oh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 全息共形场论（holographic CFT）的热两点函数可探测黑洞奇点（black hole singularity）；当存在反弹测地线（bouncing geodesic）时，领头阶相关函数在 $t_c$ 附近呈现幂律奇异性，其附加指数 $b_t$ 由奇点附近的 Kasner 指数决定，并给出显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Čeplak 与 Valach）通过数值方法提出了 $b_t$ 与 Kasner 指数的关系式，但仅适用于一类特殊黑洞膜（black brane），对更一般情形缺乏解析公式，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用从奇点反射的波在多大频率推迟相关函数（retarded correlator）中产生的指数小项，推导 $b_t=(d-1)\max\left(\frac{p_i-p_t}{1-p_t},\frac{1}{2}\right)$，其中 $p_t$、$p_i$ 分别为时间与空间方向的 Kasner 指数。

Result: 该公式重现了 Čeplak 与 Valach 对特殊黑洞膜提出的关系；对更一般黑洞膜，公式与多数数值数据吻合，且拟合效果显著优于其关系式的推广；并对 Kasner 指数已知但 $b_t$ 尚未计算的理论给出预测。

Conclusion: $b_t$ 可由奇点附近的 Kasner 指数解析确定，表明热两点函数的奇异性结构直接编码了黑洞奇点的几何信息，所提公式具有较广的适用性。

Abstract: Thermal two-point functions of holographic CFTs can probe the black hole singularity. When a bouncing geodesic exists, the correlator at leading order in $1/N$ develops a singularity $G(t, \vec{x}=0) \propto (t_c-t)^{-2Δ+b_t}$, where $Δ$ is the scaling dimension of the operator. We show that the additional exponent $b_t$ is determined by the Kasner exponents near the singularity. From the wave reflected off the singularity, which gives an exponentially small term in the large-frequency retarded correlator, we derive the formula $b_t=(d-1) \max \left( \frac{p_i-p_t}{1-p_t}, \frac{1}{2} \right)$ for planar black branes in AdS$_{d+1}$, where $p_t$ and $p_i$ are the Kasner exponents of the time and spatial directions. We compare our formula with the numerical values of Čeplak and Valach. It reproduces the relation that they proposed for a special class of black branes. For more general black branes, where no formula had been found, our formula agrees with most of their numerical data and fits them much better than the extension of their relation. We also give predictions for $b_t$ in theories where the Kasner exponents are known, but $b_t$ has not been computed.

</details>


### [229] [M-theory anomaly localization and holographic $c$-extremization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12802)
*Carolina Matté Gregory*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了超对称 $AdS_3\times M_8$（$M_8=P(\mathcal O\oplus\mathcal L)\to B_6$）背景试算反常多项式中三次项与 $C_3\wedge X_8$ 项的贡献，并通过 $c$-extremization 与 $X_8$ push-forward 给出中心电荷及其修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要在弦论/全息框架下确定此类 $AdS_3\times M_8$ 背景对应的中心电荷与反常结构，并厘清各项反常贡献（纯 R 对称、混合 R–Betti、引力反常）的来源与必要性。

Method: 将来自 $C_3$ 的 Abel 流纳入，把射影不变量（projective invariant）$J$ 识别为经 $c$-extremization 消元后出现的分母；通过 $X_8$ 的 push-forward 确定各类反常，并在经典极值点处求值；对三个通量量子化背景（含非多项式解）进行计算，并用直接射影纤维积分（projective fibre integration）与 localization 结果比对。

Result: 恢复了中心电荷形式 $c^{(0)}=-I_3+3I_+I_-/J$，并得到线性于整体未平移通量的中心电荷修正；混合项对两个中心电荷贡献相同且在一般情况下是必需的；在 wrapped-$M5$ $S^4$ bundle 上，相同耦合在 $N^3$ 与 $N$ 阶重现了已知场论反常。

Conclusion: 所提公式能统一描述上述背景的反常与中心电荷修正，并与独立已知结果一致；但将其解释为完整 CFT 中心电荷需满足幺正 SCFT、可归一化真空、合适超对称边界条件、完整的全局流混合扇区及对省略反常扇区的控制等条件，另讨论了条件性的欧几里得相对等变（relative-equivariant）解释。

Abstract: We compute the cubic and $C_3\wedge X_8$ caracteristic class contributions to the trial anomaly polynomial of supersymmetric $AdS_3\times M_8$ backgrounds with $M_8=P(\mathcal O \oplus \mathcal L)\to B_6$. Including the Abelian currents descending from $C_3$, we identify the projective invariant $J$ as the denominator arising from their elimination by $c$-extremization and recover the central charge $c^{(0)}=-I_3+3I_+I_-/J$. The $X_8$ push-forward determines the pure R-symmetry, mixed R--Betti and gravitational anomalies. Evaluating it at the classical extremum gives the associated central-charge corrections, linear in the overall unshifted flux. The mixed term contributes equally to both central charges and is required in general. We evaluate these formulae for three flux-quantized backgrounds, including non-polynomial solutions. Direct projective fibre integration reproduces the mixed coefficient obtained by localization. On the wrapped-$M5$ $S^4$ bundle, the same couplings reproduce the independently known field-theory anomaly through orders $N^3$ and $N$. Their interpretation as full CFT central charges requires a unitary SCFT with a normalizable vacuum, suitable supersymmetric boundary conditions, a complete mixing sector of global currents and control of omitted anomaly sectors. A conditional Euclidean relative-equivariant interpretation is discussed separately.

</details>


### [230] [Global Green-Schwarz response on quaternionic sphere bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12810)
*Yuqi Li,Hao-Yu Sun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文刻画十维物理背景下归一化 Green-Schwarz 相（normalized Green-Schwarz phase）对全局背景数据（global background data）的依赖，指出在特定四元数线丛（quaternionic line bundle）与 Spin(16) 丛背景上，该相加响应等于一个整相对示性数（relative characteristic number）的三分之一（模整数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域反常多项式（local anomaly polynomial）只描述局域变化，而归一化 Green-Schwarz 相还依赖全局背景数据；文中旨在阐明这一全局依赖性。

Method: 研究对象为闭自旋七维流形（closed spin seven-manifold）上的十维四元数线三球丛（three-sphere bundle）；背景保留两个指定 Spin(16) 规范丛与基上的整微分平凡化（integral differential trivialization，含边界识别）。计算结合一般非零的局域 Green-Schwarz 积分与通过十二维 Atiyah-Patodi-Singer 填充（filling）计算的实 eta 不变量（real eta invariant）。

Result: 两项之和确定一个整相对示性数，相加响应为该数的三分之一（模整数）；当基的第三、第四有理上同调群（rational cohomology groups）为零时，响应等于八倍 Crowley-Goette 不变量，且不要求基单连通。以实际四元数线丛校准，在 Sp(2) 上给出相加值三分之一，复现已知的三次根相（third-root phase）；同一固定十维流形上的不同整 String 数据可给出全部三种相值，从而显示响应可探测的全局背景信息。

Conclusion: 物理识别保留既定费米子数据（fermion data），而向整拓扑模形式（integral topological modular forms）与顶点算子超代数（vertex operator superalgebras）的独立应用需要相容的整提升（integral lifts）；该响应计算既不构造微观实现，也不给出完整理论的等价性。

Abstract: Local anomaly polynomials describe local variations, but a normalized Green-Schwarz phase also depends on global background data. We explain this dependence for ten-dimensional three-sphere bundles of quaternionic lines over closed spin seven-manifolds. The backgrounds retain two specified $\mathrm{Spin}(16)$ gauge bundles and an integral differential trivialization on the base, including its boundary identifications. The trivialized class is the sum of half the first Pontryagin class of the tangent bundle and the second Chern class of the quaternionic line's underlying complex bundle. For these backgrounds, the calculation combines a generally nonzero local Green-Schwarz integral with a real eta invariant evaluated by a twelve-dimensional Atiyah-Patodi-Singer filling. Together, these terms determine an integral relative characteristic number; the additive response is one third of that number, modulo integers. When the base's third and fourth rational cohomology groups vanish, the response equals eight times the Crowley-Goette invariant, without assuming that the base is simply connected. A calibration using the actual quaternionic line bundle gives additive value one third on $\mathrm{Sp}(2)$, recovering the known third-root phase. Distinct integral String data on one fixed ten-manifold give all three phase values, showing which global background information the response can detect. The physical identifications retain their prescribed fermion data, while the separate application to integral topological modular forms and vertex operator superalgebras requires compatible integral lifts. The response calculation constructs neither a microscopic realization nor an equivalence of complete theories.

</details>


### [231] [Einstein--Yang--Mills Plane Waves: Uniqueness and an Exact Curvature Double Copy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12204)
*Metin Gurses,Tahsin Cagri Sisman,Bayram Tekin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将 Coleman 的非阿贝尔平面波解（Coleman's non-abelian plane waves）与 Güven 的引力耦合结果推广到 $D$ 维，并研究其在反德西特空间（anti-de Sitter space）及高阶曲率引力中的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Coleman 发现四维 Yang–Mills 理论存在满足线性场方程的纯非阿贝尔平面波，Güven 证明其在引入引力后仍为精确解；本文旨在考察这些波在任意维度和更一般背景下的存在性与唯一性。

Method: 在 $D$ 维中分析 Coleman 型剖面（线性于横向坐标），通过能密度有界性与“黎曼张量（Riemann tensor）等于规范场强的色收缩平方”两个条件刻画其独特性；在反德西特空间中构造 $D\geq4$ 的闭式解族，并在二次（quadratic）与 Gauss–Bonnet 引力中检验奇异性。

Result: Coleman 剖面在规范等价意义下被双重唯一确定：既是唯一具有有界能密度的剖面，也是唯一使完整黎曼张量等于其源规范场强色平方的剖面。反德西特空间中 $D\geq4$ 均存在闭式解族，但此时黎曼张量不能等于该色平方；横向空间为球面（sphere）的波恒为奇异。在二次与 Gauss–Bonnet 引力中，仅当波使光散焦（defocus light）或高阶曲率项与 Einstein 项量级相当时，双曲横向空间（hyperbolic transverse space）才可避免奇异。

Conclusion: 这些解可经系数调整推广到一般引力与规范理论（generic gravity and gauge theories），表明 Coleman 型波解在更广的维度与理论框架中具有结构上的稳健性。

Abstract: Coleman found plane waves of Yang--Mills theory that are genuinely non-abelian in four dimensions and yet obey linear field equations, and Güven showed that they remain exact when gravity is included. We study these waves in $D$ dimensions. Coleman's profile, which is linear in the transverse coordinates, is singled out twice: up to gauge, it is the only profile with bounded energy density, and the only one for which the full Riemann tensor equals the color-contracted square of the Yang--Mills field strength that sources it. In anti-de Sitter space, we construct a closed-form family for every $D\geq4$; there, the full Riemann tensor cannot equal this color square. Waves whose transverse space is a sphere are always singular. In quadratic and Gauss--Bonnet gravity, a hyperbolic transverse space avoids this only if the waves defocus light or the higher-curvature terms become comparable to the Einstein term. All solutions extend, with changed coefficients, to generic gravity and gauge theories.

</details>


<div id='physics.plasm-ph'></div>

# physics.plasm-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [A Mathematical Multiscale Framework for Nonlinear Modeling of the Heliosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11999)
*Daniele Telloni,Sabrina Guastavino,Michele Piana*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一个融合全球日球层大尺度结构与局地湍流物理的数学与数值框架，通过在双峰确定性背景流上叠加随机驱动的非线性湍流级联，生成高度逼真的合成连续介质，为未来多航天器日球层探测任务提供计算高效的解析基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内日球层是宏观理想磁流体力学与微观充分发展随机湍流强耦合的高度非线性介质，而现有全球三维数值求解器受网格分辨率与偏微分方程形式的根本限制，无法解析小尺度湍流级联、磁间歇性及局地热力学异常，由此形成尺度分离鸿沟。

Method: 在双峰确定性背景流（bimodal deterministic background flow）上叠加随机驱动的非线性湍流级联，采用离散乘性二项级联（discrete multiplicative binomial cascade，p-model）复现分数幂谱密度、磁压缩性与小尺度间歇性的理论约束；并以解析模型施加动力扰动，包括运动学激波陡化、连续电流片拓扑反转，以及形式化为非对称无力磁通量绳的太阳抛射物。

Result: 该统一模型可在任意分布坐标上生成精确的时空数组，克服单点观测的空间简并性，并提供计算高效的解析基线，用于支撑处于可行性研究阶段的原位多航天器日球层任务的性能模型。

Conclusion: 通过将全球日球层结构与局地湍流物理协调整合，该框架弥合了大尺度与微观尺度之间的分离鸿沟，为多航天器观测数据的合成与任务性能评估提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The inner heliosphere is a highly nonlinear continuous medium where macroscopic ideal magnetohydrodynamics is inextricably coupled with microscopic, fully developed stochastic turbulence. State-of-the-art global 3D numerical solvers, while proficient at capturing the large-scale propagation of extreme space physics phenomena such as coronal mass ejections and corotating interaction regions, are computationally constrained by grid resolution and fundamental partial differential equation formulations. Consequently, they inherently do not resolve the local small-scale turbulent cascade, magnetic intermittency, and localized thermodynamic anomalies. To bridge this fundamental scaleseparation gap, this paper presents a novel mathematical and numerical framework that harmoniously integrates global heliospheric structures with local turbulent physics. The framework generates a highly realistic synthetic continuous medium by superimposing a stochastically driven nonlinear turbulent cascade onto a bimodal deterministic background flow. This cascade reproduces theoretical constraints for fractional power spectral densities, magnetic compressibility, and small-scale intermittency via a discrete multiplicative binomial cascade (p-model). This multiscale continuous background is dynamically perturbed by analytical models of large-scale nonlinear transients, including kinematic shock steepening, continuous current sheet topological reversals, and solar ejecta formalized as asymmetric force-free magnetic flux ropes. By generating exact spatio-temporal arrays across arbitrary distributed coordinates, this unified mathematical model overcomes the spatial degeneracy of single-point observations and provides a computationally efficient analytical baseline for performance models supporting future in-situ multi-spacecraft heliospheric missions, which are currently in their feasibility study phase.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [DFT-Agent Bench: Scaffold Effects and Execution Provenance in Autonomous DFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10629)
*Shijie Liu,Jin Yang,Weilin Wan,Jingtao Han,Jia LI,Tianqi Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 DFT-Agent Bench，一个包含 200 余个 Quantum ESPRESSO 任务的基准，用于评测大语言模型作为自主智能体执行第一性原理材料模拟的能力，并比较 ReAct 基线（baseline）与完整 v5.1 脚手架（scaffold）的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被日益用作第一性原理材料模拟的自主智能体（autonomous agent），但缺乏覆盖多类 DFT 流水线、且能核验执行契约（execution contract）的实证评测基准。

Method: 构建含 200+ Quantum ESPRESSO 任务的基准，覆盖结构弛豫（relaxation）、电子结构（electronic structure）、状态方程（equation-of-state）与弹性张量（elastic-tensor）等流水线；每个任务配有基于 QE 的基准真值（ground truth）和来源审计（provenance audit）。在统一执行框架（execution harness）下，于两个模型上比较近裸 ReAct 基线与打包的 v5.1 脚手架，并做固定 100 任务、禁用检索（retrieval）的分析。

Result: 完整脚手架使能带+DOS 提升 +0.08/+0.11、d 电子 vc-relax 提升 +0.17（两个模型一致）；高分弛豫与状态方程单元提升空间有限；弹性结果依赖模型（deepseek-v4-pro 0.18→0.40，claude-opus-4-8 0.62→0.59）。冻结 100 任务分析支持总体趋势：DeepSeek 提升 +0.17，Opus 相对历史池基线提升 +0.10（p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: 来源门控（provenance gate）可识别可能获得高评分但不完整或未标注的执行，其定位是执行契约检查而非欺诈检测；结果刻画的是所评测的 DFT 工作流，匿名评审材料含冻结诊断切片、提示词、评分器与求解器版本。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents for first-principles materials simulation. We report empirical findings from DFT-Agent Bench, a benchmark of 200+ Quantum ESPRESSO tasks spanning relaxation, electronic structure, equation-of-state, and elastic-tensor pipelines over multiple material tiers. Each task carries a QE-derived ground truth with public-reference cross-checks where available, and a provenance audit checks whether the required QE execution contract was met. Under one execution harness, we compare a near-bare ReAct baseline with a bundled v5.1 scaffold on two models. The complete scaffold improves bands+DOS by +0.08/+0.11 and d-electron vc-relax by +0.17 on both models, while high-scoring relaxation and equation-of-state cells leave little room for improvement. The elastic result is model-dependent (0.18 to 0.40 for deepseek-v4-pro and 0.62 to 0.59 for claude-opus-4-8). A fixed 100-task analysis with retrieval disabled supports the aggregate pattern: the fully frozen DeepSeek comparison gains +0.17, while the Opus comparison gains +0.10 against a historical pool baseline (p < 0.001). The provenance gate catches incomplete or unmarked executions that can receive high rubric scores; it is an execution-contract check, not a test for intentional fraud. These results characterize the evaluated DFT workflows. The anonymous review artifact contains the frozen diagnostic slice, prompts, scorer, and solver revisions: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/231e31re91287h/README.md.

</details>


### [234] [Benchmarking language models and agents for scientific reasoning in evolving materials laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12014)
*Xuyu Dong,Yixuan Li,Jianguo Huang,Feng Chen,Yiyun Zhou,Bo An,Zheng Liu,Da Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MatScope 基准用于评估大语言模型（large language models）及多智能体系统（multi-agent systems）在材料科学中区分相近材料状态与定量推理的能力，涵盖无辅助推理、智能体辅助知识迁移与渐进观测下的预测修正三类任务，结果显示细粒度估计与定量比较仍是显著短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（benchmark）已覆盖领域知识、多模态解读与科学工具使用，但模型区分相近材料状态、基于定量证据进行准确推理的能力评估不足，而这对于比较材料响应、评估机制及随实验证据累积维持可靠判断至关重要。

Method: 提出 MatScope 评估三项相互关联的能力：M1 通过覆盖十类研究功能的 200 个任务测试无辅助、特定条件下的推理，以铝合金与高熵合金（high-entropy alloys）为代表性材料体系；M2 测试智能体辅助下相关知识向未解决材料问题的迁移；MatEvoBench-20 测试跨科学依赖链（scientific dependency chains）的初始预测及随观测累积对仍隐藏响应的修正，共覆盖十一种模型部署。

Result: 给定输入的计算与多数物理解释表现良好；细粒度响应估计、相近状态比较及有定量依据的选择明显较弱。相关文献可改善部分估计，但条件与基线不匹配限制了迁移；渐进观测可校准部分下游响应，同时会使先前准确的预测变得不稳定。

Conclusion: MatScope 为未来自演化材料实验室（self-evolving materials laboratories）与材料感知世界模型（materials-aware world models）中可靠科学推理的发展确立了可度量目标。

Abstract: Large language models and the multi-agent systems built around them could accelerate materials discovery by connecting scientific knowledge with experimental decisions. Existing benchmarks establish capabilities in domain knowledge, multimodal interpretation and scientific tool use. As language models increasingly support materials research workflows, their ability to distinguish closely related material states and reason accurately with quantitative evidence remains insufficiently evaluated. These abilities are essential for comparing material responses, assessing mechanisms and maintaining reliable judgements as experimental evidence accumulates. We introduce MatScope to evaluate three connected capabilities. M1 tests unaided, condition-specific reasoning through 200 tasks spanning ten research functions, using aluminium and high-entropy alloys as representative material systems. M2 tests agent-assisted transfer of related knowledge to unresolved materials questions. MatEvoBench-20 tests initial forecasts across scientific dependency chains and revisions of still-hidden responses as observations accumulate. Across eleven model deployments, including GPT-6 Astra and Claude Fable 5.1, given-input calculations and many physical explanations are handled well. Fine-grained response estimates, comparisons between similar states and quantitatively supported choices remain substantially weaker. Related literature improves selected estimates, while condition and baseline mismatches limit transfer. Progressive observations calibrate some downstream responses and destabilize earlier accurate predictions. MatScope establishes measurable targets for developing reliable scientific reasoning in future self-evolving materials laboratories and materials-aware world models.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [Linear response in generalized Lotka--Volterra systems with a finite number of species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12074)
*Koya Katayama,Sosuke Ito*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该文推导出适用于任意有限物种数、不依赖随机性假设的雅可比矩阵数值范围与谱的几何界，并将长期稳定性与短期稳定性同丰度加权相互作用的对称分量联系起来，将最大振荡频率同反对称分量联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory, RMT）被广泛用于预测大型随机生态系统的响应，但其预测可能无法准确描述单个有限生态系统。

Method: 在不假设随机性的前提下，推导对任意有限物种数均成立的雅可比矩阵（Jacobian matrices）数值范围与谱（spectra）的严格几何界（geometric bounds）；并将这些界应用于随机系综，以标准RMT参数给出系综平均的约束。

Result: 这些界将长期稳定性与短期稳定性关联到丰度加权相互作用（abundance-weighted interactions）的对称分量（symmetric component），并以反对称分量（skew-symmetric component）约束最大振荡频率；应用于海洋食物网（marine food webs）实证数据时，即便基于RMT的预测失效，这些界仍能对短期稳定性和最大振荡频率给出有信息量的约束。

Conclusion: 研究确立了局域生态系统响应的结构性约束（structural constraints），作为基于RMT的统计预测对大型随机生态系统的补充。

Abstract: Understanding how species interactions shape ecosystem responses to perturbations is a central challenge in ecology. Random matrix theory (RMT) has been widely used to predict the responses of large random ecosystems, but its predictions may not accurately describe individual finite ecosystems. Here, we derive rigorous geometric bounds on the numerical ranges and spectra of Jacobian matrices that are valid for any finite number of species without assuming randomness. These bounds link long-term stability and short-term stability to the symmetric component of abundance-weighted interactions, and constrain the maximum oscillation frequency by the skew-symmetric component. Applied to random ensembles, the bounds also yield constraints on ensemble averages of long-term stability, short-term stability, and the maximum oscillation frequency in terms of standard RMT parameters. Using empirical data from marine food webs, we show that our bounds provide informative constraints on short-term stability and the maximum oscillation frequency even where RMT-based predictions fail. These results establish structural constraints on local ecosystem responses that complement RMT-based statistical predictions for large random ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [$λ_g$-Integrals on triple, one-part admissible covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12450)
*Renzo Cavalieri,David Klompenhouwer,Paolo Rossi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文计算了亏格为 g 的可容许覆盖（admissible covers）模空间上形如 λ_gψ^d 的全部交数（intersection numbers），并将其编码为与 KdV 层级（KdV hierarchy）哈密顿量相关的生成函数（generating functions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要仅说明研究目标是有效计算可容许覆盖模空间上的交数并建立其与可积系统的联系，未明确阐述问题背景或动机。

Method: 研究对象为带一个全分歧点（full ramification）与两个任意分歧型（profile）分支点的亏格 g 可容许覆盖模空间；将交数不变量编码于适当的生成函数中，并与 KdV 层级（KdV hierarchy）的哈密顿量（Hamiltonians）建立关系。

Result: 得到形如 λ_gψ^d 的全部交数（intersection numbers）的有效计算，并给出其生成函数表述及其与 KdV 层级哈密顿量的联系。

Conclusion: 可容许覆盖模空间上的此类交数可由生成函数统一刻画，且该生成函数属于 KdV 层级的哈密顿量体系，提示这些不变量与可积层级（integrable hierarchy）之间存在结构对应。

Abstract: We effectively compute all intersection numbers of the form $λ_gψ^d$ on moduli spaces of genus $g$ admissible covers with one point of full ramification and two additional branch points with arbitrary profile. We encode these invariants in appropriate generating functions, which we relate to the Hamiltonians of the KdV hierarchy.

</details>


<div id='physics.hist-ph'></div>

# physics.hist-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Prestatability in the Macroscopic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09989)
*Andrea Palessandro*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 论文探讨宏观描述所需变量能否被事先确定，区分"形式可预先陈述性"与"动力学可预先陈述性"，并证明在无限微观态空间极限下，候选变量的未来相关性等价于停机问题，因此固定微观相空间可支持未来变量无法完全预先陈述的宏观描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察一个物理系统的宏观描述（macroscopic description）所需变量能否在事先被陈述，这一问题关系到宏观描述的可预测性与还原论假设的边界。

Method: 将宏观描述表示为固定微观相空间（microscopic phase space）的粗粒化（coarse-graining），把问题拆分为形式可预先陈述性（formal prestatability）与动力学可预先陈述性（dynamical prestatability）两类障碍；在有限与无限微观态空间两种情形下分析，并构造一种受限的预测架构（restricted predictive architecture）。

Result: 两类障碍在有限微观态空间中均消失；在无限极限（infinite limit）下，穷尽性的形式可预先陈述性可能失效，而受限预测架构仍可保持形式可预先陈述。在普适动力学（universal dynamics）下，候选变量的未来相关性被证明等价于停机问题（halting problem）。

Conclusion: 固定微观相空间可以支持未来变量无法完全预先陈述的宏观描述，表明宏观描述的可预先陈述性存在根本性的计算限制。

Abstract: We examine whether the variables required for a future macroscopic description of a given physical system can themselves be stated in advance. Representing macroscopic descriptions as coarse-grainings of a fixed microscopic phase space, we distinguish formal prestatability, concerning the existence of an effective complete vocabulary of admissible macroscopic distinctions, from dynamical prestatability, concerning whether the future relevance of those distinctions is decidable. Both obstructions disappear for finite microscopic state spaces. In the infinite limit, exhaustive formal prestatability can fail, while a restricted predictive architecture may remain formally pre-statable. We construct such an architecture and show that, under universal dynamics, the future relevance of a candidate variable is equivalent to the halting problem. Thus a fixed microscopic phase space can support macroscopic descriptions whose future variables are not fully pre-statable.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.TR'></div>

# q-fin.TR [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [Is price-impact decay statistically identifiable by propagator estimation? Diffusive prices force the inferred kernel to be transient and fine-tuned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10574)
*Shunta Fujiwara,Yuki Sato,Kiyoshi Kanazawa*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 该文证明了一个不可行定理（no-go theorem）：对于扩散型价格（diffusive prices），渐近价格冲击衰减无法通过传播子估计（propagator estimation）进行统计识别；所谓"精细平衡衰减"实为长记忆订单流下Wiener–Hopf反卷积的固有性质，而非真实冲击衰减的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经济学物理学（econophysics）中，市场订单流（market-order flow）具有长记忆（long memory）而价格动态却呈扩散行为，这一"扩散之谜（diffusivity puzzle）"长期未解；传播子模型（propagator model）被视为有希望的解决方案，其数据推断结果被报告与"冲击衰减与订单流记忆精细平衡"一致，作者旨在检验该推断是否有统计依据。

Method: 基于长记忆订单流下Wiener–Hopf反卷积（Wiener–Hopf deconvolution）的普适定理，给出两条结论：（i）推断的传播子必为瞬态（transient），即使真实冲击为永久性（permanent）；（ii）在温和条件下，其按细平衡指数（fine-balance exponent）衰减，该指数仅由订单流自相关决定。并构造一个保留订单拆分（order splitting）与平方根律（square-root law）但冲击严格永久的可精确求解零模型（null model）作为演示。

Result: 零模型的推断传播子呈现人为的精细平衡衰减，而真实冲击从未衰减；说明此类衰减来自反卷积而非冲击衰减的证据。作者进一步指出，非线性传播子估计因属Wiener–Hopf类型，只要真实metaorder具长记忆便同样受该定理约束。

Conclusion: 该文提出的不可行定理（no-go theorem）表明，渐近冲击衰减对传播子估计不可统计识别，传播子模型的"精细平衡"一致性不能作为冲击衰减的证据，从而对扩散之谜的既有解释构成根本性挑战。

Abstract: Despite the long memory of market-order flow, price dynamics remain diffusive---a seemingly paradoxical coexistence known in econophysics as the diffusivity puzzle. The propagator model has long been regarded as a promising solution: price impact decays in time, finely balanced against the order-flow memory, and propagators inferred from data have been reported to be consistent with this fine balance. Here we prove a no-go theorem: asymptotic impact decay is not statistically identifiable by propagator estimation, since for diffusive prices the fine-tuned decay is returned whether or not the true impact decays. It rests on universal theorems for the Wiener--Hopf deconvolution by which propagators are inferred under long-memory order flow: (i) the inferred propagator is necessarily transient, even when the true impact is permanent; (ii) under a mild condition, it decays with the fine-balance exponent, fixed by the order-flow autocorrelation alone. The fine-tuned decay is thus a property of the deconvolution, not evidence of impact decay. We demonstrate this with an exactly solvable null model that retains order splitting and the square-root law but whose impact is strictly permanent: the inferred propagator shows an artificial fine-tuned decay although the true impact never decays. Nonlinear propagator estimation, being of Wiener--Hopf type, is subject to the same theorems whenever true metaorders have long memory.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.other'></div>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [Spin Conductivity: Insight From Perturbation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11077)
*Xu Chen,Mingbo Dou,Yu Sui,Xianjie Wang,L. L. Tao*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文在微扰理论框架下给出自旋电导率（spin conductivity）的一个替代性且基础的推导，其结果与Kubo线性响应理论及量子动理学理论一致，并阐明了时间反演奇偶分量分别来源于零阶与一阶波函数修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋电导率研究在交错磁体（altermagnetic materials）等新兴材料中日益活跃，但既有推导多依赖Kubo线性响应（Kubo linear response）或量子动理学（quantum kinetic theory）等较复杂形式体系，需要一个更基础、更直观的推导以提供物理洞见。

Method: 采用微扰理论（perturbation theory）进行替代性且基础的推导，分别考察时间反演偶（$\mathcal{T}$-even）与时间反演奇（$\mathcal{T}$-odd）自旋电导率，并追踪其与波函数修正阶次的对应关系。

Result: 所推导的$\mathcal{T}$-偶自旋电导率表达式与Kubo线性响应理论和量子动理学理论的结果完全一致；清洁极限（clean limit）下$\mathcal{T}$-奇自旋电导率表达式与已有结果精确吻合。分析表明，$\mathcal{T}$-奇分量来自零阶波函数修正的贡献，$\mathcal{T}$-偶分量来自一阶波函数修正的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作从微扰理论角度为自旋电导率提供了物理洞见，并有助于加深对磁性材料中电荷-自旋转换（charge-to-spin conversion）机制的理解，但摘要未给出具体数值或实验验证。

Abstract: The investigation on the spin conductivity has now been blooming in burgeoning altermagnetic materials. Here, an alternative and elementary derivation for the spin conductivity expression is presented. This is done within the context of perturbation theory. It indicates that our derived expression for the $\mathcal{T}$-even ($\mathcal{T}$ for time reversal) spin conductivity agrees exactly with those derived from the Kubo linear response theory and quantum kinetic theory. In addition, the expression for the $\mathcal{T}$-odd spin conductivity in the clean limit is exactly consistent with those presented previously. We show that the $\mathcal{T}$-odd ($\mathcal{T}$-even) spin conductivity arises from the contribution of zeroth-order (first-order) wave function correction. Our work provides some physical insight into the spin conductivity from the perturbation theory and shines more light on the charge-to-spin conversion mechanism in magnetic materials.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Interpretable machine learning of formation energies and band gaps in hybrid perovskites through SHAP analysis and SISSO modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09944)
*Christopher Xia,Hao Zhang,Qingyuan Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究将XGBoost机器学习筛选、SHAP分析与SISSO描述符发现相结合，对4,456种杂化有机-无机钙钛矿（hybrid organic-inorganic perovskites）的形成能与带隙进行高精度预测，并将其推广至18万余候选化合物，同时给出可解释的四描述符解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确且可解释的性质预测（property prediction）对探索杂化有机-无机钙钛矿至关重要，而单纯的高精度黑箱模型难以揭示结构与性质之间的关系。

Method: 基于4,456种化合物构建优化XGBoost模型，结合SHAP分析与基于相关性的特征剪枝，并用SISSO进行描述符发现（descriptor discovery）；同时将XGBoost与CGCNN、UPET在约17,000种共有化合物上进行比较。

Result: XGBoost测试集R²在形成能上达0.99、带隙上达0.93，并应用于逾180,000个候选物；XGBoost与CGCNN预测分布大体重叠，而UPET预测显著偏低，提示其迁移性（transferability）存在局限。每类性质经筛选保留8个主要特征，SISSO由此导出含四个非线性描述符的表达式。

Conclusion: 所得表达式对形成能耦合分子与无机性质，对带隙则强调相对原子尺寸与B位特征；该框架可支持候选化合物的高效优先排序（candidate prioritization）及对杂化钙钛矿结构-性质关系的可解释分析。

Abstract: Accurate and interpretable property prediction is essential for exploring hybrid organic-inorganic perovskites. Here, we combine machine-learning screening with SHAP analysis and SISSO descriptor discovery. Using 4,456 compounds, optimized XGBoost models achieved test-set $R^2$ values of 0.99 for formation energy and 0.93 for band gap and were applied to over 180,000 candidates. Comparison across approximately 17,000 common compounds revealed broadly overlapping distributions from XGBoost and CGCNN but substantially lower predictions from UPET, suggesting potential limitations in its transferability to hybrid organic-inorganic perovskites. SHAP analysis and correlation-based pruning retained eight primary features for each property, from which SISSO derived expressions comprising four nonlinear descriptors. These expressions couple molecular and inorganic properties for formation energy and emphasize relative atomic sizes and B-site characteristics for band gap. This framework supports efficient candidate prioritization and interpretable analysis of learned structures-property relationships in hybrid perovskites.

</details>


### [241] [Ultrafast Liquid Jet X-ray Total Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12290)
*Adam F. Sapnik,Philip A. Chater,Dean S. Keeble,Tobias A. Bird,John S. O. Evans,Federica Bertolotti,Antonietta Guagliardi,Norberto Masciocchi,Daniel T. Bowron,Lise J. Støckler,Elodie A. Harbourne,Anders B. Borup,Rebecca S. Silberg,Viktoria A. Kaznelson,Stefanie Punke,Mariia Svyrydenko,Maryna I. Bodnarchuk,Dmitry N. Dirin,Simon C. Boehme,Maksym V. Kovalenko,Bo B. Iversen,Emil S. Bozin,Kirsten M. Ø. Jensen,Gabriele Ansaldi,Karen Appel,Alexander Gierke,Jörg Hallmann,Aliaksandr Leonau,Anders Madsen,Johannes Möller,Jan-Etienne Pudell,Angel Rodriguez-Fernandez,Andreas Schmidt,Roman Shayduk,Cornelius Strohm,Joana Valerio,Ulrike Boesenberg,David A. Keen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作基于欧洲XFEL的MID装置，从毛细管与液体射流样品获得高质量总散射数据，并实现液相中泵浦—探测总散射测量与溶剂结构的三维映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线自由电子激光（XFEL）的时间分辨能力可将总散射与对分布函数（PDF）分析拓展至飞秒至皮秒尺度的结构动力学研究，但需将此前单脉冲高质量数据获取推进到溶液样品的时间分辨实验。

Method: 在欧洲XFEL的材料成像与动力学（MID）仪器上，采集Q_max达16.4 Å^-1的总散射数据，样品包括常规毛细管和液体射流（liquid jet）；对CsPbBr3纳米晶实施泵浦—探测（pump-probe）总散射，并对水相Keggin簇数据做经验势结构精修（EPSR）。

Result: 尽管存在较大的空气与溶剂背景，仍从液体分散体系与溶液中分子簇恢复出可用于定量结构分析的PDF；泵浦—探测测量分辨出与通量相关的光致各向同性晶格应变；EPSR揭示Keggin簇的位点特异性水合环境并实现周围溶剂结构的三维映射。

Conclusion: 该进展为追踪溶液中超快结构变化以及支配分子行为与反应性的溶剂环境演化提供了可行途径。

Abstract: X-ray free-electron lasers (XFELs) provide the temporal resolution needed to extend total scattering and pair distribution function (PDF) analysis to investigate structural dynamics on femtosecond-to-picosecond timescales. We recently demonstrated that high-quality total scattering data and PDFs can be obtained from individual XFEL pulses; here, we take the next step towards time-resolved studies of structural dynamics in solution. Using the Materials Imaging and Dynamics (MID) instrument at the European XFEL, we obtain total scattering data up to $Q_{\max} = 16.4\,\mathring{\mathrm{A}}^{-1}$ from both conventional capillary samples and, critically, from samples delivered by a liquid jet. Despite the large air and solvent backgrounds inherent to this setup, PDFs suitable for quantitative structural analysis are recovered from liquid dispersions and molecular clusters in solution. We further demonstrate pump-probe total scattering from $\mathrm{CsPbBr}_3$ nanocrystals in a liquid jet, resolving a fluence-dependent photoinduced isotropic lattice strain. Additionally, empirical potential structure refinement (EPSR) of XFEL total scattering data from an aqueous Keggin cluster reveals site-specific hydration environments and enables three-dimensional mapping of the surrounding solvent structure. Together, these advances open the door to tracking not only ultrafast structural changes in solution, but also the evolving solvent environments that govern molecular behaviour and reactivity.

</details>


### [242] [Controlled Oxidation of Tellurium Nanoparticles for Telecom Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10135)
*Biman Jana*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过常温空气渐进氧化（ambient-air exposure）制备溶液法碲（Te）纳米颗粒聚集体，发现其表面氧化程度低于此前强制空气氧化样品，器件在405、532、1550 nm均具光响应，最高响应度分别为10.1、14.4、8.5 mA/W，并给出1550 nm下17.7/40.2 s的上升/下降时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面氧化（surface oxidation）在半导体纳米颗粒聚集体中显著影响电荷输运，需要阐明氧化程度对颗粒间输运与暗电流抑制之间平衡的作用，以指导溶液法碲基光探测器（photodetector）的设计。

Method: 采用溶液法（solution-processed）制备碲纳米颗粒聚集体，并通过常温空气逐步暴露实现可控氧化；结合结构与光谱分析表征表面物种组成，并制备光探测器测试其光电性能。

Result: 结构与光谱分析显示颗粒相互连接，表面共存α-TeO2与含羟基（hydroxyl）物种，氧化态碲的相对贡献低于既往强制空气氧化样品；器件在405、532、1550 nm均响应，最高响应度依次为10.1、14.4、8.5 mA/W；1550 nm下上升/下降时间为17.7/40.2 s，散粒噪声限比探测率（shot-noise-limited specific detectivity）估计为1.3×10^6 Jones。

Conclusion: 结果表明控制表面氧化对平衡颗粒间电荷输运与暗电流（dark current）抑制至关重要，为面向短波红外（short-wave infrared）应用的溶液法碲基光探测器开发提供了依据。

Abstract: Surface oxidation plays an important role in governing charge transport in semiconductor nanoparticle assemblies. Here, we investigate the optoelectronic properties of solution-processed tellurium (Te) nanoparticle ensembles prepared through gradual ambient-air exposure. Structural and spectroscopic analyses reveal interconnected elemental Te nanoparticles with coexisting alpha-TeO2 and hydroxyl-containing surface species, exhibiting a lower relative contribution of oxidized Te than previously investigated forced-air-oxidized ensembles. The resulting photodetectors demonstrate photoresponse at 405, 532, and 1550 nm, with maximum responsivities of 10.1, 14.4, and 8.5 mA/W, respectively. At 1550 nm, the device exhibits rise and fall times of 17.7 and 40.2 s, respectively, and an estimated shot-noise-limited specific detectivity of 1.3 * 10^6 Jones. These findings highlight the importance of controlling surface oxidation to balance interparticle charge transport and dark-current suppression, offering insights for the development of solution-processed Te-based photodetectors for short-wave infrared applications.

</details>


### [243] [Hidden memory above the Neel temperature programs antiferromagnetic order in Cr2O3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10173)
*Josiah Keagy,Sumukh Mahesh,Wei-Cheng Liao,Jing Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在Cr2O3中，于奈尔温度（Néel temperature, T_N）以上的顺磁态施加足够强电场可写入一种长寿命的可逆内建电偏置，随后无电场磁冷即得到与常规磁电冷却方向相反的单畴态，说明电编程记忆与反铁磁畴选择之间存在耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁序（antiferromagnetic order）调控对磁电与反铁磁自旋电子器件至关重要，但Cr2O3等原型磁电反铁磁体（magnetoelectric antiferromagnet）的畴选择通常依赖在T_N附近或T_N以下同时施加电场与磁场，途径受限。

Method: 测量块体Cr2O3晶体在不同热历史与场历史下的磁电系数（magnetoelectric coefficient, α_zz），并在T_N以上仅以电场进行“ conditioning”，随后进行无电场的磁场冷却。

Result: T_N以上的强电场可建立一个撤场后仍保留的可逆状态；随后的无电场磁冷产生单畴态，其奈尔矢量（Néel vector）取向与常规磁电场冷却所得相反。该电致状态可反复写入、擦除并在高温下弛豫。

Conclusion: 观测结果与一种亚稳的表面相关极性态（metastable surface-associated polar state）相符，该极性态可能提供内建偏置；由此揭示Cr2O3中电编程记忆与反铁磁畴形成之间存在非预期耦合。

Abstract: Control of antiferromagnetic order is central to magnetoelectric and antiferromagnetic spintronic devices. In the prototypical magnetoelectric antiferromagnet Cr2O3, domains are conventionally selected by simultaneous application of electric and magnetic fields during cooling through the Neel temperature (T_N) or at a fixed temperature below T_N. Here, we show that antiferromagnetic order can instead be programmed by electric-field conditioning entirely in the paramagnetic state above T_N. By measuring the magnetoelectric coefficient (alpha_zz) of bulk Cr2O3 crystals under different thermal and field histories, we find that a sufficiently large electric field above T_N establishes a reversible state that persists after the field is removed. Subsequent magnetic-field cooling with no applied electric field produces a single-domain state with the opposite Neel-vector orientation from conventional magnetoelectric field cooling. The electrically induced state can be reversibly written, erased, and allowed to relax at high temperatures, demonstrating a long-lived internal electric bias that governs domain selection. The observed behavior is consistent with a metastable surface-associated polar state that may generate this internal bias. These results reveal an unexpected coupling between electrically programmed memory and antiferromagnetic domain formation in Cr2O3.

</details>


### [244] [Temperature-Driven Evolution of Magnetization Dynamics in Cobalt-Doped Fe$_5$GeTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10225)
*Sourabh Singh,Soumik Aon,Andrew F. May,Joost Ridderbos,Ramanathan Mahendiran,Wilfred G. van der Wiel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用宽频铁磁共振（FMR）研究钴掺杂 Fe5GeTe2 单晶的温度依赖磁化动力学，发现约200 K以下出现双模响应与动态交叉，该现象在静态磁化测量中不明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属性范德华铁磁体（van der Waals ferromagnets）的磁化动力学对其磁相互作用理解及高频自旋电子学（spintronics）应用评估具有重要意义，而化学掺杂体系中的动态行为尚待厘清。

Method: 采用宽频铁磁共振谱（broadband ferromagnetic resonance, FMR）测量钴掺杂 Fe5GeTe2（CFGT）单晶的变温磁化动力学，并对共振场与线宽的频率依赖进行拟合，提取有效 g 因子、Gilbert 阻尼参数与有效磁各向异性。

Result: 室温附近有效 g 因子约 2.45、有效 Gilbert 阻尼参数约 0.15；有效磁各向异性随降温显著演化，向 200 K 时易面（easy-plane）贡献增强；约 200 K 以下出现额外共振并发展为持续至 25 K 的双模响应，两支共振场与线宽的温度依赖不同，且静态磁化中未见相应显著异常。

Conclusion: CFGT 中存在体磁强计（bulk magnetometry）难以察觉的动态交叉（dynamical crossover），表明 FMR 对化学取代范德华铁磁体的磁行为具有高敏感性。

Abstract: Understanding magnetization dynamics in metallic van der Waals ferromagnets is essential to uncover their magnetic interactions and evaluate their potential for high-frequency spintronics applications. In this study, we investigate the temperature-dependent magnetization dynamics of a single crystal of cobalt-doped $Fe_{5}GeTe_{2}$ (CFGT) using broadband ferromagnetic resonance (FMR) spectroscopy. At high temperatures, the FMR response is dominated by a single broad resonance that shifts toward lower magnetic fields upon cooling. Fits to the frequency dependence of the resonance field and linewidth yield an effective $g$ factor of approximately 2.45 and an effective Gilbert damping parameter of approximately 0.15 near room temperature. The effective magnetic anisotropy evolves strongly with temperature, with an increasingly pronounced easy-plane contribution upon cooling toward 200 K. Below approximately 200 K, an additional resonance emerges and evolves into a distinct two-mode response that persists to the lowest measured temperature of 25 K. The two branches exhibit different temperature dependences of their resonance fields and linewidths, and the emergence of the additional mode is not accompanied by a comparably pronounced anomaly in the static magnetization. These results reveal a dynamical crossover in CFGT that is not readily apparent from bulk magnetometry and highlight the sensitivity of FMR to the magnetic behavior of chemically substituted van der Waals ferromagnets.

</details>


### [245] [A Transport-Constrained Gradient-Flow Description of Continuum Dislocation Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10232)
*Paria Behnoud,Anthony Rollett,Carlos Mora-Corral,Kaushik Dayal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文为连续位错动力学（continuum dislocation dynamics）建立了满足输运约束（transport-constrained）的梯度流（gradient flow）表述，使用约束 Onsager 原理选取输运速度，从而兼具变分梯度流结构与几何位错守恒一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度流框架是缺陷与微结构演化模型的基础，但既有位错演化模型通常只具备变分梯度流结构或几何守恒性中的一项，缺乏同时满足两者的表述。

Method: 以位错密度（dislocation density）与相容滑移场（compatible slip field）为主状态变量，用几何守恒律限制其容许速率，并通过约束 Onsager 原理（constrained Onsager principle）变分地选取输运速度。

Result: 所得模型同时具有变分梯度流结构与几何位错守恒的一致性，其热力学结构给出的位错驱动力包含弹性应力贡献（对应经典 Peach–Koehler 力）以及芯部能量与非凸晶格能（nonconvex lattice energy）的贡献。

Conclusion: 对反号位错对（opposite-sign dislocation pair）的简化模型研究表明，晶格能维持芯部致密，外加剪应力可促进湮灭或在超过阈值时使位错对分离。

Abstract: Gradient flow frameworks are fundamental to numerous models of defect and microstructure evolution. This work develops a gradient flow formulation for continuum dislocation dynamics, with the key distinction that the admissible rates are transport-constrained. Using the dislocation density and compatible slip fields as the primary state variables, their admissible rates are restricted by geometric conservation laws. The corresponding transport velocities are then selected variationally through a constrained Onsager principle.
