Comparison with isostructural NaCl connects these extended interactions to local underscreening of predominantly Coulombic forces. Our results provide a key microscopic description of finite-range polar correlations and a framework for understanding local symmetry breaking in centrosymmetric materials.

</details>


### [280] [GibbScope: interactive calculation and visualization of phase diagrams in the browser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12616)
*Ramya Ramachandran,Zhuocheng Huang,Jiayang Wang,Marat I. Latypov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: GibbScope 是一个基于 Web 的交互式二元相图计算与可视化框架，用 Rust 编写并编译为 WebAssembly，可在浏览器中零安装地于亚秒级内重算完整相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相图随实验与计算数据不断被修正和争议，但长期以来仍以静态图形式传播，与一个世纪前无异，缺乏实时交互能力。

Method: 采用 Rust 实现并编译为 WebAssembly 的新 CALPHAD 引擎，在浏览器端完成相图重算；支持交互式调整 CALPHAD 参数、叠加不同评估结果、实验数据逐点失配（misfit）显示，并集成 SGTE 数据库集合。

Result: 与成熟 CALPHAD 软件验证表明，多数数据库的不变温度（invariant temperatures）一致至 0.01 K 以内，联结线成分（tie-line compositions）一致至 1e-6 以内；可便捷访问 SGTE 集合中 2745 个热力学数据库，其中 1200 余个数据库的所有相均已完整建模。

Conclusion: GibbScope 使相图成为可实时更新的“活的”研究对象，兼具高精度、零部署成本与对大规摸数据库的易访问性。

Abstract: Phase diagrams are a constant work in progress as Gibbs-energy models are fitted, revised, and disputed in the light of an endless stream of experimental and computational data. Yet, phase diagrams are still largely communicated as static figures, in much the same way as a century ago. We present GibbScope, a web-based framework for interactive calculation and visualization of binary phase diagrams. Written in Rust and compiled to WebAssembly, the new CALPHAD engine recomputes a complete phase diagram in the browser in a fraction of a second with zero installation on the user or server side. The high speed allows new ways of interacting with phase diagrams and underlying assessments. Validation against well-established CALPHAD software shows agreement to within 0.01 K for invariant temperatures and within 1e-6 for tie-line compositions for most databases. GibbScope offers interactive CALPHAD parameter adjustments, overlays of different assessments and point-by-point misfit to experimental data, which makes the diagram a live, working object. In addition, GibbScope provides convenient access to 2745 thermodynamic databases from the SGTE collection (Hallstedt, 2025) and their live adjustment. For over 1200 of these databases, all phases are modeled in full with the current implementation.

</details>


### [281] [Out-of-plane interference effects in the angle-resolved photoemission of epitaxial graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12733)
*E. Favorite,P. Krüger,Ph. Rosenzweig,R. Joshi,S. Williams,B. Matta,K. Küster,M. Hashimoto,U. Starke,H. Nakamura*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用光子能量依赖的角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）研究单层石墨烯/零层石墨烯（ZLG）/SiC异质结构中的末态相互作用，识别出由层间干涉引起的多个共振，并首次发现复制带强度随电子动能标度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SiC热解外延是制备大面积高质量单晶石墨烯的有效方法，但石墨烯与重构碳缓冲层（零层石墨烯，ZLG）及SiC衬底相互作用对光电子发射测量的影响除Dirac锥复制带外尚不清楚。

Method: 采用同步辐射光源进行光子能量依赖的角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）测量，并结合多重散射模型（multiple scattering model）分析光电子在石墨烯/ZLG/SiC异质结构中的末态相互作用。

Result: 在Dirac锥光电子强度随光子能量的变化中识别出多个源于层间干涉的共振；首次证明$(6\sqrt{3}\times6\sqrt{3})\text{R}30^\circ$重构诱导的复制带相对强度随电子动能标度变化；分辨出四个源自ZLG的非色散电子态。

Conclusion: SiC与ZLG的三维（3D）结构会体现在二维（2D）外延石墨烯的光电子发射中；该工作揭示三维衬底架构如何决定二维层的光电子发射，为利用ARPES研究复杂异质结构电子结构建立了关键框架。

Abstract: The epitaxial growth of monolayer graphene by Si sublimation of 6H-SiC(0001) is one of the most effective ways of producing large-scale monocrystalline graphene samples of high quality. However, graphene is left interacting with a reconstructed carbon buffer layer known as zerolayer graphene (ZLG) and the SiC substrate underneath. Beyond the mere creation of Dirac cone replicas, the effect of this interaction on photoemission measurements is poorly understood. We utilized synchrotron radiation for photon-energy-dependent angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) to investigate the complex final-state interactions of photoelectrons within the heterostructure of monolayer graphene, ZLG, and SiC. We identify multiple resonances in the photoemission intensity of the Dirac cone as a function of photon energy that originate from interlayer interference, confirmed with a multiple scattering model. We also demonstrate for the first time that the relative intensity of the replica bands induced by the $(6\sqrt{3} \times 6\sqrt{3})\text{R}30^\circ$ reconstruction scales with the electron kinetic energy. Finally, we resolved four nondispersing electronic states, which originate from the ZLG. Our findings show that the three-dimensional (3D) structure of SiC and ZLG manifests itself in the photoemission from two-dimensional (2D) epitaxial graphene. By revealing how 3D substrate architecture dictates the photoemission of a 2D layer, this work establishes a crucial framework for investigating the electronic structure of complex heterostructures via ARPES.

</details>


### [282] [Finite temperature and pressure structural relaxations including zero-point motion and anharmonicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12736)
*Henricus R. A. ten Eikelder,Florian Knoop,Florian Trybel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 WARP 方法，在有限温度/压力下对晶体做对称性约束的可变晶胞弛豫，以随机温度相关有效势方法计入非谐性与零点运动；在铝、超高压镧氢化物（LaH10）和氢氧镁石 Mg(OD)2 上验证，与实验及声子/路径积分计算一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限温度与压力下晶体平衡结构应由吉布斯自由能（Gibbs free energy）极小化决定，而非静态 Born-Oppenheimer 能量；需要一个能同时处理热膨胀、非谐性（anharmonicity）、零点运动（zero-point motion）并保持晶体对称性的弛豫方法。

Method: WARP（Wyckoff-constrained Anharmonic Relaxations at Pressure and temperature）基于随机温度相关有效势方法（stochastic temperature-dependent effective potential method），进行对称性约束的可变晶胞弛豫；位置更新投影到 Wyckoff 位置，应变投影到对称应变，从而弛豫只可能提升而不会降低对称性。此外还用到在 150 个超胞上微调的机器学习势（machine-learned potential）。

Result: 铝的热膨胀在低温区与测量吻合，包括 50 K 以下的量子平台；兆巴压力下镧氢化物状态方程与基于声子及路径积分（path-integral）的计算一致，LaH10 由菱方静态极小松弛到观测的立方相；氢氧镁石 Mg(OD)2 的晶格参数、键长与热膨胀与中子衍射一致，并再现 O-D 同位素效应。

Conclusion: WARP 为有限温度压力下含非谐性与零点运动、且保持对称性的晶体结构弛豫提供了可行方案，在金属铝、高压镧氢化物与含氢矿物体系的基准测试中与实验和多类理论方法结果一致。

Abstract: At finite temperature and pressure, the equilibrium structure of a crystal minimizes the Gibbs free energy rather than the static Born-Oppenheimer energy. We present WARP (Wyckoff-constrained Anharmonic Relaxations at Pressure and temperature), a symmetry-constrained, variable-cell relaxation based on the stochastic temperature-dependent effective potential method, including anharmonicity and zero-point motion. Every position update is projected onto Wyckoff positions and every strain onto symmetric strains, so a relaxation can raise the symmetry but never lower it. For aluminum, the thermal expansion follows measurements at cryogenic temperatures, including the quantum plateau below 50 K. For lanthanum hydrides at megabar pressures, equations of state agree with phonon-based and path-integral calculations, and LaH$_{10}$ relaxes from its rhombohedral static minimum to the observed cubic phase, also with a machine-learned potential fine-tuned on 150 supercells. For brucite, Mg(OD)$_2$, lattice parameters, bond distances and thermal expansion agree with neutron diffraction, and the O-D isotope effect is captured.

</details>


### [283] [High Pressure Material Phase Identification with Gaussian Kernel Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12840)
*Cameron Ryan,Yasantha Pasindu Hetti Kankanamalage,Shuai Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于“原子探测器”与高斯和输入表示的高效相识别（phase identification）方法，并借助神经正切核（Neural Tangent Kernels, NTK）理论提供解释与支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相识别需要依据分子动力学（Molecular Dynamics, MD）模拟中原子的相对位置对材料结构进行分类，而常用方法计算代价较高，因此需要兼顾精度与效率的新方法。

Method: 在笛卡尔坐标下围绕原点设置一组“原子探测器”（atom detectors），在每个探测器处计算以各原子为中心的高斯函数之和，并将其作为神经网络的输入；同时借鉴机制性神经网络理论（mechanistic neural network theory），特别是神经正切核进行分析。

Result: 论文给出理论与实验证据表明该方法在相识别任务上高效有效，精度达到与常用方法相当或更优的水平，同时获得显著的计算加速（computational speedup）。

Conclusion: 基于原子探测器的高斯和表示结合神经网络，可在保持分类精度的同时大幅提升相识别效率，NTK 理论为其有效性提供了依据。

Abstract: Phase identification is a computational task involving the classification of material structure using the positions of atoms. Using data from Molecular Dynamics (MD) simulations, our task is to identify the material phase of a set of atoms, based on how the simulations predict their positioning relative to each other. By leveraging existing research on mechanistic neural network theory, particularly Neural Tangent Kernels (NTK), we develop a new and efficient method for classifying simulated material samples. Our method involves defining a set of "atom detectors" that are given positions around the origin in cartesian coordinates. The sum of Gaussians centered at each atom is calculated at each atom detector, and is used as the input to a neural network. We present theoretical and experimental evidence to show that this method is highly effective and efficient at the task. Our method obtains better or similar accuracy scores to commonly used methods while having a major computational speedup.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

### [284] [Memory flows: geometry and dynamics of sequential retrieval in input-driven Hopfield networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09995)
*Simone Betteti,Matteo Martin,Morten Gram Pedersen,Sandro Zampieri,Giacomo Baggio*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文构建双时间尺度、输入驱动的 Hopfield 网络，用慢反馈变量沿给定记忆转移图调制快速联想检索，并借助慢-快与几何奇异扰动理论解析记忆固定、失稳与逃离的几何机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典联想记忆（associative memory）模型主要刻画向存储模式的收敛，而序列检索（sequential retrieval）的理论相对有限，缺乏可解析的框架来描述记忆间的自主转移。

Method: 采用双时间尺度输入驱动的 Hopfield 网络，快速联想检索由沿预设记忆转移图（memory-transition graph）演化的慢反馈变量调制；运用慢-快（slow-fast）与几何奇异扰动理论（geometric singular perturbation theory），以记忆流形（memory manifolds）刻画记忆固定，并推导约化慢动力学。

Result: 识别出支配检索记忆失稳与逃离的几何区域，并给出转移起始、自维持检索（self-sustained retrieval）与崩溃的显式判据，以逃离时间（escape times）、增益阈值与不动点表达。

Conclusion: 结果为 Hopfield 网络中反馈驱动的序列检索提供了可解析处理的框架，在保持自主图约束转移的同时维持解析可处理性。

Abstract: Associative memory models classically describe retrieval as convergence to stored patterns, but the theory of sequential retrieval remains comparatively limited. We develop a two-timescale input-driven Hopfield network in which fast associative retrieval is modulated by a slow feedback variable evolving over a prescribed memory-transition graph. This yields autonomous, graph-constrained transitions while preserving analytical tractability. Using slow-fast and geometric singular perturbation theory, we characterize memory fixation via memory manifolds and identify the geometric regions that govern stability loss and escape from retrieved memories. We further derive reduced slow dynamics and explicit criteria for transition onset, self-sustained retrieval, and collapse, expressed through escape times, gain thresholds, and fixed points. Together, these results provide a tractable framework for analyzing feedback-driven sequential retrieval in Hopfield networks.

</details>


### [285] [How Path Integral Diffusion Decides a Finite Spin Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11826)
*Michael Chertkov*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究证明各向同性 PID 型扩散生成器等价于经时间变换的高斯随机定位，故连续时间生成器"决定什么"与协议无关，协议仅决定"何时"与"代价"；在 Bethe 格与 SK 自旋玻璃上，轨迹通常先做镜像二选一，再做一次复合的玻璃态决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注一个概念性问题：当 AI 扩散型生成器（diffusion-type generator）采样受挫自旋玻璃（frustrated spin glass）时，它首先决定哪些宏观分支、以及何时决定；并希望厘清采样结论在多大程度上依赖于具体扩散协议（protocol）。

Method: 以从点源出发的路径积分扩散（Path Integral Diffusion, PID）桥扩散模型为对象，证明各向同性 PID 均为时间变换的高斯随机定位（Gaussian stochastic localization），且任意信噪比调度（signal-to-noise schedule）都可由某 PID 协议实现。用"部分承诺"（partial commitments，即对目标所确定的嵌套构型类逐时刻赋予的概率）描述生成器状态，其为沿类对比运动的鞅（martingale），并借助平方磁化对比（squared magnetization contrasts）给出各层级信息获取速率；每次分裂近似等价于信噪比为 1.04/λ_P 的二元高斯信道，λ_P 为父类相关矩阵的主特征值。

Result: 在最多 64 个自旋的 Bethe 格与 Sherrington–Kirkpatrick（SK）玻璃上，轨迹普遍先将权重转移到零场测度的两个镜像半区之一，再转向其内部的玻璃态类（glassy classes）；这些类在玻璃转变附近出现并在降温中保持身份。嵌套玻璃层级 λ_P 相近而被一并决定，故轨迹通常只发生一到两次可辨事件——镜像选择与一次复合玻璃态决策——其分离随耦合强度与系统尺寸增大，与谱预测一致。对有限步生成器，协议的影响体现在后期局部阶段而非宏观决策。

Conclusion: 连续时间扩散生成器的宏观采样决策由目标测度的嵌套类结构决定，与具体 PID 协议无关；协议设计只影响决策的时机与代价，以及有限步情形下的局部细节。该结果为理解扩散采样在复杂能量景观中的层级决议机制提供了定量框架。

Abstract: When an AI diffusion-type generator samples a frustrated spin glass, which macroscopic alternatives does it decide first, and when? We study this for bridge-diffusion models of the Path Integral Diffusion (PID) type started from a point source. We show that every isotropic PID is a time-changed Gaussian stochastic localization, and conversely every signal-to-noise schedule is realized by a PID protocol. What a continuous-time generator decides therefore does not depend on the protocol, which only sets when and at what cost. We describe the generator's state by its partial commitments: the probabilities it assigns, at each moment, to nested classes of configurations fixed by the target. The partial commitments are martingales that move only along class contrasts. Their ensemble average gives the information about each level at an exact rate set by squared magnetization contrasts. Each split is decided, approximately, like a binary Gaussian channel at signal-to-noise ratio 1.04/λ_P, with λ_P the leading eigenvalue of the parent's correlation matrix. The theory is applied to Bethe-lattice and Sherrington--Kirkpatrick (SK) glasses with up to 64 spins. It is observed generically that a trajectory first shifts its weight to one of the two mirror halves of the zero-field measure and then to the glassy classes inside it. These classes appear near the glass transition and keep their identity on cooling. Nested glassy levels have similar λ_P and are decided together, so a trajectory commits in one or two distinct events---the mirror choice and a compound glassy decision---which separate further with coupling and size, as the spectra predict. For a generator that takes finitely many steps, the protocol matters through the late, local stage rather than the macroscopic decisions.

</details>


### [286] [Spatial effects of Kuramoto modeling on the power-grid frequencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12132)
*Kristóf Benedek,Michelle T. Cirunay,Sangita Dutta,Attila Balogh,Bálint Hartmann,Géza Ódor*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究以匈牙利16个输电网站点一个月的高频（100 ms）频率测量为实证基础，结合Kuramoto模型（Kuramoto model, KM）在387节点输电网络与二维晶格上的模拟，揭示空间耦合与异质性如何塑造电网频率动力学，并指出单一机制（确定性非线性或独立高斯驱动）均无法同时再现实测频谱与增量统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网频率动力学由空间结构、内在非线性动力学与外部驱动共同决定，但其相对贡献尚不清晰。作者希望借助Kuramoto模型与实测数据对比，厘清各因素的作用，并检验KM能否同时复现频谱标度与增量统计特征。

Method: 分析匈牙利输电网络16个站点一个月的100 ms频率数据，提取功率谱、1秒频率增量及其局部逆方差相关性；同时构建387节点输电网络与二维晶格上的KM模拟，分别在有/无非相关噪声条件下进行，并采用基于实测局部方差的超统计（superstatistics）高斯混合模型刻画增量分布。

Result: 实测功率谱呈两段标度区间，指数约为1.63与2.23；1秒频率增量快速去相关，而局部逆方差的相关性缓慢衰减，初期呈幂律 τ^{-0.26(2)}，持续约15分钟。超统计高斯混合可解释大部分非高斯增量分布，但无法解释罕见极端事件。低维网络KM模拟给出与实测一致的代数衰减自相关与功率谱（符合稳态下的Wiener-Khinchin关系）；在异质输电网络上，部分同步与频率锁定产生持续空间图样与多峰频率分布。然而确定性KM与独立高斯驱动均不能同时再现实测频谱与增量统计，且实测波动在各变电站间高度共享，模拟波动主要反映节点相对运动。

Conclusion: 空间耦合的非线性动力学与外部驱动（含15分钟功率调度）共同贡献于观测到的频率波动。仅靠KM空间结构或独立噪声驱动不足以复现实测统计，需将网络内在非线性与外部调度驱动结合考虑。

Abstract: Spatial coupling and heterogeneity can generate complex power-grid frequency dynamics. We investigate the contributions of spatial structure, intrinsic nonlinear dynamics, and external driving using the Kuramoto model (KM), with and without uncorrelated noise, and compare its predictions with real-grid measurements.
  We analyse one month of 100 ms frequency measurements from 16 Hungarian transmission-grid sites and simulate the KM on a 387-node transmission network and two-dimensional lattices. The measured power spectra exhibit two scaling regimes, with exponents of approximately 1.63 and 2.23. One-second frequency increments decorrelate rapidly, while correlations of their local inverse variance decay slowly, initially as a power law, $τ^{-0.26(2)}$, up to 15 minutes. A superstatistical Gaussian mixture based on measured local variances captures much of the non-Gaussian increment distribution, but not the rare extreme events.
  We also estimate the non-stationary long-time frequency autocorrelation exponent. KM simulations on low-dimensional networks exhibit algebraically decaying autocorrelations and power spectra, consistent with the measurements and the Wiener-Khinchin relation in the steady state. On the heterogeneous transmission network, partial synchronization and frequency locking produce persistent spatial patterns and multi-peaked frequency distributions. However, neither deterministic KM dynamics nor independent Gaussian driving reproduces the measured spectra and increment statistics simultaneously. Moreover, empirical frequency fluctuations are largely shared across substations, whereas simulated fluctuations mainly reflect relative node motion. These findings suggest that spatially coupled nonlinear dynamics contribute to observed frequency fluctuations alongside external driving, including 15-minute power dispatches.

</details>


<div id='physics.acc-ph'></div>

# physics.acc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [287] [Physics in Safety and Safety in Physics: Lessons from the LHC and its Underground Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10041)
*Alexander S. Sakharov*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 该文主张将安全视为大型地下粒子物理设施物理与运行结构的内在组成部分，而非与科学性能分离的附属系统。作者以LHC、ATLAS和CMS为主要案例，提出“安全中的物理（physics in safety）”与“物理中的安全（safety in physics）”两个概念，并以“安全运行相空间（safe operational phase space）”及连接不同构型间的容许轨迹来形式化这一关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往对大型地下粒子物理设施的讨论通常将科学性能与保障其运行安全的系统分开处理，缺乏统一的物理与运行视角。该文旨在弥合这一割裂，说明物理定律、测量与模型既决定危险源与防护屏障，防护屏障又反过来界定开展物理实验的机器、探测器、通道与干预状态。

Method: 以LHC、ATLAS和CMS为主要案例研究，提出“安全中的物理”与“物理中的安全”概念，并用“安全运行相空间”及连接不同构型间的容许轨迹（admissible trajectories）进行形式化，强调安全不仅约束状态，也约束状态之间的转移。框架应用于束流与磁体保护、低温与缺氧危险、探测器特定风险构型、人员通道、维护及辐射防护，并比较ATLAS与CMS的不同架构。

Result: 分析表明，经充分验证的防护（validated protection）不仅限制科学活动，还可扩大可持续运行（sustainable operation）的范围。安全约束同时作用于容许状态及其转移路径，因而构成实验物理与运行结构不可分割的部分。

Conclusion: 该视角为理解LHC的安全提供了统一的物理与运行框架，并可将防护、可维护性（maintainability）与可恢复性（recoverability）纳入未来加速器与地下设施（如HL-LHC、FCC和muon collider）的设计之中。

Abstract: Large underground particle-physics facilities are usually discussed by separating scientific performance from the systems that make their operation safe. We develop a complementary view in which safety is treated as an intrinsic part of the physical and operational structure of an experiment. Using the LHC, ATLAS, and CMS as principal case studies, we introduce the concepts of physics in safety and safety in physics: physical laws, measurements, and models determine hazards and protective barriers, while those barriers in turn define the machine, detector, access, and intervention states in which physics can be performed. We formulate this relation through a \emph{safe operational phase space} and through admissible trajectories connecting different configurations, emphasizing that safety constrains not only states but also transitions between them. The framework is applied to beam and magnet protection, cryogenic and oxygen-deficiency hazards, detector-specific risk configurations, personnel access, maintenance, and radiation protection, with particular attention to the different architectures of ATLAS and CMS. We argue that validated protection does not merely restrict scientific activity but can enlarge the domain of sustainable operation. This viewpoint provides a unified physical and operational framework for understanding safety at the LHC and for incorporating protection, maintainability, and recoverability into the design of future accelerator and underground facilities such as HL-LHC, FCC and muon collider.

</details>


<div id='physics.bio-ph'></div>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [288] [Learning the interactions underlying multicellular migration using many-body stochastic inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10138)
*Bram J. Hoogland,Tom Brandstätter,Emily Brieger,Joachim O. Rädler,Chase P. Broedersz*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出多体随机推断框架（many-body stochastic inference framework），可从实验轨迹直接学习细胞间相互作用模型，并在三类细胞中揭示多细胞迁移（multicellular migration）的极简预测性相互作用律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动物质与活物质（active and living matter）中涌现集体行为（emergent collective behavior）背后的相互作用难以直接测量，多细胞迁移即为典型难题：细胞间复杂相互作用塑造集体运动却缺乏直接观测手段。

Method: 构建多体随机推断框架，从短时细胞动力学（short-time cell dynamics）推断不同动力学依赖关系与复杂度下的相互作用模型，并依据模型对涌现集体行为的预测能力进行模型选择。

Result: 在三种行为上不同的细胞类型中，该方法获得了极简且具预测性的细胞间相互作用模型：非癌上皮细胞（epithelial cells）通过相互排斥与速度对齐（velocity alignment）协调迁移；癌性间充质细胞（mesenchymal cells）还表现出此前未被识别的非互易相互作用（nonreciprocal interactions），并通过两种机制与自驱动（self-propulsion）耦合——纤维肉瘤细胞中的速度抑制（velocity inhibition）与乳腺癌细胞中的速度增强（velocity enhancement）。

Conclusion: 非互易相互作用使细胞能够调节群体（flocks）的起始与速度，凸显其在集体迁移中的作用，并可能在高细胞密度下增强癌细胞侵袭性（invasiveness）。该框架为从数据中提取主动与活系统的相互作用律提供了通用且有原理的途径。

Abstract: Uncovering the interactions that give rise to emergent collective behavior in active and living matter remains a major challenge. Multicellular migration exemplifies this challenge: complex cell-cell interactions shape collective motion, but are difficult to measure directly. Here, we introduce a many-body stochastic inference framework that learns these interactions directly from experimental trajectories. We infer interaction models spanning different dynamical dependencies and complexities from short-time cell dynamics, and select among them based on their ability to predict emergent collective behaviors. Applied across three behaviorally distinct cell types, this approach yields minimal, predictive models of cell-cell interactions underlying multicellular migration. We find that non-cancerous epithelial cells coordinate multicellular migration through reciprocal repulsion and velocity alignment, while cancerous mesenchymal cells additionally exhibit previously unrecognized nonreciprocal interactions that couple to self-propulsion through two distinct mechanisms, which we term velocity inhibition in fibrosarcoma cells and velocity enhancement in breast cancer cells. Conceptually, these nonreciprocal interactions allow cells to tune the onset and speed of flocks, underscoring their role in collective migration and potentially enhancing cancer cell invasiveness at large cell densities. More broadly, our framework offers a general and principled route for extracting interaction laws of active and living systems directly from data.

</details>


### [289] [Crowding limits relative bacterial surface accumulation with density-induced effective heating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11924)
*Yaocai Liu,Rui He,Da Wei,Longfei Li,Mingcheng Yang,Junhua Yuan,Yi Peng*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究表明细菌在壁面附近的积累并非仅由流体力学与单细胞运动学决定，细胞间相互作用（cell-cell interactions）在拥挤环境中会通过增强取向涨落（orientational fluctuations）而主动削弱表面积累。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微游动体（microswimmers）近壁积累已被研究数十年，但流体力学与单细胞运动学之外，细胞间相互作用的作用长期被忽视；而在诸如人类肠道等真实拥挤环境中，这类相互作用可能同等关键。

Method: 基于对受限细胞轨迹（entrapped cell tracks）的分析，并结合一个最小模型（minimal model）进行理论刻画。

Result: 拥挤增强取向涨落，从而提高有效转动扩散（effective rotational diffusion），促进细胞逃离壁面；最小模型显示该集体效应等价于随密度提升系统的有效温度（effective temperature）。

Conclusion: 细胞间相互作用是调控细菌表面积累的重要机制，其效果可被理解为密度依赖的有效温度升高。

Abstract: The accumulation of microswimmers near walls has been studied for decades. While hydrodynamics and single-cell kinematics are well-explored, the role of cell-cell interactions remains understudied. However, in realistic, crowded environments such as the human gut, these interactions can be just as crucial. Here, we demonstrate that they actively weaken bacterial surface accumulation. Analysis of entrapped cell tracks reveals that crowding enhances orientational fluctuations, which increase the effective rotational diffusion and facilitate wall escape. A minimal model shows that this collective effect is equivalent to raising the system's effective temperature with density.

</details>

### [290] [Integer electron-count cuts in variational second-order reduced-density-matrix theory. II. A convergent hierarchy of rigorous relaxations for real-space pair-density representability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11707)
*Maho Nakata*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于点过程可实现性理论，将整数电子计数条件纳入由有限占据线性规划（LP）构成的可收敛松弛层级，用于固定单粒子密度下的实空间对密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分二阶约化密度矩阵（2-RDM）计算会将分子解离为分数电荷片段；Part I 已通过固定分划上的整数电子计数条件大幅缓解该问题，本文旨在为这些条件建立严格的收敛理论框架。

Method: 在空间分划上定义有限占据线性规划（LP）层级：每层包含全部可表示对密度，嵌套加细下逐层收紧，其交集刻画可延拓为对称 N 粒子概率测度的对密度；LP 最优值同时给出粗粒化候选与现实表示性的 L1 距离。

Result: 固定轨道基下各层给出单调递增的全组态相互作用（full configuration-interaction）能量下界（未必可达，即使对库仑排斥亦然）；Part I 的自旋求和实空间割为该层级的双胞元情形；三层粒子正性条件（含 T1、T2）不能蕴含计数条件；分数电子数原子中带整数计数条件的系综松弛更接近精确分段线性能量。

Conclusion: 整数电子计数条件构成严谨收敛松弛层级的低阶成员，兼具理论刻画与实用价值：其原始解构造可表示近似，对偶解给出可作分子计算割的违反有效不等式。

Abstract: Variational second-order reduced-density-matrix calculations can dissociate molecules into fractionally charged fragments; Part I largely removed this failure with integer electron-count conditions on fixed partitions. Building on the realizability theory of point processes, we place these conditions in a convergent hierarchy of rigorous relaxations, defined by finite occupation linear programs (LPs) on spatial partitions, for real-space pair densities at fixed one-particle density. Every level contains all representable pair densities, the levels tighten under nested refinement, and their intersection characterizes pair densities admitting a symmetric $N$-particle probability-measure extension. In a fixed orbital basis the levels give monotonically increasing lower bounds to the full configuration-interaction energy, which they need not reach, even for the Coulomb repulsion. The spin-summed real-space cuts of Part I are its two-cell members. The LP optimum is also a measure: at fixed one-particle density it equals the $L^1$ distance of the coarse-grained candidate from representability and increases under refinement to that of the candidate, with a vanishing coarse-graining error; its primal law constructs a representable approximation, and its dual a violated valid inequality, used as a cut in molecular calculations. The count conditions are not implied by the usual positivity conditions: moment data that satisfy all three-particle positivity conditions, including $T_1$ and $T_2$, give through Gaussian orbitals a smooth pair density that violates an integer count. For atoms at fractional electron number, ensemble relaxations with the integer-count condition lie closer to the exact piecewise-linear energy than the zero-variance fixed-$N$ form.

</details>


### [291] [Experimentally Grounded Machine Learning of PrecursorSurface-Reactant Chemistry in Atomic Layer Deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10024)
*Bonwook Gu,Trinh Ngoc Le,Zunair Masroor,Minhyeok Lee,Han-Bo-Ram Lee*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: AJIN是一个机器学习框架，从560条实验观测结果中学习判断前驱体-基底-反应物组合能否形成ALD过程，最佳模型在吸附与沉积预测上分别达到79.2%和84.8%的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前驱体、基底与反应物的组合是否能形成原子层沉积（atomic layer deposition, ALD）过程，目前仍依赖试错实验（trial-and-error experiments）确定，缺乏高效的先验筛选手段。

Method: 构建AJIN（ALD Joint Interpretable Network）框架，汇编涵盖76种前驱体、27种基底、20种反应物及25-620摄氏度区间的560条实验ALD与区域选择性ALD结果；遵循ALD循环逻辑，先以图神经网络（graph neural network）嵌入结合温度预测吸附，再加入反应物预测沉积；采用SHAP与消融分析解释贡献因素。

Result: 最佳模型CHGNet-MLP吸附平衡准确率为79.2%、沉积为84.8%，并可部分迁移至未见前驱体；SHAP与消融分析表明基底化学主导吸附，而前驱体、基底与反应物共同影响沉积；对实验室ALD实验的检验中，新基底情形预测正确率83%，新前驱体-基底吸附情形为71%。

Conclusion: AJIN提供了有实验依据的工具，可用于优先筛选ALD与区域选择性ALD化学体系，减少对试错实验的依赖。

Abstract: Whether a precursor-substrate-reactant combination yields an atomic layer deposition (ALD) process is still settled by trial-and-error experiments. Here we introduce AJIN (ALD Joint Interpretable Network), a machine-learning framework that learns this decision from experimental outcomes. We compiled 560 experimentally observed ALD and area-selective ALD outcomes spanning 76 precursors, 27 substrates, 20 reactants and 25-620 degrees Celsius. Following the ALD cycle, AJIN predicts adsorption from graph-neural-network embeddings of the precursor and substrate with temperature, and deposition by adding the reactant. The best model (CHGNet-MLP) reaches balanced accuracies of 79.2% for adsorption and 84.8% for deposition and transfers partially to unseen precursors. SHAP and ablation analyses show that substrate chemistry governs adsorption, whereas precursor, substrate and reactant all contribute to deposition. Tested against our laboratory's ALD experiments, AJIN correctly predicted 83% of new-substrate cases and 71% of new precursor-substrate adsorption cases, providing an experimentally grounded tool for prioritizing ALD and area-selective ALD chemistries.

</details>


### [292] [Toward universal excited-state machine-learned potentials: a local excitation Hamiltonian for size consistency and transferability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11019)
*Pascal Friederich*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出 LEH-MACE，将激发能表示为由原子中心块（atom-centred blocks）组装的局域激发哈密顿量（local excitation Hamiltonian）的本征值，从而使非相互作用分子激发态的并集性质严格成立；并构建约 30 万个 TD-DFT 几何构型的数据集进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials）有望使跨化学空间的激发态模拟常规化，但激发能不同于基态能，并非原子贡献之和；非相互作用分子的激发态应为各分子状态的并集，而池化多态读出（pooled multistate readouts）至多只能对最低态复现该性质。

Method: LEH-MACE 以原子中心块组装局域激发哈密顿量，并以其特征值作为激发能，从而精确满足尺寸一致性（size consistency）；同时基于超过 10 万个分子的约 30 万个平衡、热扰动及激发态几何构型，生成 TD-DFT 能量与力数据用于训练与评估。

Result: LEH-MACE 相对直接读出的优势随与训练数据距离的增大而增大，在 S1 以上最为显著：对长共轭低聚物（long conjugated oligomers），其 S2、S3 误差约为直接读出的一半。对分子对（molecule pairs），仅 LEH-MACE 保持了单分子精度，而 softmin 读出在较大发色团（chromophore）的 S1 动力学上仍更优。

Conclusion: 精确的尺寸一致性是通向通用激发态机器学习势的一步；但针对特定任务（如大发色团 S1 动力学），softmin 等读出方式仍有优势，提示不同读出策略存在适用边界。

Abstract: Universal machine-learned interatomic potentials could make excited-state simulations across chemical space routine, but excitation energies, unlike ground-state energies, are not sums of atomic contributions. The excited states of noninteracting molecules are the union of their states, which pooled multistate readouts reproduce at best for the lowest state. We introduce LEH-MACE, whose excitation energies are the eigenvalues of a local excitation Hamiltonian assembled from atom-centred blocks, so this union holds exactly. We generated TD-DFT energies and forces for about 300,000 equilibrium, thermal, and excited-state geometries of over 100,000 molecules. LEH-MACE's advantage over direct readouts grew with the distance from the training data and was largest above $S_1$, with about half their $S_2$ and $S_3$ errors for long conjugated oligomers. For molecule pairs, only LEH-MACE kept its single-molecule accuracy, while a softmin readout remained superior for $S_1$ dynamics of larger chromophores. Exact size consistency is thus one step toward such potentials.

</details>


### [293] [Extending the reach of real-space real-time TDDFT: new developments in the Octopus code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12018)
*Nicolas Tancogne-Dejean,Martin Lüders,Esra Ilke Albar,Heiko Appel,Franco P Bonafé,Alexander Buccheri,Alessandro Casalino,Benshu Fan,Hans Fangohr,Umberto De Giovannini,Henning Glawe,Hannes Hübener,Simon V. B. Jensen,Christian Jöns,Lukas Konecny,Martin Lang,Ruipeng Li,I-Te Lu,Jehferson Mello,Henri Menke,Sumit Naskar,Zhengwei Nie,Sebastian T. Ohlmann,Micael J. T. Oliveira,Subhojit Pal,Jonas Reimann,Rajarshi Sinha-Roy,Jiuyu Sun,Francesco Troisi,Angel Rubio*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: Octopus 是基于实空间、实时形式（real-space, real-time formulation）的含时密度泛函理论（time-dependent density functional theory, TDDFT）开源软件包，本文报告其自 2020 年以来的多项新进展：泛函、相对论与全电子方法、晶格动力学、超越偶极近似的光-物质相互作用、激子动力学及算法与性能提升，巩固其作为研究电子激发与非平衡动力学的高性能平台地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作为对前期电子结构软件专题（J. Chem. Phys. 152, 124119 (2020)）工作的延续，需系统汇报 Octopus 的最新发展，以拓展可处理体系范围并提升对光-物质相互作用与非平衡动力学的描述能力。

Method: 在实空间、实时 TDDFT 框架内新增交换-关联泛函（exchange-correlation functionals），推进相对论与全电子方法（relativistic and all-electron methods）以支持芯电子谱学，并加入应力张量计算、几何优化、机器学习力场（machine-learned force fields）支持与声子计算等晶格动力学功能；同时实现超越偶极近似（beyond the dipole approximation）的光-物质耦合、激子动力学方法，以及新的传播格式（propagation schemes）、虚时演化（imaginary-time evolution）和可处理逾十亿格点的重构网格处理等算法改进，并支持 AMD GPU 与现代化构建系统。

Result: 新增功能扩展了可处理体系范围并开启芯电子谱学应用；晶格动力学能力使光致晶格动力学可在实时框架下获得全从头（fully ab initio）描述；算法与技术改进显著提升了性能与可扩展性（scalability）。

Conclusion: 上述发展共同使 Octopus 成为用途广泛的高性能平台，可用于研究分子、纳米结构（nanostructures）与扩展体系中的电子激发、光-物质相互作用及非平衡动力学（nonequilibrium dynamics）。

Abstract: Octopus is an open-source package implementing time-dependent density functional theory in a real-space, real-time formulation. Here we report on the developments since our previous contribution to the Special Topic Issue on Electronic Structure Software [J. Chem. Phys. 152, 124119 (2020)]. New exchange-correlation functionals extend the range of systems that can be treated, while advances in relativistic and all-electron methods open the door to core-electron spectroscopy. New capabilities for lattice dynamics - stress-tensor calculation, geometry optimization, support for machine-learned force fields, and phonon calculations - enable a fully ab initio description of light-induced lattice dynamics in real time. We also present developments that move beyond the dipole approximation, capturing light-matter interaction more faithfully. Improved methods for exciton dynamics complement a set of algorithmic advances - new propagation schemes, imaginary-time evolution, and a refactored grid handling more than a billion points - that together substantially improve performance and scalability. Combined with technical improvements such as AMD GPU support and a modernized build system, these developments consolidate Octopus as a versatile, high-performance platform for exploring electronic excitations, light-matter interaction, and nonequilibrium dynamics in molecules, nanostructures, and extended systems.

</details>


<div id='math.GR'></div>

# math.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [294] [A novel Non-Homogeneous Local Representation of the Braid Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10850)
*Arash Pourkia*

Main category: math.GR

TL;DR: 提出辫群 $B_n$ 的非齐次局部表示：通过追踪各股线标签，用位置依赖于交叉、系数依赖于有序标签的 $2\times2$ 块构造表示；该表示在齐次特化下与未约化 Burau 对角等价，且能检测 Bigelow 的 $B_5$ Burau 核非平凡交换子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Burau 表示（Burau representation）并非忠实，其核（kernel）存在非平凡元素，因此有必要构造能区分这些元素的新型 Burau 型表示，以探索忠实表示（faithful representation）的可能性。

Method: 以 ansatz $M_{xy}=\begin{pmatrix}a_x&a_y\\ b_{xy}&c_{xy}\end{pmatrix}$ 为起点，利用 $n\geq3$ 时任意有序三元股线的三次辫关系（cubic braid relations）在非退化分支定出 $c_{xy}=0$、$b_{xy}=(1-a_x)/a_x$；再由"移动标签"规则得到显式的半直积表示（semidirect-product representation），并证明其满足 Artin 关系，辅以符号矩阵的精确计算。

Result: 在齐次特化（homogeneous specialization）$a_x=a$ 下，所得矩阵表示与未约化 Burau 表示对角等价；而非齐次特化能够检测 Bigelow 给出的显式非平凡 $B_5$ Burau 核交换子（Burau-kernel commutator）。

Conclusion: 依赖于股线的系数可区分经典 Burau 表示无法察觉的非平凡辫元素，表明非齐次股线追踪构造是忠实 Burau 型表示的一个有前景的候选。

Abstract: We construct a non-homogeneous local representation of the braid group $B_n$ by tracking the labels of individual strands through successive crossings. Each generator acts through a $2\times2$ block whose position is determined by the crossing and whose coefficients depend on the ordered labels of the participating strands. Starting with the ansatz \[ M_{xy}=\begin{pmatrix}a_x&a_y\\ b_{xy}&c_{xy}\end{pmatrix}, \] for $n\geq3$, the cubic braid relations for arbitrary ordered triples of strands determine $c_{xy}=0$ and $b_{xy}=(1-a_x)/a_x$ in the nondegenerate branch. The moving-label rule leads to an explicit semidirect-product representation, for which we prove the Artin relations and use exact evaluations of the resulting symbolic matrices. In the special homogeneous specialization, where $a_x=a$ for every strand label $x$, the resulting matrix representation is diagonally equivalent to unreduced Burau. We prove that a non-homogeneous specialization of our representation detects Bigelow's explicit nontrivial $B_5$ Burau-kernel commutator. Thus the strand-dependent coefficients distinguish a nontrivial braid that is invisible to the classical Burau representation. This separation provides evidence that the non-homogeneous strand-tracking construction is a promising candidate for a faithful Burau-type representation.

</details>


<div id='physics.data-an'></div>

# physics.data-an [[Back]](#toc)

### [295] [Domain Adaptive Graph Neural Networks for Neutrino Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10057)
*Hemalata Nayak,Giuseppe Cerati,Aleksandra Ćiprijanović,Tammy Walton*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 该研究首次将域自适应（domain adaptation）引入图神经网络（GNN），用于液氩时间投影室（LArTPC）中微子重建，在四个扰动目标域上均提升了性能且源域性能无明显下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱相互作用粒子（如中微子）的探测需要高效精确的方法以搜寻新物理；然而模拟数据与实验数据之间的域偏移（domain shift）会降低模型性能，甚至可能伪装或掩盖新物理信号。

Method: 以 MicroBooNE 公开数据集作为有标签源域（source domain），构造四个无标签扰动目标域（target domain）：固定尺度变化、中等与严重高斯噪声、以及随探测器位置变化的信号变化；训练中引入基于对抗的域自适应（adversarial-based domain adaptation）。

Result: 四个目标域的性能均得到改善，源域性能未出现实质性下降；依变化类型与网络任务不同，目标域精度（precision）最高提升 0.19，召回率（recall）最高提升 0.37。

Conclusion: 结果说明域自适应具有弥合模拟与实验之间差距的潜力，可为当前及未来的 LArTPC 中微子实验构建更可信的模型。

Abstract: Weakly interacting particles such as neutrinos require highly efficient and precise detection methods to enable searches for new physics. Deep learning, and particularly Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), has shown promise for reconstruction tasks in liquid argon time projection chamber (LArTPC) neutrino experiments. However, differences between simulations and experimental data introduce domain shifts that can degrade model performance and potentially mimic or obscure new-physics signals. Here, we demonstrate that robust learning across datasets can still be achieved by using domain adaptation during training. We introduce, to our knowledge for the first time, domain-adaptation-enabled GNNs for robust LArTPC reconstruction tasks. We use MicroBooNE open dataset as our labeled source domain, and introduce four perturbed datasets as our unlabeled target domains-- including a fixed scale variation, moderate and severe gaussian noise, and a signal variation as a function of the position in the detector. The inclusion of adversarial-based domain adaptation during training improves performance for all four target domains, without a substantial drop in performance in the source domain. Precision in the target domain was improved up to 0.19 and recall up to 0.37, depending on the variation and the network task. These results demonstrate a potential of domain adaptation to bridge the gap between simulations and experiments, and build more trustworthy models for current and future LArTPC neutrino experiments.

</details>

### [296] [Roadmap on Nanoscale Superconductivity for Quantum Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10076)
*Oleksandr Dobrovolskiy,Hermann Suderow,Francesco Tafuri,Annica M. Black-Schaffer,Jose L. Lado,Asle Sudbø,Daniela Stornaioulo,Chuan Li,Anna E. Böhmer,Lan Maria Tran,Andrzej J. Zaleski,Adrian Crisan,Massimiliano Polichetti,Armando Galluzzi,Ali Gencer,Bernd Aichner,Neven Barišić,Wolfgang Lang,Tomas Samuely,Martin Gmitra,Tristan Cren,Mateo Calandra,Peter Samuely,Jeroen Custers,Rosa Córdoba,Vladimir M. Fomin,Nicola Poccia,Pavol Szabó,Fabrizio Porrati,Gleb Kakazei,Jan Aarts,Jason Robinson,Javier E. Villegas,Matthias Althammer,Hans Huebl,Akashdeep Kamra,Mathias Weiler,J. Hugo Dil,Daniil Evtushinsky,Beena Kalisky,Yonathan Anahory,Simon Bending,Peter Liljeroth,Abdou Hassanien,Isabel Guillamón,Edwin Herrera,Alejandro V. Silhanek,Joris Van de Vondel,Anna Palau,Ilya Charaev,Maria Sidorova,Floriana Lombardi,Thilo Bauch,Cheryl Feuillet-Palma,Vasily Stolyarov,Dimitri Roditchev,Vladimir M. Krasnov,Benedikt Hampel,María José Martínez-Pérez,Javier Sesé,Dieter Koelle,Stefano Poletto,Alessandro Bruno,Davide Massarotti*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该路线图（Roadmap）由欧洲COST Action CA21144（SuperQuMap）网络推动，围绕超导（superconductivity）作为宏观量子现象的核心，梳理从量子材料、超导相位调控、先进显微表征到量子器件与量子计算应用的研发路径与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025年量子科学与技术年之际，量子力学百年与超导发现114周年凸显宏观量子现象的重要性；超导的宏观量子相干（macroscopic quantum coherence）为可观测、可操控的量子体系提供直接证据，因而有必要系统规划超导基量子技术的发展路线。

Method: 以COST Action CA21144（SuperQuMap）网络内部的密集讨论与合作为基础，组织路线图，涵盖量子材料、超导相位操控（结合磁性、三维制备与可调控关联）、先进显微技术（角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）与扫描探针（scanning probes））以及网络内研制的量子器件，并纳入产业伙伴视角。

Result: 路线图明确了各方向的具体活动与进展：拓扑（topology）与电子关联（electronic correlations）被视为技术跃迁的潜在催化剂；磁性、三维制备与可调关联推动超导相位操控；先进表征手段实现对量子行为的可视化；所开发量子器件对现代量子计算（quantum computing）方法具有变革性影响。

Conclusion: 该路线图从基础研究延伸至实际应用，系统描绘超导基量子技术的研发现状与未来方向，强调宏观量子相干（耗散less电流与磁通量子化）在超导量子电路中的基础作用，并期望通过材料、调控与器件协同推动量子技术跃迁。

Abstract: In 2025, the Year of Quantum Science and Technology, we celebrate a century of quantum mechanics, witnessing a surge in activities that illuminate its inherent strangeness and drive technological innovation. Superconductivity, discovered 114 years ago, stands as a prime example, offering direct and compelling evidence of macroscopic quantum phenomena. Beyond its ability to conduct immense currents without loss, superconductivity reveals the quantum realm operating on a scale we can directly observe and manipulate. The macroscopic quantum coherence, where an ensemble of particles is described by a single wave function, leads to remarkable consequences: dissipation-less current and flux quantization - the basic properties exploited in superconducting quantum circuit fabrication. This Roadmap has been inspired by intensive discussions and collaborations emerging from the European COST Action CA21144 (SuperQuMap - Superconducting Nanodevices and Quantum Materials for Coherent Manipulation). The roadmap outlines the network's concrete activities, driving advancements in superconductor-based quantum technologies and charting future directions. Spanning fundamental research to practical applications, the roadmap incorporates insights from industry partners developing quantum computation. It begins by exploring quantum materials, highlighting how topology and electronic correlations could catalyze a quantum leap in technology. We then delve into manipulating the superconducting phase, leveraging advancements in magnetism, 3D fabrication, and tunable correlations. Further, we showcase the advanced microscopy techniques - such as angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy and scanning probes - used to visualize quantum behavior. Finally, and crucially, we detail the quantum devices developed within the network, and their transformative impact on modern quantum computing approaches.

</details>


### [297] [Pelletization Pressure Engineering of the Superconducting Properties of Fluorine-Doped PrFeAsO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10083)
*Jayant Kumar,Konrad Kwatek,Tatiana Zajarniuk,Taras Palasyuk,Yaroslaw Konopelnyk,Tomasz Cetner,Cezariusz Jastrzębski,Shiv J. Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究考察压片压力（pelletization pressure）对氟掺杂 PrFeAsO0.7F0.3（Pr1111）超导体的结构、微观结构及超导性能的影响，发现超导响应随压力呈非单调变化，870 MPa 压制的样品综合性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在明确压片压力这一工艺参数在常压常规合成（CSP-AP）流程中对 Pr1111 铁基超导体性能的调控作用。

Method: 前驱粉体在 174–1131 MPa 范围内压制成片，卸压后所有样品经相同的两步常压常规合成（CSP-AP）处理，并通过结构、EDX、拉曼光谱（Raman spectra）与超导性能表征进行对比。

Result: 所有样品以四方 P4/nmm 相为主；晶格参数 a 与晶胞体积 V 在约 900 MPa 前随压力减小、之后增大；EDX 所得实际氟含量随压力升高而增加，拉曼特征振动模式变化不大。超导响应非单调，870 MPa 样品起始转变温度 44.2 K，较最低压力样品高约 5 K，且转变更窄、相对密度最高、高场有效磁临界电流密度（critical current density）较优、有效热激活磁通流激活能（activation energy）较大；更高压力下性能劣化，伴随晶格参数增大、杂相增多与相对密度下降。

Conclusion: 压片压力是 CSP-AP 工艺中调控 Pr1111 结构、微观结构与超导性能的有效工艺参数，存在约 870 MPa 的最优区间。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of the pelletization pressure on the structural, microstructural, and superconducting properties of fluorine-doped PrFeAsO0.7F0.3 (Pr1111). Precursor powders are pressed into pellets at pressures ranging from 174 to 1131 MPa, after which the applied pressure is released and all samples undergo an identical two-step conventional synthesis process at ambient pressure (CSP-AP). All samples predominantly exhibit the tetragonal P4/nmm structure, and the lattice parameter a and unit-cell volume V decrease with increasing pelletization pressure up to an intermediate-pressure range (~900 MPa) before increasing at higher pressures. The actual fluorine-content derived from energy-dispersive X-ray spectroscopy (EDX) also increase with pelletization pressure, while Raman spectra show only modest variations in the characteristic vibrational modes associated with Fe and As. The superconducting response exhibits a non-monotonic dependence on the pelletization pressure. Among the investigated samples, the sample pelletized at 870 MPa exhibits the most favorable overall superconducting properties, with a superconducting onset temperature of 44.2 K, ~5 K higher than that of the lowest-pressure sample. It also displays a narrower superconducting transition, the highest relative sample density, a favorable effective magnetic critical current density at high fields, and a relatively large effective thermally activated flux-flow activation energy. At higher pelletization pressures, the superconducting response deteriorates, coinciding with increases in the lattice parameters, increased secondary-phase contributions, and reduced relative density. These findings establish the pelletization pressure as a relevant processing parameter for modifying the structural, microstructural and superconducting properties of Pr1111 during CSP-AP.

</details>


### [298] [Mimicry of $\textit{d}$-wave Andreev Signatures by Interlayer Pairing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10130)
*Zhongyi Zhang,Shengshan Qin,Chui-Zhen Chen,Hoi Chun Po,Jiangping Hu,Xianxin Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出：无节点的层间配对（interlayer pairing）在不均匀耦合的点接触（point contact）下可自然产生类似 d 波（d-wave）的 Andreev 输运特征，从而调和双层镍酸盐（bilayer nickelates）中体隙无节点与 d 波输运信号之间的矛盾证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层镍酸盐高 $T_{\rm c}$ 超导的主流理论指向层间配对，但配对对称性（pairing symmetry）存在争议：体隙测量显示无节点（nodeless bulk gaps），输运测量却呈现 d 波特征，二者冲突亟需统一解释。

Method: 将点接触势视为在层赝自旋（layer pseudospin）上作用的等效 Zeeman 场——该势在层交换镜面对称（layer-exchanging mirror symmetry）下为奇；据此分析其在能隙内束缚的 Andreev 态（Andreev states），并在 La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ 的实际模型中模拟顶层势垒下的亚能隙电导（subgap conductance）。

Result: 镜面选择性的 Andreev 响应可随势垒宽度与强度扫过能隙；在 La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ 实际模型中，顶层势垒的模拟亚能隙电导可再现通常被解释为 d 波配对指纹的取向依赖零偏压电导峰（ZBCP）。

Conclusion: 无节点层间配对态本身即可产生 d 波式 Andreev 特征，为双层镍酸盐中相互冲突的实验证据提供了自然统一的解释，并深化了对非常规超导（unconventional superconductivity）的理解。

Abstract: The recent discovery of high-$T_{\rm c}$ superconductivity in bilayer nickelates has attracted enormous attention. Despite dominant theoretical studies pointing to interlayer pairing, the pairing symmetry is strongly debated due to conflicting evidence on nodeless bulk gaps and $d$-wave-like transport signatures. Here, we demonstrate that a nodeless interlayer pairing can naturally mimic $d$-wave Andreev signatures when the point contact is not evenly coupled to the two layers. The phenomenon can be understood by recognizing how a contact potential, which is odd under the layer-exchanging mirror symmetry, acts like an effective Zeeman field on the layer pseudospin. This pseudo field then binds in-gap Andreev states that sweep through the gap with the width and strength of the barrier. We further demonstrate in a realistic model for La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ that the simulated subgap conductance for a top-layer barrier can reproduce the orientation dependent ZBCP usually interpreted as a fingerprint of $\textit{d}$-wave pairing. By revealing this mirror-selective Andreev response from an interlayer pairing state, our work offers a natural scenario to reconcile the conflicting experimental evidence in bilayer nickelates and advances our understanding of unconventional superconductivity.

</details>


### [299] [Current-voltage characteristics of layered cuprate superconductors with intrinsic Josephson junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10183)
*T. B. Charikova,V. N. Neverov,M. R. Popov,S. D. Popov,N. G. Shelushinina*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该综述总结高度各向异性铜氧化物超导体（cuprate superconductors）中本征约瑟夫森效应（intrinsic Josephson effect）相关的电流-电压特性研究，指出隧穿特性支持以d波（d-wave）为主的序参量对称性，并证实U ~ I^γ幂律依赖关系与理论预言一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层高温超导（HTSC）化合物中存在本征约瑟夫森效应，其c方向多分支电流-电压特性可作为探针，用以检验空穴型与电子型铜氧化物超导体序参量对称性的差异。

Method: 将CuO₂层视为超导板、缓冲层视为介电中间层，把材料视作本征约瑟夫森结（intrinsic Josephson junctions）堆栈，分析最优掺杂p型与n型铜氧化物的隧穿特性及多分支I-V曲线。

Result: 隧穿特性表明序参量以d波对称性为主，并在亚能隙区（U < 2Δ/e）表现出高电导；空穴掺杂Bi基与Tl基化合物符合γ = 0.33–0.50的幂律，电子掺杂材料在双带/双能隙模型（two-band/two-gap model）下符合γ = 0.70–0.76。

Conclusion: 实验结果与理论预言的U ~ I^γ幂律依赖关系相符，空穴型材料的单调d_{x²−y²}波对称性与电子型材料的非单调d波对称性分别对应不同的γ取值。

Abstract: A brief review of the results for the current-voltage characteristics study in highly anisotropic cuprate superconductors is presented, considering the existence of the intrinsic Josephson effect in multilayer HTSC compounds. We analyzed the observations of multi-branch current-voltage characteristics for current in the c-direction both for the hole-doped and electron-doped cuprate SCs wich conveniently viewed as a strack of intrinsic Josephson junctions, where the CuO2 layers serve as superconducting plates and the buffer layers as dielectric interlayers. It was shown that the tunneling characteristics of intrinsic Josephson junctions in optimally doped p- and n-type cuprates indicate a predominantly d-wave symmetry of the order parameter, demonstrating high conductivity in the subgap regime: U < 2delta/e. The analysis carried out confirms the theoretically predicted power-law dependences U ~ I^gamma with gamma = (0.33 - 0.50) in the hole-doped Bi- and Tl-compounds, which is typical for SCs with monotonic dx^2-y^2 - wave symmetry of the order parameter and with gamma = (0.70 - 0.76) for electron-doped SCs with a non-monotonic d-wave symmetry within the framework of the two-band/two-gap model.

</details>


### [300] [Ferromagnetic resonance signatures of edge states for distinguishing chiral and helical $p$-wave superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10623)
*Shun Muto,Yuya Ominato,Takeo Kato,Mamoru Matsuo,Ai Yamakage*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究铁磁绝缘体与二维手性（chiral）及螺旋（helical）p波超导体界面的铁磁共振（ferromagnetic resonance, FMR）调制，计算含边界与体准粒子激发的表面动态自旋磁化率，发现边缘对吉尔伯特阻尼（Gilbert damping）的贡献在两种拓扑态下具有不同的频率标度（δ/(ℏω)² 与 1/ℏω）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性与螺旋 p 波超导态（及其 d 矢量方向）的区分和约束是凝聚态物理中的重要问题，现有探测手段有限，因此需要发展互补的探针方法，本文旨在检验 FMR 调制能否作为此类探针。

Method: 理论计算铁磁绝缘体与二维手性、螺旋 p 波超导体界面的表面动态自旋磁化率（dynamical spin susceptibility），同时纳入边缘（edge）与体（bulk）准粒子激发；考虑零温下保持动量（momentum-conserving）的界面交换作用，并进一步分析界面交换无序与洁净超导边界两种情形。

Result: 在零温、动量守恒条件下，当激发能远大于谱展宽（ℏω≫2δ）时，边缘对吉尔伯特阻尼的贡献呈现不同频率标度：手性态为 δ/(ℏω)²，螺旋态为 1/ℏω。对于洁净超导边界，即使界面交换无序使动量转移随机化，边缘诱导的伊辛自旋各向异性（Ising spin anisotropy）依然存在；当磁化方向与边缘态自旋轴对齐时，低能边缘响应被抑制。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明 FMR 调制可作为与现有方法互补的探针，用于区分手性与螺旋态并约束其 d 矢量（d-vector）方向。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate ferromagnetic resonance (FMR) modulation at interfaces between ferromagnetic insulators and two-dimensional chiral and helical $p$-wave superconductors. We evaluate the surface dynamical spin susceptibility, including both edge and bulk quasiparticle excitations. For momentum-conserving interfacial exchange at zero temperature, the edge contribution to the Gilbert damping exhibits distinct frequency scalings for the excitation energies well above the spectral broadening, $\hbarω\gg2δ$: $δ/(\hbarω)^{2}$ for the chiral state and $1/\hbarω$ for the helical state. For a clean superconducting boundary, edge-induced Ising spin anisotropy persists even when interfacial exchange disorder randomizes momentum transfer. The low-energy edge response is suppressed when the magnetization aligns with the edge-state spin axis. These results establish FMR modulation as a probe complementary to existing methods for distinguishing the chiral and helical states and constraining their $d$-vector directions.

</details>


### [301] [Identifying Contributions to the Superconducting Diode Effect through Nonreciprocal Superconducting Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10669)
*Ryo Iijima,Hisakazu Matsuki,Fugo Tokoro,Susumu Hirata,Akito Daido,Ryusuke Hisatomi,Yoichi Shiota,Shutaro Karube,Youichi Yanase,Teruo Ono*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在对非中心对称 $[Nb/V/Ta]_{40}$ 超晶格的研究中，临界电流、临界磁场与超导转变温度三项基本超导性质均呈现非互易响应；通过角度依赖测量分离出磁失配诱导的面外涡旋贡献后，在 $B_{\mathrm{c}2}$ 附近发现显著增强的超导二极管效应（superconducting diode effect, SDE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易超导响应可灵敏探测破缺空间反演与时间反演对称性的超导态，但 SDE 中微小磁场失配引起的涡旋贡献会混淆对其本质的解读，因此需要区分内禀非互易性与涡旋诱导的伪贡献。

Method: 研究非中心对称 $[Nb/V/Ta]_{40}$ 超晶格，测量非互易临界磁场 $\Delta B_{\mathrm{c}2}$ 的角度依赖关系，从而将磁失配诱导的面外涡旋贡献与面内响应分离开来，并考察 $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ 的非互易行为。

Result: 在临界电流、临界磁场和临界温度三方面均观测到非互易性；在 $B_{\mathrm{c}2}$ 附近 SDE 急剧增强，并伴随 $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ 的非互易响应，其现象学特征与已报道具有有限动量库珀配对（finite-momentum Cooper pairing）的轨道 FFLO（orbital-FFLO）态的层状超导体相似。

Conclusion: 研究建立了评估面外涡旋对 SDE 贡献的测量方案（protocol），并表明非互易响应可作为非常规超导态的互补探针。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal superconducting responses provide a sensitive probe of superconducting states in systems where inversion and time-reversal symmetries are broken. However, in the superconducting diode effect (SDE), where the critical current differs for opposite current directions, nonreciprocity can also arise from vortex-induced contributions caused by minute magnetic field misalignment, complicating its interpretation. Here, we investigate a noncentrosymmetric $[Nb/V/Ta]_{40}$ superlattice and demonstrate nonreciprocity in all three fundamental superconducting properties: the critical current, critical field, and superconducting transition temperature. By measuring the angular dependence of the nonreciprocal critical field $ΔB_{\mathrm{c} 2}$, we separate the contribution of misalignment-induced out-of-plane vortices from the in-plane response. We identify a sharply enhanced SDE emerging near $B_{\mathrm{c} 2}$, accompanied by nonreciprocal responses in $T_{\mathrm{c}}$. These observations exhibit phenomenology similar to that reported in layered superconductors, where an orbital-FFLO state with finite-momentum Cooper pairing has been reported. Our results establish a protocol for assessing out-of-plane vortex contributions to the superconducting diode effect and demonstrate that nonreciprocal responses provide complementary probes of unconventional superconducting states.

</details>


### [302] [Sub-Thouless energy scale in interface-dominated unconventional superconductor junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10675)
*Léon Goedegebuur,Tim Kokkeler,Yukio Tanaka*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在非常规超导体—正常金属—正常金属结中同时考虑金属间电阻并施加 Tanaka–Nazarov 边界条件，可揭示一个低于 Thouless 能量的新低能标度，它取代 Thouless 能量支配零偏压电导峰附近的谱学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有理论通常以 Thouless 能量（Thouless energy）作为扩散金属结的主导低能标度，但可能忽略正常金属之间的界面电阻对低能输运与谱学特征的贡献，难以解释实验观测到的异常低偏压电导现象。

Method: 推导非常规超导体—正常金属—正常金属结的电导，计入两正常金属之间的电阻，并在超导体/金属与金属/金属两个界面同时应用 Tanaka–Nazarov 边界条件（boundary conditions）。

Result: 发现在实验相关的强界面（strong-interface）区域存在一个新的特征低能标度，其值低于 Thouless 能量；该标度控制局域态密度（local density of states, LDOS）的曲率以及三重态超导结零偏压电导峰附近电导凹陷的宽度。对零偏压 LDOS 与微分电导（differential conductance）给出普适的 s 波近邻化扩散金属（s-wave proximitized diffusive metal）表达式，仅依赖界面电阻与体电阻，而与超导能隙及 Thouless 能量无关。

Conclusion: 该框架为非常规超导体中观测到的异常低偏压电导特征提供了可能来源，并为实验杂化结（hybrid junctions）中界面物理与关联电子物理的解释提供了更精细的机制。

Abstract: We derive the conductance in unconventional superconductor - normal metal - normal metal junctions, taking into account the resistance between the normal metals. We show that applying Tanaka--Nazarov boundary conditions at both superconductor and normal metal interfaces reveals a new characteristic low-energy scale which is sub-Thouless in the experimentally relevant strong-interface regime. This scale governs, among other quantities, the curvature of the local density of states (LDOS) and the width of conductance dips near the zero-bias conductance peak in triplet superconductor junctions, thereby replacing the Thouless energy as the dominant low-energy parameter. We derive universal $s$-wave proximitized diffusive metal expressions for the zero-bias LDOS and differential conductance which solely depend on the interface and bulk resistances, independent of the superconducting gap and Thouless energy scale. This framework provides a possible origin of the anomalous low-bias conductance features observed in unconventional superconductors, offering a refined mechanism for interpreting interface and correlated electron physics in experimental hybrid junctions.

</details>


### [303] [Multilayer Heater Nano cryotrons: Electrothermal Modeling of Geometry-Dependent Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10868)
*Baibhari Priya Barua,Md Rahatul Islam Udoy,Shamiul Alam,Md Shafayat Hossain,Ahmedullah Aziz*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一个有限元（finite-element）框架，用于研究多层加热器纳米低温管（nano cryotron, hTron）的器件几何结构对开关电流（switching current）的影响，并结合二维电热仿真与三维热流分析，与既有实验测量进行对比校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 器件几何结构对 hTron 开关电流的影响缺乏系统性、可校准的定量分析框架，需将热场分布与设计指标（如开关电流密度、加热器电流与功率需求）建立联系。

Method: 器件由 50 nm Au/5 nm Ti 加热器、100 nm SiO2 间隔层、23.6 nm NbN 沟道及 Si 衬底构成。采用二维电热仿真考察 100、500、1000 nm 三种宽度下的九种加热器—沟道宽度组合；三维仿真刻画层间热流与加热器—沟道重叠区附近的沟道热扩散。加热器电损耗与热输运相互耦合，而沟道开关阈值由温度相关的本构关系（constitutive relation）单独求得；并通过已发表的整体温度测量校准开关定律，用加热器电流测量评估器件响应。

Result: 模型—测量对比显示开关电流密度（switching-current density）随加热系统性下降，但在低驱动平台区及过渡形状上存在与几何相关的偏差。文中提出统一的提取流程（extraction protocol），用于给定抑制水平下的加热器电流与加热器功率需求，并将拟合的器件幅度与受控几何效应分离。

Conclusion: 所得对比将热场与设计指标联系起来，同时区分了校准（calibration）与验证（validation），并将瞬态性能论断排除在稳态分析（stationary analysis）之外。

Abstract: This paper presents a finite-element framework to study how device geometry affects the switching current of multilayer heater nano cryotrons (hTrons). The device comprises a 50 nm Au/5 nm Ti heater, a 100 nm SiO2 spacer, a 23.6 nm NbN channel, and a Si substrate. Two-dimensional electrothermal simulations examine nine heater and channel width combinations using widths of 100, 500, and 1000 nm. Three-dimensional simulations show how heat flows through the layers and spreads across the channel near the heater-channel overlap. Heater electrical losses and heat transport are coupled, whereas the channel switching threshold is obtained separately from a temperature-dependent constitutive relation. Published global temperature measurements support the switching-law calibration, and heater-current measurements assess the resulting device response. The model measurement comparisons show a systematic reduction of switching-current density with heating, but also geometry-dependent discrepancies in the low-drive plateau and transition shape. A common extraction protocol is formulated for heater-current and heater-power requirements at a specified suppression level, while separating fitted device amplitudes from controlled geometry effects. The resulting comparisons connect thermal fields to design metrics while distinguishing calibration from validation and excluding transient-performance claims from the stationary analysis

</details>


### [304] [Superconductivity and Polar Metallicity in Atomically Thin Nitride Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10976)
*Huma Nawaz,Dawei Wang,C. S. Ting,Laurent Bellaiche*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算研究TiN/AlN与δ-NbN/AlN交替薄层构成的1:1超晶格，发现极性界面与超导态可以共存，TiN的Tc提升约2 K，δ-NbN保持>20 K，其机制为极性金属中电荷重分布对AlN内电场的屏蔽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规观点认为极性材料与超导材料在原子级薄层尺度集成时会相互竞争并抑制超导序，本文旨在检验并挑战这一传统认识。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），研究由TiN/AlN和δ-NbN/AlN交替原子级薄层组成的1:1超晶格体系。

Result: 致密绝缘势垒并未淬灭超导性：TiN的转变温度（Tc）较体相提高近2 K，δ-NbN仍保持超过20 K的较高Tc；该稳健性源于极性金属（polar metal）的形成，即氮化物层内电荷重分布部分屏蔽了AlN层的强内建电场。

Conclusion: 该屏蔽机制与几何受限（geometric confinement）共同造就准二维电子环境，使此类超晶格成为在二维极限下探索结构反演对称性破缺（structural inversion-symmetry breaking）与超导性共存的新平台。

Abstract: The integration of polar and superconducting materials in the atomically thin limit typically results in a competition that suppresses the superconducting order. Here, we challenge this conventional view by employing first-principles calculations to investigate 1:1 superlattices composed of alternating atomically thin layers of TiN/AlN and δ-NbN/AlN. Contrary to the expectation that dense insulating barriers would quench superconductivity, we find that the polar interface can coexist with a superconducting state. We observe that the transition temperature (Tc) of TiN is enhanced by nearly 2 K over its bulk value, while δ-NbN maintains a relatively high Tc exceeding 20 K. We demonstrate that this resilience arises from the formation of a polar metal, where the strong internal electric fields of the AlN layers are partially screened by a charge redistribution within the nitride layers. This screening mechanism, along with the geometric confinement, creates a quasi-two-dimensional electronic environment, establishing these superlattices as a novel platform for exploring the coexistence of structural inversion-symmetry breaking and superconductivity in the two-dimensional limit.

</details>


### [305] [Visualizing d-wave pairing symmetry at the atomic scale in a cuprate high-Tc superconductor by Josephson tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11339)
*Zhen Liang,Tianheng Wei,Jinpeng Lu,Wei Ren,Takenori Fujii,Takao Watanabe,Ziqiang Wang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用 s 波超导针尖的扫描约瑟夫森隧道显微镜（SJTM）在最优掺杂 Bi-2223 中观测到约瑟夫森隧道信号的晶胞内空间调制，为 d 波配对对称性提供原子尺度实空间证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对对称性（pairing symmetry）对理解库珀配对（Cooper pairing）微观机制至关重要，但变号超导序参量（sign-changing superconducting order parameter）的局域表征在实验上仍具挑战性。

Method: 采用配备 s 波超导针尖的扫描约瑟夫森隧道显微镜（scanning Josephson tunneling microscopy, SJTM），在极低温下测量最优掺杂（optimally doped）Bi2Sr2Ca2Cu3O10+δ（Bi-2223）的晶胞内不同位置。

Result: 约瑟夫森隧道信号在晶胞内呈现显著调制：下方 CuO2 面 O 位点上信号明显，而 Cu 位点及铜氧方块（Cu-plaquette）中心与对角线处信号近乎消失；该晶胞内（intra-unit-cell）图案与 s 波针尖—d 波样品间对称性相关隧穿抵消的理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 研究为最优掺杂高温超导（high-Tc）Bi-2223 的 d 波配对对称性提供原子尺度、实空间证据，并确立 SJTM 作为研究非常规超导体配对对称性的有力手段。

Abstract: Superconducting pairing symmetry provides crucial insight into the microscopic mechanisms of Cooper pairing. However, probing the local manifestations of sign-changing superconducting order parameters remains experimentally challenging. Here, we investigate the superconducting condensate in optimally doped (OPT) Bi2Sr2Ca2Cu3O10+δ (Bi-2223) using scanning Josephson tunneling microscopy (SJTM), equipped with an s-wave superconducting tip. Measurements at ultra-low temperatures reveal a pronounced modulation of the Josephson tunneling signal at different locations inside the crystallographic unit cell. A distinct Josephson tunneling signal is detected above O sites in the underlying CuO2 plane, whereas the signal nearly vanishes above the Cu-plaquette diagonals, including Cu sites and plaquette centers. This unique intra-unit-cell spatial pattern agrees with the theoretical prediction for the symmetry-dependent cancellation of Josephson tunneling between an s-wave superconducting tip and a d-wave superconducting sample. Our findings provide atomic-scale, real-space evidence for d-wave pairing symmetry in OPT high-Tc Bi-2223 and establish SJTM as a powerful approach for investigating the pairing symmetry of unconventional superconductors.

</details>


### [306] [Supercurrent-controlled magnon transport via suppression of the superconducting gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11781)
*Tetsuma Mandokoro,Yoichi Shiota,Hisakazu Matsuki,Ryusuke Hisatomi,Shutaro Karube,Akito Daido,Youichi Yanase,Teruo Ono*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过在铁磁体/超导体异质结构中施加超电流，实验观测到磁振子输运信号随超电流增大而减弱，并归因于超电流诱导的超导能隙减小与Bogoliubov准粒子增强的磁振子耗散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态物理中不同低能激发之间的相互作用是核心议题，而磁振子（magnon）与超导凝聚体两种宏观量子态之间的相互作用的实验研究仍十分有限；同时现有磁振子调控方式常伴随焦耳热损耗。

Method: 采用铁磁体/超导体异质结构（ferromagnet/superconductor heterostructure），测量磁振子输运信号随超电流（supercurrent）变化的响应，并基于超导能隙（superconducting gap）与Bogoliubov准粒子（Bogoliubov quasiparticles）的图像解释其物理机制。

Result: 磁振子输运信号随超电流增大而减小；该抑制源于超电流引起的超导能隙减小，从而通过Bogoliubov准粒子增强磁振子向超导体中的耗散，提供了磁振子与超导体中准粒子相互作用的直接实验证据。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了通过超导性（superconductivity）调控磁振子的新途径，且调控依赖超电流而非焦耳热，为能效型磁振子器件（magnonic devices）提供了可能。

Abstract: Understanding interactions between different low-energy excitations is a central theme in condensed-matter physics. Magnons and superconducting condensates represent two distinct macroscopic quantum states, yet their mutual interaction remains largely unexplored. Here, we demonstrate supercurrent-controlled magnon transport in a ferromagnet/superconductor heterostructure. We observe that the magnon transport signal decreases as the supercurrent increases. This suppression originates from a supercurrent-induced reduction of the superconducting gap, which enhances magnon dissipation into the superconductor via Bogoliubov quasiparticles. Our results provide direct experimental evidence of a supercurrent-controlled interaction between magnons and quasiparticles in the superconductor. The approach used in this study relies on supercurrents, enabling magnon modulation without Joule heating. These findings establish a new route to control magnons via superconductivity and pave the way for energy-efficient magnonic devices.

</details>


### [307] [Magnetotropic signatures of Fulde-Ferrell Pairing in a Pauli-limited superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12032)
*Gulnaz Rakhmanova,Mikhail Lemeshko,Kimberly A. Modic,Ragheed Alhyder*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文计算了洁净、泡利极限（Pauli-limited）超导体在单平面波（single-plane-wave）Fulde-Ferrell配对下的共振扭力磁强计（resonant torsion magnetometry）响应，指出一阶转变线的软化峰不能单独作为有限动量配对的判据，而在Fulde-Ferrell相内的宽软化及上临界场处的有限台阶才是区分性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共振扭力磁强计测量自由能（free energy）的角度曲率，对磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）敏感，可作为一种探测超导配对态的测量手段；本文旨在明确有限动量配对在该测量量上的可辨识响应。基于摘要，动机在于区分Fulde-Ferrell态与常规均匀凝聚态的信号特征。

Method: 针对洁净、泡利极限超导体中的单平面波Fulde-Ferrell配对，解析计算共振扭力磁强计的响应；通过转动磁场驱动从均匀凝聚态（uniform condensate）到有限动量相的一阶转变，以及随后进入正常态的连续转变，并分析各转变处自由能角度曲率的软化行为。

Result: 一阶转变线产生尖锐软化（sharp softening），其积分权重（integrated weight）由磁化跃变（magnetization jump）固定，因此该凹陷本身不足以识别有限动量。具区分度的是Fulde-Ferrell相内的宽软化（broad softening），源于能隙（gap）与配对动量（pairing momentum）的弛豫，其后在上临界场（upper critical field）处出现有限台阶（finite step）。

Conclusion: 有限动量配对的判别不能依赖一阶线处的软化凹陷，而应依据Fulde-Ferrell相内的宽软化及其在上临界场处的有限台阶；该结果为共振扭力磁强计实验识别Fulde-Ferrell态提供了特征判据。

Abstract: Resonant torsion magnetometry measures the angular curvature of the free energy and is sensitive to magnetic anisotropy. We calculate its response for a clean Pauli-limited superconductor with single-plane-wave Fulde-Ferrell pairing. Field rotation drives a first-order transition from the uniform condensate into the finite-momentum phase and a continuous transition into the normal state. The first-order line produces a sharp softening whose integrated weight is fixed by the magnetization jump, so the dip alone does not identify finite momentum. The distinguishing response is a broad softening inside the Fulde-Ferrell phase, caused by relaxation of the gap and pairing momentum, followed by a finite step at the upper critical field.

</details>


### [308] [Connate Topological Superconductivity and Vortex-Position-Controlled Majorana Modes in the Intergrowth Superconductor Th$_2$Mo$_2$Ir$_2$Si$_4$C](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12080)
*Jiayang Li,Qing Wang,Xun-Jiang Luo,Mingliang Tian,Ning Hao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本工作将新合成的插层化合物 Th₂Mo₂Ir₂Si₄C 认定为共生拓扑超导（connate topological superconductivity）候选材料，并揭示了两条通往涡旋马约拉纳零能模（vortex Majorana zero modes）的微观路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该化合物已被实验证实为体超导（bulk superconductor），但其拓扑电子结构尚未被探索，因此需要从理论上判定其拓扑性质并厘清马约拉纳涡旋态的产生机制。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）与紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）计算电子结构，并进行涡旋 Bogoliubov–de Gennes（BdG）计算及有限长涡旋计算，以分析涡旋线的拓扑不变量与马约拉纳端模。

Result: 发现由 Mo–d_{xz/yz} 与 Th–d_{x^2−y^2} 轨道间自旋–轨道耦合（interorbital spin–orbit coupling）驱动的 Mo–Th 能带反转（band inversion）与螺旋表面态（helical surface states）；并确认一个较稳健的表面态相关 Kitaev 相与一个弱能隙、源于 Th 轨道的 Kitaev 相，后者在涡旋芯移动时涡旋线能隙闭合再打开并使一维拓扑不变量反转，符合有效 Kitaev 链质量反转（mass inversion）。

Conclusion: 有限长涡旋计算给出了马约拉纳端模在空间与自旋分辨上的特征；轨道 Kitaev 相在无螺旋表面态的修改模型中亦可存在。尽管轨道导出的能隙较小限制了实验可观测性的定量预测，该机制为多轨道超导体（multiorbital superconductors）中超越常规表面态情形的马约拉纳涡旋提供了新途径。

Abstract: The recently synthesized intergrowth compound Th$_2$Mo$_2$Ir$_2$Si$_4$C is an experimentally established bulk superconductor whose topological electronic structure remains unexplored. Here, we identify it as a candidate for connate topological superconductivity and reveal two microscopic routes to vortex Majorana zero modes. First-principles and tight-binding-model calculations uncover Mo--Th band inversion and helical surface states enabled by interorbital spin--orbit coupling between Mo--d$_{xz/yz}$ and Th--d$_{x^2-y^2}$ orbitals. Vortex Bogoliubov--de Gennes calculations further establish a relatively robust surface-state-associated Kitaev phase and a weakly gapped, Th-orbital-derived Kitaev phase. For the latter, shifting the vortex core closes and reopens the vortex-line gap and reverses its one-dimensional topological invariant, consistent with an effective Kitaev-chain mass inversion. Finite-length-vortex calculations reveal distinct spatial and spin-resolved signatures of their Majorana end modes. The orbital Kitaev phase can also exist without helical surface states in a modified model. Although the small orbital-derived gap limits quantitative predictions of experimental observability in this compound, the uncovered mechanism suggests a route to Majorana vortices in multiorbital superconductors beyond the conventional surface-state scenario.

</details>


### [309] [Quantum-geometry-controlled pseudogap and non-Fermi-liquid behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12168)
*Yu Miura,Youichi Yanase*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文利用多轨道 T-矩阵近似（multiorbital T-matrix approximation）研究量子几何（quantum geometry）对涨落现象的影响，发现增强的量子度规（quantum metric）可抑制平带与色散带体系中的涨落诱导赝能隙（pseudogap）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究主要关注量子度规对平均场（mean-field）超导性质（如超流权重与相干长度）的作用，而量子几何在涨落现象中的角色尚不清楚，尽管涨落在平带体系中尤为重要。

Method: 采用保留布洛赫波函数（Bloch wave functions）轨道结构的多轨道 T-矩阵近似，并分析量子度规可独立于能带色散（band dispersion）调控的模型。

Result: 增强的量子度规在平带与色散带体系中均抑制涨落诱导的赝能隙；在色散带体系中还伴随从非费米液体（non-Fermi-liquid）到费米液体（Fermi-liquid）行为的渡越（crossover）。

Conclusion: 谱演化表明存在由量子度规驱动的、从类 BEC 强耦合区到类 BCS 弱耦合区的渡越；量子度规被视为超越平均场理论的、调控相互作用驱动涨落现象的控制参数。

Abstract: The quantum metric plays an essential role in flat-band superconductivity, governing the superfluid weight and coherence length. While previous studies have primarily focused on mean-field superconducting properties, the role of quantum geometry in fluctuation phenomena remains largely unexplored, despite the importance of fluctuations in flat-band systems. To address this issue, we employ a multiorbital T-matrix approximation that retains the orbital structure of the Bloch wave functions. By analyzing models in which the quantum metric can be tuned independently of the band dispersion, we show that an enhanced quantum metric suppresses the fluctuation-induced pseudogap in both flat-band and dispersive-band systems. In dispersive-band systems, this suppression is accompanied by a crossover from non-Fermi-liquid to Fermi-liquid behavior. This spectral evolution suggests a quantum-metric-driven crossover from a BEC-like strong-coupling regime to a BCS-like weak-coupling regime. More broadly, our results establish the quantum metric as a control parameter of interaction-driven fluctuation phenomena beyond mean-field theory.

</details>


### [310] [Real Topology on Symmetry-Invariant Loops Enforces Superconducting Pairing Zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12454)
*Hongyu Lu,Hao Jin,Wang Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于一维实拓扑（1D real topology）的普适配对阻碍机制：定义在反幺正对称不变环上的一维实拓扑可在无需费米面拓扑或全局布洛赫带拓扑的条件下强制产生配对零点（pairing zeros），并给出充分条件与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有配对阻碍机制主要依赖费米面拓扑（Fermi-surface topology）或全局布洛赫带拓扑（global Bloch-band topology），适用范围受限，因此需要建立更一般的、不依赖这些前提的配对阻碍判据。

Method: 在反幺正对称（antiunitary symmetry）不变环上定义一维实拓扑，对多种配对通道（含零与有限库珀对动量，Cooper-pair momenta）推导配对零点存在的充分条件与下界；定理适用于任意空间维度，且不要求配对带全局孤立；并以最小两轨道模型（two-orbital model）进行验证。

Result: 证明此类拓扑强制的配对零点若孤立，必然携带奇涡度（odd vorticity），并可产生Bogoliubov-de Gennes节点与零能边界模（zero-energy boundary modes），在最小两轨道模型中得到了展示。

Conclusion: 研究重塑了对正常态拓扑如何阻碍超导序的理解，凸显一维实拓扑超越单粒子能带现象的重要作用，并为识别具有节点超导（nodal superconductivity）的候选材料提供了普适框架。

Abstract: Normal-state topology can obstruct superconducting pairing and give rise to gapless nodes in the quasiparticle spectrum. However, existing pairing-obstruction mechanisms largely rely on Fermi-surface topology or global Bloch-band topology. Here, we establish a new and general mechanism: one-dimensional (1D) real topology, defined on antiunitary-symmetry-invariant loops, enforces pairing zeros without requiring Fermi-surface or global Bloch-band topology. We derive sufficient conditions and lower bounds for pairing zeros across diverse pairing channels, for both zero and finite Cooper-pair momenta. The theorems apply to systems of arbitrary spatial dimensions without requiring the pairing bands to be globally isolated. We further show that such topologically enforced pairing zeros, if isolated, necessarily carry odd vorticity and can give rise to Bogoliubov-de Gennes nodes and zero-energy boundary modes, as demonstrated in a minimal two-orbital model. Our results reshape the understanding of how normal-state topology can obstruct the superconducting order, highlight the significant role of 1D real topology beyond single-particle band phenomena, and provide a general framework for identifying candidate materials hosting nodal superconductivity.

</details>


### [311] [Scalable operating currents in amorphous W-N/FePt superconducting rectifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12476)
*S. Russo,A. Sanson,Yeonkyu Lee,Jeehoon Kim,N. Haberkorn*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 全溅射制备的W-N/FePt双层膜在零场下表现出高效超导二极管效应（superconducting diode effect），效率约75-80%，极性可由剩余磁化方向调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现无需外加磁场的超导二极管效应，并利用剩磁（remanent magnetization）提供内禀对称性破缺，简化器件结构与工作条件。

Method: 室温全溅射制备非晶W-N超导薄膜与48 nm厚FePt（条纹畴剩磁）双层，经光刻制成微桥（microbridge），在18、24、28 nm三种W-N厚度下与单层W-N参比样品对比I-V特性。

Result: 双层超导转变温度约4.1-4.3 K；零场下I-V呈显著非互易性（nonreciprocity），垂直排列时响应最大、磁化反转时极性反转、平行排列时被强烈抑制；3 K下W-N由18增至28 nm使正负极性耗散阈值电流间隔由约0.3 mA增至1.55 mA，三种厚度效率均约75-80%。

Conclusion: 该行为与非对称磁杂散场（stray field）调控的涡旋（vortex）进入与运动一致，表明溅射W-N/FePt双层是零场超导二极管的可扩展材料平台。

Abstract: We demonstrate high-efficiency, field-free superconducting diode behavior in W-N/FePt bilayers fabricated entirely by sputtering at room temperature. The devices combine amorphous W-N superconducting thin films with a 48-nm-thick FePt layer exhibiting remanent stripe-domain magnetization. We used lithographically patterned microbridges to compare W-N/FePt bilayers with single-layer W-N reference films at three nominal W-N thicknesses (18, 24, and 28 nm). The bilayers exhibit superconducting transition temperatures of approximately 4.1-4.3 K. At zero applied magnetic field, the current-voltage (I-V) characteristics exhibit pronounced nonreciprocity, controlled by the orientation of the in-plane remanent FePt magnetization relative to the transport current. The diode response is maximized for perpendicular alignment, reverses polarity upon magnetization reversal, and is strongly suppressed when the magnetization is aligned with the current. This behavior is consistent with asymmetric vortex entry and motion governed by the magnetic stray fields generated near the bridge edges. At 3 K with the remanent magnetization perpendicular to the current, increasing the W-N thickness from 18 to 28 nm widens the current interval between the dissipation thresholds for opposite polarities from about 0.3 to 1.55 mA. All three bilayers exhibit diode efficiencies of approximately 75-80% in this configuration.

</details>


### [312] [Direct imaging of vortex lattices across fractional fillings in two-dimensional Josephson junction arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12548)
*Cequn Li,Austin R. Kaczmarek,Omri Lesser,Maciej W. Olszewski,Katja C. Nowack*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用扫描SQUID显微镜在二维Au-Nb约瑟夫森结阵列中直接成像了填充因子f=1/8至1/2的一系列公度涡旋晶格，并揭示了f=1/4处两种结构共存及非公度填充下的缺陷结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约瑟夫森结阵列（Josephson junction arrays）被预测可承载丰富的公度涡旋晶格（commensurate vortex lattices），但此前缺乏对其构型的直接成像，输运测量也无法分辨涡旋排布及有序性破缺方式。

Method: 采用扫描超导量子干涉仪显微镜（scanning SQUID microscopy），对二维Au-Nb约瑟夫森结阵列中的单个涡旋进行分辨与直接成像。

Result: 成像出f=1/8至1/2的完整公度涡旋序，其中若干态无可见输运特征；f=1/4处存在4×4与2×4单胞两种结构不同的涡旋序共存，与阻挫XY模型（frustrated XY model）中的能量简并一致；偏离公度填充时，f=1/2棋盘格在孤立多余涡旋与空位周围保持有序，而f=1/3阶梯结构碎裂为由畴壁分隔的畴。

Conclusion: 结果表明阻挫、竞争构型与缺陷共同决定了人工超导晶格中公度涡旋序的空间相干性。

Abstract: Josephson junction arrays are predicted to host a rich sequence of commensurate vortex lattices. However, direct imaging of these configurations remains limited, while transport measurements cannot resolve the underlying vortex arrangements or how order breaks down. Using scanning superconducting quantum interference device (SQUID) microscopy, we resolve individual vortices and directly image this long-predicted hierarchy spanning filling factors $f$ = 1/8 to 1/2 in two-dimensional Au-Nb Josephson junction arrays, including several states with no discernible transport signatures. At $f$ = 1/4, two structurally distinct vortex orders with 4$\times$4 and 2$\times$4 unit cells coexist, consistent with their energetic degeneracy in the frustrated XY model. Away from commensurate filling, we resolve defect structures tied to the underlying vortex-lattice order: the $f$ = 1/2 checkerboard retains its order around isolated excess vortices and vacancies, whereas the $f$ = 1/3 staircase fragments into domains separated by domain walls. These results reveal how frustration, competing configurations, and defects shape the spatial coherence of commensurate vortex order in engineered superconducting lattices.

</details>


### [313] [Ambient-pressure superconductivity in bulk single crystals of La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{10}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12788)
*R. Zhang,P. Reiss,A. Krajewska,M. Isobe,H. Takagi,P. Puphal*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过高压高温法生长出四层Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐La₅Ni₄O₁₃块体单晶，并经拓扑化学还原得到方平面相La₅Ni₄O₁₀，证实其为常压块体超导体（T_c^onset≈18 K、屏蔽分数>60%），表明仅靠层数即可将镍价调控至超导区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐的超导电性局限于高压下的多层化合物或依赖外延应变稳定的方平面无限层薄膜，缺乏常压块体平台。

Method: 采用高压高温（HPHT）法生长四层RP镍酸盐La₅Ni₄O₁₃块体单晶并复现五层La₆Ni₅O₁₆合成；再用CaH₂进行拓扑化学还原（topotactic reduction）获得方平面相La₅Ni₄O₁₀，并进行输运、磁化与比热测量。

Result: La₅Ni₄O₁₀为常压块体超导体，T_c^onset≈18 K，屏蔽分数超过60%，比热跃变证实块体配对转变；超导与密度波或金属—绝缘体转变共存；上临界场各向异性小（γ≈2.4）且趋于T_c时仍有限，指示具有层间强耦合的三维超导。

Conclusion: 方平面镍酸盐R_{n+1}Ni_nO_{2n+2}构成块体材料平台，仅通过层数即可将镍价调至超导区间，无需化学替代、压力或外延约束。

Abstract: Superconductivity in the Ruddlesden-Popper (RP) nickelates has so far been confined either to multilayer compounds under high pressure or to square-planar infinite-layer-type films stabilized by epitaxial strain. Here we report the high-pressure high-temperature (HPHT) growth of bulk single crystals of the quadruple-layer RP nickelate La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{13}$ ($n=4$) and reproduce the quintuple-layer La$_6$Ni$_5$O$_{16}$ ($n=5$) synthesis, two members of the series that had previously been accessible only as thin films. Topotactic reduction of La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{13}$ single crystals with CaH$_2$ yields the square-planar phase La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{10}$, which we show to be a bulk, ambient-pressure superconductor with $T_\mathrm{c}^\mathrm{onset} \approx 18\,\mathrm{K}$, a shielding fraction above 60\%, and a specific-heat jump that confirms a bulk pairing transition. All crystals show besides superconductivity a coexistence with a density-wave or metal-insulator transition. The upper critical field shows a small anisotropy, $γ\approx 2.4$, which remains finite as $T \to T_c$, indicating three-dimensional superconductivity with substantial coupling between the four-layer blocks.
