These results identify the square-planar nickelates $R_{n+1}$Ni$_n$O$_{2n+2}$ as a bulk platform in which the layer number alone tunes the nickel valence into the superconducting regime, without chemical substitution, pressure, or epitaxial constraint.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [314] [Thermochemical Rossby waves in rapid rotation: from swallowtail geometry to nonlinear selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11719)
*Oleg N. Kirillov*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究利用精确复三次色散关系与Bilharz判据，证明快速旋转下热与溶质双扩散对流的不稳定阈值由燕尾型波前（swallowtail）几何控制，从而将codensity模型的直线对角阈值弯折为平滑并最终出现折角的曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热浮力与溶质浮力同时失稳时，codensity模型假定二者线性叠加（给出直线型起始对角阈值），但球壳实验/计算显示阈值弯曲并出现折角（kink），故需一个可解析刻画该偏离的模型。

Method: 研究Busse--Simitev旋转环带（rotating annulus）模型，其简正模（normal modes）满足精确复三次色散关系（complex cubic dispersion relation）；将Bilharz判据与中性曲线包络（neutral-curve envelope）结合，在快速旋转极限下解析求解。

Result: 在快速旋转极限下得到Arnold意义下的显式燕尾波前，随扩散系数比（diffusivity ratio）减小使阈值先平滑后折角化；尖点边位于不稳定域内，折角端终止于燕尾奇点，且燕尾扩散比具有小、大Prandtl数的解析极限。折角处宽而快的热Rossby波与窄而慢的组分Rossby波同时达到中性；隐弧标示不稳定波数带的产生与合并。

Conclusion: 同一几何进入非线性选择：平均标量反馈通过中性弧法线与标量响应起作用，共存裕度随折角消失；完整三次系数预测参考角点处稳健共存及合并附近窄双稳区间，有限旋转角与周期胞模拟予以验证。

Abstract: When heat and solute both destabilise a rapidly rotating fluid, do their buoyancies add? Codensity models assume so, giving a straight onset diagonal; spherical shells show curved and kinked thresholds. We study the Busse--Simitev rotating annulus, whose normal modes obey an exact complex cubic dispersion relation. Combining the Bilharz criterion with the neutral-curve envelope yields, in the rapid-rotation limit, an explicit swallowtail wave front, in Arnold's sense, that bends the codensity diagonal into smooth and then kinked thresholds as the diffusivity ratio decreases. Its cusp edges lie inside the unstable domain and its exposed kink ends at the swallowtail singularity; these are singularities of wave-number selection, without coalescence of temporal eigenvalues, and the swallowtail diffusivity ratio has analytical small- and large-Prandtl-number limits. At a kink, broad, fast thermal and narrow, slow compositional Rossby waves become neutral together; hidden arcs mark the birth and merger of unstable wave-number bands. The same geometry enters nonlinear selection: mean-scalar feedback factors through neutral-arc normals and scalar responses, so the mean-profile coexistence margin vanishes with the kink angle as the waves merge. Complete cubic coefficients predict robust coexistence at a reference corner and a narrow bistable interval near merger. Exact finite-rotation corners and periodic-cell simulations confirm the coexisting waves and their invasion thresholds.

</details>


### [315] [Variational principles for dissipative continua: transport theorems, inertia, and the minimum-maximum duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12045)
*Wolfgang Flachberger,Lukas Einkemmer*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文厘清了热力学极值原理（thermodynamic extremal principle, TEP）的极值性质，并揭示耗散过程两类变分原理（最小原理与最大原理）之间的联系：极值方向由所处区域决定，而底层 Rayleighian 泛函在两种情形下均为凸且取极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散过程的变分原理分为两族：非平衡定态下的最小原理（Helmholtz、Kirchhoff、Prigogine）与连续介质力学中弛豫过程的最大原理（Ziegler、Hackl、Fischer）。二者此前在扩散问题上已被明确区分，但其联系与一般化理解仍待阐明，惯性的作用亦有争议。

Method: 通过考察 TEP 的极值性质建立两族原理的联系，并以“冻结状态的弛豫（最大耗散）—开放系统中的驱动定态（最小耗散）”区分极值方向；同时分析 Reynolds 输运定理（Reynolds transport theorem）以确定连续介质的能量率，并将能量率写作“率乘以驱动力”的形式，讨论惯性项在变分中的处理（驱动力需在变分中固定）。

Result: 极值方向由工况区域决定，而 Rayleighian 泛函（Rayleighian functional）在两种情形下保持凸性并被极小化。含惯性时须在变分中固定驱动力，虽不如无惯性情形优雅（此时驱动力为状态函数），但写成率乘驱动力仍给出正确方程；当冻结力不依赖于被变分的率时，极值表述依然成立。该结论直接适用于非定常 Stokes 流（unsteady Stokes flow），对 Navier–Stokes 流（Navier–Stokes flow）则需变换至物质坐标（material coordinates），此时颗粒加速度为状态的二阶时间导数，Euler 描述下非变分的对流项不再出现。

Conclusion: 该框架在 Navier–Stokes 族（Stokes、非定常 Stokes、Navier–Stokes 流，按复杂度递增）以及耗散拉格朗日力学（dissipative Lagrangian mechanics）上得到了检验，说明所提出的极值分析具有一致性与适用性。

Abstract: Variational principles for dissipative processes come in two families: minimum principles at nonequilibrium stationary states (Helmholtz, Kirchhoff, Prigogine) and maximum principles for relaxation in continuum mechanics (Ziegler, Hackl, Fischer). We contribute to the understanding of the thermodynamic extremal principle (TEP) by investigating its extremal properties, and uncover the connection between the two families, whose distinction was recently made precise for diffusion: the extremal character is decided by the regime, which is either relaxation at frozen state, as in a closed system (maximum dissipation), or a driven stationary state in an open system (minimum dissipation), while the underlying Rayleighian functional is convex and minimized in both cases. We further examine the Reynolds transport theorem, a useful tool for fixing the energy rates of continua, and the role of inertia, recently argued to lie beyond the reach of Rayleighian principles. With inertia the driving force must be held fixed during the variation, which is less elegant than in the inertialess case, where it is a state function. Writing the energy rate as rates times driving forces still yields the correct equations, and an extremal statement survives whenever the frozen force does not depend on the varied rate. This holds directly for unsteady Stokes flow, and for Navier-Stokes flow after a change to material coordinates, in which the particle acceleration is the second time derivative of the state and the convective term, nonvariational in the Eulerian description, does not appear. The framework is tested on the Navier-Stokes family (Stokes, unsteady Stokes, and Navier-Stokes flow, in order of increasing complexity) and on dissipative Lagrangian mechanics.

</details>


### [316] [Tuning fluid frictional drag through flow-induced aggregation of wormlike micelles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10365)
*Lucas Warwaruk,Amanda Tiano,Berith Isaac,Gareth H. McKinley*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究考察阳离子表面活性剂TTAC与芳香族反离子NaSal混合体系的流变学与非牛顿流体动力学，发现其在稀溶液中通过剪切诱导结构实现层流增阻（最高50%）与湍流减阻（最高30%），且无高分子降解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电两亲性表面活性剂（amphiphilic surfactants）可自组装形成蠕虫状胶束（wormlike micelles）等超分子结构，但其在层流与湍流中作为摩擦阻力改性剂（friction drag modifier）的潜力尚需系统表征。

Method: 采用毛细断裂（capillary pinch-off）实验与泰勒-库埃特（Taylor-Couette）流动实验，结合线性黏弹性测量，对比稀溶液与非稀溶液区域的流变响应，并分析剪切诱导结构（flow-induced structures）对黏度与湍流阻力的影响。

Result: 非稀区域呈单模Jeffreys模型（single-mode Jeffreys model）描述的线性黏弹性，并表现强应变硬化（strain-stiffening）；稀区域在高剪切速率下因胶束端对端连接形成流动诱导结构，使层流黏度与黏性阻力增加达50%，并使稳定环形泰勒涡（Taylor vortices）失稳、湍流阻力增加逾200%；随雷诺数（Reynolds number）升高则平滑过渡至减阻状态，阻力降低约30%。

Conclusion: 表面活性剂是层流与湍流两种流态下独特而有效的流体摩擦阻力改性剂，可通过调节组分与工况同时实现显著增阻与减阻，且避免了聚丙烯酰胺（polyacrylamide）等高分子减阻策略中的剪切降解（shear-induced degradation）问题。

Abstract: Charged amphiphilic surfactants are versatile polymorphic molecules which can form supramolecular structures, such as wormlike micelles. We study the rheology and non-Newtonian fluid dynamics of the cationic surfactant trimethyltetradecylammonium chloride (TTAC) combined with the aromatic counter-ion sodium salicylate (NaSal). In the non-dilute regime, this solution exhibits a linear viscoelastic response consistent with a single-mode Jeffreys model, and in capillary pinch-off experiments, fluid filaments form extended cylindrical threads, indicating strong strain-stiffening. In the dilute regime the linear viscoelasticity and extensional rheology become immeasurably small, but the TTAC/NaSal mixture develops flow-induced structures at high shear rates, as the result of an end-to-end concatenation of micelles produced by shear-induced flow alignment. These flow-induced structures augment both the measured viscosity and the viscous drag in laminar flows at high shear rates (by as much as 50 %). In turbulent Taylor-Couette flow, the flow-induced structures produce a sudden and dramatic destabilizing influence on the stable toroidal Taylor vortices, resulting in upwards of a 200 % enhancement in turbulent drag. However, with increasing Reynolds number, the turbulent flow transitions smoothly to a state of drag reduction, generating up to a 30 % reduction in fluid frictional drag but without the shear-induced degradation that plagues drag reduction strategies based on solutions of high molecular weight polymer, such as polyacrylamide. These results show that surfactants are a unique and effective modifier of fluid frictional drag in both laminar and turbulent flow regimes, capable of producing both significant levels of drag enhancement and drag reduction by tuning the composition and operating conditions.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [317] [Sitewise Generator Decomposition and Non-Equilibrium Steady States for Generalized Zero-Range Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11485)
*Berend van Tol*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究带边界驱动的广义零程过程（generalized zero-range processes），证明其边界驱动生成元在平衡边际乘积上可分解为单点生成元之和，并由此得到调和模型（harmonic models）在非对称边图上的混合测度传播、非平衡稳态（NESS）显式刻画及激波行为与大偏差结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（[20,11,12]）表明一大类广义零程过程的平衡稳态具有乘积形式，而 [15] 引入的调和模型及 [19] 的混合测度传播结果此前主要在对称情形下讨论；本文旨在利用该乘积结构把相关结论推广到带非对称边（asymmetric edges）的边界驱动情形。

Method: 质量仅在相邻格点间转移且转移率只依赖出发站点；对平衡边际（可能非齐次）乘积积分时，将边界驱动生成元分解为作用在单点上的生成元之和，并借助该代数结构分析调和模型与非对称链模型。

Result: 证明 [19] 的混合测度传播（propagation of mixture measures）在非对称边图上仍成立；在非对称边链几何上识别出一类模型，其 NESS 恰等于对称调和模型链上的 NESS；并给出另一类可显式求解、呈现激波（shock）行为的非对称链模型，陈述并证明其密度剖面对应的大偏差（large deviations）原理。

Conclusion: 边界驱动生成元的单点可分解性是分析此类非平衡系统的有效工具，可将调和模型的现有结果推广至非对称设定，并获得具有激波行为的可解非对称模型及其大偏差刻画。

Abstract: In this paper we study the generalized zero-range processes considered in [20, 11, 12] with boundary driving. In these processes mass is moved between neighboring sites with rates depending only on the departure site. For a wide class of such models, the equilibrium stationary states are known to be of product form. We show that for such models, the boundary-driven generator can be decomposed into a sum of generators acting on single sites when integrating against (possibly inhomogeneous) products of the equilibrium marginals. We exploit this structure to derive new results for the harmonic models introduced in [15]. In particular, we prove that the propagation of mixture measures in [19] is still valid on graphs with asymmetric edges. On the chain geometry with asymmetric edges, we identify a class of models for which the NESS is given precisely by the NESS of the symmetric harmonic model on the chain. In addition to these results for harmonic models, we also present a new class of asymmetric models on the chain for which the NESS can be found explicitly and exhibits shock behavior. The large deviations for the density profile corresponding to these NESS are stated and proved.

</details>


### [318] [Brown Measures and Spectral Convergence for Arbitrary GUE Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12785)
*Benoit Collins,Yu Kitagawa,Akihiro Miyagawa,Wangjun Yuan,Ping Zhong*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文证明了独立 GUE 矩阵（Gaussian unitary ensemble）任意非交换多项式（noncommutative polynomial）的经验特征值测度弱收敛于自由半圆变量（free semicircular variables）对应多项式的 Brown 测度，并给出特征值集合的 Hausdorff 收敛与谱半径收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将自由概率（free probability）中 Brown 测度的理论结果与随机矩阵（random matrix）有限维谱行为严格对应，处理任意非交换多项式而非仅单项式或特定结构的情形。

Method: 弱收敛证明结合基于 Brown 测度二阶矩（second moment）的判据、复高斯旋转（complex Gaussian rotation）与凸性；支撑集定理（support theorem）使用线性化（linearization）与矩阵 Dyson 方程（matrix Dyson equation）的刚性（rigidity）。

Result: 得到经验特征值测度的弱收敛、特征值集合的 Hausdorff 距离收敛及谱半径的概率收敛；等价结论对独立复 Ginibre 矩阵的 $*$-多项式成立；并证明自由半圆变量多项式的 Brown 测度支撑等于其算子谱。

Conclusion: 进一步证明无伴随项非恒定多项式在自由循环变量（free circular variables）下的绝对连续性（absolute continuity）；矩阵 Dyson 方程通过正则化对数势（regularized logarithmic potentials）恢复 Brown 测度，并对漂移校正的非退化椭圆自由随机游走（free random walks）给出极限域内部的定量势收敛与向自由乘性布朗运动（free multiplicative Brownian motion）Brown 测度的弱收敛。

Abstract: For arbitrary noncommutative polynomials in independent GUE matrices, we prove that the empirical eigenvalue measure converges weakly in probability to the Brown measure of the corresponding polynomial in free semicircular variables. We also prove convergence of the entire eigenvalue set in Hausdorff distance and convergence of the spectral radius, both in probability. Equivalently, these matrix conclusions hold for arbitrary $*$-polynomials in independent complex Ginibre matrices. Independently, we prove that the support of the Brown measure of every polynomial in free semicircular variables equals its operator spectrum. The proof of weak convergence combines a criterion based on the second moment of the Brown measure with complex Gaussian rotation and convexity. The support theorem uses linearization and rigidity of matrix Dyson equations.
  We also prove absolute continuity for nonconstant polynomials without adjoints in free circular variables. A matrix Dyson equation recovers Brown measures of arbitrary semicircular polynomials through regularized logarithmic potentials. For drift-corrected nondegenerate elliptic free random walks, it yields quantitative convergence of potentials in the interior of the limiting domain and weak convergence of Brown measures to that of free multiplicative Brownian motion.

</details>


### [319] [The satisfiability conjecture for random k-SAT formulas. Human-friendly exposition of the recent OpenAI resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10139)
*David Gamarnik*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该短文以"人类友好"方式详细讲解 OpenAI 对可满足性猜想（satisfiability conjecture）的解答，该猜想近期先后由 Carenini 与 OpenAI 内部模型解决，此前仅大常数 k 情形由 Ding、Sly、Sun 用二阶矩方法（second moment method）证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可满足性猜想是离散概率（discrete probability）领域最重要的未解问题之一，涉及随机 k-SAT 实例的可满足性阈值，一般 k 情形长期缺乏证明，而原始证明技术门槛高，需要更易读的阐述。

Method: 本文并非给出新证明，而是对 OpenAI 关于该猜想的解答进行"人类友好"且详细的阐释性说明（exposition），梳理其证明思路。

Result: 摘要指出该猜想已被 Carenini 与 OpenAI 先后解决，OpenAI 的工作是其内部模型所解 370 个问题之一；本文据此提供 OpenAII 解法的详细讲解。

Conclusion: 可满足性猜想在一般 k 情形下已获解决，本文旨在以易于理解的形式呈现 OpenAI 的证明，降低理解门槛。

Abstract: The satisfiability conjecture was one of the most important open problems in discrete probability. It was resolved recently first by Carenini~\cite{GCarenini}, and shortly after by OpenAI~\cite{k-sat-OpenAI}, among other 370 problems solved by its internal model. The conjecture concerned the satisfiability property of random instances of the k-SAT problem. It posits the existence of a fixed value for the clause-to-variables ratio around which the probability that the formula is satisfiable drops sharply from nearly one to nearly zero as the ratio increases. While the case of large constant $k$ was resolved a decade ago by Ding, Sly and Sun~\cite{ding2015proof,ding2022proof} using the heavy machinery of the second moment method, the case of general $k$ eluded proof until ~\cite{GCarenini} and~\cite{k-sat-OpenAI}.
  In this short note we provide a ''human-friendly'' and detailed exposition of the OpenAI's resolution of the conjecture.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [320] [Periodic Readout Induces a First-Order Generalization Transition in a Quantum Perceptron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10887)
*Fabio Benatti,Masoud Gharahi,Giovanni Gramegna,Stefano Mancini,Vincenzo Parisi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文用量子感知机（quantum perceptron）模型证明周期读出结构带来的表达力伴随统计代价，在热力学极限下刻画记忆与泛化之间的定量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子分类器（variational quantum classifiers）通过可观测量期望值对电路参数的傅里叶依赖可生成高度结构化的决策边界，但表达的丰富性可能带来统计代价，需要量化其典型泛化行为。

Method: 考虑读出为周期性方波函数（square-wave function）的量子感知机，采用教师-学生副本对称理论（teacher-student replica-symmetric theory）在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下确定其典型泛化性能。

Result: 超过临界振荡频率后，增大训练负载会诱发从弱对齐到强教师对齐相的一阶相变（first-order transition），泛化出现不连续改善；但在固定负载下，频率继续增大会使平衡泛化误差最终趋于随机水平（chance level），尽管所有训练样本被完美分类。

Conclusion: 增强随机标签存储能力的同一周期结构可能延迟学习的发生；结果揭示由周期读出纯粹导致的记忆（memorization）与泛化（generalization）之间的定量权衡，并为理解傅里叶丰富的变分量子分类器提供基准。

Abstract: Variational quantum classifiers can generate highly structured decision boundaries through the Fourier dependence of observable expectation values on circuit parameters. We show that expressivity can come at a sharp statistical cost, by considering a quantum perceptron whose readout is the simplest periodic classifier, namely a square-wave function of the weighted inputs, and determine its typical generalization in the thermodynamic limit using a teacher-student replica-symmetric theory. Above a critical oscillation frequency, increasing the training load induces a first-order transition from weakly to strongly teacher-aligned phases, resulting in a discontinuous improvement in generalization. At fixed load, however, increasing the frequency eventually drives the equilibrium generalization error to chance level, despite perfect classification of all the training examples. Thus, the same periodic structure that enhances random-label storage can delay the onset of learning. Our results expose a quantitative tradeoff between memorization and generalization generated purely by periodic readout, and provide a benchmark for understanding Fourier-rich variational quantum classifiers.

</details>


### [321] [A geometrical approach for obtaining exact analytical solutions for a scalar field in a one-dimensional dynamical cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11595)
*Maxwell F. de Araújo,Odemar Julião do N. Neto,Stanley S. Coelho,Jeferson Danilo L. Silva,Alessandra N. Braga,Danilo T. Alves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作指出Cole-Schieve几何方法（geometrical approach）不仅可用于数值计算，更可作为推导Moore方程（Moore equation）精确解析解（exact analytical solutions）的有力工具，并给出若干原创解析解，同时将已知解析解集合扩展至无限类运动规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态卡西米尔效应（dynamical Casimir effect）中，Moore方程（Moore equation）的精确解析解在文献中数量极少，仅局限于特定几类运动规律；Cole-Schieve几何方法长期仅被视为数值手段，其解析潜力未被挖掘。

Method: 将Cole和Schieve（1995）针对初始静止腔体建立的几何方法（geometrical approach）重新诠释为推导精确解析解的途径，并对其加以推广：证明只要在Moore函数（Moore function）已确定的任一时空区域（spacetime region）上，均可构造精确解。

Result: 获得三类原创精确解析解：匀速移动镜面的膨胀腔、镜面渐近趋近静止镜面的收缩腔，以及重新导出Law（1994）关于共振镜面运动（resonant mirror motion）的首个解析解；推广后的几何方法可将已知解析解从少数几类运动扩展为无限集合。

Conclusion: Cole-Schieve几何方法（geometrical approach）既是数值工具，也是解析推导框架；通过将新导出的运动规律彼此组合并与既有轨迹类别组合，可系统生成新的可解析处理运动类别，从而显著扩充Moore方程精确解析解的已知集合。

Abstract: The dynamical Casimir effect was first predicted by G. T. Moore in 1969, who considered a real scalar field inside a cavity with a moving mirror in ($1+1$)D, satisfying Dirichlet boundary conditions. Moore wrote the general solution for the field in terms of a function that satisfies a functional equation nowadays known as the Moore equation.In the literature, exact analytical solutions to the Moore equation form a rather small set, restricted to specific classes of laws of motion. Cole and Schieve (1995) developed a geometrical approach that yielded a general formula for the solution of the Moore equation for cavities initially at rest. However, its application has always been done through numerical calculations, and their approach has therefore long been regarded solely as a numerical method. In this work, we show that their method can also be employed as a powerful method for deriving exact analytical solutions, and show the applicability of this approach to deriving analytical solutions to the Moore equation. We obtain original exact analytical solutions for an expanding cavity with a uniformly moving mirror, a contracting cavity with a mirror that approaches the static one asymptotically, and, in addition, we recover the first analytical solution to the Moore equation for a resonant mirror motion, originally obtained by Law in 1994. Moreover, we extend the Cole-Schieve approach, showing that exact solutions can be constructed from any spacetime region where the Moore function has already been determined. We show that this extended geometrical approach allows us to expand the set of known analytical solutions to the Moore equation from the few classes of motion reported in the literature to an infinite set, as it enables the treatment of new classes of laws of motion obtained by combining those presented here with each other as well as with previously known classes of trajectories.

</details>


### [322] [Covariance and the use of the Schrodinger equation in quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10333)
*J. D. Franson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文用二次量子化场论计算含推迟效应的Aharonov-Bohm效应，指出在推迟效应显著时会出现分数相移、协变性缺失与因果性破坏，据此主张基于薛定谔方程或费曼路径积分的量子场论无法完整描述所有可观测现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薛定谔方程（Schrodinger equation）本身不具备协变性（covariance），但量子场论（quantum field theory）常以其描述时间演化。散射理论（scattering theory）在相互作用于 $t \rightarrow \pm\infty$ 渐近极限下消失时给出协变结果，但存在不满足该条件的情形，作者希望考察这类情形下场论的适用性。

Method: 以Aharonov-Bohm效应为例，采用二次量子化场论（second-quantized field theory）同时描述所有电子与电磁场，并借助费曼传播子（Feynman propagator）直接计算推迟矢量势（retarded vector potentials）的影响，与基于薛定谔方程/费曼路径积分（Feynman path integrals）的常规处理相对照。

Result: 当电磁场推迟效应可忽略时，结果与Aharonov-Bohm效应的通常表达式一致；而当推迟效应显著时，计算预测出现分数相移（fractional phase shift）、协变性缺失（lack of covariance）以及因果性破坏（violations of causality）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于薛定谔方程或费曼路径积分的量子场论在无法正确描述全部可观测现象的意义上是不完备的（incomplete）。

Abstract: The Schrodinger equation is not covariant. Nevertheless, quantum field theory is often formulated using the Schrodinger equation to describe the time evolution of the system, which is equivalent to using Feynman path integrals. It is well known that scattering theory gives covariant results provided that the interaction vanishes in the asymptotic limit of $t \rightarrow \pm \infty$, but there are other situations of interest that do not satisfy those conditions. As an example, the Aharonov-Bohm effect is derived here using second-quantized field theory to describe all the electrons as well as the electromagnetic field, which allows the effects of retarded vector potentials to be calculated in a straightforward way using the Feynman propagator. The results are in agreement with the usual expression for the Aharonov-Bohm effect when the retardation of the electromagnetic field is negligible, but they predict a fractional phase shift, a lack of covariance, and violations of causality when retardation effects are significant. These results suggest that quantum field theory based on the Schrodinger equation or Feynman path integrals is incomplete in the sense that it cannot give a correct description of all observable phenomena.

</details>


### [323] [Twisted Compactification, Quotient Quantum Codes and Logical Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09538)
*Po-Shen Hsin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文建立“商码（quotient code）= 扭紧致化（twisted compactification）”的对应关系，并双向使用该对应：既用母码的转移定理分析子码，也用量子场论的对称性背景构造新码、新逻辑门与新型拓扑序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: balanced-product、bivariate-bicycle、genon 码及五量子比特码等均可写成商码，此前缺乏统一理论框架；论文旨在揭示这类码的晶格形式即扭紧致化，从而系统化构造与分析量子码。

Method: 对 Pauli 稳定子码的单项式商（monomial quotient）给出转移定理（transfer theorem），用 Tate 上同调群计数新逻辑算符并给出距离界；对高阶形式与杯积对称，扭转为纠缠对角电路，有限覆盖平凡化背景时得到“装扮商（dressed quotient）”，装扮的 Clifford 层级至多比扭转次数少一。

Result: 阿贝尔 Pauli 码经装扮可得非阿贝尔拓扑序（如 D(D₄)）；三次扭转下码维数等于杯积中二次方程解数，可证明其非 Pauli 稳定子码；环面与板上的距离随尺寸线性增长。S³ 上三个环面码无逻辑量子比特，而在 ℝP³ 上同一电路实现逻辑 CCZ；三维费米子环面码的四分之一转扭转给出从 [[768,2,9]] 起的线性距离码，其 p+ip 泵浦为保持码空间的有限时间准局域演化，逻辑作用为 T（至多 Clifford 因子）。

Conclusion: 扭转可同时生成码、逻辑门与拓扑序；但扭转阿贝尔规范理论只能达到交换子群 [G,G] 中心的量子双重（quantum double）D(G)，界定了该构造的可达范围。

Abstract: Many quantum codes, including balanced-product, bivariate-bicycle and genon codes and the five-qubit code, are quotient codes: a larger code folded onto itself by identifying each check with its image under a deck transformation composed with a symmetry. We show that this is the lattice form of twisted compactification, in which a theory on $X\times F$ carries a symmetry background along $F$, and we use the correspondence in both directions. For monomial quotients of Pauli stabilizer codes, a transfer theorem gives the logical qudits, logical operators and distance bounds from those of the parent, with a Tate cohomology group counting the logical operators that are new. For higher-form and cup-product symmetries the twist is an entangling diagonal circuit; when a finite cover trivializes the background, the code is a dressed quotient, so abelian Pauli codes give non-abelian topological order such as $D(D_4)$. The dressings have Clifford level at most one less than the degree of the twist, and for cubic twists the code dimension is a count of solutions of quadratic equations in cup products, which certifies that the code is not a Pauli stabilizer code when it is not a power of two; on tori and slabs the distance is linear in the size. Twists also create logical gates: three toric codes on $S^3$ have no logical qubits, but on $\mathbb{RP}^3$ the same circuit is a logical $CCZ$. A quarter-turn twist of the 3D fermionic toric code gives codes of linear distance, from $[[768,2,9]]$ up, whose $p+ip$ pump is an explicit finite-time quasi-local evolution that preserves the code space exactly; using the spin-topological response formula of Barkeshli, Hsin and Kobayashi, we identify its logical action as $T$ up to Clifford factors. Finally, twisting abelian gauge theories reach only the quantum doubles $D(G)$ with $[G,G]$ central.

</details>


### [324] [An optimal quantum strategy for the $2$-hat game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11759)
*Georgios Baziotis,Sang-Jun Park,Sang-Gyun Youn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将双人帽子游戏建模为两输入、三输出的非局域博弈（non-local game），证明其量子交换值（quantum commuting value）与量子值相等，均为 (15+√33)/36 ≈ 0.576238，并给出最优量子优势的构造与无信号值（no-signalling value）2/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为双人帽子游戏（two-player hat game）的量子非局域性刻画，核心在于确定该博弈的量子值与量子交换值是否一致，并明确其相对无信号上界的优势程度。

Method: 将该游戏表述为两输入、三输出的非局域博弈；利用共享的非最大纠缠三态（non-maximally entangled qutrit state）构造最优策略；借助博弈在二面体群（dihedral group）D_8 下的对称性，应用表示论（representation theory）大幅约化底层矩阵问题的复杂度以证明最优性。

Result: 得到 ω_q(H_2) = ω_qc(H_2) = (15+√33)/36 ≈ 0.576238，即量子值与量子交换值重合；同时确定无信号值 ω_ns(H_2) = 2/3，最优量子优势由非最大纠缠 qutrit 态实现。

Conclusion: 该工作给出 H_2 帽子游戏量子值的精确解析表达及其与量子交换值的等同性，并示范了群对称性与表示论在非局域博弈最优性证明中的有效性。

Abstract: We study the two-player hat game as a two-input, three-output non-local game. We calculate the quantum commuting value and show that it coincides with the quantum one. In particular, we show that \[ω_{\rm q}(H_2)=ω_{\mathrm{qc}}(H_2)=\frac{15+\sqrt{33}}{36}\approx 0.576238.\] We achieve the optimal quantum advantage using a shared non-maximally entangled qutrit state. To establish optimality, we exploit the game's symmetry under the dihedral group $D_8$ and apply its representation theory to substantially reduce the complexity of the underlying matrix problem. We also determine the no-signalling value $ω_{\mathrm{ns}}(H_2)=2/3$.

</details>


### [325] [What an Observer Reads off a Holographic Screen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11356)
*Kenan Oggad,James F. Glazebrook,Chris Fields*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究以量子比特阵列构成的"全息屏"上观察者读出的谱信息。核心结论是：读出的内容仅由其权重剖面（profile）决定，且"在任意加权下都携带某剖面"的充要条件是各量子比特的非零迹在二元域上线性无关，从而将读出与划分（partition）、上下文性（contextuality）及模糊球极点距离等联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望回答一个基础问题：当全息屏（holographic screen）不是曲面而是量子比特阵列时，观察者究竟能从中读到什么。为此需要在广义全息原理（generalized holographic principle）框架下，刻画可分离系统间相互作用在屏上的加权表示及其可读谱结构。

Method: 将两系统相互作用表示为屏上相互对易、取值为正负一的观测量的加权和（含整体前因子），以权重列表"剖面"刻画谱。通过定义一个量子比特的"迹"（包含它的集合列表），证明读出在每种加权下都携带剖面当且仅当各不同非零迹在二元域（field with two elements）上线性无关；并用分组无限细化的塔（tower）取射有限集（profinite set）边界，进而视为凝聚集（condensed set）。

Result: 等权且权重和为一时，扩散探针（diffusion probe）在单位时间读出的跑动维数（running dimension）低于二的量不超过量子比特数倒数的两倍；几何剖面则经其能级计数给出任意正高度的平台。线性无关的读出在可逆重编码意义下是其覆盖量子比特的划分；相依的读出仅在权重的有限个真线性子空间上携带剖面。正兼容剖面可在凝聚集上以某距离分离局部常值函数。

Conclusion: 该框架可认证概率上下文性（probabilistic contextuality）与强上下文性（strong contextuality），并给出模糊球（fuzzy sphere）两极间距离的首阶亏损；在一夫一妻纠缠（monogamous entanglement）模型中，代表操作性 ER=EPR 的权重一线程可同时占据的对，恰为部分匹配（partial matchings）。

Abstract: What does an observer read off a holographic screen that is an array of qubits rather than a surface? In the generalized holographic principle the interaction between two separable systems acts on such a screen as a weighted sum, up to an overall prefactor, of commuting observables with values plus and minus one. When the weights are nonnegative and the prefactor is one and the joint eigenspaces are lines, everything a probe reads off the spectrum depends only on the list of weights, which we call the profile. On the screen with equal weights summing to one the running dimension read by a diffusion probe falls short of two at unit time by at most twice the reciprocal of the number of qubits; a geometric profile gives instead, through its level count, a plateau of any positive height. An observer correlates sets of qubits; its readout carries a profile at a given weighting when the levels it reads, counted with multiplicity, are those of a screen with one weight per set. We prove that a readout carries a profile at every weighting exactly when the distinct nonzero traces of its qubits are linearly independent over the field with two elements, the trace of a qubit being the list of sets that contain it; such a readout is a partition of the qubits it covers up to an invertible recoding, and a dependent one carries a profile only on finitely many proper linear subspaces of weightings. Refining a grouping without end builds a tower whose boundary is a profinite set and hence a condensed set, on which a strictly positive compatible profile separates the locally constant functions by a distance. Applications certify probabilistic and strong contextuality and give the leading shortfall of the distance between the poles of the fuzzy sphere; in a model of monogamous entanglement the pairs that weight-one threads representing operational ER=EPR can occupy at once are exactly the partial matchings.

</details>


### [326] [Quantum Robustness via Sandwiched Rényi Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12738)
*Luc Rey-Bellet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究在态仅有近似信息时观测值期望的最大可能变化，给出在 sandwiched Rényi 散度（sandwiched Rényi divergence）预算下最坏情形期望的完全刻画，并证明其可经量子经典化归约为经典概率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态通常只能近似已知，因此需要量化观测值期望在最坏情况下的偏差；已有界（如 Pinsker 型不等式）多限于特定散度或浅层结论，缺少对任意阶 α 的统一处理。

Method: 在参考态的 sandwiched Rényi 散度约束集合上取最坏期望，借助凸对偶（convex duality）将其化为关于 sandwiched Schatten 范数的一维优化；核心技术是经典化定理（classicalization theorem），构造谱区间上的概率测度 ω_α 使量子问题与经典问题重合。

Result: 对 α∈[1/2,∞] 得到显式最优值：α=1/2 为保真度风险（fidelity risk）、α=1 为熵风险（entropic risk）、α=∞ 为量子条件风险价值（quantum conditional value-at-risk）；经典 Hoeffding、Bernstein 与 Markov–Krein 界及带 Bernstein 修正的 Pinsker 型不等式随之适用。

Conclusion: ω_α 仅在 α=1/2 时为 Born 测度；当 α>1/2 时，观测值与态的非对易性（noncommutativity）使方差严格低于 Born 方差，从而削弱所有对方差敏感的界，即所谓量子收缩（quantum deflation）。

Abstract: If a quantum state is known only approximately, how much can the expectation of an observable change? We answer with the worst-case expectation over all states within a sandwiched Rényi divergence budget of a reference state, for every order $α\in [1/2,\infty]$. Convex duality gives this value as a one-dimensional optimization over a sandwiched Schatten norm, running from a fidelity risk at $α= 1/2$, through the entropic risk at $α= 1$, to a quantum conditional value-at-risk at $α= \infty$. The main result is a classicalization theorem: the quantum problem coincides with the classical one for a probability measure $ω_α$ on the spectral interval of the observable, constructed from Heinävaara's tracial joint spectral measure and given by Stahl's theorem at $α= 1$. The Hoeffding, Bernstein and Markov--Krein bounds of classical probability thereby apply to the quantum problem, and yield Pinsker-type inequalities with Bernstein-type refinements. The measure $ω_α$ is the Born measure only at $α= 1/2$; for $α> 1/2$, noncommutativity of the observable with the state strictly lowers its variance below the Born variance, and with it every variance-sensitive bound: a quantum deflation.

</details>


### [327] [Unified architecture for arbitrary two-qubit gates with neutral atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12779)
*Nader Mostaan,Peter Schmelcher,Rick Mukherjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种中性原子量子处理器（neutral-atom quantum processor）中双量子比特门（two-qubit gates）的统一架构，基于可调各向异性的 XXZ 相互作用（XXZ interaction with tunable anisotropy）实现完整双量子比特门集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中性原子平台在指令集灵活性上受限，难以原生实现任意双量子比特门，导致电路深度（circuit depth）与编译开销（compilation overhead）增加。作者旨在以单一资源解锁整套双量子比特门。

Method: 方法上仅依赖两个要素：可调各向异性的 XXZ 相互作用与单量子比特操作（single-qubit operations）。作者引入基于里德伯原子（Rydberg atom）的可调 XXZ 相互作用实现，并结合最优控制的全局微波脉冲（optimally controlled global microwave pulses）。

Result: 该方案实现了 B、QFT、iSWAP 与 $\sqrt{\mathrm{iSWAP}}$ 门，以及由 CZ 经 B 门连续插值至 iSWAP 的连续门族（continuous gate family）。

Conclusion: 该工作使中性原子平台可原生实现任意双量子比特门，并支持在指令集之间灵活切换，从而降低量子算法实现中的电路深度与编译开销。

Abstract: We propose a unified architecture for two-qubit gates in neutral-atom quantum processors which allows the implementation of the complete set of two-qubit gates. Our approach rests on a single resource that unlocks the entire gate set: an XXZ interaction with tunable anisotropy, combined only with single-qubit operations. We introduce a Rydberg-atom realization of the tunable XXZ interaction and combine it with optimally controlled global microwave pulses to implement the B, QFT, iSWAP, and $\sqrt{\mathrm{iSWAP}}$ gates, as well as a continuous gate family interpolating from CZ to iSWAP via the B gate. This work allows the implementation of any two-qubit gate natively in neutral-atom platforms, enabling flexible switching between instruction sets to reduce circuit depth and compilation overhead in quantum algorithm implementation.

</details>


### [328] [Effective Time Advance of Quantum Clocks under Postselection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12609)
*Youka Kaku,Akira Matsumura,Tomohiro Fujita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出量子后选择（quantum postselection）可在双生子佯谬（twin paradox）中有效逆转时间膨胀（time dilation）结果，使旅行者出现"时间超前"而非滞后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典狭义相对论预言旅行双生子因相对论时间膨胀而更年轻；作者希望探究量子测量中的后选择与干涉是否能改变这一结论。

Method: 采用可解析求解的量子时钟模型（quantum clock model），对质心运动进行后选择以产生反常弱值（anomalous weak values）；并推广至弱引力场（weak gravitational field）及类氢原子体系分析。

Result: 后选择质心运动可通过反常弱值产生有效时间超前（effective time advance），尽管每条运动分支仍服从普通时间膨胀；在弱引力场中对简单相干态（coherent states）亦成立，并可在原子波函数中显现。

Conclusion: 量子干涉与后选择能够产生超越常规相对论时间膨胀的有效时间超前效应。

Abstract: In the familiar twin paradox, the traveling twin ages less than the one who stays behind as a consequence of relativistic time dilation. Here, we show that quantum postselection can effectively reverse this outcome. Using an analytically solvable quantum clock model, we demonstrate that postselection of the center-of-mass motion produces an effective time advance through anomalous weak values, even though every individual motional branch undergoes ordinary time dilation. We extend this analysis to a weak gravitational field and show that an effective time advance can arise even for simple coherent states of the quantum clock motion. We further illustrate this effect using a hydrogen-like atom, revealing how it manifests itself in the atomic wavefunction. Our results demonstrate that quantum interference and postselection can produce an effective time advance beyond conventional relativistic time dilation.

</details>


### [329] [An efficient classical algorithm for OTOCs in chaotic quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12773)
*Yunchao Liu,Yihui Quek,Shreya Vardhan,Tianci Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出并实现了一种多项式时间经典算法，可在一维随机量子电路的典型实例中高效估计四点及八点乱序关联函数（OTOC），表明在涨落较大的混沌敏感区域OTOC估计具有平均情形下的经典可计算性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTOC估计被视为可验证量子优势（verifiable quantum advantage）的候选任务，并被证明对张量网络、蒙特卡洛等常规经典算法困难；作者试图借助量子混沌（quantum chaos）的洞察重新审视这一困难性假设。

Method: 将OTOC组织为快速收敛的级数：主导项来自复制希尔伯特空间（replicated Hilbert space）中置换自由度（permutation degrees of freedom）内的历史贡献，次主导项来自该空间之外的偏离；并论证混沌导致的退相位（dephasing）机制压制次主导项。

Result: 理论分析与数值证据均支持次主导项的压制；实际实现的一维电路算法可高精度估计OTOC^{(1)}与OTOC^{(2)}，且在更大电路深度下相比Google近期二维实验具有更高信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）。

Conclusion: 混沌并非经典模拟的不可逾越障碍，反而可被算法利用；向高维推广及判定二维OTOC中量子优势的地位仍为开放问题（open problems）。

Abstract: Estimating out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) in individual instances of random quantum circuits has been proposed as a candidate for verifiable quantum advantage, and demonstrated to be hard for conventional classical algorithms such as tensor networks and Monte Carlo. Using insights from quantum chaos, we develop and implement a polynomial-time classical algorithm for the four-point ${\rm OTOC}^{(1)}$ and the eight-point ${\rm OTOC}^{(2)}$ in typical instances of one-dimensional (1D) quantum circuits. Our results demonstrate average-case classical tractability of estimating OTOCs in the sensitive regime where circuit-to-circuit fluctuations are large. Our algorithm organizes the OTOC into a rapidly converging series, where the leading term includes contributions from histories within permutation degrees of freedom in the replicated Hilbert space, and subleading terms arise from excursions outside this space. Although chaos is often viewed as an insurmountable barrier to classical simulation, our algorithm exploits a form of dephasing caused by chaos, which suppresses the subleading terms. We provide strong theoretical and numerical evidence to support this suppression. Further, we demonstrate that practical implementations of our algorithm estimate ${\rm OTOC}^{(1)}$ and ${\rm OTOC}^{(2)}$ in 1D circuits with high accuracy, achieving higher signal-to-noise ratio at larger circuit depths compared to recent 2D experiments by Google. Extending our algorithm to higher dimensions, and resolving the status of quantum advantage in 2D OTOCs, remain open problems.

</details>


### [330] [Entanglement percolation enhanced by the power of choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10119)
*Yanxuan Shao,Adilson E. Motter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于“选择之力”（power-of-choice, PoC）协议，通过反转经典爆炸渗流中Achlioptas过程的选择判据，使纠缠渗流转变（entanglement percolation transition）提前发生，从而减少建立量子通信网络所需的纠缠资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信网络理想上依赖最大纠缠态（maximally entangled states），但纠缠产生与分发过程中的不完美使实际分布态仅为部分纠缠，需借助局域操作与经典通信（local operations and classical communication, LOCC）协议构建合用的纠缠网络，因此有必要降低所需纠缠资源。

Method: 提出PoC协议，其思路源于对经典爆炸渗流（explosive percolation）中Achlioptas过程选择准则的反转；该协议可仅利用局域信息（local information）实现。

Result: PoC协议可使纠缠渗流转变提前发生，从而减少建立量子通信所需的纠缠资源；且不同于爆炸渗流，其局域信息实现不会带来明显性能损失。

Conclusion: 反转Achlioptas选择准则的PoC方案为纠缠网络构建提供了一种资源节约且易于局域实施的途径，并显示出与爆炸渗流在可实现性上的差异。

Abstract: Quantum communication networks are best supported by maximally entangled states, but imperfections in entanglement generation and distribution generally limit the distributed states to being only partially entangled. Therefore, protocols typically consisting of local operations and classical communication are required to create suitably entangled networks. Here, we introduce a power-of-choice (PoC) protocol based on reversing the selection criteria in the Achlioptas process previously considered in classical explosive percolation. The resulting PoC protocol anticipates the entanglement percolation transition, thereby reducing the entanglement resources needed to establish quantum communication. Notably, in contrast to explosive percolation, the PoC protocol admits an implementation using local information with no appreciable loss of performance.

</details>


### [331] [Regev's Reduction Reaches Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10283)
*Stephen P. Jordan,Quynh T. Nguyen,Leo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明 Regev 归约结合 BPQM 量子解码所能达到的最低温度，恰等于树网络上含噪信道的重构阈值温度，并将其等同于 1RSB 预言的重构温度与动力学玻璃转变温度，从而在严格意义上展示了超越经典局部采样的低温可达性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典自旋玻璃（spin glass）哈密顿量在低温下的吉布斯采样以及 D-正则 max-k-LINSAT 的平均情形优化问题在低温下变难，经典局部采样器（如 Metropolis Monte Carlo、Glauber 动力学）被猜想无法在重构温度以下高效平衡，而此前缺乏严格的量子方法保证。

Method: 采用 Regev 归约（利用量子傅里叶变换在格与码相关问题间做归约）处理特定经典自旋玻璃哈密顿量的吉布斯分布采样，并用称为 Belief Propagation with Quantum Messages (BPQM) 的量子解码算法求解归约中产生的解码问题；借助 Renes 关于经典与量子信道的对偶性，给出可达最低温度与重构阈值温度相等性的证明，并辅以覆盖数百组问题参数的大规模数值实验。

Result: 主要贡献是证明了 Regev+BPQM 可达的最低温度精确等于树网络含噪信道的重构阈值温度；经由 Renes 对偶性可得，量子解码可行的条件恰为对偶问题上的经典重构不可行。Mézard 与 Montanari 将该重构温度识别为 1RSB 腔方程预言的动力学玻璃转变温度，数值实验在数百组参数下与该预测高度吻合。

Conclusion: 结果表明 Regev+BPQM 严格达到了一个（据作者所知）不存在匹配经典保证的温度，并逼近经典局部动力学高效吉布斯采样的猜想极限，凸显了量子方法在低温采样中的优势。

Abstract: Regev's reduction uses a quantum Fourier transform to carry out reductions between various problems related to lattices and codes. We apply it to the problem of sampling from Gibbs distributions of certain classical spin glass Hamiltonians, which can equivalently be thought of as cost functions for average-case instances of an optimization problem called $D$-regular max-$k$-LINSAT. This problem becomes hard at low temperature. Here, we use a quantum decoding algorithm called Belief Propagation with Quantum Messages (BPQM) to solve the decoding problem arising from Regev's reduction. Our main contribution is a proof that the minimum temperature reachable using Regev+BPQM is exactly equal to the reconstruction threshold temperature for noisy channels transmitting signals on a tree network. This equality can be understood via Renes' duality between classical and quantum channels, which we use to show that quantum decoding is possible exactly when classical reconstruction on the dual problem is impossible. M{é}zard and Montanari identified this reconstruction temperature with a dynamical glass transition predicted by the one-step replica-symmetry breaking (1RSB) cavity equation. It is conjectured that local samplers such as Metropolis Monte Carlo and Glauber dynamics cannot equilibrate efficiently below this reconstruction temperature but may do so above it; our large numerical experiments show close agreement with this prediction across hundreds of different problem parameters. Therefore, our results rigorously show that Regev+BPQM reaches a temperature for which, to our knowledge, there is no matching classical guarantee, and brings Regev+BPQM to the conjectured limit of efficient Gibbs sampling by classical local dynamics.

</details>


### [332] [Hidden Coherence and Controlled Quasihole Motion in a Fermionic Laughlin State on a Superconducting Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10687)
*Kui Zhao,Hao Wu,Lei-Yi-Nan Liu,Gui-Han Liang,Cheng-Lin Deng,Cai-Ping Fang,Hao-Tian Liu,Wei-Ping Yuan,Qingyuan Wu,Ziting Wang,Wei-Guo Ma,Hao Li,Jia-Chi Zhang,Tian-Ming Li,Jia-Cheng Song,Zhen-Ting Bao,Zheng-He Liu,Yang He,Yong-Xi Xiao,Yu Liu,Zhongcheng Xiang,Dongning Zheng,Guangming Xue,Haifeng Yu,Kai Xu,Zhao Liu,Kaixuan Huang,Jian Cui,Heng Fan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在七个超导量子比特（superconducting qubits）构成的 Haldane 球（Haldane sphere）平台上制备三电子 ν=1/3 Laughlin 态，并重构其多体密度矩阵，从而实验刻画分数霍尔态（fractional quantum Hall, FQH）波函数中的多体相干性与准空穴（quasihole）响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FQH 态承载分数化激发（fractionalized excitations）与几何相位（geometric phases），但其波函数所编码的多体相干性（many-body coherence）在实验中长期难以分辨，缺乏微观层面的直接探测手段。

Method: 在 Haldane 球上用七个超导量子比特制备三电子 ν=1/3 Laughlin 态并重构多体密度矩阵；借助 Read 的复合玻色子构造（composite-boson construction）提取涡旋修饰的相干性，并以测量布居数构造 Fock 对角参考态作对照；另制备单磁通附加准空穴，通过受控移动评估几何相位特征。

Result: 涡旋修饰相干（vortex-dressed coherence）在更大间距范围内保持可观测，且明显超过电子相干；该延展分布在 Fock 对角参考态中被抑制，表明其依赖非对角多体相干。单磁通准空穴的分数电荷响应与有限尺寸基准（finite-size benchmarks）相符，受控移动给出有效几何相位签名。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了对有限费米型 Laughlin 态相干结构与准空穴响应的微观测量，展示了在可编程量子处理器（programmable quantum processors）上探测强关联 FQH 波函数的实验路径。

Abstract: Fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states support fractionalized excitations and geometric phases, yet experimentally resolving the many-body coherence encoded in their wave functions remains challenging. Here, we prepare a three-electron $ν=1/3$ Laughlin state on the Haldane sphere using seven superconducting qubits and reconstruct its many-body density matrix. Through Read's composite-boson construction, we reveal vortex-dressed coherence that remains appreciable over a larger separation range than electron coherence. The extended profile is suppressed in a Fock-diagonal reference constructed from the measured populations, demonstrating its dependence on off-diagonal many-body coherence. We further prepare a one-flux-added quasihole and characterize its fractional charge response against finite-size benchmarks. Controlled quasihole motion yields an effective geometric-phase signature from the reconstructed dominant-eigenstate trajectory. These complementary measurements provide microscopic access to the coherent structure and quasihole response of a finite fermionic Laughlin state, demonstrating an experimental route to probing strongly correlated FQH wave functions on programmable quantum processors.

</details>


### [333] [One Measurement, Many Fronts in Kitaev and Cluster Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10912)
*Nicholas Musat,Stephan Haas,Rishabh Jha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文精确求解了 Kitaev 链、横场 cluster 链及幂律耦合 Kitaev 链中单点投影测量（projective measurement）后的扰动传播，发现远离测量点的每个二次可观测量都通过一个“前沿”（front）响应：前沿位置与展宽只由准粒子色散（quasiparticle dispersion）决定，强度由测量算符产生最快准粒子的能力与观测算符探测它们的能力两个因子决定，所有衰减规律与剖面均以闭式给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单点投影测量通过两条途径扰动量子链：读出结果经基态关联（ground-state correlations）瞬间移动远处期望值，测量本身抹去被测点与其余部分的关联；两种变化均向外传播，需厘清其传播规律与依赖因素。

Method: 对 Kitaev 链、横场 cluster 链以及幂律耦合（power-law couplings）的 Kitaev 链进行精确求解，分析测量后各二次可观测量的响应前沿位置、展宽、强度与衰减规律，并以闭式（closed form）给出全部结果。

Result: 前沿位置与展宽仅依赖准粒子色散；强度依赖两个算符因素——被测算符产生最快准粒子的强度与观测算符探测它们的强度，任一因子为零则前沿更弱且衰减更快。一次测量同时发射多个前沿，位置与宽度相同而衰减规律各异。Kitaev 链中密度与键能（近邻哈密顿量的两项）的衰减规律在拓扑相（topological phase）与平凡相（trivial phase）间互换；cluster 链中测量对称奇自旋与对称偶自旋在固定哈密顿量下改变临界衰减规律；幂律耦合下展宽规律本身改变。

Conclusion: 单点测量引发的传播可由准粒子图像统一刻画：共同前沿几何由色散确定，衰减规律则编码了算符内容与相的性质，且全部结果可解析获得。

Abstract: A projective measurement of one site in the ground state of a quantum chain disturbs the chain in two ways: learning the outcome shifts distant expectation values at once, through ground-state correlations, and the measurement erases the correlations between the measured site and the rest. Both changes travel outward. We solve this problem exactly for the Kitaev chain, the cluster chain in a transverse field, and the Kitaev chain with power-law couplings, and we find that far from the measured site every quadratic observable responds through a front. Where the front is and how fast it broadens depend only on the quasiparticle dispersion. How strong it is depends on two operators: how strongly the measured one creates the fastest quasiparticles and how strongly the observed one detects them; if either factor vanishes, the front is weaker and decays faster. One measurement therefore launches many fronts, one per observable, with a common position and width but different decay laws. In the Kitaev chain the density and the bond energy, the two terms of its nearest-neighbor Hamiltonian, respond to one density measurement with decay laws that trade places between the topological and the trivial phase; in the cluster chain, measuring a symmetry-odd spin instead of a symmetry-even one changes the critical decay law at a fixed Hamiltonian; and with power-law couplings the width law itself changes. All decay laws and profiles are obtained in closed form.

</details>


### [334] [Deterministically fabricated C-band single-photon sources based on quantum dot circular Bragg grating cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10988)
*Monica Pengerla,Yury Berdnikov,Alexander Kosarev,Dominik M. Feder,Avijit Barua,Aris Koulas-Simos,Paweł Holewa,Sven Rodt,Imad Limame,Elizaveta Semenova,Stephan Reitzenstein*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将金属有机气相外延（MOVPE）生长的C波段InAs/InP量子点确定性集成到圆形布拉格光栅（circular Bragg grating）谐振腔中，获得单光子发射性能良好的电信波长光源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展光纤量子网络（fiber-based quantum networks）需要电信波段、可按需发射单光子的高质量光源，而MOVPE生长与确定性器件集成的结合是实现规模化的重要途径。

Method: 采用MOVPE生长C波段InAs/InP量子点（quantum dots），确定性集成至圆形布拉格光栅谐振器，并在非共振激发下测量辐射寿命、谱线宽度、单光子纯度与双光子干涉（two-photon interference）可见度。

Result: 辐射寿命T1=(1400±50) ps，线宽ΔE_FWHM=(41.0±4.0) μeV；后选择单光子纯度为(0.95±0.02)，原始双光子干涉可见度V_12ns=(35±2)%，时间后选择后提升至(70±18)%。

Conclusion: 结果将MOVPE生长、确定性器件集成与电信C波段发射相结合，为光纤量子网络的可扩展半导体量子光源提供了可行路径；作者讨论了辐射速率调控、电荷稳定化与更选择性激发等提升光子不可区分性的策略。

Abstract: Telecom-wavelength quantum light sources are essential components for scalable fiber-based quantum networks, where on-demand emission of single photons is required for high secret key rates. Here, we demonstrate the deterministic integration of metal-organic vapor-phase epitaxy (MOVPE)-grown C-band InAs/InP quantum dots into circular Bragg grating resonators. The integrated emitters exhibit a radiative lifetime of $T_1 = (1400 \pm 50)~\mathrm{ps}$ and a spectral linewidth of $ΔE_{\mathrm{FWHM}} = (41.0 \pm 4.0)~μ\mathrm{eV}$. Under non-resonant excitation, we measure a post-selected single-photon purity of $(0.95 \pm 0.02)$ and a raw two-photon interference visibility of $V_{12\mathrm{ns}} = (35 \pm 2)\%$, which increases to $(70 \pm 18)\%$ under temporal post-selection. We discuss strategies to improve photon indistinguishability, including radiative-rate engineering, charge stabilization, and more selective excitation. Our results combine MOVPE growth, deterministic device integration, and telecom C-band emission, providing a route toward scalable semiconductor quantum light sources for fiber-based quantum networks.

</details>


### [335] [Bridging Gaps with Tensor Networks: From the Haldane Gap and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11049)
*Sirui Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作对 OpenAI 关于周期自旋-1海森堡链（spin-1 Heisenberg chain）均匀能隙（uniform gap）的计算机辅助证明进行核验、消化与形式化，借助张量网络图（tensor-network diagrams）与 Lean 4 完成，并将能隙下界改进至所有偶数 L≥36 时 Δ_L > 0.0729。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（large language models）生成的证明往往难以理解、推广或直接形式化，因而需要将其转化为透明、可复用的物理机制与可验证形式证明。

Method: 沿空间与虚时间方向读取配分函数网络（partition-function network），追踪有限环界（finite-ring bounds）如何经双倍链长与逆温度传播至所有更长的偶数环；并使用张量网络图与 Lean 4 进行核验与形式化。

Result: 在相同有限输入下将自旋-1能隙界收紧 19 倍，得到所有偶数 L≥36 时 Δ_L > 0.0729；识别出反射正定性（reflection positivity）为该证明背后的普遍条件，并在另外七条自旋链与梯子（spin chains and ladders）中证明了均匀能隙。

Conclusion: 将机器生成的证明消化为可理解的物理机制，可获得更紧的界、更普遍的定理以及可复用的形式化验证，表明该分析路径具有推广价值。

Abstract: Proofs generated by large language models are often difficult to understand, generalize, or formalize directly. Here we verify, digest, and formalize OpenAI's computer-assisted proof of a uniform gap in the periodic spin-1 Heisenberg chain using tensor-network diagrams and Lean 4. Reading the partition-function network along space and imaginary time shows how finite-ring bounds propagate across doubled lengths and inverse temperatures to all longer even rings, sharpening the spin-1 bound from the same finite inputs by a factor of 19 to $Δ_L > 0.0729$ for all even $L \geq 36$. We identify reflection positivity as the general condition behind the proof and certify uniform gaps in seven additional spin chains and ladders. More broadly, digesting machine-generated proofs into transparent physical mechanisms yields sharper bounds, broader theorems, and reusable formal verifications.

</details>


### [336] [Quantum entanglement as a resource for self-organization and stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11175)
*Kazuki Ikeda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在一个受驱动的耗散玻色子晶格（driven-dissipative bosonic lattice）中，局域纠缠（local entanglement）可促成一种输运，将空间涨落放大为扩展的图灵斑图（Turing patterns）。对含噪声与无序的全非线性动力学，作者给出所有可分离演化（separable evolutions）下斑图强度的有限时间严格上界，并证明量子动力学可超越该界限，即使纠缠随后消失，宏观有序仍可通过单一定常 Lindblad 方程持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：量子纠缠（quantum entanglement）能否产生在纠缠本身消失之后仍然持续存在的宏观有序。该问题连接量子资源与非平衡自组织（self-organization in nonequilibrium systems），具有基础与潜在应用价值。

Method: 采用受驱动耗散玻色子晶格模型，分析含噪声与无序的全非线性动力学；推导允许任意经典关联（classical correlations）的可分离演化下斑图强度的有限时间严格界限；并引入对环境响应机制的建模，其中环境通过抑制局域相干性（local coherence）并激活经典输运来回应涌现的有序，整体由单一时间无关的 Lindblad 方程描述。

Result: 量子动力学在斑图形成（formation）与噪声下的稳定化（stabilization）两个阶段均产生超出可分离界限的斑图强度；超额强度还给出超越可分离极限的累积输运贡献的下界。环境响应使斑图在纠缠丧失后仍持续存在，且两个阶段均由同一时间无关的 Lindblad 方程导出。

Conclusion: 结果表明瞬态量子资源（transient quantum resources）可建立持久的宏观有序，为非平衡系统中利用自组织指明新方向，即纠缠可作为宏观秩序生成的“种子”，而秩序可在纠缠消退后依靠经典输运维持。

Abstract: Can quantum entanglement generate macroscopic order that persists after the entanglement itself disappears? We establish such a mechanism in a driven-dissipative bosonic lattice, where local entanglement enables transport that amplifies spatial fluctuations into extended Turing patterns. For the full nonlinear dynamics with noise and disorder, we derive rigorous finite-time bounds on pattern intensity for all admissible separable evolutions, allowing arbitrary classical correlations. Quantum dynamics produces pattern intensities beyond these bounds during both formation and stabilization under noise. The excess intensity also provides a lower bound on the accumulated transport contribution beyond the separable limit. We further introduce an environment that responds to emerging order by suppressing local coherence and activating classical transport. The patterns then persist as local entanglement is lost, with both stages arising from a single time-independent Lindblad equation. These results show how transient quantum resources can establish persistent macroscopic order, opening new directions for harnessing self-organization in nonequilibrium systems.

</details>


### [337] [A Microscopic Model Underpinning Macroscopic QED in Active Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11557)
*Daigo Oue*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为活性介质（active media）中的宏观量子电动力学（macroscopic quantum electrodynamics）建立微观基础：从受驱动的三能级系统出发，导出具有非平衡稳态布居的有效二能级描述，并借助量子中心极限方法（quantum central limit approach）获得集体朗之万动力学（collective Langevin dynamics）。由同一微观稳态同时给出磁化率与量子噪声流关联，并证明光学透明时噪声仍有限并可加热邻近量子探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观量子电动力学的储库算符通常作为唯象输入，缺乏与微观非平衡稳态的直接联系；本文旨在为活性介质情形建立微观基础，使光学响应与量子噪声来自同一微观来源。

Method: 以受驱动三能级系统为起点，导出具有非平衡稳态布居（nonequilibrium stationary populations）的有效二能级描述，再用量子中心极限方法得到集体朗之万动力学，从而在同一稳态下计算电磁磁化率（electromagnetic susceptibility）与量子噪声流关联（quantum noise-current correlations）。

Result: 光学响应由布居差（population difference）决定，而噪声关联分别依赖两个能级的布居；因此在光学透明（optical transparency）处，磁化率的虚部消失而量子涨落仍为有限值。所得噪声统计可用有效储库占据数（effective reservoir occupation）及涉及两个真空模的纯化（purification）解释，并满足相应的有效温度涨落—耗散关系（fluctuation–dissipation relation）。

Conclusion: 该构造将常规宏观量子电动力学的储库算符与微观稳态直接对接；透明处的残余涨落可导致邻近量子探针（quantum probe）的有限加热，表明噪声不可忽略且具可观测后果。

Abstract: We establish a microscopic foundation for macroscopic quantum electrodynamics in active media. Starting from a driven three-level system, we derive an effective two-level description with nonequilibrium stationary populations and employ a quantum central limit approach to obtain collective Langevin dynamics. This construction yields the electromagnetic susceptibility and quantum noise-current correlations from the same microscopic stationary state, providing a direct connection to the reservoir operators of conventional macroscopic quantum electrodynamics. We show that the optical response is governed by the population difference, whereas the noise correlations depend separately on the populations of the two levels. Consequently, at optical transparency, the imaginary part of the susceptibility vanishes while quantum fluctuations remain finite. We interpret the resulting noise statistics in terms of an effective reservoir occupation and a purification involving two vacuum modes, and establish a corresponding effective-temperature fluctuation--dissipation relation. Finally, we demonstrate that the residual fluctuations at transparency can induce finite heating of a nearby quantum probe.

</details>


### [338] [Pauli propagation in adaptive local frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11647)
*Jonnathan Pineda-Jiménez,Mario Collura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出泡利传播（Pauli propagation）的代价依赖于展开算子所用的局域基，并将其泡利复杂度拆分为与局域旋转无关的占据部分和可由合适基消除的"味"部分，进而提出逐门旋转基的帧自适应泡利传播（FAPP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泡利传播的计算代价取决于演化算子展开所采用的局域基，但基选择对复杂度的影响缺乏定量刻画，因此需要理解复杂度的基依赖结构并据此设计更优的基选择策略。

Method: 将泡利复杂度分解为占据部分（在局域旋转下不变，受算子质量与长度分布下界约束）与味部分（可由合适基消除）；提出FAPP，逐门将每个量子比特的基旋转至算子局域极化（local polarisation）的主轴，精确且保持在泡利传播的内存结构之内。

Result: 该简单规则可实现最优局域帧的大部分收益，且极化可预测任意局域帧的增益上限；在横纵场Ising链中，固定内存下截断误差最多降低一个数量级、内存降低与规模无关的三倍；无限二维格点上达到给定认证精度所需字符串最多减少七倍；掺杂T门的Clifford电路中减少二至八倍。

Conclusion: 泡利传播代价的基依赖性可被清晰分解，局域极化是选择有效基的关键量，FAPP以低开销实现了显著的内存与精度收益。

Abstract: The cost of Pauli propagation depends on the local basis in which the evolving operator is expanded. We show that its Pauli complexity splits into an occupation part, invariant under local rotations and bounded from below by the operator mass and length distributions, and a flavour part that a suitable basis can remove. We introduce frame-adaptive Pauli propagation (FAPP), which rotates the basis of every qubit, gate by gate, to the principal axes of the operator's local polarisation, exactly and within the memory structure of Pauli propagation. This simple rule achieves most of what the best local frame can, and the polarisation predicts how much any local frame can gain. In the Ising chain in transverse and longitudinal fields, FAPP lowers the truncation error at fixed memory by up to an order of magnitude and the memory by a size-independent factor of three; on the infinite two-dimensional lattice it needs up to seven times fewer strings for a given certified accuracy, and in Clifford circuits doped with T gates two to eight times fewer.

</details>


### [339] [AI-Driven Discovery of Entanglement Witness in Spin Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11935)
*Jiwei Tian,Mouyang Cheng,Mingda Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 EntNet 框架，仅用低阶局域自旋观测量即可高精度区分纠缠态与完全可分离态，并具备跨尺寸、跨模型的迁移性与物理解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从实验可及观测量出发探测多体态纠缠仍然困难，亟需一种可迁移且可解释的纠缠检测方法。

Method: 构建最多四体阶的、局域幺正不变的 Pauli 关联量表示，稀疏选择 16 个不变特征通道，并用带旋转位置嵌入（rotary positional embeddings）的 Transformer 学习其空间结构；对象为一维自旋-1/2 链、短程哈密顿量下的混合态。

Result: 在已认证纠缠态上召回率（recall）超过 98%，对可分离态假阳性率接近零；在系统尺寸及含阻挫、无序的自旋模型间迁移时精度损失极小；决策边界追踪 Jordan-Wigner 准粒子的激发能隙。

Conclusion: 确立了从局域关联函数出发、数据驱动发现纠缠目击者（entanglement witnesses）的可迁移且具物理可解释性的框架。

Abstract: Entanglement is a crucial resource in quantum science and engineering, but detecting it in many-body states from experimentally accessible observables remains challenging. Here we introduce EntNet, an AI-driven framework to discover compact combinations of spin observables that accurately distinguish entangled from fully separable many-body mixed states in one-dimensional spin-$1/2$ chains governed by short-range Hamiltonians. We construct local-unitary-invariant representations of measurable Pauli correlators up to four-body order, sparsely select 16 candidate invariant feature channels, and learn their spatial structure using a Transformer architecture with rotary positional embeddings. Using only expectation values of these low-order observables, EntNet achieves $>98\%$ recall on certified entangled states with near-zero false-positive rates on separable states. Remarkably, it exhibits transferability across system sizes and distinct Hamiltonian families, including frustrated and disordered spin models, with minimal loss of accuracy. Beyond detection, EntNet provides physical insight into entanglement detection by revealing a selective spatial organization of pair correlations, while its finite-temperature decision boundary tracks the excitation gap of Jordan-Wigner quasiparticles. These results establish a transferable and physically interpretable framework for data-driven discovery of entanglement witnesses from local correlation functions.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [340] [The structure of the pion at large $N_c$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12771)
*Isaac Zhu,Fernando Romero-López,Phiala E. Shanahan*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 在大$N_c$极限下利用Twisted Eguchi-Kawai（TEK）约化模型计算了π介子夸克动量分数$\langle x\rangle_q$，得到$\langle x\rangle_q^{\overline{\mathrm{MS}}}(2\,\mathrm{GeV}) = 0.267(105)$，显著小于$N_c=3$的现有结果，暗示大$N_c$极限下π介子光锥动量主要由胶子携带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有$N_c=3$的唯象与格点QCD测定已给出π介子夸克动量分数，但大$N_c$极限下的对应值尚缺乏直接计算，而该极限对理解π介子部分子结构的胶子-夸克分配具有理论意义。

Method: 采用纯规范TEK作用量在不同格距下生成规范组态，并以最大扭曲质量费米子（maximally twisted mass fermions）计算不同夸克质量下的π介子关联函数；通过Feynman-Hellmann方法提取裸矩阵元，并使用微扰重整化因子（renormalization factors）转换至$2\,\mathrm{GeV}$的$\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$方案。

Result: 对多个格距、π介子质量及$N_c$值的全局分析给出物理π介子质量、大$N_c$极限下$\langle x\rangle_q^{\overline{\mathrm{MS}}}(2\,\mathrm{GeV}) = 0.267(105)$，该值明显低于$N_c=3$的唯象和格点QCD测定结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明在大$N_c$极限下π介子的光锥动量（light-cone momentum）主要由胶子携带，而非夸克，提示夸克动量分数具有显著的$N_c$依赖性。

Abstract: We compute the pion quark momentum fraction in the large-$N_c$ limit using the Twisted Eguchi-Kawai (TEK) reduced model. Gauge configurations for different values of the lattice spacing are generated with the pure-gauge TEK action, while the pion correlators are computed for different quark masses with maximally twisted mass fermions. The bare matrix element corresponding to the quark momentum fraction is obtained using the Feynman-Hellmann method, and we use perturbative renormalization factors to connect to the $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ scheme at $2\,\mathrm{GeV}$. A global analysis at several lattice spacings, pion masses, and values of $N_c$ yields $\langle x\rangle_q^{\overline{\mathrm{MS}}}(2\,\mathrm{GeV}) = 0.267(105)$ at the physical pion mass in the large-$N_c$ limit. This value is significantly smaller than existing $N_c=3$ determinations from phenomenology and lattice QCD, indicating that, in the large-$N_c$ limit, the pion's light-cone momentum is carried predominantly by gluons.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [341] [Algebraic classification of single-field inflationary dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07331)
*Jinn-Ouk Gong,Wei-Chen Lin*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文通过精确退压三次方程（depressed cubic equation）对正则单场暴胀（canonical single-field inflation）动力学进行代数分类，并据此构建统一动力学相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究倾向将暴胀模型归入固定类别，缺乏对暴胀轨迹所经历动力学区间的刻画，本文旨在转向描述轨迹穿越的动力学区域及其对原初扰动（primordial perturbations）的影响。

Method: 对暴胀子速度（inflaton velocity）建立精确三次方程，由其根结构确定分支切换边界（branch-swap boundary）与下坡运动极限边界；同时利用长波极限下共动曲率扰动（comoving curvature perturbation）共轭动量的守恒性，给出规范不变差异增长或衰减的判据。

Result: 超慢滚暴胀（ultra-slow-roll inflation）自然出现在分支切换边界处；结合两类判据，在哈勃与势慢滚参数（Hubble and potential slow-roll parameters）平面内构建了统一动力学相图，并展示其对常滚暴胀（constant-roll inflation）与简单玩具模型的适用性。

Conclusion: 分类框架将重点从模型归类转向理解暴胀轨迹所经历的动力学区域及其对原初扰动的含义，为单场暴胀动力学提供统一的代数与相图描述。

Abstract: We develop an algebraic classification of canonical single-field inflationary dynamics using an exact depressed cubic equation for the inflaton velocity. Its root structure identifies a branch-swap boundary distinct from the limiting boundary of downhill motion, where ultra-slow-roll inflation naturally emerges. Independently, the conservation of momentum conjugate to the comoving curvature perturbation in the long-wavelength limit provides a criterion for the growth or decay of the gauge-invariant difference between comoving and uniform-density curvature perturbations. Combining these criteria, we construct a unified dynamical chart in terms of the Hubble and potential slow-roll parameters, illustrating its applicability to constant-roll inflation and simple toy models. Our classification shifts the emphasis from assigning models to fixed categories toward understanding the dynamical regimes traversed by inflationary trajectories and their implications for primordial perturbations.

</details>


### [342] [Gravitational Production of Non-minimally Coupled Vector Dark Matter in Warm Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10090)
*Phongsakorn Sereewat*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究在暖暴胀（warm inflation）中，与大质量矢量暗物质（massive vector dark matter）与爱因斯坦张量非最小耦合下的纯引力产生机制，综合考虑冻结产生（freeze-in）与宇宙学引力粒子产生（CGPP）两个通道并给出可行的暗物质质量窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯引力产生为暗物质提供不依赖新物理耦合的产生途径，而暖暴胀背景与爱因斯坦张量非最小耦合可能显著改变产生率，需要定量评估其对参数空间的限制。

Method: 取背景势 V=λφ^4 与线性依赖温度的耗散系数，用 CMB 观测约束并以耗散比 10^{-4}≤Q_0≤1 参数化；分别计算经引力子交换的标准模型热浴冻结产生（含 ξ 诱导的接触相互作用振幅）与 CGPP，并合并两通道。

Result: 冻结产生中 ξ 接触项通过纵向模式增强轻暗物质、通过相消干涉压低重暗物质；CGPP 中慢滚参数（slow-roll parameter）并不小，使轻暗物质产量相对冷暴胀最高提升三个数量级。合并后 CGPP 排除大部分轻冻结产生分支，仅剩 m_X≲2.4×10^{-5} GeV、强耗散下 10^{10} GeV 附近的窄窗口以及 m_X≳10^{14}–10^{15} GeV。

Conclusion: 在暖暴胀中，引力产生通道可将暗物质参数空间限制为轻质量端点、强耗散下的窄窗口与重质量分支；对全部 |ξ|≲1，仅重质量分支始终可行。

Abstract: We study the purely gravitational production of a massive vector dark matter with a non-minimal coupling to the Einstein tensor, $ξG_{μν}X^μX^ν$, in warm inflation. The background, $V=λφ^4$ with a dissipation coefficient linear in temperature, is constrained by CMB observations and parametrized by the dissipation ratio $10^{-4}\le Q_0\le1$. We consider both unavoidable production channels: freeze-in from the Standard Model bath through graviton exchange, and cosmological gravitational particle production (CGPP). For freeze-in, we compute the scattering amplitudes including the contact interaction induced by $ξ$, which enhances the production of light dark matter through the longitudinal mode and suppresses that of heavy dark matter through destructive interference. For CGPP, the slow-roll parameter is not small during warm inflation, which enhances the production of light dark matter by up to three orders of magnitude relative to cold inflation. Combining both channels, CGPP excludes most of the light freeze-in branch, leaving $m_X\lesssim2.4\times10^{-5}$ GeV, a narrow window around $10^{10}$ GeV for strong dissipation, and $m_X\gtrsim10^{14}$--$10^{15}$ GeV. Only the heavy branch remains viable for all $|ξ|\lesssim1$.

</details>


### [343] [Constraining Cosmic Deceleration using the Solar System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10596)
*Paridhi Jain,Oem Trivedi,Maxim Khlopov,Jackson Levi Said,Maxim Krasnov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文探讨太阳系动力学（Solar System dynamics）能否对当前宇宙学减速参数（cosmological deceleration parameter）$q_0$ 提供独立约束。通过将宇宙加速映射为弱的胡克型径向微扰（weak Hooke-type radial perturbation），结合行星近日点进动（planetary perihelion precession）、海外天体（trans-Neptunian objects）与奥尔特云（Oort cloud）动力学给出 $|q_0|$ 的上限估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规宇宙学探针（cosmological probes）对 $q_0$ 的测量存在方法依赖性，作者希望寻找一种独立于传统宇宙学观测的互补途径（complementary approach），检验太阳系轨道动力学是否可作为探测宇宙加速的实验室。

Method: 将宇宙加速等效为弱的胡克型径向微扰，利用行星近日点进动、海外天体与奥尔特云动力学推导 $q_0$ 的界限；并采用蒙特卡罗不确定性传播（Monte Carlo uncertainty propagation）与敏感性分析（sensitivity analyses）评估估计的统计稳健性及其对轨道测量的依赖。

Result: 行星数据给出较为稳健的界限 $|q_0|\lesssim 10^9$；太阳系外部区域可给出接近 $10^5$ 的更强限制，但结果更具模型依赖性（model dependent）。蒙特卡罗传播与敏感性分析揭示了这些估计的统计稳健性及对轨道测量精度的依赖。

Conclusion: 太阳系动力学可作为探测宇宙加速的互补实验室；未来的高精度天体测量（precision astrometry）与外行星探测任务（outer planet missions）有望将上述界限提升数个量级。

Abstract: We investigate whether Solar System dynamics can provide independent constraints on the present day cosmological deceleration parameter $q_0$, offering a complementary approach to conventional cosmological probes. By relating cosmic acceleration to a weak Hooke type radial perturbation, we derive bounds using planetary perihelion precession, trans-Neptunian objects, and Oort cloud dynamics. We obtain relatively robust planetary bounds of $|q_0|\lesssim 10^9$, while the outer Solar System yields potentially stronger, albeit more model dependent, constraints approaching $10^5$. Through Monte Carlo uncertainty propagation and sensitivity analyses, we explore the statistical robustness of these estimates and their dependence on orbital measurements. Finally, we demonstrate how future precision astrometry and outer planet missions could improve these limits by several orders of magnitude, establishing Solar System dynamics as a complementary laboratory for probing cosmological acceleration.

</details>


### [344] [Is Dark Energy Inhomogeneous?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11094)
*Oem Trivedi,Abraham Loeb*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 论文探讨宇宙中是否存在暗能量缺失区域（dark energy deficiency），提出一个唯象框架（phenomenological framework）通过非线性膨胀、相干本动速度（coherent peculiar velocities）与密度-动量关联区分暗能量缺失与普通物质空洞（matter voids）。结果表明10%的暗能量缺失可使典型非线性空洞膨胀抑制约2.7%，并产生60–100 km/s的本动速度残差；约20%、尺度达数百Mpc的缺失还可能影响局部哈勃常数（Hubble constant）推断，或与H0张力（H0 tension）相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质缺失星系（dark matter deficient galaxies）的发现引出一个基本问题：宇宙中是否也可能存在足够大的暗能量缺失区域。该研究由此动机出发，检验驱动宇宙加速膨胀的暗能量是否真正均匀。

Method: 构建一个唯象框架（phenomenological framework），从非线性膨胀（nonlinear expansion）、相干本动速度与密度-动量关联（density-momentum correlations）三方面区分真实的暗能量缺失与普通物质空洞，并结合各观测手段提出检验策略。

Result: 10%的暗能量缺失可将代表性非线性空洞的膨胀压低约2.7%，在30–50 Mpc尺度上产生量级为60–100 km/s的本动速度残差；延伸至数百Mpc、约20%的暗能量缺失亦可能影响局部哈勃常数推断，从而可能对H0张力有所贡献，但须满足严格的各向同性与动力学一致性约束（isotropy and dynamical consistency constraints）。

Conclusion: 研究提出可借助DESI、Rubin、本动速度巡天（peculiar velocity surveys）与宇宙空洞星表（cosmic void catalogues）进行观测检验，为验证暗能量的均匀性开辟新途径。

Abstract: The discovery of dark matter deficient galaxies motivates a fundamental question: Could sufficiently large regions of the Universe be deficient in dark energy as well? We investigate this possibility by developing a phenomenological framework that distinguishes genuine dark energy deficiencies from ordinary matter voids through nonlinear expansion, coherent peculiar velocities and density-momentum correlations. We show that a $10\%$ dark energy deficiency can suppress the expansion of a representative nonlinear void by approximately $2.7\%$, generating peculiar velocity residuals of order $60$-$100\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$ across scales of $30$-$50\, Mpc$. Intriguingly, we find that deficiencies of order $20\%$ extending over several hundred megaparsecs could also influence the locally inferred Hubble constant, potentially contributing to the $H_0$ tension, subject to stringent isotropy and dynamical consistency constraints. We identify observational strategies involving DESI, Rubin, peculiar velocity surveys, and cosmic void catalogues, opening a new avenue for testing whether the dark energy driving cosmic acceleration is truly homogeneous.

</details>


### [345] [Vacuum energy interacting with dark matter and dark radiation: alleviating the $H_0$-$S_8$ seesaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11835)
*Li-Yang Gao,She-Sheng Xue,Xin Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究晚期真空与暗扇区之间的能量交换能否抵消早期解决方案（暗辐射、早期暗能量）造成的S8升高。结果显示交换可降低S8但无法抬升H0，因而无助于缓解哈勃张力，且当前数据并不要求该交换存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升H0的ΛCDM扩展（如暗辐射与早期暗能量）同时会抬高S8，形成相互制约；作者希望检验晚期能量交换机制是否能"解耦"这两者的关联。

Method: 设定耦合率按m̄H^α标度，α≤−2时在复合前可忽略；能量沿测地冷暗物质的四速度转移，使真空产生的暗物质不带密度扰动。将其加入暗辐射模型（m̄Λ̃CDM）与单参数EDE模型（m̄EDE），用Planck 2018、DESI DR2 BAO、Pantheon+（CBS）及加入SH0ES标定（CBSH）进行约束。

Result: CBS下后验偏好真空向暗物质能量流（1.8–2.7σ），S8降低0.02–0.08但H0未提升（m̄Λ̃CDM给出H0=68.83±0.91 km/s/Mpc，低于SH0ES 3.1σ）。CBSH下交换消除了暗辐射引起的S8升高（S8=0.800±0.016，H0=71.34±0.70），对EDE（α=−2）约抵消三分之一，耦合显著度为1.1–2.0σ。S8下降集中于z≲0.3，KiDS-Legacy红移处透镜振幅仅降1–2%，剪切χ²变化小于3，EDE的过剩剪切χ²仅被减小未被消除。

Conclusion: 晚期能量交换可部分抵消早期解决方案带来的S8升高，但不能缓解哈勃张力（Hubble tension），且现有数据无需引入该交换机制。

Abstract: Extensions of $Λ$CDM that raise $H_0$ with additional energy before recombination, such as dark radiation and early dark energy (EDE), also raise $S_8$. We study whether a late-time energy exchange between the vacuum and the dark sector can offset this increase. The coupling rate scales as $\bar m H^α$ and is negligible before recombination for $α\le -2$; the energy is transferred along the four-velocity of geodesic cold dark matter, so the dark matter produced by the vacuum carries no density perturbation and slows the growth of structure. We add the exchange to dark radiation ($\bar m\tildeΛ$CDM) and to a one-parameter EDE model ($\bar m$EDE), and constrain the models with Planck 2018, DESI DR2 BAO and Pantheon+ data (CBS), and with the SH0ES calibration added (CBSH) to test the exchange when $H_0$ is held at a high value. For CBS the posteriors favour an energy flow from the vacuum into dark matter at 1.8-2.7$σ$, and the exchange lowers $S_8$ by 0.02-0.08 but does not raise $H_0$: $\bar m\tildeΛ$CDM gives $H_0 = 68.83 \pm 0.91$ km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$, 3.1$σ$ below SH0ES. For CBSH the exchange removes the increase of $S_8$ caused by dark radiation, giving $S_8 = 0.800 \pm 0.016$ at $H_0 = 71.34 \pm 0.70$ km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$, and about a third of that caused by EDE for $α= -2$, with the coupling at 1.1-2.0$σ$. The reduction of $S_8$ is concentrated at $z \lesssim 0.3$: at the redshifts probed by KiDS-Legacy the lensing amplitude decreases by only 1-2%, the shear $χ^2$ of the dark-radiation models changes by less than 3, and the exchange reduces but does not remove the excess shear $χ^2$ of EDE. A late-time exchange can therefore offset part of the $S_8$ increase of early-time solutions, but it does not ease the Hubble tension, and current data do not require it.

</details>


### [346] [Gravitational waves and the exit from de Sitter in Inflation without an Inflaton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12772)
*Daniele Bertacca,Raul Jimenez,Sabino Matarrese,Mariam Abdelaziz,Marisol Traforetti*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文在"无暴胀子暴胀（Inflation without an Inflaton, IWI）"框架下，用二阶 de Sitter（dS）引力子的梯度应力库仑型势产生原初曲率，并给出经由光滑退出进入辐射主导阶段的完整演化与观测预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是寻找不依赖暴胀子（inflaton）标量场的暴胀与曲率扰动产生机制，检验纯 de Sitter 背景下引力子非线性效应能否替代传统暴胀子图像并给出可观测谱。

Method: 方法包括：以 Bunch-Davies 演化求解二阶 dS 引力子的推迟横向无迹（transverse-traceless）解及单圈修正，将曲率从 dS 阶段经光滑退出演化至辐射主导；以"真空衰变超曲面"承载守恒曲率，并引入全局耗尽时钟（depletion clock）使各尺度均匀获得增强，再借助能量-动量守恒把耗尽的真空能转入辐射组分。

Result: 主要结果为：深辐射主导期 Δ²_ζ = c_w Δ_h⁴ e^{N_w}/20，谱指数 n_s−1 = −4ε、张标比相关 n_T = −2ε；谱为绝热、高斯型，超视界 ζ 守恒优于 10⁻⁴，更换绝热退出流体影响小于 0.2%；直接辐射退出要求 31≲N_w≲50，对应 3×10⁻⁶≲r≲0.036；干涉仪频段 Ω_GW h² 约 2×10⁻²¹–3×10⁻¹⁷。

Conclusion: 结论是 IWI 可给出自洽、绝热高斯的曲率谱且超视界守恒良好；引力波信号对 BBO（r≳0.01）、终极 DECIGO（r≳10⁻⁵）与 CMB B 模（r≳10⁻³）可及，且 k_end e^{−N_w} 以上标量谱陡化为 k⁻¹，在 N_w≳45 的尖锐释放下抑制 μ 型畸变。

Abstract: In Inflation without an Inflaton (IWI), second-order de Sitter (dS) gravitons generate primordial curvature as the Coulomb-type potential of their gradient stress. Each pair contributes $(p/k)^2$, yielding exponential enhancement over the contributing e-folds. With exact Bunch-Davies evolution, this solution satisfies all four scalar second-order Einstein equations in pure dS. The retarded transverse-traceless solution instead has a UV kernel of order unity, $(k/p)^2$ below the scalar kernel, and a one-loop correction $Δ^2_{h,2}/Δ^2_h\simeq0.04\,Δ^2_h$. Its source acts before internal freeze-out, but oscillatory cancellations prevent tensor enhancement.
  We evolve the curvature through a smooth exit into radiation domination. Conserved curvature resides on the vacuum-decay hypersurface. A global depletion clock holds each scale on zero-shear time for $N_{\rm w}$ e-folds after horizon exit before local release, giving all scales equal enhancement. Energy-momentum conservation feeds the depleted vacuum energy into a non-vacuum sector, modeled as radiation. Deep in radiation domination, $Δ^2_ζ=c_wΔ_h^4e^{N_{\rm w}}/20$, with clock-dependent $c_w$, $n_s-1=-4ε$, and $n_T=-2ε$. The spectrum is adiabatic and Gaussian; super-horizon $ζ$ is conserved to better than $10^{-4}$, and changing the adiabatic exit fluid alters it by less than $0.2\%$. A direct radiation exit requires $31\lesssim N_{\rm w}\lesssim50$, or $3\times10^{-6}\lesssim r\lesssim0.036$.
  At interferometer frequencies, $Ω_{\rm GW}h^2\simeq2\times10^{-21}$--$3\times10^{-17}$, accessible to BBO for $r\gtrsim0.01$ and ultimate DECIGO for $r\gtrsim10^{-5}$; CMB B-mode searches probe $r\gtrsim10^{-3}$. Above $k_{\rm end}e^{-N_{\rm w}}$, the scalar spectrum steepens to $k^{-1}$, suppressing $μ$-distortions for sharp release when $N_{\rm w}\gtrsim45$.

</details>


### [347] [Coupled dark energy and apparent phantom crossing: a potential-independent lower bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12792)
*Domenico Sapone,Esteban Arratia*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 基于DESI重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillation）等数据所偏好的暗能量状态方程符号翻转（phantom crossing），本文证明若由与暗物质耦合的标量场（scalar field）产生该表观翻转，则耦合强度存在由膨胀历史 $H(z)$ 决定的下界，并给出定量约束与可观测后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI联合CMB与Ia型超新星（type Ia supernovae）数据偏好过去 $w<-1$、现今 $w>-1$ 的暗能量，而标准标量场无法跨越 $w=-1$ 这条线；与暗物质耦合可使该跨越表观出现，因此需确定耦合必须多强。

Method: 将表观跨越归因于复合时期（recombination）之后暗物质质量的损失，其最小比例由 $H(z)$ 固定；对常数耦合 $β=d\ln m/dφ$，场的速度受 $H(z)$ 限制，由此导出与势函数无关的下界 $β_{\rm min}[H(z)]$，并应用于DESI DR2最佳拟合、公开马尔可夫链（Markov chains）及已发表的耦合精质（coupled quintessence）拟合。

Result: DESI DR2最佳拟合要求暗物质在 $z\simeq 0.8$ 前损失约5–7%质量，对应 $β_{\rm min}=0.047$–$0.058$；计入数据不确定度后，95%置信下界为 $|β|>0.025$–$0.038$。已发表的耦合精质拟合均满足该下界，其中常数耦合模型仅再现浅度跨越。

Conclusion: 耦合存在最小值，因而其两类效应具有最小规模：暗物质粒子间额外吸引至少为引力的千分之几，且引力透镜（lensing）信号比暗物质成团所预示的至少弱几个百分点；该下界对任意势函数成立，并在场于观测范围内静止时恰好取到。

Abstract: Baryon acoustic oscillation measurements from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), combined with the cosmic microwave background (CMB) and type Ia supernovae, prefer a dark energy whose equation of state was below $-1$ in the past and is above $-1$ today. A canonical scalar field cannot cross this line, but it can appear to do so if it is coupled to dark matter, and we ask how strong this coupling must be, for any potential. We show that the apparent crossing requires the dark matter to lose a minimum fraction of its mass after recombination, fixed by the expansion history $H(z)$. For a constant coupling $β=d\ln m/dφ$, the mass can change only as fast as the field moves, and the speed of the field is itself set by $H(z)$, so that the coupling must be larger than a minimum value $β_{\rm min}[H(z)]$. This bound holds for any potential, applies to the largest value of the coupling when the coupling depends on the field, and is reached exactly when the field comes to rest within the observed range. For the DESI DR2 best fits, the dark matter must lose about 5-7% of its mass by $z\simeq 0.8$, which requires $β_{\rm min}=0.047$-$0.058$; including the uncertainties of the data, the public DESI DR2 chains give the 95% lower limit $|β|>0.025$-$0.038$. We also apply the bound to published fits of coupled quintessence: all of them respect it, and those with a constant coupling reproduce only a shallow version of the crossing. Since the coupling cannot be smaller than $β_{\rm min}$, two known effects of the coupling have a minimum size: an extra attraction between dark matter particles of at least a few tenths of a per cent of gravity, and a lensing signal at least a few per cent weaker than the clustering of dark matter would suggest.

</details>


### [348] [Little Red Dots from Primordial Black Holes: Non-Gaussianity and Accretion Evade $μ$-Distortion Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12861)
*Shyam Balaji,Bryce Cyr,Sarah R. Geller,David I. Kaiser,Dario L. Lorenzoni,Evan McDonough,Wenzer Qin*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文首次对能产生超大质量黑洞种子且符合CMB谱畸变限制的非高斯原初扰动进行贝叶斯分析，发现对数正态与截断幂律谱在|f_NL|=O(1)、峰值振幅A=O(10^-3)时可解释"小红点"（LRDs）并规避μ畸变约束，且结果对吸积历史敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST在z≳4观测到的"小红点"（Little Red Dots, LRDs）暗示存在质量≳10^6 M_⊙的早期超大质量黑洞（supermassive black holes, SMBHs），其快速出现难以用基于Pop III恒星遗迹吸积与并合的常规种子机制解释，因而激发对原初黑洞（primordial black holes, PBHs）种子的兴趣；但产生如此大质量种子所需的功率谱增强受宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）谱畸变约束。

Method: 对功率谱增强模型（对数正态与截断幂律）开展马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）分析，变化局域非高斯性（local non-Gaussianity）与吸积历史，在满足COBE/FIRAS的μ畸变约束（分有/无要求产生黑洞种子两种情形）下进行贝叶斯探索。

Result: 两类模型均可在|f_NL|=O(1)、峰值振幅A=O(10^-3)时孕育LRDs并规避μ畸变界限，前提是纳入标准的爱丁顿限制吸积（Eddington-limited accretion）。结果对f_NL的正负值均有偏好；对负f_NL的偏好强烈依赖吸积历史，当黑洞占空比（duty cycle）f_duty≲0.1时消失。

Conclusion: JWST高红移LRDs族群可由原初扰动播种，同时与CMB数据相容；下一代CMB谱畸变巡天任务对该假说的检验具有重要意义。

Abstract: Recent JWST observations of "Little Red Dots" (LRDs) at $z \gtrsim 4$ suggest the existence of early supermassive black holes (SMBHs) with masses $\gtrsim 10^6\,M_\odot$. Their rapid emergence challenges conventional seeding and growth scenarios based on accretion and mergers of Population III stellar remnants, motivating renewed interest in primordial black holes (PBHs) formed from primordial perturbations.
