Explaining such massive seeds requires power spectrum enhancements that are constrained by cosmic microwave background (CMB) spectral distortions. In this work we perform the first comprehensive Bayesian exploration of non-Gaussian primordial perturbations capable of producing SMBH seeds while remaining consistent with CMB spectral distortion constraints, and examine the role of accretion history in constraining these models. We perform a suite of Markov chain Monte Carlo analyses of power spectrum enhancements (lognormal and cut-power-law) with varying local non-Gaussianity and accretion histories, satisfying the COBE/FIRAS $μ$-distortion constraint with and without the requirement that the model produces SMBH seeds. We find that both models can seed LRDs and evade $μ$-distortion bounds for $|f_{\rm NL}|= O(1)$ and peak amplitude $A= O(10^{-3})$, when standard Eddington-limited accretion is also incorporated. Interestingly, the results exhibit a preference for both positive and negative values of $f_{\rm NL}$. The preference for negative $f_{\rm NL}$ is strongly dependent on the accretion history, and is removed if the black holes have a small duty cycle, $f_{\rm duty}\lesssim 0.1$. These results demonstrate that the JWST high-$z$ LRD population can be seeded by primordial perturbations while remaining compatible with CMB data, and highlight the importance of next-generation CMB spectral distortion missions to test this hypothesis.

</details>


<div id='physics.pop-ph'></div>

# physics.pop-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [349] [Cosmic Corpses: Dead stars as Factories of the most Extreme Objects in the Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09988)
*Ankit C. Khunt,Swagat S. Mishra*

Main category: physics.pop-ph

TL;DR: 该文为教学性综述，阐述恒星演化终极产物——白矮星、中子星与黑洞——的形成与性质，并涉及原初黑洞及暗物质可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜空表面宁静掩盖了由引力与物质属性共同决定的漫长恒星演化历史，需澄清量子力学、核相互作用与广义相对论如何支配致密天体的形成与特征。

Method: 采用教学性（pedagogical）论述方式，围绕白矮星（white dwarfs）、中子星（neutron stars）、黑洞（black holes）三类天体展开概念性讲解，并简要讨论原初黑洞（primordial black holes）的起源机制。

Result: 说明量子力学（quantum mechanics）、核相互作用（nuclear interactions）与广义相对论（General Relativity）分别主导上述致密天体的形成与独特性质；原初黑洞可能源于早期宇宙（early Universe）中罕见的大密度涨落（large density fluctuations）。

Conclusion: 致密天体的多样性由基本物理规律统一解释，原初黑洞作为暗物质（dark matter）成分的可能性值得关注，但摘要仅作简要提及，未给出定量结论。

Abstract: The apparent serenity of the night sky conceals a long history of stellar evolution, in which gravity and the properties of matter determine the remarkable fates of stars. In this pedagogical account, we discuss white dwarfs, neutron stars, and black holes, explaining how quantum mechanics, nuclear interactions, and General Relativity govern their formation and distinctive properties. We also highlight the possibility of primordial black holes, which may have formed from rare large density fluctuations in the early Universe, and briefly discuss their potential role as constituents of dark matter.

</details>


### [350] [Work needed to lift a mass in Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11879)
*Anamta Asif,Andrzej Czarnecki*

Main category: physics.pop-ph

TL;DR: 该文用三种方法计算史瓦西时空（Schwarzschild spacetime）中将质量从 $r_2$ 提升至 $r_1$ 所需的功，三者结果一致，均为 $W_1=mc^2[1-N(r_2)/N(r_1)]$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为本科相对论课程提供教学素材，借助绞车绳索、光子碰撞与质量衰变重建三个直观例子，阐明引力场中提升质量的功，并说明绳端局部力不相等的广义相对论（general relativity）效应。

Method: 在史瓦西时空中分别采用三种途径：（1）绞车与轻绳提升；（2）光子碰撞；（3）将质量衰变为光子并在较高半径处重建。此外，对绳索的受力平衡作广义相对论分析，其中红移因子（redshift factor）起杠杆臂作用。

Result: 三种方法在 $r_1$ 处给出相同的局部功 $W_1=mc^2[1-N(r_2)/N(r_1)]$，其中 $N(r)=\sqrt{1-r_s/r}$；与 $r_1$ 无关，当 $r_2$ 趋近视界（horizon）时功趋于 $mc^2$。绳索分析表明平衡绳索两端局部力不必相等。

Conclusion: 提升功的结果与具体方法无关，具有普适性；绳索的杠杆优势源于红移因子，但对真实绳索而言，能量条件（energy conditions）禁止张力随高度增加而减小，故该优势仅存在于理想化情形。

Abstract: We determine the work needed to lift a mass from $r_2$ to $r_1$ in Schwarzschild spacetime in three ways. First we use a winch and a light rope. Then we use a photon collision. Finally we let the mass decay into photons and reconstruct it at the higher radius. All three methods give the same local work at $r_1$, $W_1=mc^2[1-N(r_2)/N(r_1)]$, where $N(r)=\sqrt{1-r_s/r}$. Thus, independently of $r_1$, the work tends to $mc^2$ as $r_2$ approaches the horizon. The rope example also gives a useful general-relativistic version of a simple machine: a rope in equilibrium need not have equal local forces at its two ends. The redshift factor plays the role of the lever arm. For a real rope the energy conditions prevent the tension from decreasing with height, so this lever advantage is confined to the idealization. The examples are intended as teaching material for undergraduate relativity courses.

</details>


<div id='math.QA'></div>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [351] [Integrals of motion for the deformed $W$-algebra $W_{q,t}(D_l)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10580)
*Michio Jimbo,Takeo Kojima*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文研究 $D_l$ 型变形 $W$-代数与量子环面代数的自由场实现，证明局部与相应的非局部运动积分（integrals of motion）在同一 Fock 空间上彼此对易。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $D_l$ 型变形 $W$-代数 $W_{q,t}(D_l)$ 存在无穷族对易元，可视为 $D_l$ 型 KdV 层级（KdV hierarchy）经典哈密顿量的两参数变形；同时量子环面代数 $U_q(D_l^{(1)})$ 具有四种自由场实现，二者关系有待阐明。

Method: 利用量子环面代数在仿射 Dynkin 图（affine Dynkin diagram）四个端点对应的四种自由场实现（free field realization），为每个端点构造无穷族对易算子即非局部运动积分；局部与非局部运动积分共同作用于由 $l+1$ 个自由场构成的 Fock 空间（Fock space），并通过直接计算给出证明。

Result: 给出直接的计算性证明，表明两类运动积分在同一 Fock 空间上相互对易。

Conclusion: 局部运动积分与非局部运动积分在共享 Fock 空间上构成对易算子族，为 $D_l$ 型可积结构（integrable structure）的变形对应提供了计算层面的证据。

Abstract: The deformed $W$-algebra $W_{q,t}(D_l)$ of type $D_l$ possesses an infinite family of commuting elements that can be viewed as a two-parameter deformation of the classical Hamiltonians for the KdV hierarchy of type $D_l$; these conserved charges are termed local integrals of motion (IMs). Concurrently, the quantum toroidal algebra $U_q(D_l^{"})$ of type $D_l$ admits four distinct representations via free field realizations, each corresponding to one of the four end nodes of the affine Dynkin diagram. Utilizing these representations, we associate an infinite set of commuting operators with each node, which we define as non-local integrals of motion. Both local and non-local integrals of motion act simultaneously on a shared Fock space consisting of $l+1$ free fields. We provide a direct, computational proof demonstrating that these local and non-local integrals of motion mutually commute.

</details>


### [352] [FRT presentation of the $q$-Onsager comodule algebra and refined $q$-characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12874)
*Pascal Baseilhac,Azat M. Gainutdinov*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文引入Hopf代数上带余单位的余模代数（counital comodule algebra）概念，并系统研究B=O_q（q-Onsager代数）、H=量子圈代数（quantum loop algebra）L U_q sl_2 的情形，建立FRT型表示并应用于q-特征标（q-character）的精细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为刻画q-Onsager代数与量子圈代数之间的结构联系，需要一个统一的代数框架（带余单位的余模代数）来组织余作用、余单位及诱导映射，并借此深化对既有生成函数定理与q-特征标构造的理解。

Method: 定义Hopf K-代数H上的带余单位余模代数(B,δ_B,ε_B)及其典范映射ι_B: B→H；对B=O_q（q-Onsager代数，含q-Dolan-Grady型、交错型、根向量型、Drinfeld型四种表示）与H=L U_q sl_2（K=C）构造3参数族的FRT型表示，用满足反射代数关系（reflection algebra relation）的K-算子表述，并结合Ding-Frenkel型L-算子计算像。

Result: 建立了以K-算子为核心的FRT型表示；δ_{O_q}与ι_{O_q}分别将K-算子映为"dressed" K-算子与K-矩阵，ε_{O_q}将其映为反射方程（reflection equation）最一般的非对角解；据此计算了表示(ii)-(iv)下O_q生成函数在δ_{O_q}与ι_{O_q}下的像，并得到Lu-Wang生成函数在Drinfeld第二实现中的像及其在余积下的像，强化了Przeździecki的两个定理。

Conclusion: 利用该FRT型表示，以O_q的虚根向量（imaginary root vectors）构造了L U_q sl_2的新型q-特征标，细化了Frenkel-Reshetikhin的结果，并说明了该细化的表示论意义。

Abstract: We introduce a concept of counital comodule algebra $(B,δ_B,ε_B)$ over a Hopf ${\mathbb K}$-algebra $H$, where $δ_B: B\rightarrow B \otimes H$ and $ε_B: B\rightarrow {\mathbb K}$ are coaction and counit homomorphisms, respectively. This structure gives a canonical (counital) comodule algebra map $ι_B: B \rightarrow H$. In this paper, we study in details the case $B=O_q$, and $H$ is the quantum loop algebra ${\cal L}U_qsl_2$ for ${\mathbb K}={\mathbb C}$. Here, $O_q$ is the $q$-Onsager algebra with four presentations: (i) the $q$-Dolan-Grady type; (ii) the alternating type; (iii) the root vectors type; (iv) the Drinfeld type. For $(O_q,δ_{O_q},ε_{O_q})$, in fact a 3-parameter family of counital comodule algebras, we establish a Faddeev-Reshetikhin-Takhtajan (FRT) type presentation, which is written in terms of a K-operator satisfying a reflection algebra relation. The coaction $δ_{O_q}$ (resp. $ι_{O_q}$) sends the K-operator to a `dressed' K-operator (resp. K-matrix) using Ding-Frenkel type L-operators of ${\cal L}U_qsl_2$; the counit $ε_{O_q}$ sends the K-operator to the most general (non-diagonal) solution of the reflection equation. The FRT presentation allows to compute the image via $δ_{O_q}$ and $ι_{O_q}$ of the generating functions of $O_q$ for each presentation (ii)-(iv). Moreover, the images of Lu-Wang's generating functions of $O_q$ in the so-called Drinfeld second realization ${}^{Dr}\!{\cal L}U_qsl_2$ are obtained, as well as their images via the (ordinary) coproduct of ${}^{Dr}\!{\cal L}U_qsl_2$, which enhance two theorems by Przeździecki. Finally, we apply our FRT presentation to the construction of new type $q$-characters for ${\cal L}U_qsl_2$, in imaginary root vectors of $O_q$, refining those of Frenkel-Reshetikhin, and we explain representation-theoretic meaning of such refining.

</details>


### [353] [Holomorphic integer-graded vertex operator superalgebras are purely even](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12417)
*Chongying Dong,Kaiwen Sun,Haowu Wang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文证明：在自对偶（self-contragredient）、有理（rational）、C₂-余有限（C₂-cofinite）、整分次、全纯且属于共形场论型（CFT-type）的顶点算子超代数（vertex operator superalgebra）中，奇数部分必为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确定此类全纯顶点算子超代数的结构，即排除其存在非平凡奇数部分（odd part）的可能性。

Method: 先假设奇数部分非零，证明它会是某个具有四个单模（simple modules）的强有理（strongly rational）尖顶点算子代数（pointed VOA）上范畴维数为 -1 的单流（simple current）；再证明强有理 VOA 的任何非真空单流具有严格正的最低共形权重（lowest conformal weight）。

Result: 非零奇数部分的假设导出的推论与 Dong-Jiao-Xu 关于范畴维数正性的定理相矛盾，故奇数部分只能为零。

Conclusion: 满足上述条件的整分次全纯顶点算子超代数必为纯偶（purely even），即不存在奇数部分。

Abstract: We prove that a simple, self-contragredient, rational, $C_2$-cofinite, integer-graded holomorphic vertex operator superalgebra of CFT-type has zero odd part. We first show that a hypothetical nonzero odd part would be a simple current of categorical dimension $-1$ over a strongly rational pointed VOA with four simple modules. We then prove that every nonvacuum simple current of a strongly rational VOA has strictly positive lowest conformal weight. This leads to a contradiction with Dong-Jiao-Xu's theorem on the positivity of categorical dimension.

</details>


### [354] [Property F implies weak integrality for non-degenerate pivotal braided fusion categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12836)
*Justin Asher,Jonah Berean-Dutcher,Clay Cordova,Julio Parra-Martinez,Richard Tuddenham*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文证明：具有性质 F（property F）的非退化 pivotal 编织融合范畴（pivotal braided fusion category）必为伪酉（pseudo-unitary）且弱整（weakly integral）；由此所有具性质 F 的模范畴（modular category）均为弱整。结果进而经 Drinfeld 中心（Drinfeld centre）推广至特定退化情形，并与任意子编织的计算能力相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画性质 F 对范畴结构与维数算术的约束，并在不预设球性（sphericality）或伪酉性的条件下建立伪酉性与弱整性之间的必然联系。物理动机在于将拓扑序（topological order）的算术性质与任意子编织（anyon braiding）的通用量子计算能力相关联。

Method: 对具性质 F 的非退化 pivotal 编织融合范畴作范畴论论证，导出伪酉性与弱整性；再通过过渡到 Drinfeld 中心，把结论扩展至满足特定正维数条件的退化范畴，涵盖所有存在伪酉 Galois 共轭（Galois conjugate）的编织融合范畴。

Result: 证明了具性质 F 的非退化 pivotal 编织融合范畴是伪酉且弱整的，且无需假设球性或伪酉性；所有具性质 F 的模范畴均为弱整。经 Drinfeld 中心的推广覆盖了 Müger 中心（Müger centre）与伴随子范畴（adjoint subcategory）之交上维数为正的退化范畴。

Conclusion: 非整的总量子维数平方（squared total quantum dimension）意味着编织群（braid group）的像无限，而这是通用量子计算的必要条件，从而在拓扑序算术与任意子编织计算能力之间建立了联系。

Abstract: We prove that every non-degenerate pivotal braided fusion category with property F is pseudo-unitary and weakly integral. In particular, every modular category with property F is weakly integral. Neither sphericality nor pseudo-unitarity is assumed. Passing to the Drinfeld centre extends the result to degenerate categories whose dimensions are positive on the intersection of the Müger centre with the adjoint subcategory; these include every braided fusion category with a pseudo-unitary Galois conjugate. Physically, the result connects the arithmetic of topological orders to the computational power of anyon braiding: non-integer squared total quantum dimension guarantees infinite braid group images, a necessary condition for universal quantum computation.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [355] [From many-body Schrödinger dynamics to magnetic Vlasov equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10583)
*Li Chen,Sebastian Stegmüller*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究无自旋费米子（spinless fermions）在非常数磁场中随时间演化的薛定谔动力学，在标度（scaling）$\hbar = N^{-1/3}$ 下取平均场（mean-field）与半经典（semiclassical）联合极限，证明其收敛到磁性 Vlasov 方程（magnetic Vlasov equation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画多体量子动力学在平均场与半经典联合极限下向经典动理学方程——磁性 Vlasov 方程——的有效约化，并处理非常数磁场带来的技术困难。

Method: 借助速度平移相干态（velocity-shifted coherent states）与所诱导的磁性 Husimi 测度（magnetic Husimi measure），构造出余项为 $\hbar^{1/2-}$ 阶的精确 BBGKY 层级（BBGKY hierarchy）。

Result: $k$-粒子磁性 Husimi 测度弱 $L^1$ 收敛到磁性 Vlasov 方程解的 $k$ 个副本（$k$-copies）；当初始 Husimi 测度的二阶矩相应收敛时，收敛亦在 Wasserstein 距离（Wasserstein distance）$\mathbb{W}_2$ 下成立。

Conclusion: 在给定标度下，费米子薛定谔动力学可有效近似为磁性 Vlasov 方程，收敛性以弱 $L^1$ 建立，并在二阶矩收敛条件下提升至 $\mathbb{W}_2$ 度量。

Abstract: We analyse the combined mean-field and semiclassical limit from the time-dependent Schrödinger dynamics of $N$ spinless fermions in a non-constant magnetic field to the magnetic Vlasov equation with scaling $\hbar = N^{-1/3}$. With the velocity-shifted coherent states and the induced magnetic Husimi measure, we derive an exact BBGKY hierarchy with a remainder of order $\hbar^{1/2-}$. The main result is the weak $L^1$ convergence of the $k$-particle magnetic Husimi measures to the $k$-copies of the solution to the magnetic Vlasov equation. Furthermore, the convergence holds in the Wasserstein distance $\mathbb{W}_2$ when the second moment of initial Husimi measure correspondingly converges.

</details>


### [356] [Mean Field Limit Towards Kinetic Equation with Repulsive Dirac-Delta Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10603)
*José A. Carrillo,Li Chen,Paul Nikolaev*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文从适度相互作用（moderate interaction）的二阶粒子系统（second order particle system）出发，严格推导出一维含排斥 Dirac-Delta 势（repulsive Dirac-Delta potential）的单粒子有效动理学方程（effective kinetic equation），并证明经验测度（empirical measure）与 Vlasov 方程解之间的 Wasserstein 距离（Wasserstein distance）趋于零且给出收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标在于为一维排斥 Dirac-Delta 势相互作用粒子系统提供从微观动力学到 Vlasov 型平均场动理学方程（Vlasov equation）的严格推导，并量化收敛性，从而补充该奇异性势情形下的理论依据。

Method: 方法结合粒子轨迹（particle trajectory）估计与平滑化调制能量（smoothed version of modulated energy）的估计，用于控制经验测度与 Vlasov 解之间的 Wasserstein 距离并导出收敛速率。

Result: 论文证明了经验测度与 Vlasov 方程解之间的 Wasserstein 距离收敛到零，并通过轨迹估计与平滑调制能量估计的组合获得了明确的收敛速率（convergence rate）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在一维排斥 Dirac-Delta 势与适度相互作用条件下，二阶粒子系统的单粒子有效动理学描述可由 Vlasov 方程严格刻画，且收敛性具有可量化的速率。

Abstract: A one-particle effective kinetic equation with repulsive Dirac-Delta potential in one dimension is rigorously derived from a moderate interacting second order particle system. It is proved that the Wasserstein distance between the empirical measure and the solution of Vlasov equation converges to zero. A convergence rate is obtained by a combination of the estimates for particle trajectory and a smoothed version of modulated energy.

</details>


### [357] [Microlocal properties of Cauchy evolution under metric variations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12488)
*Alexander Strohmaier*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文在无穷维洛伦兹度规空间上对波型方程的柯西演化（Cauchy evolution）建立微局部分析（microlocal analysis），把演化算子视为度规的分布型函数，并通过限制到有限维度规族赋予其严格含义，进而刻画其对度规依赖的奇异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柯西演化算子依赖于背景洛伦兹度规，而度规空间是无穷维的，直接讨论其对度规的分布型依赖性缺乏严格框架；因此需要借助有限维限制给出精确定义，并理解这种依赖的奇异性结构及其物理含义。

Method: 将柯西演化算子视为度规的分布型函数，通过限制到有限维度规族（finite-dimensional families of metrics）赋予其严格意义；对向量丛上的正规双曲算子（normally hyperbolic operators）确定算子值波前集（operator-valued wavefront sets），其参数余向量由度规变分沿各条零测地线（null geodesics）的积分给出；并推广至狄拉克方程（Dirac equation）的柯西演化。

Result: 参数余向量沿单条零测地线的度规变分积分给出；在超静态端（ultrastatic ends），度规依赖还决定时间平移生成元及其正负能量分裂；更一般地，参数奇异性可区分两张特征叶（characteristic sheets），并可用于重构描述奇异性传播的普通柯西传播关系。推论包括参数平均下的光滑化（smoothing）以及与平均零正变分相关的极锥界（polar-cone bounds），从而与平均零能量条件（averaged null energy conditions）建立联系。

Conclusion: 该工作为无穷维度规空间上的柯西演化提供了严格的微局部分析框架，揭示度规依赖的奇异性由零测地线上的变分积分刻画，并借此联系奇异性传播、能量分裂与平均零能量条件，且可延伸至狄拉克方程情形。

Abstract: We develop the microlocal analysis of the Cauchy evolution for wave-type equations on the infinite-dimensional space of Lorentzian metrics. We regard the Cauchy evolution operator as a distributional function of the metric, and give this notion a precise meaning through restrictions to finite-dimensional families of metrics. We study the singularities of this metric dependence. For normally hyperbolic operators on vector bundles, we determine the resulting operator-valued wavefront sets. Their parameter covectors are given by integrals of metric variations along individual null geodesics. At ultrastatic ends, metric dependence also determines the time-translation generators and their positive- and negative-energy splitting. More generally, the parameter singularities distinguish the two characteristic sheets and can be used to reconstruct the ordinary Cauchy propagation relation, which describes propagation of singularities. Consequences include smoothing under parameter averaging and polar-cone bounds associated with averaged null-positive variations, linking the result to averaged null energy conditions. The analysis also covers the Cauchy evolution of the Dirac equation.

</details>


### [358] [Quantitative global regularity for Navier--Stokes equations with odd viscosity on the cubic three-torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12862)
*Jinzheng Li,Yuqi Li,Hao-Yu Sun*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究立方环面（cubic torus）上带有常数奇粘性算子（odd-viscosity operator，取旋度的垂向导数）的三维不可压 Navier–Stokes 方程，证明在奇粘性系数足够大时任意二阶 Sobolev 空间初值与定常外力均导致唯一整体强解，并在 Lean 4 中完成形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维不可压 Navier–Stokes 方程（Navier–Stokes equation）的整体正则性是公开难题，本文通过引入奇粘性色散（odd-viscous dispersion）项，考察其能否在无需小初值假设下抑制非线性并在大系数时保证整体适定性。

Method: 将快速旋转流体（rapidly rotating fluids）的共振平均（resonant-averaging）框架适配到该奇粘性色散情形，结合加权格点计数（weighted lattice counts）与带符号的修正能量估计（signed corrected-energy estimates）；并利用直接的动能与加权拟涡能（enstrophy）障碍，借助有限证书（finite certificates）把估计接入解析论证。

Result: 当固定奇粘性系数的绝对值超过一个与范数无关的充分阈值时，对任意实值、零均值、无散的二阶 Sobolev 初值与独立定常外力存在唯一整体强解；无外力时正时刻解光滑。得到奇/常粘性之比的充分上界：在每个固定归一化外力半径下为初值半径的多项式，并在外力主导区（force-dominated regime）给出单独界。此外在显式有界数据与外力区域（含半径 97/40 倍粘性的无外力球与七个闭合外力矩形）对任意系数给出正则性。

Conclusion: 论文给出的是充分性结果，未声称多项式指数或前因子最优，原方程的最优粘性阈值（sharp viscosity threshold）仍为公开问题；并明确区分充分公式的极小化与原方程的尖锐性。三个主要定理均在 Lean 4 与 Mathlib 中形式化验证。

Abstract: We study the three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes equation on the cubic torus with a constant odd-viscosity operator given by the vertical derivative of the curl. Arbitrary real, mean-zero, divergence-free initial data and an independent steady force, both in the second-order Sobolev space, admit a unique global strong solution when the magnitude of one fixed odd-viscosity coefficient is at least a norm-uniform sufficient threshold. In the unforced case the solution is smooth at positive times. The qualitative argument adapts the resonant-averaging framework for rapidly rotating fluids to the different odd-viscous dispersion. Weighted lattice counts and signed corrected-energy estimates yield a sufficient bound for the odd-to-ordinary viscosity ratio that is polynomial in the normalized initial-data radius at each fixed normalized force radius, together with a separate bound in a specified force-dominated regime. No optimality is claimed for the polynomial exponents or prefactors; determining the sharp viscosity threshold for the original equation remains open. Direct kinetic and weighted enstrophy barriers also give regularity for every coefficient on explicit bounded data and force regions, including the closed unforced ball of homogeneous second-order Fourier Sobolev radius ninety-seven fortieths times the viscosity and seven closed forced rectangles. We specify how finite certificates enter the analytic argument and distinguish minimization of a sufficient formula from sharpness for the original equation. All three main theorems are formally verified in Lean 4 with Mathlib.

</details>

### [359] [Rapid Heat Removal from Chip Cores: Near-Junction Active Cooling of Power Transistors via Electron Entropy Transport and Spatial Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11716)
*Xiangying Li*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该文针对芯片散热瓶颈，以RF功率GaN HEMT（热点超过5 kW/cm²）为最难案例，提出基于电子熵输运（electron entropy transport）的水平近结主动冷却拓扑，通过将热电薄膜平铺于漏极旁的金刚石衬底上，使体焦耳热垂直导出，从而打破Peltier吸热与Joule产热的耦合限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 芯片性能受共同瓶颈制约：核心产热速度快于热量导出速度。经典片上热电冷却（thermoelectric cooling）存在固有矛盾——Peltier吸收随电流线性增长，而Joule产热随电流平方增长，垂直柱状结构中自身导热电阻会将约一半焦耳热送回冷端，限制净冷却效果。

Method: 基于Onsager框架（Onsager framework）揭示不对称性来源：Peltier吸收局域于Seebeck系数（Seebeck coefficient）跃变的界面，而Joule产热充满整个体积。据此设计水平近结拓扑：热电薄膜平铺于器件漏极（电子出口）旁的金刚石衬底上，体焦耳热垂直进入衬底；并建立横向翅片模型（lateral fin model）给出回流因子gamma的闭式解。

Result: 垂直柱结构gamma = 1，水平薄膜gamma_max = sqrt(2)·L_z/L_x，在L_z = 10 µm、L_x = 500 µm时约为0.03；解耦使净冷却上限与临界逃逸电流（critical runaway current）提升约1/gamma。等电阻条件下，同一薄膜将最大冷却从13 mW提升至500 mW，临界电流从0.65 A提升至24 A。

Conclusion: 通过时间常数与线性稳定性分析（含温度敏感参数）界定了微秒脉冲冷却的工作窗口与安全裕度。该架构不改动器件结构，其“界面吸热、体热垂直导出”原理适用于任何具热核心的芯片，为近结热管理（near-junction thermal management）提供可复现的设计路径。

Abstract: Chip performance is limited by a shared bottleneck: the core produces heat faster than it can be moved out. We study the hardest case, RF power GaN HEMTs, whose hotspots exceed 5 kW/cm^2, and propose a horizontal near-junction active cooling topology built on electron entropy transport. Classical on-chip thermoelectric cooling faces a contest: Peltier absorption grows linearly with current, Joule heating with its square. In a vertical pillar, its own conduction resistance locks about half the Joule heat back toward the cold end, capping net cooling. From the Onsager framework we show the origin of this asymmetry: Peltier absorption localizes at the interface where the Seebeck coefficient jumps, whereas Joule heating fills the volume. The topology exploits this: a thermoelectric thin film lies flat on a diamond substrate beside the device drain (the electron exit), so bulk Joule heat leaves vertically into the substrate. A lateral fin model gives a closed form for the recirculation factor gamma: the vertical pillar yields gamma = 1, while the horizontal film yields gamma_max = sqrt(2) L_z/L_x, about 0.03 for L_z = 10 um, L_x = 500 um. The decoupling scales the net cooling ceiling and the critical runaway current by roughly 1/gamma. At equal resistance, the same film lifts maximum cooling from 13 mW to 500 mW, and the critical current from 0.65 A to 24 A. A time-constant and linear stability analysis with temperature-sensitive parameters defines the working window and safety margins for microsecond pulse cooling. The architecture leaves the device structure untouched; the principle, absorb at an interface and extract bulk heat vertically, applies to any chip with a hot core and offers a reproducible design path for near-junction thermal management.

</details>

### [360] [Pluriclosed flow on Kähler manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11614)
*Jeffrey Streets*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明了 Kähler 背景（Kähler backgrounds）上多闭流（pluriclosed flow）的最大存在性猜想（maximal existence conjecture），并由此给出 Aubin-Yau 定理与 Calabi-Yau 定理的先验推广，同时解决了 Calabi-Yau 背景上广义 Kähler 结构（generalized Kähler structures）的连通性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要仅说明所解决的问题类型（最大存在性猜想、连通性问题），未明确交代研究动机，故动机部分无法据摘要进一步展开。

Method: 核心工具为多闭流（pluriclosed flow），研究框架限定于 Kähler 背景（Kähler backgrounds）；摘要未披露具体技术细节或证明策略。

Result: 证明了 Kähler 背景下多闭流的最大存在性猜想，并得到 Aubin-Yau 定理与 Calabi-Yau 定理的先验推广（a priori generalizations）。

Conclusion: 作为推论，解决了 Calabi-Yau 背景上广义 Kähler 结构（generalized Kähler structures）的连通性问题（connectedness）。

Abstract: We prove the maximal existence conjecture for pluriclosed flow on Kähler backgrounds. This leads to a priori generalizations of the Aubin-Yau and Calabi-Yau theorems, and resolves the question of connectedness of generalized Kähler structures on Calabi-Yau backgrounds.

</details>


### [361] [On Continuum Theories in a Locally Conformal Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12656)
*Begüm Ateşli,Oğul Esen,Miroslav Grmela,Michal Pavelka*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: We study continuum and kinetic dynamics on locally conformally symplectic (LCS) and locally conformally cosymplectic (LCCos) manifolds. The main geometric tool is the Lichnerowicz-de Rham differential, which allows local geometric data that are compatible only up to conformal rescaling to glue into global structures.
  In the LCS setting, we analyze the locally conformal Vlasov equation from the viewpoint of continuity. Its source term does not represent particle creation or annihilation: it measures the mismatch between the ordinary phase-space volume and the conformally weighted volume determined by the Lee form. Absorbing this weight into the density yields a source-free continuity equation. We also compute the entropy production of the transported density, and illustrate the framework through a thermostatted ensemble, an LCS-driven Vlasov model with a momentum-dependent gyroscopic force, and their combination.
  We then propose a new definition of LCCos manifolds in which the defining one-form and two-form carry the same conformal weight. Unlike the usual convention, this allows local cosymplectic Hamiltonian systems to glue directly into global LCCos Hamiltonian dynamics, and it normalizes the evolution vector field by the conformal unit rather than by the ordinary unit. We develop the corresponding Hamiltonian, evolution and continuum dynamics, and illustrate them with a time-dependent mass example exhibiting transport in the distinguished time direction, momentum-space drift, and conformal source terms.

</details>


### [362] [Gamma classes, central connection matrices, and tt* geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11338)
*Martin A. Guest*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文从拓扑-反拓扑融合（topological-antitopological fusion）视角重新审视复射影空间（complex projective space）的 gamma 类（gamma class）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复射影空间的 gamma 类此前已在 Dubrovin、Galkin-Golyshev-Iritani 与 Cotti-Dubrovin-Guzzetti 等工作中出现，但其中心连接矩阵（central connection matrix）的结构尚未从拓扑-反拓扑融合角度加以阐释。

Method: 采用拓扑-反拓扑融合的框架，对相关文献中出现的中心连接矩阵进行解构（deconstruct）分析。

Result: 摘要未给出具体计算或数值结果，仅说明对中心连接矩阵实现了基于拓扑-反拓扑融合视角的解构。

Conclusion: 拓扑-反拓扑融合为理解复射影空间 gamma 类及其中心连接矩阵提供了一个统一的解释视角。

Abstract: We review the gamma class of complex projective space, but from the point of view of topological-antitopological fusion. We deconstruct the central connection matrix which appears in the work of Dubrovin, Galkin-Golyshev-Iritani, and Cotti-Dubrovin-Guzzetti.

</details>

### [363] [Efficient classical simulation of Fermi-Hubbard models with Kramers symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10039)
*Chaithanya Rayudu,Jun Takahashi,Ojas Parekh,Andrew Zhao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对具有Kramers时间反演对称性的费米子系统，提出量子蒙特卡罗算法，并证明其配分函数可在多项式时间内估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子蒙特卡罗方法（quantum Monte Carlo）广泛用于求解量子多体问题，但其性能缺乏严格保证，尤其是模拟相互作用费米子（interacting fermions）时理论认识不足。

Method: 针对一大类具有Kramers时间反演对称性（Kramers time-reversal symmetry）的费米子系统，发展相应的量子蒙特卡罗算法；该类系统涵盖多类重要的Hubbard型模型（Hubbard-type models）。

Result: 证明该族系统的配分函数（partition function）可在多项式时间（polynomial time）内估计；该族系统已知同时满足无符号问题（sign problem）与Lee-Yang零点自由性定理（Lee-Yang zero-freeness theorem）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无符号问题与Lee-Yang零点自由性这两条性质结合，可能更广泛地支撑量子多体系统的高效经典算法（classical algorithms）。

Abstract: Quantum Monte Carlo methods are widely used to solve quantum many-body problems. However, rigorous guarantees for their performance are poorly understood, especially for simulating interacting fermions. In this work, we develop a quantum Monte Carlo algorithm for a broad family of fermionic systems exhibiting Kramers time-reversal symmetry, and prove that their partition functions can be estimated in polynomial time. This family, which contains many important classes of Hubbard-type models, is known to both avoid the sign problem and obey a Lee-Yang zero-freeness theorem. Thus our results suggest that these two properties together may enable efficient classical algorithms for quantum many-body systems more broadly.

</details>
